




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

vinayapiṭakai


cūḷavaggapāḷi


1. kammakkhandhakaṃ

1. taj‍janīyakammaṃ

1. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū attanā bhaṇḍanakārakā kalahakārakā vivādakārakā bhassakārakā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā, yaipi cañ‍ñai bhikkhū bhaṇḍanakārakā kalahakārakā vivādakārakā bhassakārakā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā tai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘mā khau tumhai, āyasmantau, aisau ajaisi| balavābalavaṃ paṭimantaitha| tumhai taina paṇḍitatarā ca byattatarā ca bahussutatarā ca alamattatarā ca alamatthatarā ca (syā॰ ka॰)| mā cassa bhāyittha| mayampi tumhākaṃ pakkhā bhavissāmā’’ti| taina anuppan‍nāni caiva bhaṇḍanāni uppaj‍janti  uppan‍nāni ca bhaṇḍanāni bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattanti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū attanā bhaṇḍanakārakā kalahakārakā vivādakārakā bhassakārakā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā, yaipi cañ‍ñai bhikkhū bhaṇḍanakārakā  kalahakārakā vivādakārakā bhassakārakā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā tai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vakkhanti – ‘mā khau tumhai, āyasmantau, aisau ajaisi| balavābalavaṃ  paṭimantaitha| tumhai taina paṇḍitatarā ca byattatarā ca bahussutatarā ca alamattatarā ca| mā cassa bhāyittha| mayampi tumhākaṃ pakkhā bhavissāmā’ti| taina anuppan‍nāni caiva bhaṇḍanāni uppaj‍janti uppan‍nāni ca bhaṇḍanāni bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattantī’’ti|

2. atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū attanā  bhaṇḍanakārakā kalahakārakā vivādakārakā bhassakārakā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā, yaipi cañ‍ñai bhikkhū bhaṇḍanakārakā kalahakārakā vivādakārakā bhassakārakā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā tai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘mā khau tumhai, āyasmantau, aisau ajaisi| balavābalavaṃ paṭimantaitha| tumhai taina paṇḍitatarā ca byattatarā ca bahussutatarā ca alamattatarā ca| mā cassa bhāyittha| mayampi tumhākaṃ pakkhā bhavissāmā’ti| taina anuppan‍nāni caiva bhaṇḍanāni uppaj‍janti, uppan‍nāni ca bhaṇḍanāni bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattantī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi  buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ maughapurisānaṃ ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā attanā bhaṇḍanakārakā…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā, yaipi cañ‍ñai bhikkhū bhaṇḍanakārakā…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā tai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vakkhanti – ‘mā khau tumhai, āyasmantau, aisau ajaisi| balavābalavaṃ paṭimantaitha| tumhai taina paṇḍitatarā ca byattatarā ca bahussutatarā ca alamattatarā ca| mā cassa bhāyittha| mayampi tumhākaṃ pakkhā bhavissāmā’ti? taina anuppan‍nāni caiva bhaṇḍanāni uppaj‍janti, uppan‍nāni ca bhaṇḍanāni bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattanti| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya pasan‍nānaṃ vā bhiyyaubhāvāya| atha khvaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānañ‍caiva appasādāya pasan‍nānañ‍ca aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattāyā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā tai paṇḍukalauhitakai (syā॰) bhikkhū anaikapariyāyaina vigarahitvā dubbharatāya duppausatāya mahicchatāya  asantuṭṭhitāya asantuṭṭhatāya (syā॰), asantuṭṭhiyā (sī॰) saṅgaṇikāya kausaj‍jassa  avaṇṇaṃ bhāsitvā anaikapariyāyaina subharatāya supausatāya appicchassa santuṭṭhassa sal‍laikhassa dhutassa pāsādikassa apacayassa vīriyārambhassa viriyārambhassa (sī॰), vīriyārabbhassa (ka॰) vaṇṇaṃ bhāsitvā bhikkhūnaṃ tadanucchavikaṃ tadanulaumikaṃ  dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karautu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbaṃ| paṭhamaṃ paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū caudaitabbā, caudaitvā sāraitabbā, sāraitvā āpattiṃ āpatti (sī॰ syā॰) āraupaitabbā, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

3. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū attanā bhaṇḍanakārakā kalahakārakā vivādakārakā bhassakārakā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā, yaipi cañ‍ñai bhikkhū bhaṇḍanakārakā kalahakārakā vivādakārakā bhassakārakā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā tai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘mā  khau tumhai, āyasmantau, aisau ajaisi| balavābalavaṃ paṭimantaitha| tumhai taina paṇḍitatarā ca byattatarā ca bahussutatarā ca alamattatarā ca| mā cassa bhāyittha| mayampi tumhākaṃ pakkhā bhavissāmā’ti| taina anuppan‍nāni caiva bhaṇḍanāni uppaj‍janti, uppan‍nāni ca bhaṇḍanāni bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattanti| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū attanā bhaṇḍanakārakā…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā, yaipi cañ‍ñai bhikkhū bhaṇḍanakārakā…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā tai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘mā khau tumhai, āyasmantau, aisau ajaisi| balavābalavaṃ paṭimantaitha| tumhai taina paṇḍitatarā ca byattatarā ca bahussutatarā ca alamattatarā ca| mā cassa bhāyittha| mayampi tumhākaṃ pakkhā bhavissāmā’ti| taina anuppan‍nāni caiva bhaṇḍanāni uppaj‍janti, uppan‍nāni ca bhaṇḍanāni bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattanti| saṅghau paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti| yassāyasmatau khamati paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū attanā bhaṇḍanakārakā…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā, yaipi cañ‍ñai bhikkhū bhaṇḍanakārakā…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā tai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘mā khau tumhai, āyasmantau, aisau ajaisi| balavābalavaṃ paṭimantaitha| tumhai taina paṇḍitatarā ca byattatarā ca bahussutatarā ca alamattatarā ca| mā cassa bhāyittha| mayampi tumhākaṃ pakkhā bhavissāmā’ti| taina anuppan‍nāni caiva bhaṇḍanāni uppaj‍janti, uppan‍nāni ca bhaṇḍanāni bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattanti| saṅghau paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti| yassāyasmatau khamati paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū attanā bhaṇḍanakārakā…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā, yaipi cañ‍ñai bhikkhū bhaṇḍanakārakā…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā tai upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘mā khau tumhai, āyasmantau, aisau ajaisi| balavābalavaṃ paṭimantaitha| tumhai taina paṇḍitatarā ca byattatarā ca bahussutatarā ca alamattatarā ca| mā cassa bhāyittha| mayampi tumhākaṃ pakkhā bhavissāmā’ti| taina anuppan‍nāni caiva bhaṇḍanāni uppaj‍janti, uppan‍nāni ca bhaṇḍanāni bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattanti| saṅghau paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti| yassāyasmatau khamati paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘kataṃ saṅghaina paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ

4. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca adhammakammañ‍caiva (syā॰) hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| asammukhā kataṃ hauti, appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| anāpattiyā kataṃ  hauti, adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| asammukhā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ  hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| acaudaitvā kataṃ  hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ

5. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| sammukhā kataṃ hauti, paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca  hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisitāya  āpattiyā kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| sammukhā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca  hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina  kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca  hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgataṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

ākaṅkhamānachak‍kaṃ

6. pari॰ 323 ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya| bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi  gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya| adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya| buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘tiṇṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; aikau bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; aikau gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, aikau ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, aikau atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ  karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

ākaṅkhamānachak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

aṭṭhārasavattaṃ

7. ‘‘taj‍janīyakammakataina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā – na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sammati (syā॰) sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti sā āpatti na āpaj‍jitabbā, añ‍ñā vā tādisikā, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhatarā; kammaṃ na garahitabbaṃ, kammikā na garahitabbā| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathau ṭhapaitabbau, na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā, na savacanīyaṃ kātabbaṃ, na anuvādau paṭṭhapaitabbau, na aukāsau kāraitabbau, na caudaitabbau, na sāraitabbau, na bhikkhūhi na bhikkhū bhikkhūhi (syā॰) sampayaujaitabba’’nti|

taj‍janīyakammai aṭṭhārasavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

nappaṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ

8. atha khau saṅghau paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ akāsi| tai saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatā sammā vattanti, laumaṃ pātainti, naitthāraṃ vattanti, bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘mayaṃ, āvusau, saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatā sammā vattāma, laumaṃ pātaima, naitthāraṃ vattāma, kathaṃ nu khau amhaihi paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰…| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitu|

pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| upasampādaiti, nissayaṃ daiti, sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

pari॰ 420 ‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ garahati, kammikai garahati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ  nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, savacanīyaṃ karauti, anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, aukāsaṃ  kāraiti, caudaiti, sāraiti, bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti bhikkhū bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti (syā॰) – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

nappaṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

paṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ

9. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na upasampādaiti, na nissayaṃ daiti, na sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau na auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ na āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ na garahati, kammikai na garahati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, na pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, na savacanīyaṃ karauti, na anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, na aukāsaṃ kāraiti, na caudaiti, na sāraiti, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

paṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. ‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| taihi, bhikkhavai, paṇḍukalauhitakaihi bhikkhūhi saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘mayaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatā  sammā vattāma, laumaṃ pātaima, naitthāraṃ vattāma, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācāmā’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatā sammā vattanti, laumaṃ pātainti, naitthāraṃ vattanti, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācanti| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatā sammā vattanti, laumaṃ pātainti, naitthāraṃ vattanti, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācanti| saṅghau paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatā sammā vattanti, laumaṃ pātainti, naitthāraṃ vattanti, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācanti| saṅghau paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatā sammā vattanti, laumaṃ pātainti, naitthāraṃ vattanti, taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācanti| saṅghau paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘paṭippassaddhaṃ saṅghaina paṇḍukalauhitakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyakammaṃ| khamati saṅghassa,

tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taj‍janīyakammaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. niyassakammaṃ

11. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā saiyyasakau bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi; apissu bhikkhū pakatā pakatattā (sī॰ syā॰) parivāsaṃ daintā mūlāya paṭikassantā mānattaṃ daintā abbhaintā| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā saiyyasakau bālau bhavissati abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharissati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi; apissu bhikkhū pakatā parivāsaṃ daintā mūlāya paṭikassantā mānattaṃ daintā abbhaintā’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

atha  khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, saiyyasakau bhikkhu bālau abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi; apissu bhikkhū pakatā parivāsaṃ daintā mūlāya paṭikassantā mānattaṃ daintā abbhaintā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, bhikkhavai, tassa maughapurisassa ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma sau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau bālau bhavissati abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharissati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi; apissu bhikkhū pakatā parivāsaṃ daintā mūlāya paṭikassantā mānattaṃ daintā abbhaintā| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau saiyyasakassa  bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ niyasakammaṃ (ka॰) karautu – nissāya tai vatthabbanti| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbaṃ| paṭhamaṃ saiyyasakau bhikkhu caudaitabbau, caudaitvā sāraitabbau, sāraitvā āpattiṃ āraupaitabbau āpatti āraupaitabbā (sī॰ syā॰), āpattiṃ āraupaitvā byattaina bhikkhunā  paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

12. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ saiyyasakau bhikkhu bālau abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi; apissu bhikkhū pakatā parivāsaṃ daintā mūlāya paṭikassantā mānattaṃ daintā abbhaintā| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ karaiyya – nissāya tai vatthabbanti| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ saiyyasakau bhikkhu bālau abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi; apissu bhikkhū pakatā parivāsaṃ daintā mūlāya paṭikassantā mānattaṃ daintā abbhaintā| saṅghau saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ karauti – nissāya tai vatthabbanti| yassāyasmatau khamati saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassassa kammassa karaṇaṃ – nissāya tai vatthabbanti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ saiyyasakau bhikkhu bālau abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi; apissu bhikkhū pakatā parivāsaṃ daintā mūlāya paṭikassantā mānattaṃ daintā abbhaintā| saṅghau saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ karauti – nissāya tai vatthabbanti| yassāyasmatau khamati saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassassa kammassa  karaṇaṃ – nissāya tai vatthabbanti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘kataṃ saṅghaina saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ – nissāya tai vatthabbanti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ

13. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| asammukhā kataṃ hauti, appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā  kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca  hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… asammukhā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina  kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ

14. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| sammukhā kataṃ hauti, paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti  – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sammukhā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ niyassakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

ākaṅkhamānachak‍kaṃ

15. pari॰ 323 ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ karaiyya| bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ  karaiyya| adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau  saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ karaiyya| buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘tiṇṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; aikau bālau hauti, abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; aikau gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi  gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, aikau ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, aikau atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, niyassakammaṃ karaiyya|

ākaṅkhamānachak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

aṭṭhārasavattaṃ

16. ‘‘niyassakammakataina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā – na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā| yāya āpattiyā  saṅghaina niyassakammaṃ kataṃ hauti sā āpatti na āpaj‍jitabbā, añ‍ñā vā tādisikā, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhatarā; kammaṃ na garahitabbaṃ, kammikā na garahitabbā| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathau ṭhapaitabbau, na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā, na savacanīyaṃ kātabbaṃ, na anuvādau paṭṭhapaitabbau, na aukāsau kārāpaitabbau, na caudaitabbau, na sāraitabbau, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaitabba’’nti|

niyassakammai aṭṭhārasavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

17. atha khau saṅghau saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ akāsi – nissāya tai vatthabbanti| sau saṅghaina niyassakammakatau kalyāṇamittai  saivamānau bhajamānau payirupāsamānau uddisāpaintau paripucchantau bahussutau hauti, āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, saṅghaina niyassakammakatau sammā vattāmi, laumaṃ pātaimi, naitthāraṃ vattāmi| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰…| ‘‘taina  hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitu|

nappaṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ

18. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| upasampādaiti, nissayaṃ daiti, sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

pari॰ 420 ‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina niyassakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ garahati, kammikai garahati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, savacanīyaṃ karauti, anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, aukāsaṃ kāraiti, caudaiti, sāraiti, bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

nappaṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

paṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ

19. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na upasampādaiti, na nissayaṃ daiti, na sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau na auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina niyassakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ na āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ na garahati, kammikai na garahati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, na pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, na savacanīyaṃ karauti, na anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, na aukāsaṃ kāraiti, na caudaiti, na sāraiti, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ  paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

paṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

20. ‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| taina, bhikkhavai, saiyyasakaina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaina niyassakammakatau sammā vattāmi, laumaṃ pātaimi, naitthāraṃ vattāmi, niyassassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ saiyyasakau bhikkhu saṅghaina niyassakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, niyassassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ saiyyasakau bhikkhu saṅghaina niyassakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, niyassassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ saiyyasakau bhikkhu saṅghaina niyassakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, niyassassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ saiyyasakau bhikkhu saṅghaina niyassakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, niyassassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau  khamati saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘paṭippassaddhaṃ saṅghaina saiyyasakassa bhikkhunau niyassakammaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

niyassakammaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. pabbājanīyakammaṃ

21. idaṃ vatthu pārā॰ 431 ādayau taina khau pana samayaina assajipunabbasukā nāma nāma bhikkhū (ka॰) kīṭāgirismiṃ āvāsikā haunti alaj‍jinau pāpabhikkhū| tai cūḷava॰ 292 ādayau aivarūpaṃ anācāraṃ ācaranti – mālāvacchaṃ raupaintipi raupāpaintipi, siñ‍cantipi siñ‍cāpaintipi, aucinantipi aucināpaintipi, ganthaintipi ganthāpaintipi, aikatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ karauntipi  kārāpaintipi, ubhatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, mañ‍jarikaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, vidhūtikaṃ vidhutikaṃ (syā॰) karauntipi kārāpaintipi, vaṭaṃsakaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, āvaiḷaṃ karauntipi  kārāpaintipi, uracchadaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi| tai kulitthīnaṃ kuladhītānaṃ kulakumārīnaṃ kulasuṇhānaṃ kuladāsīnaṃ aikatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, ubhatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, mañ‍jarikaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, vidhūtikaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, vaṭaṃsakaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, āvaiḷaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, uracchadaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi| tai kulitthīhi kuladhītāhi kulakumārīhi kulasuṇhāhi kuladāsīhi saddhiṃ aikabhājanaipi bhuñ‍janti, aikathālakaipi  pivanti, aikāsanaipi nisīdanti, aikamañ‍caipi tuvaṭṭainti, aikattharaṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti, aikapāvuraṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti, aikattharaṇapāvuraṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti, vikālaipi bhuñ‍janti, maj‍jampi pivanti, mālāgandhavilaipanampi dhārainti, nac‍cantipi, gāyantipi, vādaintipi, lāsaintipi; nac‍cantiyāpi nac‍canti, nac‍cantiyāpi gāyanti, nac‍cantiyāpi vādainti, nac‍cantiyāpi lāsainti; gāyantiyāpi nac‍canti, gāyantiyāpi gāyanti, gāyantiyāpi vādainti, gāyantiyāpi lāsainti; vādaintiyāpi nac‍canti, vādaintiyāpi gāyanti, vādaintiyāpi vādainti, vādaintiyāpi lāsainti; lāsaintiyāpi nac‍canti, lāsaintiyāpi gāyanti, lāsaintiyāpi vādainti, lāsaintiyāpi lāsainti; aṭṭhapadaipi kīḷanti, dasapadaipi kīḷanti, ākāsaipi kīḷanti, parihārapathaipi kīḷanti, santikāyapi kīḷanti, khalikāyapi kīḷanti, ghaṭikāyapi kīḷanti, salākahatthainapi kīḷanti, akkhainapi kīḷanti, paṅgacīrainapi kīḷanti, vaṅkakainapi kīḷanti, maukkhacikāyapi kīḷanti, ciṅgulakainapi kīḷanti, pattāḷhakainapi kīḷanti, rathakainapi kīḷanti, dhanukainapi kīḷanti, akkharikāyapi kīḷanti, manaisikāyapi kīḷanti, yathāvaj‍jainapi kīḷanti; hatthismimpi sikkhanti, assasmimpi sikkhanti, rathasmimpi sikkhanti, dhanusmimpi sikkhanti, tharusmimpi sikkhanti; hatthissapi puratau dhāvanti, assassapi puratau dhāvanti, rathassapi puratau puratau dhāvanti (syā॰) dhāvantipi ādhāvantipi; ussaiḷaintipi, apphauṭaintipi, nibbujjhantipi, muṭṭhīhipi yujjhanti; raṅgamajjhaipi saṅghāṭiṃ pattharitvā nac‍cakiṃ nac‍cantiṃ (sī॰ syā॰) aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘idha, bhagini, nac‍cassū’’ti; nalāṭikampi dainti; vividhampi anācāraṃ ācaranti|

22. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kāsīsu vassaṃvuṭṭhau vassaṃvutthau (sī॰ syā॰) sāvatthiṃ gacchantau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya yaina kīṭāgiri tadavasari| atha khau sau bhikkhu pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya kīṭāgiriṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi pāsādikaina abhik‍kantaina paṭik‍kantaina  ālaukitaina vilaukitaina samiñ‍jitaina sammiñ‍jitaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) pasāritaina, aukkhittacakkhu iriyāpathasampan‍nau| manussā taṃ bhikkhuṃ passitvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘k‍vāyaṃ abalabalau  viya mandamandau viya bhākuṭikabhākuṭikau viya? kau imassa upagatassa piṇḍakampi dassati? amhākaṃ pana ayyā assajipunabbasukā saṇhā sakhilā sukhasambhāsā mihitapubbaṅgamā aihisvāgatavādinau abbhākuṭikā uttānamukhā pubbabhāsinau| taisaṃ khau nāma piṇḍau dātabbau’’ti|

addasā khau añ‍ñatarau upāsakau taṃ bhikkhuṃ kīṭāgirismiṃ piṇḍāya carantaṃ; disvāna yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘api, bhantai, piṇḍau labbhatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, āvusau, piṇḍau labbhatī’’ti| ‘‘aihi, bhantai, gharaṃ gamissāmā’’ti| atha khau sau upāsakau taṃ bhikkhuṃ gharaṃ naitvā bhaujaitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ, bhantai, ayyau gamissatī’’ti? ‘‘sāvatthiṃ  khau ahaṃ, āvusau, gamissāmi bhagavantaṃ dassanāyā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhantai, mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vanda, aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘duṭṭhau, bhantai, kīṭāgirismiṃ āvāsau| assajipunabbasukā nāma kīṭāgirismiṃ āvāsikā alaj‍jinau pāpabhikkhū| tai aivarūpaṃ anācāraṃ ācaranti – mālāvacchaṃ raupaintipi raupāpaintipi, siñ‍cantipi siñ‍cāpaintipi, aucinantipi, aucināpaintipi, ganthaintipi ganthāpaintipi, aikatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, ubhatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, mañ‍jarikaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, vidhūtikaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, vaṭaṃsakaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, āvaiḷaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, uracchadaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi| tai kulitthīnaṃ kuladhītānaṃ kulakumārīnaṃ kulasuṇhānaṃ kuladāsīnaṃ aikatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, ubhatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, mañ‍jarikaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, vidhūtikaṃ  harantipi harāpaintipi, vaṭaṃsakaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, āvaiḷaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, uracchadaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi| tai kulitthīhi kuladhītāhi kulakumārīhi kulasuṇhāhi kuladāsīhi saddhiṃ aikabhājanaipi bhuñ‍janti, aikathālakaipi pivanti, aikāsanaipi nisīdanti, aikamañ‍caipi tuvaṭṭainti, aikattharaṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti, aikapāvuraṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti, aikattharaṇapāvuraṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti, vikālaipi bhuñ‍janti, maj‍jampi pivanti, mālāgandhavilaipanampi dhārainti, nac‍cantipi, gāyantipi, vādaintipi, lāsaintipi; nac‍cantiyāpi  nac‍canti, nac‍cantiyāpi gāyanti, nac‍cantiyāpi vādainti, nac‍cantiyāpi lāsainti…pai॰… (cak‍kaṃ kātabbaṃ)| lāsaintiyāpi nac‍canti, lāsaintiyāpi gāyanti, lāsaintiyāpi vādainti, lāsaintiyāpi lāsainti; aṭṭhapadaipi kīḷanti, dasapadaipi kīḷanti, ākāsaipi  kīḷanti, parihārapathaipi kīḷanti, santikāyapi kīḷanti, khalikāyapi kīḷanti, ghaṭikāyapi kīḷanti, salākahatthainapi kīḷanti, akkhainapi kīḷanti, paṅgacīrainapi kīḷanti, vaṅkakainapi kīḷanti, maukkhacikāyapi kīḷanti, ciṅgulakainapi kīḷanti, pattāḷhakainapi kīḷanti, rathakainapi kīḷanti, dhanukainapi kīḷanti, akkharikāyapi kīḷanti, manaisikāyapi kīḷanti, yathāvaj‍jainapi kīḷanti; hatthismimpi sikkhanti, assasmimpi sikkhanti, rathasmimpi sikkhanti, dhanusmimpi sikkhanti, tharusmimpi sikkhanti; hatthissapi puratau dhāvanti, assassapi puratau dhāvanti, rathassapi puratau dhāvantipi ādhāvantipi; ussaiḷaintipi, apphauṭaintipi, nibbujjhantipi, muṭṭhīhipi yujjhanti; raṅgamajjhaipi saṅghāṭiṃ pattharitvā nac‍cakiṃ aivaṃ vadanti – ‘idha, bhagini, nac‍cassū’ti; nalāṭikampi dainti; vividhampi anācāraṃ ācaranti| yaipi tai, bhantai, manussā pubbai saddhā ahaisuṃ pasan‍nā taipi aitarahi assaddhā appasan‍nā| yānipi tāni saṅghassa pubbai dānapathāni tānipi aitarahi upacchin‍nāni| riñ‍canti paisalā bhikkhū, nivasanti pāpabhikkhū| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā kīṭāgiriṃ bhikkhū pahiṇaiyya, yathāyaṃ kīṭāgirismiṃ āvāsau saṇṭhahaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau sau bhikkhu tassa upāsakassa paṭissuṇitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina sāvatthi taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina sāvatthi jaitavanaṃ anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| āciṇṇaṃ khau panaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ āgantukaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudituṃ| atha  khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhikkhu, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍cisi appakilamathaina addhānaṃ āgatau, kutau ca tvaṃ bhikkhu āgacchasī’’ti? ‘‘khamanīyaṃ, bhagavā, yāpanīyaṃ bhagavā; appakilamathaina ca ahaṃ, bhantai, addhānaṃ āgatau| idhāhaṃ, bhantai, kāsīsu vassaṃvuṭṭhau sāvatthiṃ āgacchantau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya yaina kīṭāgiri tadavasariṃ| atha khvāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya kīṭāgiriṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| addasā khau maṃ, bhantai, añ‍ñatarau upāsakau kīṭāgirismiṃ piṇḍāya carantaṃ; disvāna yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aitadavauca – ‘api, bhantai, piṇḍau labbhatī’ti| ‘na khau, āvusau, piṇḍau labbhatī’ti| ‘aihi, bhantai, gharaṃ gamissāmā’ti| atha khau, bhantai, sau upāsakau maṃ gharaṃ naitvā bhaujaitvā aitadavauca – ‘kahaṃ, bhantai, ayyau gamissatī’ti? ‘sāvatthiṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau, gamissāmi bhagavantaṃ dassanāyā’ti| ‘taina hi, bhantai, mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vanda, aivañ‍ca vadaihi – duṭṭhau, bhantai, kīṭāgirismiṃ āvāsau| assajipunabbasukā nāma kīṭāgirismiṃ āvāsikā alaj‍jinau pāpabhikkhū| tai aivarūpaṃ anācāraṃ ācaranti – mālāvacchaṃ raupaintipi raupāpaintipi…pai॰… vividhampi anācāraṃ ācaranti| yaipi tai, bhantai, manussā pubbai saddhā ahaisuṃ pasan‍nā taipi aitarahi assaddhā appasan‍nā| yānipi tāni saṅghassa pubbai dānapathāni tānipi aitarahi upacchin‍nāni| riñ‍canti paisalā bhikkhū, nivasanti pāpabhikkhū| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā kīṭāgiriṃ bhikkhū pahiṇaiyya, yathāyaṃ kīṭāgirismiṃ āvāsau saṇṭhahaiyyā’ti| tatau ahaṃ bhagavā āgacchāmī’’ti|

23. atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, assajipunabbasukā nāma kīṭāgirismiṃ āvāsikā alaj‍jinau pāpabhikkhū? tai aivarūpaṃ anācāraṃ ācaranti – mālāvacchaṃ raupaintipi…pai॰… vividhampi anācāraṃ ācaranti? yaipi tai manussā pubbai saddhā ahaisuṃ pasan‍nā taipi aitarahi assaddhā appasan‍nā? yānipi tāni saṅghassa pubbai dānapathāni tānipi aitarahi upacchin‍nāni? riñ‍canti paisalā bhikkhū nivasanti pāpabhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ…pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā aivarūpaṃ anācāraṃ  ācarissanti – mālāvacchaṃ raupaissantipi raupāpaissantipi, siñ‍cissantipi siñ‍cāpaissantipi, aucinissantipi aucināpaissantipi, ganthaissantipi ganthāpaissantipi, aikatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, ubhatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, mañ‍jarikaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, vidhūtikaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, vaṭaṃsakaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, āvaiḷaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi, uracchadaṃ karissantipi kārāpaissantipi| tai kulitthīnaṃ kuladhītānaṃ kulakumārīnaṃ kulasuṇhānaṃ kuladāsīnaṃ aikatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ harissantipi harāpaissantipi, ubhatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ harissantipi harāpaissantipi, mañ‍jarikaṃ harissantipi harāpaissantipi, vidhūtikaṃ harissantipi harāpaissantipi, vaṭaṃsakaṃ harissantipi harāpaissantipi, āvaiḷaṃ harissantipi harāpaissantipi, uracchadaṃ harissantipi harāpaissantipi| tai kulitthīhi kuladhītāhi kulakumārīhi kulasuṇhāhi kuladāsīhi saddhiṃ aikabhājanaipi bhuñ‍jissanti, aikathālakaipi pivissanti, aikāsanaipi nisīdissanti, aikamañ‍caipi tuvaṭṭissanti, aikattharaṇāpi tuvaṭṭissanti, aikapāvuraṇāpi tuvaṭṭissanti, aikattharaṇapāvuraṇāpi tuvaṭṭissanti, vikālaipi bhuñ‍jissanti, maj‍jampi pivissanti, mālāgandhavilaipanampi dhāraissanti, nac‍cissantipi, gāyissantipi, vādaissantipi, lāsaissantipi; nac‍cantiyāpi nac‍cissanti, nac‍cantiyāpi gāyissanti, nac‍cantiyāpi vādaissanti, nac‍cantiyāpi lāsaissanti; gāyantiyāpi nac‍cissanti, gāyantiyāpi gāyissanti, gāyantiyāpi vādaissanti, gāyantiyāpi lāsaissanti; vādaintiyāpi nac‍cissanti, vādaintiyāpi gāyissanti, vādaintiyāpi vādaissanti, vādaintiyāpi lāsaissanti; lāsaintiyāpi nac‍cissanti, lāsaintiyāpi gāyissanti, lāsaintiyāpi vādaissanti; lāsaintiyāpi lāsaissanti; aṭṭhapadaipi kīḷissanti, dasapadaipi kīḷissanti, ākāsaipi kīḷissanti, parihārapathaipi kīḷissanti, santikāyapi kīḷissanti, khalikāyapi kīḷissanti, ghaṭikāyapi kīḷissanti, salākahatthainapi kīḷissanti, akkhainapi kīḷissanti, paṅgacīrainapi kīḷissanti, vaṅkakainapi kīḷissanti maukkhacikāyapi kīḷissanti, ciṅgulakainapi kīḷissanti, pattāḷhakainapi kīḷissanti, rathakainapi kīḷissanti, dhanukainapi kīḷissanti, akkharikāyapi kīḷissanti, manaisikāyapi kīḷissanti, yathāvaj‍jainapi kīḷissanti; hatthismimpi sikkhissanti, assasmimpi sikkhissanti, rathasmimpi sikkhissanti, dhanusmimpi sikkhissanti, tharusmimpi sikkhissanti; hatthissapi puratau dhāvissanti, assassapi puratau dhāvissanti, rathassapi puratau puratau dhāvissanti (syā॰) dhāvissantipi ādhāvissantipi; ussaiḷaissantipi, apphauṭaissantipi, nibbujjhissantipi, muṭṭhīhipi yujjhissanti; raṅgamajjhaipi saṅghāṭiṃ pattharitvā nac‍cakiṃ aivaṃ vakkhanti vadissanti (ka॰) – ‘idha, bhagini, nac‍cassū’ti; nalāṭikampi dassanti; vividhampi anācāraṃ ācarissanti| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā sāriputtamauggal‍lānai āmantaisi – ‘‘gacchatha tumhai, sāriputtā, kīṭāgiriṃ gantvā assajipunabbasukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kīṭāgirismā pabbājanīyakammaṃ karautha, tumhākaṃ aitai saddhivihārinau’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ mayaṃ, bhantai, assajipunabbasukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kīṭāgirismā pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauma, caṇḍā tai bhikkhū pharusā’’ti? ‘‘taina hi tumhai, sāriputtā, bahukaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ gacchathā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sāriputtamauggal‍lānā bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbaṃ – paṭhamaṃ assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū  caudaitabbā, caudaitvā sāraitabbā, sāraitvā āpattiṃ āraupaitabbā, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

24. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū kuladūsakā pāpasamācārā| imaisaṃ pāpakā samācārā dissanti caiva suyyanti ca| kulāni ca imaihi duṭṭhāni dissanti caiva suyyanti ca| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau assajipunabbasukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  kīṭāgirismā pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya – na assajipunabbasukaihi bhikkhūhi kīṭāgirismiṃ vatthabbanti| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū kuladūsakā pāpasamācārā| imaisaṃ pāpakā samācārā dissanti caiva suyyanti ca| kulāni ca imaihi duṭṭhāni dissanti caiva suyyanti ca| saṅghau assajipunabbasukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kīṭāgirismā pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti – na assajipunabbasukaihi bhikkhūhi kīṭāgirismiṃ vatthabbanti| yassāyasmatau khamati assajipunabbasukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kīṭāgirismā pabbājanīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ – na assajipunabbasukaihi bhikkhūhi kīṭāgirismiṃ vatthabbanti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi| suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū kuladūsakā pāpasamācārā| imaisaṃ pāpakā samācārā dissanti caiva suyyanti ca| kulāni ca imaihi duṭṭhāni dissanti caiva suyyanti ca| saṅghau assajipunabbasukānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ kīṭāgirismā pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti – na assajipunabbasukaihi bhikkhūhi kīṭāgirismiṃ vatthabbanti| yassāyasmatau khamati assajipunabbasukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kīṭāgirismā pabbājanīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ – na assajipunabbasukaihi bhikkhūhi kīṭāgirismiṃ vatthabbanti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘kataṃ saṅghaina assajipunabbasukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kīṭāgirismā pabbājanīyakammaṃ – na assajipunabbasukaihi bhikkhūhi kīṭāgirismiṃ vatthabbanti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ

25. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| asammukhā kataṃ hauti, appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… asammukhā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti  …pai॰… asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ

26. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| sammukhā kataṃ hauti, paṭipucchā  kataṃ hauti, paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| āpattiyā kataṃ hauti daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… sammukhā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina  kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ  hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ pabbājanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

ākaṅkhamānacuddasakaṃ

27. pari॰ 323 ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| kāyikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| kāyikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| kāyikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| kāyikaina micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina  micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaihi, khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘tiṇṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; aikau bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; aikau gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, aikau ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, aikau atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ  bhāsati – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ  karaiyya| aikau kāyikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, aikau vācasikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, aikau kāyikavācasikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau kāyikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, aikau vācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, aikau kāyikavācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau kāyikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, aikau vācasikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, aikau kāyikavācasikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau kāyikaina micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, aikau vācasikaina micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, aikau kāyikavācasikaina micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

ākaṅkhamānacuddasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

aṭṭhārasavattaṃ

28. ‘‘pabbājanīyakammakataina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā – na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti sā āpatti na āpaj‍jitabbā, añ‍ñā vā tādisikā, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhatarā; kammaṃ na garahitabbaṃ, kammikā na garahitabbā| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathau ṭhapaitabbau, na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā, na savacanīyaṃ kātabbaṃ, na anuvādau paṭṭhapaitabbau, na aukāsau kāraitabbau, na caudaitabbau, na sāraitabbau, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaitabba’’nti|

pabbājanīyakammai aṭṭhārasavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

29. atha khau sāriputtamauggal‍lānappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau kīṭāgiriṃ gantvā assajipunabbasukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kīṭāgirismā pabbājanīyakammaṃ akāsi – na assajipunabbasukaihi bhikkhūhi kīṭāgirismiṃ vatthabbanti| tai saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatā na sammā vattanti, na laumaṃ pātainti, na naitthāraṃ vattanti; na bhikkhū khamāpainti, ak‍kausanti, paribhāsanti; chandagāmitā dausagāmitā mauhagāmitā bhayagāmitā pāpainti; pak‍kamantipi, vibbhamantipi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatā na sammā vattissanti, na laumaṃ pātaissanti, na  naitthāraṃ vattissanti; na bhikkhū khamāpaissanti, ak‍kausissanti, paribhāsissanti; chandagāmitā dausagāmitā mauhagāmitā bhayagāmitā pāpaissanti; pak‍kamissantipi, vibbhamissantipī’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatā na sammā vattanti, na laumaṃ pātainti, na naitthāraṃ vattanti; na bhikkhū khamāpainti, ak‍kausanti, paribhāsanti; chandagāmitā dausagāmitā mauhagāmitā bhayagāmitā pāpainti; pak‍kamantipi, vibbhamantipī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ …pai॰… kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatā na sammā vattissanti, na laumaṃ pātaissanti, na naitthāraṃ vattissanti; na bhikkhū khamāpaissanti, ak‍kausissanti, paribhāsissanti; chandagāmitā dausagāmitā mauhagāmitā bhayagāmitā pāpaissanti; pak‍kamissantipi, vibbhamissantipi| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitu|

nappaṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ

30. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau  pabbājanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| upasampādaiti, nissayaṃ daiti, sāmaṇairaṃ  upaṭṭhāpaiti, bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

pari॰ 420 ‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ garahati, kammikai garahati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, savacanīyaṃ karauti, anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, aukāsaṃ kāraiti, caudaiti, sāraiti, bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

pabbājanīyakammai nappaṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

paṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ

31. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na upasampādaiti, na nissayaṃ daiti, na sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau na auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa  bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ na āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ na garahati, kammikai na garahati  – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, na pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, na savacanīyaṃ karauti, na anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, na aukāsaṃ kāraiti, na caudaiti, na sāraiti, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

pabbājanīyakammai paṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

32. ‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| taina, bhikkhavai, pabbājanīyakammakataina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatau sammā vattāmi, laumaṃ pātaimi, naitthāraṃ vattāmi, pabbājanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi  yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, pabbājanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, pabbājanīyassa  kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi| suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, pabbājanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘paṭippassaddhaṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā

tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

pabbājanīyakammaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ

33. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā sudhammau macchikāsaṇḍai cittassa gahapatinau āvāsikau hauti, navakammikau dhuvabhattikau| yadā cittau gahapati saṅghaṃ vā gaṇaṃ vā puggalaṃ vā nimantaitukāmau hauti tadā na āyasmantaṃ sudhammaṃ anapalaukaitvā saṅghaṃ vā gaṇaṃ vā puggalaṃ vā nimantaiti|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā thairā bhikkhū, āyasmā ca sāriputtau, āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau, āyasmā ca mahākac‍cānau, āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau, āyasmā ca mahākappinau, āyasmā ca mahācundau, āyasmā ca anuruddhau, āyasmā ca raivatau, āyasmā ca upāli, āyasmā ca ānandau, āyasmā ca rāhulau, kāsīsu cārikaṃ caramānā yaina macchikāsaṇḍau tadavasaruṃ|

assausi khau cittau gahapati thairā kira bhikkhū macchikāsaṇḍaṃ anuppattāti| atha khau cittau gahapati yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau cittaṃ gahapatiṃ āyasmā sāriputtau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi|

atha khau cittau gahapati āyasmatā sāriputtaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca  – ‘‘adhivāsaintu mai, bhantai, thairā svātanāya āgantukabhatta’’nti| adhivāsaisuṃ khau thairā bhikkhū adhivāsaisuṃ khau tai thairā bhikkhū (syā॰) tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha  khau cittau gahapati thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā thairai bhikkhū abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaināyasmā sudhammau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sudhammaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau cittau gahapati āyasmantaṃ sudhammaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, ayyau sudhammau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ thairaihī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sudhammau ‘pubbai khvāyaṃ cittau gahapati yadā saṅghaṃ vā gaṇaṃ vā puggalaṃ vā nimantaitukāmau na maṃ anapalaukaitvā saṅghaṃ vā gaṇaṃ vā puggalaṃ vā nimantaiti; saudāni maṃ anapalaukaitvā thairai bhikkhū nimantaisi; duṭṭhaudānāyaṃ cittau gahapati anapaikkhau virattarūpau mayī’ti cittaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, gahapati, nādhivāsaimī’’ti| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau cittau gahapati āyasmantaṃ sudhammaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, ayyau sudhammau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ thairaihī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, gahapati, nādhivāsaimī’’ti| atha khau cittau gahapati ‘kiṃ mai karissati ayyau sudhammau  adhivāsaintau vā anadhivāsaintau vā’ti āyasmantaṃ sudhammaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

34. atha khau cittau gahapati tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaisi| atha khau āyasmā sudhammau ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ cittassa gahapatinau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭiyattaṃ  passaiyya’nti pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina cittassa gahapatinau nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau cittau gahapati yaināyasmā sudhammau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā  āyasmantaṃ sudhammaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau cittaṃ gahapatiṃ āyasmā sudhammau aitadavauca – ‘‘pahūtaṃ khau tai idaṃ, gahapati, khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyattaṃ; aikā ca khau idha natthi yadidaṃ tilasaṅguḷikā’’ti| ‘‘bahumhi vata, bhantai, ratanai buddhavacanai bhantai buddhavacanai (syā॰) vij‍jamānai ayyaina sudhammaina yadaiva kiñ‍ci bhāsitaṃ yadidaṃ tilasaṅguḷikāti| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhantai, dakkhiṇāpathakā vāṇijā puratthimaṃ janapadaṃ agamaṃsu vāṇij‍jāya| tai tatau kuk‍kuṭiṃ ānaisuṃ| atha khau sā, bhantai, kuk‍kuṭī kākaina saddhiṃ saṃvāsaṃ kappaisi| sā pautakaṃ janaisi| yadā khau sau, bhantai, kuk‍kuṭapautakau  kākavassaṃ vassitukāmau hauti, kākakuk‍kuṭīti vassati; yadā kuk‍kuṭivassaṃ vassitukāmau hauti, kuk‍kuṭikākāti vassati| aivamaiva khau, bhantai, bahumhi ratanai buddhavacanai vij‍jamānai ayyaina sudhammaina yadaiva kiñ‍ci bhāsitaṃ yadidaṃ tilasaṃguḷikā’’ti| ‘‘ak‍kausasi maṃ tvaṃ, gahapati, paribhāsasi maṃ tvaṃ, gahapati| aisau tai, gahapati, āvāsau, pak‍kamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, ayyaṃ sudhammaṃ ak‍kausāmi, paribhāsāmi  na paribhāsāmi (sī॰ syā॰)| vasatu, bhantai, ayyau sudhammau macchikāsaṇḍai| ramaṇīyaṃ ambāṭakavanaṃ| ahaṃ ayyassa sudhammassa ussuk‍kaṃ karissāmi, cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’’nti| dutiyampi khau…pai॰…  tatiyampi khau āyasmā sudhammau cittaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ak‍kausasi maṃ tvaṃ, gahapati, paribhāsasi maṃ tvaṃ, gahapati| aisau tai, gahapati, āvāsau, pak‍kamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘kahaṃ, bhantai, ayyau sudhammau gamissatī’’ti? ‘‘sāvatthiṃ khau ahaṃ, gahapati, gamissāmi bhagavantaṃ dassanāyā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhantai, yañ‍ca attanā bhaṇitaṃ, yañ‍ca mayā bhaṇitaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaihi| anacchariyaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhantai, yaṃ ayyau sudhammau punadaiva macchikāsaṇḍaṃ pac‍cāgacchaiyyā’’ti|

35. atha khau āyasmā sudhammau saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina sāvatthi taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina sāvatthi jaitavanaṃ anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sudhammau yañ‍ca attanā bhaṇitaṃ  yañ‍ca cittaina gahapatinā bhaṇitaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi|

vigarahi  buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, cittaṃ gahapatiṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ dāyakaṃ kārakaṃ saṅghupaṭṭhākaṃ hīnaina khuṃsaissasi, hīnaina vambhaissasi? naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā  pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karautu – cittau tai gahapati khamāpaitabbauti| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbaṃ – paṭhamaṃ sudhammau bhikkhu caudaitabbau, caudaitvā sāraitabbau, sāraitvā āpattiṃ āraupaitabbau, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

36. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ sudhammau bhikkhu cittaṃ gahapatiṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ dāyakaṃ kārakaṃ saṅghupaṭṭhākaṃ hīnaina khuṃsaiti, hīnaina vambhaiti| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaiyya – cittau tai gahapati khamāpaitabbauti| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ sudhammau bhikkhu cittaṃ gahapatiṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ dāyakaṃ kārakaṃ  saṅghupaṭṭhākaṃ hīnaina khuṃsaiti, hīnaina vambhaiti| saṅghau sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti – cittau tai gahapati khamāpaitabbauti| yassāyasmatau khamati sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ – cittau tai gahapati khamāpaitabbauti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ sudhammau bhikkhu cittaṃ gahapatiṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ dāyakaṃ kārakaṃ saṅghupaṭṭhākaṃ hīnaina khuṃsaiti, hīnaina vambhaiti| saṅghau sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti – cittau  tai gahapati khamāpaitabbauti| yassāyasmatau khamati sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ – cittau tai gahapati khamāpaitabbauti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘kataṃ saṅghaina sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ – cittau tai gahapati khamāpaitabbauti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ

37. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| asammukhā kataṃ hauti, appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  asammukhā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ  paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

paṭisāraṇīyakammai adhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ

38. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| sammukhā kataṃ hauti, paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… sammukhā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiṃ āraupaitvā  kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

paṭisāraṇīyakammai dhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

ākaṅkhamānacatuk‍kaṃ

39. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ  karaiyya| gihīnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, gihīnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, gihīnaṃ anāvāsāya avāsāya (sī॰) parisak‍kati, gihī ak‍kausati paribhāsati, gihī gihīhi  bhaidaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaiyya| gihīnaṃ buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, gihīnaṃ dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, gihīnaṃ saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, gihī hīnaina khuṃsaiti hīnaina vambhaiti, gihīnaṃ dhammikaṃ paṭissavaṃ na sac‍cāpaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaiyya|
