‘‘pañ‍can‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau gihīnaṃ  alābhāya parisak‍kati, aikau gihīnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, aikau gihīnaṃ anāvāsāya parisak‍kati, aikau gihī ak‍kausati paribhāsati, aikau gihī gihīhi bhaidaiti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau gihīnaṃ buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau gihīnaṃ dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau gihīnaṃ saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau gihī hīnaina khuṃsaiti hīnaina vambhaiti, aikau gihīnaṃ dhammikaṃ paṭissavaṃ na sac‍cāpaiti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

ākaṅkhamānacatuk‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

aṭṭhārasavattaṃ

40. ‘‘paṭisāraṇīyakammakataina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā – na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti sā āpatti na āpaj‍jitabbā, añ‍ñā vā tādisikā, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhatarā; kammaṃ na garahitabbaṃ, kammikā na garahitabbā| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathau ṭhapaitabbau, na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā, na savacanīyaṃ kātabbaṃ, na anuvādau paṭṭhapaitabbau, na aukāsau kāraitabbau, na caudaitabbau, na sāraitabbau, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaitabba’’nti|

paṭisāraṇīyakammai aṭṭhārasavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

41. atha  khau saṅghau sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ akāsi – ‘‘cittau tai gahapati khamāpaitabbau’’ti| sau saṅghaina paṭisāraṇīyakammakatau macchikāsaṇḍaṃ gantvā maṅkubhūtau nāsakkhi cittaṃ gahapatiṃ khamāpaituṃ| punadaiva sāvatthiṃ pac‍cāgañchi| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘khamāpitau tayā, āvusau sudhamma, cittau gahapatī’’ti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, macchikāsaṇḍaṃ gantvā maṅkubhūtau nāsakkhiṃ cittaṃ gahapatiṃ khamāpaitu’’nti| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰…| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau sudhammassa bhikkhunau anudūtaṃ daitu – cittaṃ gahapatiṃ khamāpaituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana bhikkhavai dātabbau – paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau, yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ sudhammassa bhikkhunau anudūtaṃ dadaiyya cittaṃ gahapatiṃ khamāpaituṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ sudhammassa  bhikkhunau anudūtaṃ daiti cittaṃ gahapatiṃ khamāpaituṃ| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sudhammassa bhikkhunau anudūtassa dānaṃ cittaṃ gahapatiṃ khamāpaituṃ, sau  tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sudhammassa bhikkhunau anudūtau cittaṃ gahapatiṃ khamāpaituṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

42. ‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, sudhammaina bhikkhunā anudūtaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ macchikāsaṇḍaṃ gantvā cittau gahapati khamāpaitabbau – ‘khama, gahapati, pasādaimi ta’nti| aivañ‍cai vuc‍camānau khamati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai khamati, anudūtaina bhikkhunā vattabbau – ‘khama, gahapati, imassa  bhikkhunau, pasādaiti ta’nti| aivañ‍cai vuc‍camānau khamati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai khamati, anudūtaina bhikkhunā vattabbau – ‘khama, gahapati, imassa bhikkhunau, ahaṃ taṃ pasādaimī’ti| aivañ‍cai vuc‍camānau khamati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai khamati, anudūtaina bhikkhunā vattabbau – ‘khama, gahapati, imassa bhikkhunau, saṅghassa vacanainā’ti| aivañ‍cai vuc‍camānau khamati, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai  khamati, anudūtaina bhikkhunā sudhammau bhikkhu sudhammaṃ bhikkhuṃ… sā āpatti daisāpaitabbāti (sī॰ syā॰) cittassa gahapatinau dassanūpacāraṃ avijahāpaitvā savanūpacāraṃ avijahāpaitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ kārāpaitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīdāpaitvā añ‍jaliṃ paggaṇhāpaitvā taṃ āpattiṃ daisāpaitabbau’’ti sudhammaṃ bhikkhuṃ… sā āpatti daisāpaitabbāti (sī॰ syā॰)|

atha khau āyasmā sudhammau anudūtaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ macchikāsaṇḍaṃ gantvā cittaṃ gahapatiṃ khamāpaisi| sau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiti  – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, saṅghaina paṭisāraṇīyakammakatau sammā vattāmi, laumaṃ pātaimi, naitthāraṃ vattāmi| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitu|

nappaṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ

43. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| upasampādaiti, nissayaṃ daiti, sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

pari॰ 420 ‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ garahati, kammikai garahati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, savacanīyaṃ karauti, anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, aukāsaṃ kāraiti, caudaiti, sāraiti, bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ  nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ’’|

paṭisāraṇīyakammai nappaṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

paṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ

44. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na upasampādaiti, na nissayaṃ daiti, na sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau na auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ na āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ na garahati, kammikai na garahati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, na pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, na savacanīyaṃ karauti, na anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, na aukāsaṃ kāraiti, na caudaiti, na sāraiti, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

paṭisāraṇīyakammai paṭippassambhaitabbaaṭṭhārasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

45. ‘‘aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| taina taina hi (syā॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, sudhammaina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaina paṭisāraṇīyakammakatau sammā vattāmi, laumaṃ pātaimi, naitthāraṃ vattāmi, paṭisāraṇīyassa kammassa  paṭippassaddhiṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ sudhammau bhikkhu saṅghaina paṭisāraṇīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, paṭisāraṇīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ sudhammau bhikkhu saṅghaina paṭisāraṇīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, paṭisāraṇīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…  tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ sudhammau bhikkhu saṅghaina paṭisāraṇīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, paṭisāraṇīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘paṭippassaddhaṃ saṅghaina sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ

46. taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā chan‍nau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ passituṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā chan‍nau āpattiṃ  āpaj‍jitvā na icchissati āpattiṃ passitu’’nti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā  bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chan‍nau bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ passitu’’nti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ…pai॰… kathañhi nāma sau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchissati āpattiṃ passituṃ? naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karautu – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbaṃ – paṭhamaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu caudaitabbau, caudaitvā sāraitabbau, sāraitvā āpattiṃ āraupaitabbau, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

47. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ passituṃ| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ passituṃ| saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| yassāyasmatau khamati chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ passituṃ| saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| yassāyasmatau khamati chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ  – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau  bhāsaiyya|

‘‘kataṃ  saṅghaina chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

‘‘āvāsaparamparañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saṃsatha – ‘chan‍nau bhikkhu saṅghaina āpattiyā chan‍nau bhikkhu āpattiyā (sī॰ ka॰) adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammakatau – asambhaugaṃ saṅghainā’ti|

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ

48. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| asammukhā kataṃ hauti, appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ  hauti, daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… asammukhā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā  kataṃ hauti, adhammaina  kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammai adhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ

49. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| sammukhā kataṃ hauti, paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ…pai॰…| āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… sammukhā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammai dhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

ākaṅkhamānachak‍kaṃ

50. pari॰ 323 ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā  adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘tiṇṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; aikau bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; aikau gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi  – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, aikau ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, aikau atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammai

ākaṅkhamānachak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

taicattālīsavattaṃ

51. ‘‘āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammakataina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā – na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti sā āpatti na āpaj‍jitabbā, añ‍ñā vā tādisikā, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhatarā; kammaṃ na garahitabbaṃ, kammikā na garahitabbā| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihārau, saiyyābhihārau, pādaudakaṃ, pādapīṭhaṃ pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarappaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ sāditabbaṃ| na pakatattau bhikkhu sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaitabbau, na ācāravipattiyā anuddhaṃsaitabbau, na diṭṭhivipattiyā anuddhaṃsaitabbau, na ājīvavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaitabbau, na bhikkhu bhikkhūhi bhaidaitabbau na bhikkhū bhikkhūhi bhaidaitabbā (syā॰), na gihiddhajau dhāraitabbau, na titthiyaddhajau dhāraitabbau, na titthiyā saivitabbā; bhikkhū saivitabbā, bhikkhusikkhāya sikkhitabbaṃ bhikkhusikkhā sikkhitabbā (syā॰)| na pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai  vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ, pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā āsanā vuṭṭhātabbaṃ, na pakatattau bhikkhu āsādaitabbau antau vā bahi vā| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathau ṭhapaitabbau, na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā, na savacanīyaṃ kātabbaṃ, na anuvādau  paṭṭhapaitabbau, na aukāsau kāraitabbau, na caudaitabbau, na sāraitabbau, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaitabba’’nti|

āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammai

taicattālīsavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

52. atha khau saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ akāsi – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| sau saṅghaina, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ agamāsi| tattha bhikkhū naiva abhivādaisuṃ, na pac‍cuṭṭhaisuṃ, na añ‍jalikammaṃ na  sāmīcikammaṃ akaṃsu, na sak‍kariṃsu, na garu kariṃsu na garukariṃsu (ka॰), na mānaisuṃ, na pūjaisuṃ| sau bhikkhūhi asak‍kariyamānau agarukariyamānau amāniyamānau apūjiyamānau asak‍kārapakatau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ agamāsi| tatthapi bhikkhū naiva abhivādaisuṃ, na pac‍cuṭṭhaisuṃ, na añ‍jalikammaṃ na sāmīcikammaṃ akaṃsu, na  sak‍kariṃsu, na garuṃ kariṃsu, na mānaisuṃ, na pūjaisuṃ| sau bhikkhūhi asak‍kariyamānau agarukariyamānau amāniyamānau apūjiyamānau asak‍kārapakatau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ agamāsi| tatthapi bhikkhū naiva abhivādaisuṃ, na pac‍cuṭṭhaisuṃ, na añ‍jalikammaṃ na sāmīcikammaṃ akaṃsu, na sak‍kariṃsu, na garuṃ kariṃsu, na mānaisuṃ, na pūjaisuṃ| sau bhikkhūhi asak‍kariyamānau agarukariyamānau amāniyamānau apūjiyamānau asak‍kārapakatau punadaiva kausambiṃ pac‍cāgañchi| sau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, saṅghaina, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattāmi, laumaṃ pātaimi, naitthāraṃ vattāmi| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰…| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitu|

nappaṭippassambhaitabba-taicattālīsakaṃ

53. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| upasampādaiti, nissayaṃ  daiti, sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

pari॰ 420 ‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ garahati, kammikai garahati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| pakatattassa bhikkhunau abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ sādiyati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| pakatattassa  bhikkhunau saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarappaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ sādiyati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, ācāravipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, diṭṭhivipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, ājīvavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, bhikkhuṃ bhikkhū (sī॰ syā॰) bhikkhūhi bhaidaiti – imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| gihiddhajaṃ dhāraiti, titthiyaddhajaṃ dhāraiti, titthiyai saivati; bhikkhū na saivati, bhikkhusikkhāya na sikkhati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vasati, aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vasati, aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vasati; pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā āsanā na vuṭṭhāti; pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ āsādaiti  antau vā bahi vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, savacanīyaṃ karauti, anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, aukāsaṃ kāraiti, caudaiti, sāraiti, bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammai

nappaṭippassambhaitabba-taicattālīsakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

paṭippassambhaitabba-taicattālīsakaṃ

54. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na upasampādaiti, na nissayaṃ daiti, na sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau na auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa  bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ  āpattiṃ na āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ na garahati, kammikai na garahati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ sādiyati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarappaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ sādiyati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, na ācāravipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, na diṭṭhivipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, na ājīvavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, na bhikkhuṃ  bhikkhūhi bhaidaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na gihiddhajaṃ dhāraiti, na titthiyaddhajaṃ dhāraiti, na titthiyai saivati, bhikkhū saivati, bhikkhusikkhāya  sikkhati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vasati, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vasati, na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vasati, pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā āsanā vuṭṭhāti, na pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ āsādaiti antau vā bahi vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, na pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, na savacanīyaṃ karauti, na anuvādaṃ  paṭṭhapaiti, na aukāsaṃ kāraiti, na caudaiti, na sāraiti, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammai

paṭippassambhaitabbataicattālīsakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

55. ‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| taina, bhikkhavai, chan‍naina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaina, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattāmi, laumaṃ pātaimi, naitthāraṃ vattāmi, āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu saṅghaina, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyassa  kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu saṅghaina, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā  vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu saṅghaina, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘paṭippassaddhaṃ saṅghaina chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ

56. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā chan‍nau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ paṭikātuṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰…  tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā chan‍nau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchissati āpattiṃ paṭikātu’’nti? atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

atha  khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chan‍nau bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ paṭikātu’’nti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ…pai॰… kathañhi nāma sau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchissati āpattiṃ paṭikātuṃ| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karautu – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbaṃ – paṭhamaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu caudaitabbau, caudaitvā sāraitabbau, sāraitvā āpattiṃ āraupaitabbau, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ  paṭikātuṃ| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ paṭikātuṃ| saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| yassāyasmatau khamati chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ paṭikātuṃ| saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| yassāyasmatau khamati chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘kataṃ saṅghaina chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

‘‘āvāsaparamparañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saṃsatha – ‘chan‍nau bhikkhu, saṅghaina āpattiyā chan‍nau bhikkhu āpattiyā (sī॰ ka॰) appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau – asambhaugaṃ saṅghainā’ti|

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ

57. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| asammukhā kataṃ hauti, appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā  appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… asammukhā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammai

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ

58. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ  dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| sammukhā kataṃ hauti, paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… sammukhā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammai

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

ākaṅkhamānachak‍kaṃ

59. pari॰ 323 ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā  appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘tiṇṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; aikau bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; aikau gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau  hauti, aikau ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, aikau  atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammai

ākaṅkhamānachak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

taicattālīsavattaṃ

60. ‘‘āpattiyā  appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammakataina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā – na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti sā āpatti na āpaj‍jitabbā, añ‍ñā vā tādisikā, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhatarā; kammaṃ na garahitabbaṃ, kammikā na garahitabbā| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihārau, saiyyābhihārau, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarappaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ sāditabbaṃ| na pakatattau bhikkhu sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaitabbau, na ācāravipattiyā anuddhaṃsaitabbau, na diṭṭhivipattiyā anuddhaṃsaitabbau, na ājīvavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaitabbau, na bhikkhu bhikkhūhi bhaidaitabbau| na gihiddhajau dhāraitabbau, na titthiyaddhajau dhāraitabbau, na titthiyā saivitabbā; bhikkhū saivitabbā, bhikkhusikkhāya sikkhitabbaṃ| na pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā  anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ, pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā āsanā vuṭṭhātabbaṃ, na pakatattau bhikkhu āsādaitabbau antau vā bahi vā| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathau ṭhapaitabbau, na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā, na savacanīyaṃ kātabbaṃ, na anuvādau paṭṭhapaitabbau, na aukāsau kāraitabbau, na caudaitabbau, na sāraitabbau, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaitabba’’nti|

āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammai

taicattālīsavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

61. atha khau saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ akāsi – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| sau saṅghaina, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau tamhā āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ agamāsi| tattha bhikkhū naiva abhivādaisuṃ, na pac‍cuṭṭhaisuṃ, na añ‍jalikammaṃ na sāmīcikammaṃ akaṃsu, na sak‍kariṃsu, na garuṃ kariṃsu, na mānaisuṃ, na pūjaisuṃ| sau bhikkhūhi asak‍kariyamānau agarukariyamānau amāniyamānau apūjiyamānau asak‍kārapakatau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ  agamāsi| tatthapi bhikkhū naiva abhivādaisuṃ, na pac‍cuṭṭhaisuṃ, na añ‍jalikammaṃ na sāmīcikammaṃ akaṃsu, na sak‍kariṃsu, na garuṃ kariṃsu, na mānaisuṃ, na pūjaisuṃ| sau bhikkhūhi asak‍kariyamānau agarukariyamānau amāniyamānau  apūjiyamānau asak‍kārapakatau tamhāpi āvāsā añ‍ñaṃ āvāsaṃ agamāsi| tatthapi bhikkhū naiva abhivādaisuṃ, na pac‍cuṭṭhaisuṃ, na añ‍jalikammaṃ na sāmīcikammaṃ akaṃsu, na sak‍kariṃsu, na garuṃ kariṃsu, na mānaisuṃ, na pūjaisuṃ| sau bhikkhūhi asak‍kariyamānau agarukariyamānau amāniyamānau apūjiyamānau asak‍kārapakatau punadaiva kausambiṃ pac‍cāgañchi| sau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, saṅghaina, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattāmi, laumaṃ pātaimi, naitthāraṃ vattāmi| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰…| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitu|

nappaṭippassambhaitabbataicattālīsakaṃ

62. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| upasampādaiti, nissayaṃ daiti, sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

pari॰ 420 ‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ garahati, kammikai garahati…pai॰… pakatattassa bhikkhunau abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ sādiyati…pai॰… pakatattassa bhikkhunau saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarappaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ sādiyati…pai॰… pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, ācāravipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, diṭṭhivipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, ājīvavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, bhikkhuṃ bhikkhūhi bhaidaiti…pai॰… gihiddhajaṃ dhāraiti, titthiyaddhajaṃ dhāraiti, titthiyai saivati, bhikkhū na saivati, bhikkhusikkhāya na sikkhati…pai॰… pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vasati, aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vasati, aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vasati, pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā āsanā na vuṭṭhāti, pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ āsādaiti antau vā  bahi vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, savacanīyaṃ karauti, anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, aukāsaṃ kāraiti, caudaiti, sāraiti, bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti  – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammai

nappaṭippassambhaitabbataicattālīsakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

paṭippassambhaitabbataicattālīsakaṃ

63. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na upasampādaiti, na nissayaṃ daiti, na sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau na auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ na āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ na garahati, kammikai na garahati…pai॰… na pakatattassa bhikkhunau abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ sādiyati…pai॰… na pakatattassa bhikkhunau saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarappaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ sādiyati…pai॰… na pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, na ācāravipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, na diṭṭhivipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, na ājīvavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, na bhikkhuṃ bhikkhūhi bhaidaiti…pai॰… na gihiddhajaṃ dhāraiti, na titthiyaddhajaṃ dhāraiti, na titthiyai saivati, bhikkhū saivati, bhikkhusikkhāya sikkhati…pai॰… na pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vasati, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vasati, na aikacchan‍nai  āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vasati, pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā āsanā vuṭṭhāti, na pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ āsādaiti antau vā bahi vā – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā  appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, na pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, na savacanīyaṃ karauti, na anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, na aukāsaṃ kāraiti, na caudaiti, na sāraiti, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammai

paṭippassambhaitabbataicattālīsakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

64. ‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| taina bhikkhavai, chan‍naina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaina, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattāmi, laumaṃ pātaimi, naitthāraṃ vattāmi, āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu saṅghaina, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu saṅghaina, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā  appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ chan‍nau bhikkhu saṅghaina, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘paṭippassaddhaṃ saṅghaina chan‍nassa bhikkhunau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ

65. idaṃ vatthu pāci॰ 417; ma॰ ni॰ 234 ādayau taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina ariṭṭhassa nāma bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa gandhabādhipubbassa (ka॰) aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti| assausuṃ khau sambahulā bhikkhū ariṭṭhassa nāma kira bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| upasaṅkamitvā ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, āvusau ariṭṭha, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃbyā khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai  antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti|

‘‘māvusau  ariṭṭha, aivaṃ avaca| mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi| na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ  abbhācikkhanaṃ (ka॰)| na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| anaikapariyāyaināvusau ariṭṭha, antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā| alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāya| appassādā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā bahūpāyāsā (sī॰ syā॰), ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, bahudukkhā bahūpāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| maṃsapaisūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā…pai॰… tiṇuk‍kūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā…pai॰… aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… supinakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… yācitakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… rukkhaphalūpamā kāmā vuttā  bhagavatā… asisūnūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… sattisūlūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, bahudukkhā bahūpāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’’ti|

aivampi khau ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau taihi bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva taṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘‘aivaṃbyā khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti| yatau ca khau tai bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaituṃ, atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha  khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, ariṭṭha, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃbyā khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti|

‘‘kassa  nu khau nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mayā aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāsi? nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā? alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāya| appassādā kāmā vuttā mayā, bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā, bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| maṃsapaisūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā…pai॰… tiṇuk‍kūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… supinakūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā…pai॰… yācitakūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… rukkhaphalūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… asisūnūpamā kāmā vuttā  mayā… sattisūlūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā, bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| atha ca pana tvaṃ, maughapurisa, attanā duggahitaina duggahitaina diṭṭhigataina (syā॰) amhai caiva abbhācikkhasi, attānañ‍ca khaṇasi, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavasi| tañhi tai, maughapurisa, bhavissati dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau ariṭṭhassa bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karautu – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| aivañ‍ca pana bhikkhavai kātabbaṃ – paṭhamaṃ ariṭṭhau bhikkhu caudaitabbau, caudaitvā sāraitabbau, sāraitvā āpattiṃ āraupaitabbau, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

66. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ariṭṭhassa bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ  uppan‍naṃ – tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyāti| sau taṃ diṭṭhiṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau ariṭṭhassa bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ariṭṭhassa bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā  yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyāti| sau taṃ diṭṭhiṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| saṅghau ariṭṭhassa bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| yassāyasmatau khamati ariṭṭhassa bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ariṭṭhassa bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyāti| sau taṃ diṭṭhiṃ na paṭinissaj‍jati| saṅghau ariṭṭhassa bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti – asambhaugaṃ  saṅghaina| yassāyasmatau khamati ariṭṭhassa bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa karaṇaṃ – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘kataṃ saṅghaina ariṭṭhassa bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

‘‘āvāsaparamparañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saṃsatha – ‘ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau, saṅghaina pāpikāya gaddhabādhipubbau pāpikāya (sī॰ ka॰) diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau – asambhaugaṃ saṅghainā’’’ti|

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ

67. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| asammukhā kataṃ hauti, appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, pāpikāya  diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… asammukhā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… appaṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  anāpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… adaisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… daisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… acaudaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… asāraitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiṃ anāraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammai

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ

68. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| sammukhā kataṃ hauti, paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti…pai॰… sammukhā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… paṭipucchā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ  hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… adaisitāya āpattiyā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… caudaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… sāraitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti…pai॰… āpattiṃ āraupaitvā kataṃ hauti, dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammai

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

ākaṅkhamānachak‍kaṃ

69. pari॰ 323 ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘tiṇṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; aikau bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; aikau gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, aikau ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, aikau atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya| aikau buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati  – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissagai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya|

pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissagai ukkhaipanīyakammai

ākaṅkhamānachak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

taicattālīsavattaṃ

70. ‘‘pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammakataina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā – na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau  na auvaditabbā| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti sā āpatti na āpaj‍jitabbā, añ‍ñā vā tādisikā, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhatarā; kammaṃ na garahitabbaṃ, kammikā na garahitabbā…pai॰… na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathau ṭhapaitabbau, na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā, na savacanīyaṃ kātabbaṃ, na anuvādau paṭṭhapaitabbau, na aukāsau kāraitabbau, na caudaitabbau, na sāraitabbau, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaitabba’’nti|

pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammai

taicattālīsavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

71. atha khau saṅghau ariṭṭhassa bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ akāsi – asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina| sau saṅghaina, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau vibbhami| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau saṅghaina, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau vibbhamissatī’’ti? atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, ariṭṭhau  bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau  saṅghaina, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau vibbhamatī’’ti vibbhamīti (sī॰ ka॰)? ‘‘sac‍caṃ  bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ…pai॰… kathañhi nāma sau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau saṅghaina, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau vibbhamissati? naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitu|

nappaṭippassambhaitabbataicattālīsakaṃ

72. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| upasampādaiti, nissayaṃ daiti, sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissagai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

pari॰ 420 ‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ garahati, kammikai garahati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, savacanīyaṃ karauti, anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, aukāsaṃ kāraiti, caudaiti, sāraiti, bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammai

nappaṭippassambhaitabbataicattālīsakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

paṭippassambhaitabbataicattālīsakaṃ

73. ‘‘pañ‍cahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na upasampādaiti, na nissayaṃ daiti, na sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, na bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau na auvadati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ na āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ; kammaṃ na garahati, kammikai na garahati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathaṃ ṭhapaiti, na pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaiti, na savacanīyaṃ karauti, na anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaiti, na aukāsaṃ kāraiti, na caudaiti, na sāraiti, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ’’|

pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammai

paṭippassambhaitabbataicattālīsakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

74. ‘‘aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, paṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| taina, bhikkhavai, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammakataina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaina, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattāmi, laumaṃ pātaimi, naitthāraṃ vattāmi, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyassa  kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ  yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu saṅghaina, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu saṅghaina, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu saṅghaina, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ paṭippassambhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyassa kammassa paṭippassaddhi, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘paṭippassaddhaṃ  saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai, ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

kammakkhandhakau paṭhamau|

imamhi khandhakai vatthū satta|

tassuddānaṃ –

paṇḍulauhitakā  bhikkhū, sayaṃ bhaṇḍanakārakā|

tādisai upasaṅkamma, ussahiṃsu ca bhaṇḍanai||

anuppan‍nāpi jāyanti anuppan‍nāni jāyanti (sī॰ syā॰), uppan‍nānipi vaḍḍharai uppan‍nāni pavaḍḍharai (sī॰), uppan‍nāpi pavaḍḍhanti (ka॰)|

appicchā paisalā bhikkhū, ujjhāyanti padassatau parīsatau (syā॰), parassatau (sī॰)||

saddhammaṭṭhitikau buddhau, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

āṇāpaisi taj‍janīyakammaṃ sāvatthiyaṃ jinau||

asammukhāppaṭipucchāppaṭiñ‍ñāya  katañ‍ca yaṃ|

anāpatti adaisanai, daisitāya katañ‍ca yaṃ||

acaudaitvā asāraitvā, anāraupaitvā ca yaṃ kataṃ|

asammukhā adhammaina, vaggaina cāpi vaggaināpi ca (sī॰ syā॰) yaṃ kataṃ||

appaṭipucchā adhammaina, puna vaggaina vaggaināpi ca (sī॰ syā॰) yaṃ kataṃ|

appaṭiñ‍ñāya adhammaina, vaggaina cāpi vaggaināpi ca (sī॰ syā॰) yaṃ kataṃ||

anāpatti anāpattiyā (sī॰ syā॰) adhammaina, vaggaina

cāpi vaggaināpi ca (sī॰ syā॰) yaṃ kataṃ|

adaisanāgāminiyā, adhammavaggamaiva ca||

daisitāya  adhammaina, vaggaināpi tathaiva ca|

acaudaitvā  adhammaina, vaggaināpi tathaiva ca||

asāraitvā adhammaina, vaggaināpi tathaiva ca|

anāraupaitvā adhammaina, vaggaināpi tathaiva ca||

kaṇhavāranayainaiva, suk‍kavāraṃ vijāniyā|

saṅghau ākaṅkhamānau ca yassa taj‍janiyaṃ karai||

bhaṇḍanaṃ bālau saṃsaṭṭhau, adhisīlai ajjhācārai|

atidiṭṭhivipan‍nassa, saṅghau taj‍janiyaṃ karai||

buddhadhammassa  saṅghassa, avaṇṇaṃ yau ca bhāsati|

tiṇṇan‍nampi ca bhikkhūnaṃ, saṅghau taj‍janiyaṃ karai||

bhaṇḍanaṃ kārakau aikau, bālau saṃsagganissitau|

adhisīlai ajjhācārai, tathaiva atidiṭṭhiyā||

buddhadhammassa saṅghassa, avaṇṇaṃ yau ca bhāsati|

taj‍janīyakammakatau, aivaṃ sammānuvattanā||

upasampadanissayā, sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhanā|

auvādasammataināpi, na karai taj‍janīkatau||

nāpaj‍jai tañ‍ca āpattiṃ, tādisañ‍ca tatau paraṃ|

kammañ‍ca kammikai cāpi, na garahai tathāvidhau||

upausathaṃ pavāraṇaṃ, pakatattassa naṭṭhapai|

savacaniṃ na savacaniyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) anuvādau, aukāsau caudanaina ca||

sāraṇaṃ  sampayaugañ‍ca, na karaiyya tathāvidhau|

upasampadanissayā, sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhanā||

auvādasammataināpi, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi pañ‍caaṅgau (ka॰) na sammati|

tañ‍cāpaj‍jati āpattiṃ, tādisañ‍ca tatau paraṃ||

kammañ‍ca kammikai cāpi, garahantau na sammati|

upausathaṃ  pavāraṇaṃ, savacanīyā ca nauvādau||

aukāsau caudanañ‍caiva, sāraṇā sampayaujanā|

imaihaṭṭhaṅgaihi yau yuttau, taj‍janānupasammati||

kaṇhavāranayainaiva, suk‍kavāraṃ vijāniyā|

bālau āpattibahulau, saṃsaṭṭhaupi ca saiyyasau||

niyassakammaṃ sambuddhau, āṇāpaisi mahāmuni|

kīṭāgirismiṃ dvai bhikkhū, assajipunabbasukā||

anācārañ‍ca  vividhaṃ, ācariṃsu asañ‍ñatā|

pabbājanīyaṃ sambuddhau, kammaṃ sāvatthiyaṃ jinau|

macchikāsaṇḍai sudhammau, cittassāvāsikau ahu||

jātivādaina khuṃsaiti, sudhammau cittupāsakaṃ|

paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ, āṇāpaisi tathāgatau||

kausambiyaṃ chan‍naṃ bhikkhuṃ, nicchantāpattiṃ passituṃ|

adassanai  ukkhipituṃ, āṇāpaisi jinuttamau||

chan‍nau taṃyaiva āpattiṃ, paṭikātuṃ na icchati|

ukkhaipanāppaṭikammai, āṇāpaisi vināyakau||

pāpadiṭṭhi  ariṭṭhassa, āsi añ‍ñāṇanissitā|

diṭṭhiyāppaṭinissaggai diṭṭhiappaṭinissaggai (ka॰), ukkhaipaṃ jinabhāsitaṃ||

niyassakammaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ pabbājaṃ (ka॰), tathaiva paṭisāraṇī|

adassanāppaṭikammai, anissaggai ca diṭṭhiyā||

davānācārūpaghāti, micchāājīvamaiva ca|

pabbājanīyakammamhi, atiraikapadā imai||

alābhāvaṇṇā dvai pañ‍ca, dvai pañ‍cakāti nāmakā dvai pañ‍cakauti nāmakau (ka॰)|

paṭisāraṇīyakammamhi, atiraikapadā imai||

taj‍janīyaṃ niyassañ‍ca, duvai kammāpi sādisā kammaisu sadisaṃ (ka॰)|

pabbaj‍jā pabbājā (ka॰) paṭisārī ca, atthi padātirittatā||

tayau ukkhaipanā kammā, sadisā tai vibhattitau|

taj‍janīyanayaināpi, saisakammaṃ vijāniyāti||

kammakkhandhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



2. pārivāsikakkhandhakaṃ

1. pārivāsikavattaṃ

75. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina pārivāsikā bhikkhū sādiyanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ, saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma pārivāsikā bhikkhū sādiyissanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ, saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikamma’’nti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikā bhikkhū sādiyanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ, saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikamma’’nti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikā bhikkhū sādiyissanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ, saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ  pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sāditabbaṃ pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihārau, saiyyābhihārau, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| yau sādiyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mithu yathāvuḍḍhaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ, saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ|

‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pañ‍ca yathāvuḍḍhaṃ – upausathaṃ, pavāraṇaṃ, vassikasāṭikaṃ, auṇaujanaṃ, bhattaṃ| taina hi, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi yathā pārivāsikaihi bhikkhūhi vattitabbaṃ|

76. ‘‘pārivāsikaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā –

na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina parivāsau din‍nau hauti sā āpatti na āpaj‍jitabbā, añ‍ñā vā  tādisikā, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhatarā; kammaṃ na garahitabbaṃ, kammikā na garahitabbā| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathau ṭhapaitabbau, na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā, na savacanīyaṃ kātabbaṃ, na anuvādau paṭṭhapaitabbau, na aukāsau kāraitabbau, na caudaitabbau, na sāraitabbau, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā pakatattassa bhikkhunau puratau gantabbaṃ, na puratau nisīditabbaṃ| yau hauti saṅghassa āsanapariyantau saiyyāpariyantau vihārapariyantau sau tassa padātabbau| taina ca sau sāditabbau|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā pakatattaina bhikkhunā puraisamaṇaina vā pacchāsamaṇaina vā kulāni upasaṅkamitabbāni, na ārañ‍ñikaṅgaṃ samādātabbaṃ samāditabbaṃ (ka॰), na piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ samādātabbaṃ, na ca tappac‍cayā piṇḍapātau nīharāpaitabbau – mā maṃ jāniṃsūti|

‘‘pārivāsikaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā āgantukaina āraucaitabbaṃ, āgantukassa āraucaitabbaṃ, upausathai āraucaitabbaṃ, pavāraṇāya āraucaitabbaṃ| sacai gilānau hauti, dūtainapi āraucaitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā| na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā| na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā| na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā| na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā| na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā abhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā| na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau, yatthassu  bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā| na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina  bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra  pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā| na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā| na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā| na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā| na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra  antarāyā|

80. ‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau anāvāsau, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau anāvāsau, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau āvāsau, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā  sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau anāvāsau, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|
