81. ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ| pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā āsanā vuṭṭhātabbaṃ| pakatattau bhikkhu āsanaina nimantaitabbau| na pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ caṅkamantai caṅkamantaṃ (aṭṭhakathāyaṃ saṃvaṇṇaitabbapāṭhau) caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ|

82. ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā pārivāsikaina vuḍḍhataraina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mānattārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai  āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ; na aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ caṅkamantai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ|

mahāva॰ 393 ‘‘pārivāsikacatutthau cai, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ dadaiyya, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, mānattaṃ dadaiyya, taṃvīsau abbhaiyya, akammaṃ akammaṃ taṃ (syā॰), na ca karaṇīya’’nti|

catun‍navutipārivāsikavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

83. atha khau āyasmā upāli yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, pārivāsikassa bhikkhunau ratticchaidā’’ti? ‘‘tayau khau, upāli, pārivāsikassa bhikkhunau ratticchaidā| sahavāsau, vippavāsau, anāraucanā – imai khau, upāli, tayau pārivāsikassa bhikkhunau ratticchaidā’’ti|

84. taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ mahābhikkhusaṅghau san‍nipatitau hauti| na sak‍kaunti pārivāsikā bhikkhū parivāsaṃ saudhaituṃ| bhagavatau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau (syā॰, aivamuparipi) aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ nikkhipituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nikkhipitabbau| taina pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘parivāsaṃ nikkhipāmī’ti| nikkhittau hauti parivāsau| ‘vattaṃ nikkhipāmī’ti| nikkhittau hauti parivāsau|

85. taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyā bhikkhū tahaṃ tahaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu| sak‍kaunti pārivāsikā bhikkhū parivāsaṃ saudhaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ samādiyituṃ samādātuṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samādiyitabbau samāditabbau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)| taina pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā  aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā  añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘parivāsaṃ samādiyāmī’ti| samādin‍nau hauti parivāsau| ‘vattaṃ samādiyāmī’ti| samādin‍nau hauti parivāsau’’|

pārivāsikavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. mūlāyapaṭikassanārahavattaṃ

86. taina  khau pana samayaina mūlāyapaṭikassanārahā bhikkhū sādiyanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ, saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma mūlāyapaṭikassanārahā bhikkhū sādiyissanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikamma’’nti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahā bhikkhū sādiyanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikamma’’nti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahā bhikkhū sādiyissanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ! naitaṃ bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sāditabbaṃ pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihārau, saiyyābhihārau, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| yau sādiyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ mithu yathāvuḍḍhaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pañ‍ca yathāvuḍḍhaṃ – upausathaṃ, pavāraṇaṃ, vassikasāṭikaṃ, auṇaujanaṃ, bhattaṃ| taina hi, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ pañ‍ñāpaissāmi yathā mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaihi bhikkhūhi vattitabbaṃ|

87. ‘‘mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā –

na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina mūlāya paṭikassanārahau katau hauti sā āpatti na āpaj‍jitabbā, añ‍ñā vā tādisikā, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhatarā; kammaṃ na garahitabbaṃ, kammikā na garahitabbā| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathau ṭhapaitabbau, na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā, na savacanīyaṃ kātabbaṃ, na anuvādau paṭṭhapaitabbau, na aukāsau kāraitabbau, na caudaitabbau, na sāraitabbau, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā pakatattassa bhikkhunau puratau gantabbaṃ, na puratau nisīditabbaṃ| yau hauti saṅghassa āsanapariyantau saiyyāpariyantau vihārapariyantau sau tassa padātabbau| taina ca sau sāditabbau|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā pakatattaina bhikkhunā puraisamaṇaina vā  pacchāsamaṇaina vā kulāni upasaṅkamitabbāni, na ārañ‍ñikaṅgaṃ samādātabbaṃ, na piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ samādātabbaṃ, na ca tappac‍cayā piṇḍapātau nīharāpaitabbau – mā maṃ jāniṃsūti|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau  gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau āvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ; pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā āsanā vuṭṭhātabbaṃ, pakatattau bhikkhu āsanaina nimantaitabbau; na pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na chamāyaṃ nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ caṅkamantai caṅkamai  caṅkamitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina vuḍḍhataraina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mānattārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā  saddhiṃ…pai॰… abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ; na aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ caṅkamantai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ|

mahāva॰ 393 ‘‘mūlāyapaṭikassanārahacatutthau cai, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ dadaiyya, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, mānattaṃ dadaiyya, taṃvīsau abbhaiyya, akammaṃ, na ca karaṇīya’’nti|

mūlāyapaṭikassanārahavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. mānattārahavattaṃ

88. taina  khau pana samayaina mānattārahā bhikkhū sādiyanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ, saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā  …pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma mānattārahā bhikkhū sādiyissanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ, saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikamma’’nti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, mānattārahā bhikkhū sādiyanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikamma’’nti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, mānattārahā  bhikkhū sādiyissanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā sāditabbaṃ pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| yau sādiyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mānattārahānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mithu yathāvuḍḍhaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mānattārahānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pañ‍ca yathāvuḍḍhaṃ – upausathaṃ, pavāraṇaṃ, vassikasāṭikaṃ, auṇaujanaṃ, bhattaṃ| taina hi, bhikkhavai, mānattārahānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ pañ‍ñāpaissāmi yathā mānattārahaihi bhikkhūhi vattitabbaṃ|

89. ‘‘mānattārahaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā –

na upasampādaitabbaṃ…pai॰… (yathā mūlāya paṭikassanā, tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ|) na bhikkhūhi  sampayaujaitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā pakatattassa bhikkhunau puratau gantabbaṃ, na puratau nisīditabbaṃ| yau hauti saṅghassa āsanapariyantau saiyyāpariyantau vihārapariyantau sau tassa padātabbau| taina ca sau sāditabbau|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā pakatattaina bhikkhunā puraisamaṇaina vā pacchāsamaṇaina vā kulāni upasaṅkamitabbāni, na ārañ‍ñikaṅgaṃ samādātabbaṃ, na piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ samādātabbaṃ, na ca tappac‍cayā piṇḍapātau nīharāpaitabbau – mā maṃ jāniṃsūti|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, manattārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau  āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau  …pai॰… sabhikkhukau  āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū  nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau āvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ| pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā āsanā vuṭṭhātabbaṃ| pakatattau bhikkhu āsanaina nimantaitabbau| na pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na  nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ caṅkamantai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattārahaina bhikkhunā pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mānattārahaina vuḍḍhataraina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ; na aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ caṅkamantai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ|

mahāva॰ 393 ‘‘mānattārahacatutthau  cai, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ dadaiyya, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, mānattaṃ dadaiyya, taṃvīsau abbhaiyya, akammaṃ, na ca karaṇīya’’nti|

mānattārahavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. mānattacārikavattaṃ

90. taina khau pana samayaina mānattacārikā bhikkhū sādiyanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihāraṃ, saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰…  tai ujhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma mānattacārikā bhikkhū sādiyissanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikamma’’nti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikā bhikkhū sādiyanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikamma’’nti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikā bhikkhū sādiyissanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sāditabbaṃ pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, āsanābhihārau, saiyyābhihārau, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ, pādakathalikaṃ, pattacīvarapaṭiggahaṇaṃ, nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| yau sādiyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mithu yathāvuḍḍhaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pañ‍ca yathāvuḍḍhaṃ – upausathaṃ, pavāraṇaṃ, vassikasāṭikaṃ, auṇaujanaṃ, bhattaṃ| taina hi, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi yathā mānattacārikaihi bhikkhūhi vattitabbaṃ|

91. ‘‘mānattacārikaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā –

na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā| yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina mānattaṃ din‍naṃ hauti sā āpatti na āpaj‍jitabbā, añ‍ñā vā tādisikā, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhatarā; kammaṃ na garahitabbaṃ, kammikā na garahitabbā| na pakatattassa bhikkhunau upausathau ṭhapaitabbau, na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā, na savacanīyaṃ kātabbaṃ, na anuvādau paṭṭhapaitabbau, na aukāsau kāraitabbau, na caudaitabbau, na sāraitabbau, na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā pakatattassa bhikkhunau puratau gantabbaṃ, na puratau nisīditabbaṃ| yau hauti saṅghassa āsanapariyantau saiyyāpariyantau vihārapariyantau sau tassa padātabbau| taina ca sau sāditabbau|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā pakatattaina bhikkhunā puraisamaṇaina vā pacchāsamaṇaina vā kulāni upasaṅkamitabbāni, na ārañ‍ñikaṅgaṃ samādātabbaṃ, na piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ samādātabbaṃ, na ca tappac‍cayā piṇḍapātau nīharāpaitabbau – mā maṃ jāniṃsūti|

‘‘mānattacārikaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā āgantukaina āraucaitabbaṃ, āgantukassa āraucaitabbaṃ, upausathai āraucaitabbaṃ, pavāraṇāya āraucaitabbaṃ, daivasikaṃ āraucaitabbaṃ| sacai gilānau hauti, dūtainapi āraucaitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra saṅghaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra saṅghaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra saṅghaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra saṅghaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… abhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, añ‍ñatra saṅghaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra saṅghaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra saṅghaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau  āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā gantabbau, yatthassu bhikkhū nānāsaṃvāsakā, añ‍ñatra saṅghaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā anāvāsā sabhikkhukau āvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau āvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ| pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā āsanā vuṭṭhātabbaṃ| pakatattau bhikkhu āsanaina nimantaitabbau| na pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ caṅkamantai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mānattārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mānattacārikaina vuḍḍhataraina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ; na aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ caṅkamantai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ|

mahāva॰ 393 ‘‘mānattacārikacatutthau cai, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ dadaiyya, mūlāya  paṭikassaiyya, mānattaṃ dadaiyya, taṃvīsau abbhaiyya, akammaṃ, na ca karaṇīya’’nti|

92. atha khau āyasmā upāli yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, mānattacārikassa bhikkhunau ratticchaidā’’ti? ‘‘cattārau khau, upāli, mānattacārikassa bhikkhunau ratticchaidā| sahavāsau, vippavāsau, anāraucanā, ūnai gaṇai caraṇaṃ caraṇanti (ka॰) – imai khau, upāli, cattārau mānattacārikassa bhikkhunau ratticchaidā’’ti|

93. taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ mahābhikkhusaṅghau san‍nipatitau hauti| na sak‍kaunti mānattacārikā bhikkhū mānattaṃ saudhaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mānattaṃ nikkhipituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nikkhipitabbaṃ| taina mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘mānattaṃ nikkhipāmī’ti| nikkhittaṃ hauti mānattaṃ| ‘vattaṃ nikkhipāmī’ti| nikkhittaṃ hauti mānatta’’nti|

94. taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyā bhikkhū tahaṃ tahaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu| sak‍kaunti mānattacārikā bhikkhū mānattaṃ saudhaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mānattaṃ samādiyituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samādiyitabbaṃ| taina mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā, aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘mānattaṃ samādiyāmī’ti| samādin‍naṃ hauti mānattaṃ| ‘vattaṃ samādiyāmī’ti| samādin‍naṃ hauti mānatta’’nti|

mānattacārikavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. abbhānārahavattaṃ

95. taina  khau pana samayaina abbhānārahā bhikkhū sādiyanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma abbhānārahā bhikkhū sādiyissanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikamma’’nti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahā bhikkhū sādiyanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammanti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau  bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ…pai॰… kathañhi nāma, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahā bhikkhū sādiyissanti pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā sāditabbaṃ pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| yau sādiyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mithu yathāvuḍḍhaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ…pai॰… nahānai piṭṭhiparikammaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pañ‍ca yathāvuḍḍhaṃ – upausathaṃ, pavāraṇaṃ, vassikasāṭikaṃ, auṇaujanaṃ, bhattaṃ| taina hi, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ pañ‍ñāpaissāmi yathā abbhānārahaihi bhikkhūhi vattitabbaṃ|

96. ‘‘abbhānārahaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā –

na upasampādaitabbaṃ…pai॰… (yathā haiṭṭhā, tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ,) na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā pakatattassa bhikkhunau puratau gantabbaṃ, na puratau nisīditabbaṃ| yau hauti saṅghassa āsanapariyantau saiyyāpariyantau vihārapariyantau sau tassa padātabbau| taina ca sau sāditabbau|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā  pakatattaina bhikkhunā puraisamaṇaina vā pacchāsamaṇaina vā kulāni upasaṅkamitabbāni; na ārañ‍ñikaṅgaṃ samādātabbaṃ; na piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ samādātabbaṃ; na ca tappac‍cayā piṇḍapātau nīharāpaitabbau – mā maṃ jāniṃsūti|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau āvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā abhikkhukau anāvāsau gantabbau, añ‍ñatra pakatattaina, añ‍ñatra antarāyā…pai॰…| (yathā haiṭṭhā, tathā vitthāraitabbā|)

‘‘gantabbau, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā sabhikkhukā āvāsā…pai॰… anāvāsā…pai॰… āvāsā vā anāvāsā vā sabhikkhukau  āvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau anāvāsau…pai॰… sabhikkhukau āvāsau vā anāvāsau vā, yatthassu bhikkhū samānasaṃvāsakā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā sak‍kaumi aj‍jaiva gantunti|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ; pakatattaṃ bhikkhuṃ disvā āsanā vuṭṭhātabbaṃ, pakatattau bhikkhu āsanaina nimantaitabbau; na pakatattaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na nīcai caṅkamai  caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ caṅkamantai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ|

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, abbhānārahaina bhikkhunā pārivāsikaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mūlāyapaṭikassanārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mānattārahaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… mānattacārikaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ…pai॰… abbhānārahaina vuḍḍhataraina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vatthabbaṃ, na aikacchan‍nai anāvāsai vatthabbaṃ; na aikacchan‍nai āvāsai vā anāvāsai vā vatthabbaṃ; na aikāsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na nīcai āsanai nisin‍nai uc‍cai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ nisin‍nai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; na aikacaṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na nīcai caṅkamai caṅkamantai uc‍cai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ, na chamāyaṃ caṅkamantai caṅkamai caṅkamitabbaṃ|

mahāva॰ 393 ‘‘abbhānārahacatutthau cai, bhikkhavai, parivāsaṃ dadaiyya, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, mānattaṃ dadaiyya, taṃvīsau abbhaiyya, akammaṃ, na ca karaṇīya’’nti|

abbhānārahavattaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

pārivāsikakkhandhakau dutiyau|

imamhi khandhakai vatthū pañ‍ca|

tassuddānaṃ –

pārivāsikā  sādainti, pakatattāna bhikkhunaṃ|

abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jaliñ‍ca sāmīciyaṃ||

āsanaṃ  saiyyābhihāraṃ, pādau pīṭhaṃ kathalikaṃ|

pattaṃ nahānai parikammaṃ, ujjhāyanti ca paisalā||

duk‍kaṭaṃ sādiyantassa, mithu pañ‍ca yathāvuḍḍhaṃ punāparai (ka॰)|

upausathaṃ pavāraṇaṃ, vassikauṇaujabhaujanaṃ||

sammā ca vattanā tattha, pakatattassa gacchantaṃ|

yau ca hauti pariyantau, purai pacchā tathaiva ca na purai pacchāsamaṇaina (sī॰ syā॰)||

ārañ‍ñapiṇḍanīhārau, āgantukai upausathai|

pavāraṇāya dūtaina, gantabbau ca sabhikkhukau||

aikacchan‍nai  ca vuṭṭhānaṃ, tathaiva ca nimantayai|

āsanai nīcai caṅkamai, chamāyaṃ caṅkamaina ca||

vuḍḍhataraina akammaṃ, ratticchaidā ca saudhanā|

nikkhipanaṃ samādānaṃ, vattaṃva pārivāsikai ratti vā pārivāsikai (ka॰), ñātabbaṃ pārivāsikā (sī॰ syā॰)||

mūlāya  mānattārahā, tathā mānattacārikā|

abbhānārahai nayau cāpi, sambhaidaṃ nayatau sambhaidanayatau (syā॰) puna||

pārivāsikaisu tayau, catu mānattacārikai|

na samainti ratticchaidaisu ratticchaidai (itipi), ratticchaidā (syā॰), mānattaisu ca daivasi|

dvai kammā sadisā saisā, tayau kammā samāsamāti samā matāti (sī॰)||

pārivāsikakkhandhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



3. samuc‍cayakkhandhakaṃ

1. suk‍kavissaṭṭhi

97. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hauti sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau (syā॰) aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbaṃ –

appaṭicchan‍namānattaṃ

98. ‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| dutiyampi, sauhaṃ dutiyampi (sī॰ ka॰) bhantai, saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā  sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmi| ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| tatiyampi sauhaṃ tatiyampi (sī॰ ka॰), bhantai, saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’ti| ‘‘byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

99. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā  āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍naṃ  saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

appaṭicchan‍naabbhānaṃ

100. sau  ciṇṇamānattau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, abbhaitabbau –

taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘ahaṃ bhantai aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ, sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ, bhantai, ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmi|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ  saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau dutiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmi|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau tatiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmīti| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau  ñāpaitabbau –

101. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā  sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya  suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sau ciṇṇamānattau  saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘abbhitau  saṅghaina udāyī bhikkhu| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

aikāhappaṭicchan‍naparivāsaṃ

102. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hauti sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| ‘‘byattaina  bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

103. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau  aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

aikāhappaṭicchan‍namānattaṃ

104. sau parivutthaparivāsau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbaṃ –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ  aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ, bhantai, parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbaṃ yācitabbau (sī॰ aivamuparipi)| tatiyampi yācitabbaṃ yācitabbau (sī॰ aivamuparipi)| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

105. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ  aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

aikāhappaṭicchan‍naabbhānaṃ

106. sau ciṇṇamānattau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ  saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, abbhaitabbau –

‘‘taina bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa  vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ, bhantai, ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbaṃ| tatiyampi yācitabbaṃ| byattaina  bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

107. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau  aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sau ciṇṇamānattau  saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ aikāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya aikāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya  chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā aikāhappaṭicchan‍nāya  chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘abbhitau saṅghaina udāyī bhikkhu| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naparivāsau

108. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hauti sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ dvīhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… tīhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰…  catūhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ  sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

109. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyissa  bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau  aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

pārivāsikamūlāyapaṭikassanā

110. sau parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā  sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassatu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, mūlāya paṭikassitabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā  āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya  paṭikassanaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā yācitabbau (sī॰ aivamuparipi)| tatiyampi yācitabbā yācitabbau (sī॰ aivamuparipi)| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

111. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassati| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘paṭikassitau saṅghaina udāyī bhikkhu antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanā mūlāya (syā॰)| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

mānattārahamūlāyapaṭikassanā

112. sau parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ  pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya  suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassatu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, mūlāya paṭikassitabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰…  sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ  sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

113. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰…  sau parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau antarā  aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sau parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassati| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘paṭikassitau saṅghaina udāyī bhikkhu antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanā| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

tikāpattimānattaṃ

114. sau parivutthaparivāsau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰…  sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbaṃ –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya  pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi…pai॰…  sauhaṃ, bhantai, parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbaṃ| tatiyampi yācitabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

115. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰…  sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰…  sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ  tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ  chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

mānattacārikamūlāyapaṭikassanā

116. sau mānattaṃ carantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰…  sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassitvā chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, mūlāya paṭikassitabbau – taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰…  sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| ‘‘byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

117. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu…pai॰…  mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassaiyya| aisā ñatti…pai॰…  paṭikassitau saṅghaina udāyī bhikkhu antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanā| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ – taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya  suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbaṃ| tatiyampi yācitabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu…pai॰… chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti…pai॰…  din‍naṃ saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

abbhānārahamūlāyapaṭikassanā

118. sau  ciṇṇamānattau abbhānārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau abbhānārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassitvā chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, mūlāya paṭikassitabbau…pai॰…|

‘‘aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

mūlāyapaṭikassitaabbhānaṃ

119. sau ciṇṇamānattau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sauhaṃ  ciṇṇamānattau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, abbhaitabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā  bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ  appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau  antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau abbhānārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ, bhantai, ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmī’’’ti|

‘‘dutiyampi yācitabbaṃ| tatiyampi yācitabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

120. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ  suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya pañ‍cāhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya  mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sau parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya  suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sau mānattaṃ carantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā  sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sau ciṇṇamānattau abbhānārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ appaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā appaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘abbhitau  saṅghaina udāyī bhikkhu| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

pakkhappaṭicchan‍naparivāsau

121. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hauti sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunau paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

122. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā  sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau  saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

pakkhapārivāsikamūlāyapaṭikassanā

123. sau parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā  āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassitvā purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, mūlāya paṭikassitabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ  adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

124. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassati| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘paṭikassitau saṅghaina udāyī bhikkhu antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanā| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

samaudhānaparivāsau

125. ‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau – taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ  pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā  āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā  sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācāmī’’’ti|

‘‘dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

126. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya  purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā  sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina udāyissa  bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

mānattārahamūlāyapaṭikassanādi

127. sau parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassitvā purimāya  āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, mūlāya paṭikassitabbau…pai॰…|

‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau…pai॰… daiti…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

tikāpattimānattaṃ

128. sau parivutthaparivāsau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ  āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbaṃ –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sauhaṃ, bhantai, parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbaṃ| tatiyampi yācitabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

129. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|
