‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau  tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati  udāyissa bhikkhunau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ  saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

mānattacārikamūlāyapaṭikassanādi

130. sau mānattaṃ carantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassitvā purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ datvā chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, mūlāya paṭikassitabbau…pai॰…|

‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau…pai॰…|

‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ…pai॰… daiti…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

abbhānārahamūlāyapaṭikassanādi

131. sau ciṇṇamānattau abbhānārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau abbhānārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā  paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassitvā purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ datvā chārattaṃ mānattaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, mūlāya paṭikassitabbau…pai॰…|

‘‘aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau…pai॰…|

‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, chārattaṃ mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ…pai॰… daiti…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍naṃ saṅghaina udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

pakkhappaṭicchan‍naabbhānaṃ

132. sau  ciṇṇamānattau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, abbhaitabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, udāyinā bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā, aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā, vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā, uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā, añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā, aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā  sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa  mai saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya  purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ mānattaṃ carantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ ciṇṇamānattau abbhānārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jiṃ sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya  mūlāya paṭikassi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā  pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ, bhantai, ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācāmī’’’ti|

‘‘dutiyampi yācitabbaṃ| tatiyampi yācitabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

133. ‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pakkhappaṭicchan‍nāya pakkhaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivasantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivutthaparivāsau mānattārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ  pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā  āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau tissan‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sau mānattaṃ carantau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivutthaparivāsau  saṅghaṃ sau saṃghaṃ (ka॰) antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ  adāsi| sau ciṇṇamānattau abbhānārahau antarā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍naṃ| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya mūlāya paṭikassi| sau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sau parivutthaparivāsau saṅghaṃ antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ  mānattaṃ yāci| saṅghau udāyissa bhikkhunau antarā aikissā āpattiyā sañ‍caitanikāya suk‍kavissaṭṭhiyā pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāya chārattaṃ mānattaṃ adāsi| sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ udāyī bhikkhu aikaṃ āpattiṃ  āpaj‍ji sañ‍caitanikaṃ suk‍kavissaṭṭhiṃ pakkhappaṭicchan‍naṃ…pai॰… sau ciṇṇamānattau saṅghaṃ abbhānaṃ yācati| saṅghau udāyiṃ bhikkhuṃ abbhaiti| yassāyasmatau khamati udāyissa bhikkhunau abbhānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘abbhitau saṅghaina udāyī bhikkhu| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

suk‍kavissaṭṭhi samattā|

2. parivāsau

agghasamaudhānaparivāsau

134. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpan‍nau hauti – aikā āpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti dvīhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti tīhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti catūhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā  āpatti chāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti sattāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti aṭṭhāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti navāhappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ – aikā āpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā…pai॰… aikā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau tassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā  tassā agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ – aikā āpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā…pai॰… aikā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā tassā agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

135. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā  āpattiyau āpaj‍ji – aikā āpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā…pai॰… aikā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā tassā agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā tassā agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji – aikā āpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā…pai॰… aikā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā  āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā tassā agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā tassā agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā; tassā agghaina samaudhānaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi  aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpatti dasāhappaṭicchan‍nā tassā agghaina samaudhānaparivāsau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

sabbacirappaṭicchan‍naagghasamaudhānaṃ

136. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpan‍nau hauti – aikā āpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā, dvai āpattiyau dvīhappaṭicchan‍nāyau dvīhappaṭicchan‍nā (ka॰ aivaṃ yāvadasāhappaṭicchan‍nā), tissau āpattiyau tīhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, catassau āpattiyau catūhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, pañ‍ca āpattiyau pañ‍cāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, cha āpattiyau chāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, satta āpattiyau sattāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, aṭṭha āpattiyau aṭṭhāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, nava āpattiyau navāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau, dasa āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ, aikā āpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā…pai॰… dasa āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau tassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau sabbacirappaṭicchan‍nāyau tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ, aikā āpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā…pai॰… dasa āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau sabbacirappaṭicchan‍nāyau tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| ‘‘byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

137. suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji, aikā āpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā…pai॰… dasa āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau sabbacirappaṭicchan‍nāyau tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau sabbacirappaṭicchan‍nāyau tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau  āpaj‍ji – aikā āpatti aikāhappaṭicchan‍nā…pai॰… dasa āpattiyau dasāhappaṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā  āpattiyau sabbacirappaṭicchan‍nāyau tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau sabbacirappaṭicchan‍nāyau tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau sabbacirappaṭicchan‍nāyau tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ yā āpattiyau sabbacirappaṭicchan‍nāyau tāsaṃ agghaina samaudhānaparivāsau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

dvaimāsaparivāsau

138. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpan‍nau hauti dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’’nti| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa parivasantassa  laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyyanti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’’nti|

sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ  khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ  dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ  yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyyanti| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau tassa bhikkhunau itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā, aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā, vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā, uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā, añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā, aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ  yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyyanti| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācāmīti|

‘‘dutiyampi  yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| ‘‘byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

139. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ  saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyyanti| sau saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya  dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ  aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ itarissāpi  āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyyanti| sau saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tadupādāya dvai māsā parivasitabbā|

dvai māsā parivasitabbavidhi

140. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| tassa parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami  – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau aikissā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyyanti| sau saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tadupādāya dvai māsā parivasitabbā|

141. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, aikaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti| sau saṅghaṃ yaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau  itarampi āpattiṃ jānāti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikaṃ āpattiṃ jāniṃ, aikaṃ āpattiṃ na jāniṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ yaṃ āpattiṃ jāniṃ tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau itarampi āpattiṃ jānāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ itarissāpi  āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tadupādāya dvai māsā parivasitabbā|

142. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikaṃ āpattiṃ sarati, aikaṃ āpattiṃ nassarati| sau saṅghaṃ yaṃ āpattiṃ sarati tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya  dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau itarampi āpattiṃ sarati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikaṃ āpattiṃ sariṃ, aikaṃ āpattiṃ nassariṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ yaṃ āpattiṃ sariṃ, tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ  yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau itarampi āpattiṃ sarāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tadupādāya dvai māsā parivasitabbā|

143. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikāya āpattiyā nibbaimatikau, aikāya āpattiyā vaimatikau| sau saṅghaṃ yāya āpattiyā nibbaimatikau tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau itarissāpi āpattiyā nibbaimatikau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā  āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikāya āpattiyā nibbaimatikau, aikāya āpattiyā vaimatikau| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ yāya āpattiyā nibbaimatikau tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau tassā āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau itarissāpi āpattiyā nibbaimatikau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ  saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau itarissāpi āpattiyā dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāya dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tadupādāya dvai māsā parivasitabbā|

144. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikā āpatti jānappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti ajānappaṭicchan‍nā| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| tassa parivasantassa añ‍ñau bhikkhu āgacchati bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘kiṃ ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau? kissāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti? tai aivaṃ vadainti – ‘ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikā āpatti jānappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti ajānappaṭicchan‍nā| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tāyau ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau, tāsāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘yāyaṃ, āvusau, āpatti jānappaṭicchan‍nā, dhammikaṃ tassā āpattiyā parivāsadānaṃ; dhammattā ruhati| yā ca khvāyaṃ, āvusau, āpatti ajānappaṭicchan‍nā, adhammikaṃ tassā āpattiyā parivāsadānaṃ; adhammattā na ruhati| aikissā, āvusau, āpattiyā bhikkhu mānattārahau’’’ti|

145. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikā āpatti saramānappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti assaramānappaṭicchan‍nā| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| tassa parivasantassa añ‍ñau bhikkhu āgacchati bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī  kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘ki ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau? kissāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti? tai aivaṃ vadainti – ‘ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikā āpatti saramānappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti assaramānappaṭicchan‍nā| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tāyau  ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau; tāsāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘yāyaṃ, āvusau, āpatti saramānappaṭicchan‍nā dhammikaṃ tassā āpattiyā parivāsadānaṃ; dhammattā ruhati| yā ca khvāyaṃ, āvusau, āpatti assaramānappaṭicchan‍nā, adhammikaṃ tassā āpattiyā parivāsadānaṃ; adhammattā na ruhati| aikissā, āvusau, āpattiyā bhikkhu mānattārahau’’’ti|

146. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikā āpatti nibbaimatikappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti vaimatikappaṭicchan‍nā| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| tassa parivasantassa añ‍ñau bhikkhu āgacchati bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘kiṃ ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau? kissāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti? tai aivaṃ vadainti – ‘ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikā āpatti nibbaimatikappaṭicchan‍nā, aikā āpatti vaimatikappaṭicchan‍nā| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tāyau ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau; tāsāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘yāyaṃ, āvusau, āpatti nibbaimatikappaṭicchan‍nā, dhammikaṃ tassā āpattiyā parivāsadānaṃ; dhammattā ruhati| yā ca khvāyaṃ, āvusau, āpatti vaimatikappaṭicchan‍nā adhammikaṃ tassā āpattiyā parivāsadānaṃ; adhammattā na ruhati| aikissā, āvusau, āpattiyā bhikkhu mānattārahau’’ti|

147. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñattarau bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpan‍nau hauti dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ  yācaiyya’’nti| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ  yāci| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – ‘‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ  dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ itarampi māsaparivāsaṃ (syā॰ ka॰ aivamuparipi) yācaiyya’’nti|

sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau tassa bhikkhunau dvin‍naṃ  āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ahaṃ, bhantai, dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ  aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācaiyyanti| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ  dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācāmīti| dutiyampi  yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

148. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācaiyyanti| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ  dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācati| yadi  saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ  yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau dvin‍naṃ  āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsassa itarampi māsaparivāsassa (ka॰), itarassapi māsaparivāsassa (syā॰) dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsau itarampi māsaparivāsau (ka॰), itaraupi māsaparivāsau (syā॰)| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā purimaṃ upādāya dvai māsā parivasitabbā|

149. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| tassa parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami – ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| tassa mai aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ  saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ aikamāsaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ  aikamāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tassa mai parivasantassa laj‍jīdhammau auk‍kami| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ daiti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā purimaṃ upādāya dvai māsā parivasitabbā|

150. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikaṃ māsaṃ jānāti, aikaṃ māsaṃ na jānāti| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ yaṃ māsaṃ jānāti taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ yaṃ māsaṃ jānāti taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau itarampi māsaṃ jānāti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikaṃ māsaṃ jāniṃ, aikaṃ māsaṃ na jāniṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ yaṃ māsaṃ jāniṃ taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ yaṃ  māsaṃ jāniṃ taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau itarampi māsaṃ jānāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ daiti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā purimaṃ upādāya dvai māsā parivasitabbā|

151. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikaṃ māsaṃ sarati, aikaṃ māsaṃ nassarati| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ yaṃ māsaṃ sarati taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ yaṃ māsaṃ sarati taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau itarampi māsaṃ sarati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikaṃ māsaṃ sariṃ, aikaṃ māsaṃ nassariṃ| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ yaṃ māsaṃ sariṃ taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ yaṃ māsaṃ sariṃ taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau itarampi māsaṃ sarāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ  āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācati| tassa  saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ daiti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā purimaṃ upādāya dvai māsā parivasitabbā|

152. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikaṃ māsaṃ nibbaimatikau, aikaṃ māsaṃ vaimatikau| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ yaṃ māsaṃ nibbaimatikau taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ yaṃ māsaṃ nibbaimatikau taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau itarampi māsaṃ nibbaimatikau hauti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikaṃ māsaṃ nibbaimatikau, aikaṃ māsaṃ vaimatikau| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ yaṃ māsaṃ nibbaimatikau taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchān‍nānaṃ yaṃ māsaṃ nibbaimatikau taṃ māsaṃ parivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau itarampi māsaṃ nibbaimatikau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ  itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācaiyya’nti| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ itarampi māsaṃ parivāsaṃ daiti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā purimaṃ upādāya dvai māsā parivasitabbā|

153. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikau māsau jānappaṭicchan‍nau, aikau māsau ajānappaṭicchan‍nau| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| tassa parivasantassa añ‍ñau bhikkhu āgacchati bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘kiṃ ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau? kissāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti? tai aivaṃ vadainti – ‘ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau| aikau māsau jānappaṭicchan‍nau, aikau māsau ajānappaṭicchan‍nau| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tāyau ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau tāsāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘yvāyaṃ, āvusau, māsau jānappaṭicchan‍nau dhammikaṃ tassa māsassa parivāsadānaṃ; dhammattā ruhati| yau ca  khvāyaṃ, āvusau, māsau ajānappaṭicchan‍nau adhammikaṃ tassa māsassa parivāsadānaṃ; adhammattā na ruhati| aikassa, āvusau, māsassa bhikkhu mānattārahau’ti|

154. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikau māsau saramānappaṭicchan‍nau, aikau māsau assaramānappaṭicchan‍nau| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| tassa parivasantassa añ‍ñau bhikkhu āgacchati bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘kiṃ ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau? kissāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti? tai aivaṃ vadainti – ‘ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikau māsau saramānappaṭicchan‍nau, aikau māsau assaramānappaṭicchan‍nau| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tāyau ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau tāsāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘yvāyaṃ, āvusau, māsau saramānappaṭicchan‍nau dhammikaṃ tassa māsassa parivāsadānaṃ; dhammattā ruhati| yau ca khvāyaṃ, āvusau, māsau assaramānappaṭicchan‍nau adhammikaṃ tassa māsassa parivāsadānaṃ; adhammattā na ruhati| aikassa, āvusau, māsassa bhikkhu mānattārahau’ti|

155. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikau māsau nibbaimatikappaṭicchan‍nau, aikau māsau vaimatikappaṭicchan‍nau| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ daiti| tassa parivasantassa añ‍ñau bhikkhu āgacchati bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘kiṃ ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau? kissāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti? tai aivaṃ vadainti – ‘ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu dvai saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nāyau; aikau māsau nibbaimatikappaṭicchan‍nau, aikau māsau vaimatikappaṭicchan‍nau| sau saṅghaṃ dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ yāci| tassa saṅghau dvin‍naṃ āpattīnaṃ dvaimāsappaṭicchan‍nānaṃ dvaimāsaparivāsaṃ adāsi| tāyau ayaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āpan‍nau tāsāyaṃ bhikkhu parivasatī’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘yvāyaṃ, āvusau, māsau nibbaimatikappaṭicchan‍nau dhammikaṃ tassa māsassa parivāsadānaṃ; dhammattā ruhati| yau ca khvāyaṃ, āvusau, māsau vaimatikappaṭicchan‍nau adhammikaṃ tassa māsassa parivāsadānaṃ; adhammattā na ruhati| aikassa, āvusau, māsassa bhikkhu mānattārahau’’ti|

suddhantaparivāsau

156. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā  āpattiyau āpan‍nau hauti| sau āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti; rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati; āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau| sau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ; āpattipariyantaṃ  na jānāmi, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi; āpattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, rattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi; āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau tassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ| āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāmi; āpattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi, rattipariyantaṃ nassarāmi; āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| byattaina  bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

157. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji| āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati; āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍ji| āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati; āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ  āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ suddhantaparivāsau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

158. ‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, suddhantaparivāsau dātabbau; aivaṃ  parivāsau dātabbau| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, suddhantaparivāsau dātabbau? āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati; āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau – suddhantaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘āpattipariyantaṃ jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ sarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati; āpattipariyantai nibbaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau – suddhantaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘āpattipariyantaṃ aikac‍caṃ jānāti, aikac‍caṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ na jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ aikac‍caṃ sarati, aikac‍caṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ nassarati; āpattipariyantai aikac‍cai vaimatikau, aikac‍cai nibbaimatikau, rattipariyantai vaimatikau – suddhantaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ aikac‍caṃ jānāti, aikac‍caṃ na jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ aikac‍caṃ sarati, aikac‍caṃ nassarati; āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai aikac‍cai vaimatikau, aikac‍cai nibbaimatikau – suddhantaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘āpattipariyantaṃ jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ aikac‍caṃ jānāti, aikac‍caṃ na jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ  sarati, rattipariyantaṃ aikac‍caṃ sarati, aikac‍caṃ nassarati; āpattipariyantai nibbaimatikau, rattipariyantai aikac‍cai vaimatikau, aikac‍cai nibbaimatikau – suddhantaparivāsau  dātabbau|

‘‘āpattipariyantaṃ aikac‍caṃ jānāti, aikac‍caṃ na jānāti; rattipariyantaṃ  aikac‍caṃ jānāti, aikac‍caṃ na jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ aikac‍caṃ sarati, aikac‍caṃ nassarati; rattipariyantaṃ aikac‍caṃ sarati, aikac‍caṃ nassarati; āpattipariyantai aikac‍cai vaimatikau, aikac‍cai nibbaimatikau; rattipariyantai aikac‍cai vaimatikau, aikac‍cai nibbaimatikau – suddhantaparivāsau dātabbau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, suddhantaparivāsau dātabbau|

159. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, parivāsau dātabbau? āpattipariyantaṃ jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ sarati, rattipariyantaṃ sarati; āpattipariyantai nibbaimatikau, rattipariyantai nibbaimatikau – parivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘āpattipariyantaṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ sarati, āpattipariyantai vaimatikau, rattipariyantai nibbaimatikau – parivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘āpattipariyantaṃ aikac‍caṃ jānāti, aikac‍caṃ na jānāti, rattipariyantaṃ jānāti; āpattipariyantaṃ aikac‍caṃ sarati, aikac‍caṃ nassarati, rattipariyantaṃ sarati; āpattipariyantai aikac‍cai vaimatikau, aikac‍cai nibbaimatikau, rattipariyantai nibbaimatikau – parivāsau dātabbau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, parivāsau dātabbau|

parivāsau niṭṭhitau|

3. cattālīsakaṃ

160. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu parivasantau vibbhami| sau puna pac‍cāgantvā bhikkhū upasampadaṃ yāci| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau vibbhamati| vibbhantakassa, bhikkhavai, parivāsau na ruhati| sau cai puna upasampaj‍jati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau, avasaisau parivasitabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau sāmaṇairau hauti| sāmaṇairassa, bhikkhavai, parivāsau na ruhati| sau cai puna upasampaj‍jati, tassa  tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau, avasaisau parivasitabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau ummattakau hauti| ummattakassa, bhikkhavai, parivāsau na ruhati| sau cai puna anummattakau hauti, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau, avasaisau parivasitabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau khittacittau hauti| khittacittassa, bhikkhavai, parivāsau na  ruhati| sau cai puna akhittacittau hauti, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau purivutthau suparivutthau, avasaisau parivasitabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau vaidanāṭṭau hauti| vaidanāṭṭassa, bhikkhavai, parivāsau na ruhati| sau cai puna avaidanāṭṭau hauti, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau, avasaisau parivasitabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau, āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhipiyyati ukkhipiyati (syā॰), ukkhipīyati (ka॰)| ukkhittakassa, bhikkhavai, parivāsau na ruhati| sau cai puna ausāriyyati ausāriyati (syā॰), ausārīyati (ka॰), tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau, avasaisau parivasitabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau, āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhipiyyati| ukkhittakassa, bhikkhavai, parivāsau na ruhati| sau cai puna ausāriyyati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau, avasaisau parivasitabbau|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā, appaṭinissaggai, ukkhipiyyati| ukkhittakassa, bhikkhavai, parivāsau na ruhati| sau cai puna ausāriyyati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau, avasaisau parivasitabbau|

161. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mūlāyapaṭikassanārahau vibbhamati| vibbhantakassa, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanā na ruhati| sau cai puna  upasampaj‍jati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mūlāyapaṭikassanārahau sāmaṇairau hauti…pai॰… ummattakau hauti…pai॰… khittacittau hauti…pai॰… vaidanāṭṭau hauti…pai॰… āpattiyā adassanai ukkhipiyyati…pai॰… āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhipiyyati…pai॰… pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā, appaṭinissaggai, ukkhipiyyati| ukkhittakassa, bhikkhavai, mūlāyapaṭikassanā na ruhati| sau cai puna ausāriyyati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau|

162. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mānattārahau vibbhamati| vibbhantakassa, bhikkhavai, mānattadānaṃ na ruhati| sau cai puna upasampaj‍jati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau| tassa bhikkhunau mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mānattārahau sāmaṇairau hauti…pai॰… ummattakau hauti…pai॰… khittacittau hauti…pai॰… vaidanāṭṭau hauti…pai॰… āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhipiyyati…pai॰… āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhipiyyati…pai॰… pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā, appaṭinissaggai, ukkhipiyyati| ukkhittakassa, bhikkhavai, mānattadānaṃ na ruhati| sau cai puna ausāriyyati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau| tassa bhikkhunau mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

163. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mānattaṃ carantau vibbhamati| vibbhantakassa, bhikkhavai, mānattacariyā na ruhati| sau cai puna upasampaj‍jati tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau; yaṃ mānattaṃ din‍naṃ sudin‍naṃ, yaṃ mānattaṃ ciṇṇaṃ suciṇṇaṃ, avasaisaṃ caritabbaṃ|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mānattaṃ carantau sāmaṇairau hauti…pai॰… ummattakau hauti…pai॰… khittacittau hauti…pai॰… vaidanāṭṭau hauti…pai॰… āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhipiyyati…pai॰… āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhipiyyati…pai॰… pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā, appaṭinissaggai, ukkhipiyyati| ukkhittakassa, bhikkhavai, mānattacariyā na ruhati| sau cai puna ausāriyyati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau purivutthau suparivutthau; yaṃ mānattaṃ din‍naṃ sudin‍naṃ, yaṃ mānattaṃ ciṇṇaṃ suciṇṇaṃ, avasaisaṃ caritabbaṃ|

164. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abbhānārahau vibbhamati| vibbhantakassa, bhikkhavai, abbhānaṃ na ruhati| sau cai puna upasampaj‍jati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau; yaṃ mānattaṃ din‍naṃ sudin‍naṃ, yaṃ mānattaṃ ciṇṇaṃ suciṇṇaṃ| sau bhikkhu abbhaitabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abbhānārahau sāmaṇairau hauti…pai॰… ummattakau hauti…pai॰…

khittacittau hauti…pai॰… vaidanāṭṭau hauti…pai॰… āpattiyā adassanai, ukkhipiyyati…pai॰… āpattiyā appaṭikammai, ukkhipiyyati…pai॰… pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā, appaṭinissaggai, ukkhipiyyati| ukkhittakassa, bhikkhavai, abbhānaṃ  na ruhati| sau cai puna ausāriyyati, tassa tadaiva purimaṃ parivāsadānaṃ| yau parivāsau din‍nau sudin‍nau, yau parivutthau suparivutthau; yaṃ mānattaṃ din‍naṃ sudin‍naṃ, yaṃ mānattaṃ ciṇṇaṃ suciṇṇaṃ| sau bhikkhu abbhaitabbau|

cattālīsakaṃ samattaṃ|

4. chattiṃsakaṃ

165. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇā parimāṇāyau (sī॰ syā॰) appaṭicchan‍nāyau| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyau| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati aparimāṇā aparimāṇāyau (sī॰ syā॰) appaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… aparimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… aparimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi…pai॰… parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰…  parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mānattārahau…pai॰… mānattaṃ carantau…pai॰… (yathāparivāsaṃ tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ) abbhānārahau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇā appaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰…  parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi…pai॰… aparimāṇā appaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… aparimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… aparimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi…pai॰… parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰…  parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

chattiṃsakaṃ samattaṃ|

5. mānattasatakaṃ

166. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā appaṭicchādaitvā vibbhamati| sau puna sau cai puna (ka॰) upasampan‍nau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā  saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā appaṭicchādaitvā vibbhamati| sau puna upasampan‍nau tā āpattiyau chādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pacchimasmiṃ āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā paṭicchādaitvā vibbhamati| sau puna upasampan‍nau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiṃ āpattikkhandhai  yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā paṭicchādaitvā vibbhamati| sau puna upasampan‍nau tā āpattiyau chādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

167. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tassa haunti āpattiyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nacchādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiṃ āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tassa haunti āpattiyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā chādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tassa haunti āpattiyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nacchādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tassa haunti āpattiyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā chādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

168. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau jānāti, aikac‍cā āpattiyau na jānāti| yā āpattiyau jānāti tā āpattiyau chādaiti| yā āpattiyau na jānāti  tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai jānitvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai  ajānitvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā nacchādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiṃ āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau jānāti, aikac‍cā āpattiyau na jānāti| yā āpattiyau jānāti tā āpattiyau chādaiti| yā āpattiyau na jānāti tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai jānitvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai ajānitvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā chādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau jānāti, aikac‍cā āpattiyau na jānāti| yā āpattiyau jānāti tā āpattiyau  chādaiti| yā āpattiyau na jānāti tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai jānitvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai ajānitvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā nacchādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai  yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau jānāti, aikac‍cā āpattiyau na jānāti| yā āpattiyau jānāti tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau na jānāti tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai jānitvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai ajānitvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā chādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

169. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau sarati, aikac‍cā āpattiyau nassarati| yā āpattiyau sarati tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau nassarati tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai saritvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai assaritvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā nacchādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiṃ āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau sarati, aikac‍cā āpattiyau nassarati| yā  āpattiyau sarati tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau nassarati tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai saritvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai assaritvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā chādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau sarati, aikac‍cā āpattiyau nassarati| yā āpattiyau sarati tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau nassarati tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai saritvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai assaritvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā nacchādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau sarati, aikac‍cā āpattiyau nassarati| yā āpattiyau sarati tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau  nassarati tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai saritvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai assaritvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā chādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

170. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau, aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau| yāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau  pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiṃ āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau, aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau| yāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi  tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā  nibbaimatikau chādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau, aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau| yāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau, aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau| yāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau  chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

171. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā appaṭicchādaitvā sāmaṇairau hauti…pai॰… ummattakau hauti…pai॰… khittacittau hauti…pai॰… (yathā haiṭṭhā tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ) vaidanāṭṭau hauti…pai॰… tassa haunti āpattiyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi…pai॰… aikac‍cā āpattiyau jānāti, aikac‍cā āpattiyau na jānāti…pai॰… aikac‍cā āpattiyau sarati, aikac‍cā  āpattiyau nassarati…pai॰… aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau, aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau| yāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vaidanāṭṭau hauti| sau puna avaidanāṭṭau hutvā yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi  tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti…pai॰… yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai  vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti…pai॰… yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti…pai॰… yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti| tassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau purimasmiñ‍ca pacchimasmiñ‍ca āpattikkhandhai yathāpaṭicchan‍nai parivāsaṃ datvā mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

mānattasataṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. samūlāyasamaudhānaparivāsacatussataṃ

172. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā appaṭicchādaitvā vibbhamati| sau  puna upasampan‍nau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā appaṭicchādaitvā vibbhamati| sau puna upasampan‍nau tā āpattiyau chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā paṭicchādaitvā vibbhamati| sau puna upasampan‍nau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā paṭicchādaitvā vibbhamati| sau puna upasampan‍nau tā āpattiyau chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

173. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tassa haunti āpattiyau  paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nacchādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tassa haunti āpattiyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi  appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nacchādaiti; yā  āpattiyau pubbai nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tassa haunti āpattiyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nacchādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tassa haunti āpattiyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa  āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

174. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau jānāti, aikac‍cā āpattiyau na jānāti| yā āpattiyau jānāti tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau na jānāti tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai jānitvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau  pacchā jānitvā nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai ajānitvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā nacchādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau jānāti, aikac‍cā āpattiyau  na jānāti| yā āpattiyau jānāti tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau na jānāti tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai jānitvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai ajānitvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa  āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau jānāti, aikac‍cā āpattiyau na jānāti| yā āpattiyau jānāti tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau na jānāti tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai jānitvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai ajānitvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā nacchādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau jānāti, aikac‍cā āpattiyau na jānāti| yā āpattiyau jānāti tā āpattiyau chādaiti, yā āpattiyau na jānāti tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai jānitvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai ajānitvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā jānitvā chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa  āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

175. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau sarati, aikac‍cā āpattiyau nassarati| yā āpattiyau  sarati tā āpattiyau  chādaiti; yā āpattiyau nassarati tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai saritvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai assaritvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā nacchādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau sarati, aikac‍cā āpattiyau nassarati| yā āpattiyau sarati tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau nassarati tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai saritvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai assaritvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau sarati, aikac‍cā āpattiyau nassarati| yā āpattiyau sarati tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau nassarati tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai saritvā chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai assaritvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā nacchādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cā āpattiyau sarati, aikac‍cā āpattiyau nassarati| yā āpattiyau sarati tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau nassarati tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai saritvā chādaisi  tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai assaritvā nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā saritvā chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa  āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|
