176. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau, aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau| yāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau, aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau| yāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau, aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau| yāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau  pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau, aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau| yāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vibbhamitvā sau bhikkhu vibbhamitvā (ka॰) puna upasampan‍nau yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau  pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

177. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā appaṭicchādaitvā sāmaṇairau hauti…pai॰… ummattakau hauti…pai॰… khittacittau hauti…pai॰… vaidanāṭṭau hauti…pai॰… tassa haunti āpattiyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi (yathā haiṭṭhā vitthāritaṃ tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ)…pai॰… aikac‍cā āpattiyau jānāti, aikac‍cā āpattiyau na jānāti…pai॰… aikac‍cā āpattiyau sarati, aikac‍cā āpattiyau nassarati…pai॰… aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau, aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau| yāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vaidanāṭṭau hauti| sau puna avaidanāṭṭau hutvā yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti…pai॰… yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti…pai॰… yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti…pai॰… yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

178. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu mānattārahau…pai॰… mānattaṃ carantau…pai॰… abbhānārahau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā appaṭicchādaitvā vibbhamati…pai॰… (mānattārahau ca mānattacārī ca abbhānārahau ca yathā parivāsau vitthāritau tathā vitthāraitabbau)|

179. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abbhānārahau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā appaṭicchādaitvā sāmaṇairau hauti…pai॰… ummattakau hauti…pai॰… khittacittau hauti…pai॰… vaidanāṭṭau hauti…pai॰… tassa haunti āpattiyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi  …pai॰… aikac‍cā āpattiyau jānāti, aikac‍cā āpattiyau na jānāti…pai॰… aikac‍cā āpattiyau sarati, aikac‍cā āpattiyau nassarati…pai॰… aikac‍cāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau, aikac‍cāsu  āpattīsu vaimatikau| yāsu āpattīsu nibbaimatikau tā āpattiyau chādaiti; yāsu āpattīsu vaimatikau tā āpattiyau nacchādaiti| sau vaidanāṭṭau hauti| sau puna avaidanāṭṭau hutvā yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti…pai॰… yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi  tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti…pai॰… yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau nacchādaiti…pai॰… yā āpattiyau pubbai nibbaimatikau chādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti; yā āpattiyau pubbai vaimatikau nacchādaisi tā āpattiyau pacchā nibbaimatikau chādaiti| sau bhikkhu mūlāya paṭikassitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nānañ‍cassa āpattīnaṃ  purimāya āpattiyā samaudhānaparivāsau dātabbau|

samūlāyasamaudhānaparivāsacatussataṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. parimāṇādivāraaṭṭhakaṃ

180. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jitvā parimāṇā appaṭicchādaitvā…pai॰… aparimāṇā appaṭicchādaitvā…pai॰… aikanāmā appaṭicchādaitvā…pai॰…  nānānāmā appaṭicchādaitvā…pai॰… sabhāgā appaṭicchādaitvā…pai॰… visabhāgā appaṭicchādaitvā…pai॰… vavatthitā appaṭicchādaitvā…pai॰… sambhin‍nā appaṭicchādaitvā vibbhamati…pai॰… (yathā haiṭṭhā tathā vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

parimāṇādivāraaṭṭhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. dvaibhikkhuvāraaikādasakaṃ

181. ‘‘dvai  bhikkhū saṅghādisaisaṃ āpan‍nā haunti| tai saṅghādisaisai saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhinau haunti| aikau chādaiti, aikau nacchādaiti| yau chādaiti sau duk‍kaṭaṃ daisāpaitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nai yathāpaṭicchan‍nānaṃ (sī॰) cassa parivāsaṃ datvā ubhin‍nampi mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘dvai  bhikkhū saṅghādisaisaṃ āpan‍nā haunti| tai saṅghādisaisai vaimatikā haunti| aikau chādaiti, aikau nacchādaiti| yau chādaiti sau duk‍kaṭaṃ daisāpaitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nai cassa parivāsaṃ datvā ubhin‍nampi mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘dvai bhikkhū saṅghādisaisaṃ āpan‍nā haunti| tai saṅghādisaisai missakadiṭṭhinau haunti| aikau chādaiti, aikau nacchādaiti| yau chādaiti sau duk‍kaṭaṃ daisāpaitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nai cassa parivāsaṃ datvā ubhin‍nampi mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘dvai bhikkhū missakaṃ āpan‍nā haunti| tai missakai saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhinau haunti| aikau chādaiti, aikau nacchādaiti| yau chādaiti sau duk‍kaṭaṃ daisāpaitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nai cassa parivāsaṃ datvā ubhin‍nampi mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘dvai  bhikkhū missakaṃ āpan‍nā haunti| tai missakai missakadiṭṭhinau haunti| aikau chādaiti, aikau nacchādaiti| yau chādaiti sau duk‍kaṭaṃ daisāpaitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nai cassa parivāsaṃ datvā ubhin‍nampi mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘dvai bhikkhū suddhakaṃ āpan‍nā haunti| tai suddhakai saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhinau haunti| aikau chādaiti, aikau nacchādaiti| yau chādaiti sau duk‍kaṭaṃ daisāpaitabbau| ubhaupi yathādhammaṃ kārāpaitabbā|

‘‘dvai bhikkhū suddhakaṃ āpan‍nā haunti| tai suddhakai suddhakadiṭṭhinau haunti| aikau chādaiti, aikau nacchādaiti| yau chādaiti sau duk‍kaṭaṃ daisāpaitabbau| ubhaupi yathādhammaṃ kārāpaitabbā|

‘‘dvai  bhikkhū saṅghādisaisaṃ āpan‍nā haunti| tai saṅghādisaisai saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhinau haunti| aikassa hauti āraucaissāmīti, aikassa hauti na āraucaissāmīti| sau paṭhamampi  yāmaṃ chādaiti, dutiyampi yāmaṃ chādaiti, tatiyampi yāmaṃ chādaiti – uṭṭhitai aruṇai chan‍nā hauti āpatti| yau chādaiti sau duk‍kaṭaṃ daisāpaitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nai cassa parivāsaṃ datvā ubhin‍nampi  mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘dvai  bhikkhū saṅghādisaisaṃ āpan‍nā haunti| tai saṅghādisaisai saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhinau haunti| tai gacchanti āraucaissāmāti| aikassa antarāmaggai makkhadhammau uppaj‍jati na āraucaissāmīti| sau paṭhamampi yāmaṃ chādaiti, dutiyampi yāmaṃ chādaiti, tatiyampi yāmaṃ chādaiti – uṭṭhitai aruṇai chan‍nā hauti āpatti| yau chādaiti sau duk‍kaṭaṃ daisāpaitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nai cassa parivāsaṃ datvā ubhin‍nampi mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘dvai bhikkhū saṅghādisaisaṃ āpan‍nā haunti| tai saṅghādisaisai saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhinau haunti| tai ummattakā haunti| tai pacchā anummattakā hutvā aikau chādaiti, aikau nacchādaiti| yau chādaiti sau duk‍kaṭaṃ daisāpaitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nai cassa parivāsaṃ datvā ubhin‍nampi mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

‘‘dvai bhikkhū saṅghādisaisaṃ āpan‍nā haunti| tai pātimaukkhai uddissamānai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘idānaiva khau mayaṃ jānāma – ayampi kira dhammau suttāgatau suttapariyāpan‍nau anvaddhamāsaṃ uddaisaṃ āgacchatī’ti| tai saṅghādisaisai saṅghādisaisadiṭṭhinau haunti| aikau chādaiti, aikau nacchādaiti| yau chādaiti sau duk‍kaṭaṃ daisāpaitabbau| yathāpaṭicchan‍nai cassa parivāsaṃ datvā ubhin‍nampi mānattaṃ dātabbaṃ|

dvaibhikkhuvāraaikādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. mūlāyaavisuddhinavakaṃ

182. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi, aikanāmampi nānānāmampi, sabhāgampi visabhāgampi, vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ  samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyau appaṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati  dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti; adhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, adhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu avisuddhau tāhi āpattīhi|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi, aikanāmampi nānānāmampi, sabhāgampi visabhāgampi, vavatthitampi  sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyau paṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti; adhammaina mānattaṃ  daiti, adhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu avisuddhau tāhi āpattīhi|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi, aikanāmampi nānānāmampi, sabhāgampi visabhāgampi, vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti, adhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, adhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu avisuddhau tāhi āpattīhi|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi, aikanāmampi nānānāmampi, sabhāgampi visabhāgampi, vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati aparimāṇāyau appaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… aparimāṇāyau paṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… aparimāṇāyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi  …pai॰… parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti; adhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, adhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu avisuddhau tāhi āpattīhi|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi, aikanāmampi nānānāmampi, sabhāgampi visabhāgampi, vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti; adhammaina  mānattaṃ daiti, adhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu avisuddhau tāhi āpattīhi|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi, aikanāmampi nānānāmampi, sabhāgampi visabhāgampi, vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyaupi  appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina; dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti; adhammaina mānattaṃ daiti; adhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu avisuddhau tāhi āpattīhi|

10. dutiyanavakaṃ idaṃ navakaṃ paurāṇapautthakaisu avisuddhivasainaiva āgataṃ, vuc‍camānatatiyanavakaina ca saṃsaṭṭhaṃ| taṃ paṭivisaudhakaihi asaṃsaṭṭhaṃ katvā visuṃ patiṭṭhāpitaṃ| 183. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau  antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇā appaṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti; dhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, dhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu avisuddhau tāhi āpattīhi| (1)

idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ  āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti; dhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, dhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu avisuddhau tāhi āpattīhi| (2)

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti; dhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, dhammaina abbhaiti| sau bhikkhavai, bhikkhu avisuddhau tāhi āpattīhi| (3)

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi, aikanāmampi nānānāmampi, sabhāgampi visabhāgampi, vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati aparimāṇā appaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… aparimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… aparimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi…pai॰… parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ  daiti; dhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, dhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu avisuddhau tāhi āpattīhi| (4-7)

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ  mūlāya paṭikassati adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti, dhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, dhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu avisuddhau tāhi āpattīhi| (8)

dutiyanavakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

11. tatiyanavakaṃ

184. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa  saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ  samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyau appaṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ  mūlāya paṭikassati adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasāmīti mañ‍ñamānau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyau appaṭicchan‍nāyau| sau tasmiṃ bhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau purimāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarati, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇāyau appaṭicchan‍nāyau| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasāmīti mañ‍ñamānau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇāyau appaṭicchan‍nāyau| sauhaṃ tasmiṃ bhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau purimāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarāmi, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ purimāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācaiyyaṃ dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ; dhammaina mānattaṃ, dhammaina abbhāna’nti| sau saṅghaṃ purimāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ; dhammaina mānattaṃ, dhammaina abbhānaṃ| taṃ saṅghau purimāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāya paṭikassati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti; dhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, dhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu visuddhau tāhi āpattīhi ayaṃ paṭhamavārau sī syā॰ pautthakaisu paripuṇṇau dissati|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati, adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasāmīti mañ‍ñamānau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyau| sau tasmiṃ bhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau purimāāpattīnaṃ purimānaṃ āpattīnaṃ (ka॰) antarāāpattiyau sarati, aparāāpattīnaṃ  antarāāpattiyau sarati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyau| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassi, adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasāmīti mañ‍ñamānau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyau| sauhaṃ tasmiṃ bhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau purimāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarāmi, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ purimāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, aparā āpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācaiyyaṃ  dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ, dhammaina mānattaṃ, dhammaina abbhāna’nti| sau saṅghaṃ purimāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ, dhammaina mānattaṃ, dhammaina abbhānaṃ| taṃ saṅghau purimāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāya paṭikassati dhammikaina  kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti, dhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, dhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu visuddhau tāhi āpattīhi|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ  daiti| sau parivasāmīti mañ‍ñamānau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau tasmiṃ bhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau purimāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarati, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā  saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasāmīti mañ‍ñamānau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇā paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sauhaṃ tasmiṃ bhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau purimāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarāmi, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ purimāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācaiyyaṃ dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ, dhammaina mānattaṃ, dhammaina abbhāna’nti| sau saṅghaṃ purimāpattīnaṃ antarā āpattīnañ‍ca, aparā āpattīnaṃ antarā āpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ  yācati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ, dhammaina mānattaṃ, dhammaina abbhānaṃ| taṃ saṅghau purimāpattīnaṃ antarā āpattīnañ‍ca, aparā āpattīnaṃ antarā āpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāya paṭikassati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti, dhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, dhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu visuddhau tāhi āpattīhi|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati aparimāṇāyau appaṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… aparimāṇāyau paṭicchan‍nāyau…pai॰… aparimāṇāyau paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi…pai॰… parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ  yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasāmīti mañ‍ñamānau…pai॰… taṃ saṅghau purimā āpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāya paṭikassati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti, dhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, dhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu visuddhau tāhi āpattīhi imaipi cattārau vārā sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu paripuṇṇā dissanti|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ  yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyaupi  aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyau| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasāmīti mañ‍ñamānau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā  āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyau| sau tasmiṃ bhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau purimā āpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarati, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyau| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassi adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasāmīti mañ‍ñamānau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyau| sauhaṃ tasmiṃ bhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau purimā āpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarāmi, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ purimā āpattīnaṃ antarā pattīnañ‍ca, aparāāpattīna  antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācaiyyaṃ dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ, dhammaina mānattaṃ, dhammaina abbhāna’nti| sau saṅghaṃ purimā āpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ, dhammaina mānattaṃ, dhammaina abbhānaṃ| taṃ saṅghau purimā āpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāya paṭikassati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti, dhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, dhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu visuddhau tāhi āpattīhi|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sau saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yācati| tassa saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ  mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ  yācati| taṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti| sau parivasāmīti mañ‍ñamānau  antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jati parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sau tasmiṃ bhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau purimā āpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarati, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarati| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇampi aparimāṇampi…pai॰… vavatthitampi sambhin‍nampi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ yāciṃ| tassa mai saṅghau tāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasantau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sauhaṃ saṅghaṃ antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yāciṃ| taṃ maṃ saṅghau antarāāpattīnaṃ mūlāya paṭikassi adhammikaina kammaina kuppaina aṭṭhānārahaina, adhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ adāsi| sauhaṃ parivasāmīti mañ‍ñamānau antarā sambahulā saṅghādisaisā āpattiyau āpaj‍jiṃ parimāṇāyaupi aparimāṇāyaupi paṭicchan‍nāyaupi appaṭicchan‍nāyaupi| sauhaṃ tasmiṃ bhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau purimā āpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarāmi, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattiyau sarāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghaṃ purimāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāyapaṭikassanaṃ yācaiyyaṃ dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina  samaudhānaparivāsaṃ, dhammaina mānattaṃ, dhammaina abbhāna’nti| sau saṅghaṃ purimā āpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, aparāāpattīnaṃ antarāāpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāya paṭikassanaṃ yācati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ, dhammaina mānattaṃ, dhammaina abbhānaṃ| taṃ saṅghau purimā āpattīnaṃ antarā āpattīnañ‍ca, aparā āpattīnaṃ antarā āpattīnañ‍ca, mūlāya paṭikassati dhammikaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina, dhammaina samaudhānaparivāsaṃ daiti, dhammaina mānattaṃ daiti, dhammaina abbhaiti| sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu visuddhau tāhi āpattīhi’’|

tatiyanavakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

samuc‍cayakkhandhakau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

appaṭicchan‍nā  aikāha-dvīha-tīha-catūha ca|

pañ‍cāhapakkhadasan‍naṃ, āpattimāha mahāmuni||

suddhantau  ca vibbhamantau, parimāṇamukhaṃ dvai bhikkhū|

tattha sañ‍ñinau dvai yathā, vaimatikā tathaiva ca||

missakadiṭṭhinau  dvai ca, asuddhakaikadiṭṭhinau|

dvai caiva suddhadiṭṭhinau||

tathaiva ca aikau chādaiti, atha makkhamataina ca|

ummattakadaisanañ‍ca, mūlā aṭṭhārasa pan‍narasa (ka॰) visuddhatau||

ācariyānaṃ vibhaj‍japadānaṃ vibhaj‍javādīnaṃ (sī॰), tambapaṇṇidīpapasādakānaṃ|

mahāvihāravāsīnaṃ, vācanā saddhammaṭṭhitiyāti||

samuc‍cayakkhandhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



4. samathakkhandhakaṃ

1. sammukhāvinayau

185. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū asammukhībhūtānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kammāni karaunti – taj‍janīyampi, niyassampi, pabbājanīyampi, paṭisāraṇīyampi, ukkhaipanīyampi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū asammukhībhūtānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kammāni karissanti – taj‍janīyampi, niyassampi, pabbājanīyampi, paṭisāraṇīyampi, ukkhaipanīyampī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū asammukhībhūtānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kammāni karaunti – taj‍janīyampi, niyassampi, pabbājanīyampi, paṭisāraṇīyampi, ukkhaipanīyampī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ maughapurisānaṃ ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā asammukhībhūtānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kammāni karissanti – taj‍janīyampi, niyassampi, pabbājanīyampi, paṭisāraṇīyampi, ukkhaipanīyampi! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – na, bhikkhavai, asammukhībhūtānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kammaṃ kātabbaṃ – taj‍janīyaṃ vā, niyassaṃ vā, pabbājanīyaṃ vā, paṭisāraṇīyaṃ vā, ukkhaipanīyaṃ vā| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

186. ‘‘adhammavādī puggalau adhammavādī sambahulā adhammavādī saṅghau| dhammavādī puggalau dhammavādī sambahulā dhammavādī saṅghau|

kaṇhapakkhanavakaṃ

187. ‘‘adhammavādī  puggalau dhammavādiṃ puggalaṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ  raucaihīti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, adhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayapatirūpakaina|

‘‘adhammavādī puggalau dhammavādī sambahulai sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithāti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, adhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayapatirūpakaina|

‘‘adhammavādī puggalau dhammavādiṃ saṅghaṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ raucaihīti imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithāti (ka॰)| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, adhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayapatirūpakaina|

‘‘adhammavādī sambahulā dhammavādiṃ puggalaṃ sañ‍ñāpainti nijjhāpainti paikkhainti anupaikkhainti dassainti anudassainti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ raucaihīti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, adhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayapatirūpakaina|

‘‘adhammavādī  sambahulā dhammavādī sambahulai sañ‍ñāpainti nijjhāpainti paikkhainti anupaikkhainti dassainti anudassainti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithāti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, adhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayapatirūpakaina|

‘‘adhammavādī sambahulā dhammavādiṃ saṅghaṃ sañ‍ñāpainti nijjhāpainti paikkhainti anupaikkhainti dassainti anudassainti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ raucaihīti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, adhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayapatirūpakaina|

‘‘adhammavādī saṅghau dhammavādiṃ puggalaṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti  anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ raucaihīti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, adhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayapatirūpakaina|

‘‘adhammavādī saṅghau dhammavādī sambahulai sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithāti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, adhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayapatirūpakaina|

‘‘adhammavādī saṅghau dhammavādiṃ saṅghaṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ  satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ raucaihīti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, adhammaina  vūpasammati sammukhāvinayapatirūpakaina|

kaṇhapakkhanavakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

suk‍kapakkhanavakaṃ

188. ‘‘dhammavādī puggalau adhammavādiṃ puggalaṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ raucaihīti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, dhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayaina|

‘‘dhammavādī puggalau adhammavādī sambahulai sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithāti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, dhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayaina|

‘‘dhammavādī puggalau adhammavādiṃ saṅghaṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ raucaihīti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, dhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayaina|

‘‘dhammavādī sambahulā adhammavādiṃ puggalaṃ sañ‍ñāpainti nijjhāpainti paikkhainti anupaikkhainti dassainti anudassainti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ raucaihīti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, dhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayaina|

‘‘dhammavādī sambahulā adhammavādī sambahulai sañ‍ñāpainti nijjhāpainti paikkhainti anupaikkhainti dassainti  anudassainti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithāti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, dhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayaina|

‘‘dhammavādī sambahulā adhammavādiṃ saṅghaṃ sañ‍ñāpainti nijjhāpainti paikkhainti anupaikkhainti dassainti anudassainti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ raucaihīti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, dhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayaina|

‘‘dhammavādī saṅghau adhammavādiṃ puggalaṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ raucaihīti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, dhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayaina|

‘‘dhammavādī saṅghau adhammavādī sambahulai sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithāti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, dhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayaina|

‘‘dhammavādī saṅghau adhammavādiṃ saṅghaṃ sañ‍ñāpaiti nijjhāpaiti paikkhaiti anupaikkhaiti dassaiti anudassaiti – ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhāhi, imaṃ raucaihīti| aivañ‍caitaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, dhammaina vūpasammati sammukhāvinayainā’’ti|

suk‍kapakkhanavakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. sativinayau

189. idaṃ vatthu pārā॰ 380 ādayau taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatā dabbaina mal‍laputtaina jātiyā sattavassaina arahattaṃ sacchikataṃ hauti| yaṃ kiñ‍ci sāvakaina pattabbaṃ sabbaṃ taina anuppattaṃ hauti| natthi cassa kiñ‍ci uttari uttariṃ (sī॰) karaṇīyaṃ, katassa vā paticayau| atha khau āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘mayā khau jātiyā sattavassaina arahattaṃ sacchikataṃ| yaṃ kiñ‍ci sāvakaina pattabbaṃ sabbaṃ mayā anuppattaṃ| natthi ca mai kiñ‍ci uttarikaraṇīyaṃ, katassa vā paticayau| kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ saṅghassa vaiyyāvac‍caṃ karaiyya’’nti?

atha  khau āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghassa saināsanañ‍ca  pañ‍ñapaiyyaṃ bhattāni ca uddisaiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘mayā khau jātiyā sattavassaina arahattaṃ sacchikataṃ| yaṃ kiñ‍ci sāvakaina pattabbaṃ, sabbaṃ mayā anuppattaṃ| natthi ca mai kiñ‍ci uttarikaraṇīyaṃ, katassa vā paticayau| kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ saṅghassa vaiyyāvac‍caṃ karaiyya’nti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṅghassa saināsanañ‍ca pañ‍ñapaiyyaṃ bhattāni ca uddisaiyya’nti| icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, saṅghassa saināsanañ‍ca pañ‍ñāpaituṃ bhattāni ca uddisitu’’nti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, dabba| taina hi tvaṃ, dabba, saṅghassa saināsanañ‍ca pañ‍ñapaihi bhattāni ca uddisāhī’’ti uddisāti (pārā॰ 380)| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ saināsanapañ‍ñāpakañ‍ca bhattuddaisakañ‍ca samman‍natu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ dabbau mal‍laputtau yācitabbau dabbau yācitabbau (syā॰ ka॰)| yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

190. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ saināsanapañ‍ñāpakañ‍ca bhattuddaisakañ‍ca samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ saināsanapañ‍ñāpakañ‍ca bhattuddaisakañ‍ca samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa saināsanapañ‍ñāpakassa ca bhattuddaisakassa ca sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau  saṅghaina āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau saināsanapañ‍ñāpakau ca bhattuddaisakau ca| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

191. sammatau  ca panāyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau sabhāgānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| yai tai bhikkhū suttantikā taisaṃ aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti – tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ suttantaṃ saṅgāyissantīti| yai tai bhikkhū vinayadharā taisaṃ aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti – tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vinayaṃ vinicchinissantīti| yai tai bhikkhū dhammakathikā taisaṃ aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti – tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ dhammaṃ sākacchissantīti| yai tai bhikkhū jhāyinau taisaṃ aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti – tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ na byābādhissantīti| yai tai bhikkhū tiracchānakathikā kāyadaḷhibahulā kāyadaḍḍhibahulā (sī॰) viharanti taisampi aikajjhaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti – imāyapimai āyasmantau ratiyā acchissantīti| yaipi tai bhikkhū vikālai āgacchanti taisampi taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā tainaiva ālaukaina saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti; apisu bhikkhū sañ‍cic‍ca vikālai āgacchanti – mayaṃ āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ passissāmāti|

tai āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘amhākaṃ, āvusau dabba, saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihī’’ti| tai āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘kattha āyasmantā icchanti kattha pañ‍ñapaimī’’ti? tai sañ‍cic‍ca dūrai apadisanti – ‘‘amhākaṃ, āvusau dabba, gijjhakūṭai pabbatai saināsanaṃ  pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, caurapapātai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, isigilipassai kāḷasilāyaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, vaibhārapassai sattapaṇṇiguhāyaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, sītavanai sappasauṇḍikapabbhārai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, gautamakakandarāyaṃ gaumaṭakandarāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, tindukakandarāyaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, tapaudakandarāyaṃ kapautakandarāyaṃ (ka॰) saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, tapaudārāmai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, jīvakambavanai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihi| amhākaṃ, āvusau, maddakucchimhi migadāyai saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaihī’’ti|

taisaṃ  āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā aṅguliyā jalamānāya puratau puratau gacchati| taipi tainaiva ālaukaina āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau gacchanti| taisaṃ āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau aivaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti – ayaṃ mañ‍cau, idaṃ pīṭhaṃ, ayaṃ bhisi, idaṃ bibbauhanaṃ bimbauhanaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), idaṃ vac‍caṭṭhānaṃ, idaṃ passāvaṭṭhānaṃ, idaṃ pānīyaṃ, idaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ, ayaṃ kattaradaṇḍau, idaṃ saṅghassa katikasaṇṭhānaṃ, imaṃ kālaṃ pavisitabbaṃ, imaṃ kālaṃ nikkhamitabbanti| taisaṃ āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau aivaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā punadaiva vaiḷuvanaṃ pac‍cāgacchati|

192. taina khau pana samayaina maittiyabhūmajakā maittiyabhummajakā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) bhikkhū navakā caiva haunti appapuñ‍ñā ca| yāni saṅghassa lāmakāni saināsanāni tāni taisaṃ pāpuṇanti lāmakāni ca bhattāni| taina khau pana samayaina rājagahai manussā icchanti  thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhisaṅkhārikaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ dātuṃ – sappimpi, tailampi, uttaribhaṅgampi| maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ pana bhikkhūnaṃ pākatikaṃ dainti – yathārandhaṃ yathāraddhaṃ (syā॰) kaṇājakaṃ bilaṅgadutiyaṃ| tai pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantā thairai bhikkhū pucchanti – ‘‘tumhākaṃ, āvusau, bhattaggai kiṃ ahausi, tumhākaṃ kiṃ ahausī’’ti kiṃ nāhausi (syā॰ kaṃ॰)? aikac‍cai thairā aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘amhākaṃ, āvusau, sappi ahausi, tailaṃ ahausi, uttaribhaṅgaṃ ahausī’’ti| maittiyabhūmajakā pana bhikkhū aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘amhākaṃ, āvusau, na kiñ‍ci ahausi – pākatikaṃ yathārandhaṃ kaṇājakaṃ bilaṅgadutiya’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati saṅghassa catuk‍kabhattaṃ daiti nic‍cabhattaṃ| sau bhattaggai saputtadārau upatiṭṭhitvā parivisati – añ‍ñai audanaina pucchanti, añ‍ñai sūpaina pucchanti, añ‍ñai tailaina pucchanti, añ‍ñai uttaribhaṅgaina pucchanti| taina khau pana samayaina kalyāṇabhattikassa gahapatinau bhattaṃ svātanāya maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ uddiṭṭhaṃ hauti| atha  khau kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati ārāmaṃ agamāsi kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| sau yaināyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ  nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau kalyāṇabhattikaṃ gahapatiṃ āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati āyasmatā dabbaina mal‍laputtaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kassa, bhantai, amhākaṃ gharai svātanāya bhattaṃ uddiṭṭha’’nti? ‘‘maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ khau, gahapati, bhikkhūnaṃ tumhākaṃ gharai svātanāya bhattaṃ uddiṭṭha’’nti| atha khau kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati anattamanau ahausi| kathañhi nāma pāpabhikkhū amhākaṃ gharai bhuñ‍jissantīti gharaṃ gantvā dāsiṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘yai, jai, svai bhattikā āgacchanti tai kauṭṭhakai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā kaṇājakaina bilaṅgadutiyaina  parivisā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ ayyā’’ti khau sā dāsī kalyāṇabhattikassa gahapatinau pac‍cassausi|

atha khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū – hiyyau khau, āvusau, amhākaṃ kalyāṇabhattikassa gahapatinau bhattaṃ uddiṭṭhaṃ; svai amhai kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati saputtadārau upatiṭṭhitvā parivisissati; añ‍ñai audanaina pucchissanti, añ‍ñai sūpaina pucchissanti, añ‍ñai  tailaina pucchissanti, añ‍ñai uttaribhaṅgaina pucchissantīti| tai tainaiva saumanassaina  na cittarūpaṃ rattiyā supiṃsu| atha khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina kalyāṇabhattikassa gahapatinau nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| addasā khau sā dāsī maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū dūratauva āgacchantai; disvāna kauṭṭhakai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘nisīdatha, bhantai’’ti| atha khau maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau na tāva bhattaṃ siddhaṃ bhavissati yathā mayaṃ kauṭṭhakai nisīdāpiyāmā’’ti nisīdāpaiyyāmāti (ka॰)| atha khau sā dāsī kaṇājakaina kāṇājakaina (syā॰ kaṃ॰) bilaṅgadutiyaina upagañchi – bhuñ‍jatha, bhantaiti| ‘‘mayaṃ khau, bhagini, nic‍cabhattikā’’ti| ‘‘jānāmi ayyā nic‍cabhattikāti| api cāhaṃ hiyyauva gahapatinā āṇattā – ‘yai, jai, svai bhattikā āgacchanti, tai kauṭṭhakai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā kaṇājakaina bilaṅgadutiyaina parivisā’ti| bhuñ‍jatha, bhantai’’ti| atha khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū – hiyyau khau, āvusau, kalyāṇabhattikau gahapati ārāmaṃ agamāsi dabbassa mal‍laputtassa  santikai| nissaṃsayaṃ khau mayaṃ dabbaina mal‍laputtaina gahapatinau antarai paribhin‍nāti santikai paribhin‍nāti (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| tai tainaiva daumanassaina na cittarūpaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu| atha khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantā ārāmaṃ gantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭisāmaitvā bahārāmakauṭṭhakai saṅghāṭipal‍latthikāya nisīdiṃsu tuṇhībhūtā maṅkubhūtā pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā pajjhāyantā  appaṭibhānā|

atha khau maittiyā bhikkhunī yaina maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘vandāmi ayyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū nālapiṃsu| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau maittiyā bhikkhunī maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘vandāmi ayyā’’ti| tatiyampi khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū nālapiṃsu| ‘‘kyāhaṃ ayyānaṃ aparajjhāmi? kissa maṃ ayyā nālapantī’’ti? ‘‘tathā hi pana tvaṃ, bhagini, amhai dabbaina mal‍laputtaina vihaiṭhiyamānai ajjhupaikkhasī’’ti? ‘‘kyāhaṃ, ayyā, karaumī’’ti? ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, bhagini, icchaiyyāsi, aj‍jaiva bhagavā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ  mal‍laputtaṃ nāsāpaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘kyāhaṃ, ayyā, karaumi? kiṃ mayā sak‍kā kātu’’nti? ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhagini, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, nacchan‍naṃ nappatirūpaṃ, yāyaṃ, bhantai, disā abhayā anītikā anupaddavā sāyaṃ disā sabhayā saītikā saupaddavā; yatau nivātaṃ tatau savātaṃ tatau pavātaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰); udakaṃ mañ‍ñai ādittaṃ; ayyainamhi dabbaina mal‍laputtaina dūsitā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ ayyā’’ti khau maittiyā bhikkhunī maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭissutvā paṭissuṇitvā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau maittiyā sā maittiyā (syā॰ ka॰) bhikkhunī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, nacchan‍naṃ nappatirūpaṃ, yāyaṃ, bhantai, disā abhayā anītikā anupaddavā sāyaṃ disā sabhayā saītikā saupaddavā; yatau nivātaṃ tatau savātaṃ; udakaṃ mañ‍ñai ādittaṃ; ayyainamhi dabbaina mal‍laputtaina dūsitā’’ti|

193. atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sarasi tvaṃ, dabba, aivarūpaṃ kattā  yathāyaṃ bhikkhunī āhā’’ti? ‘‘yathā maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā jānātī’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sarasi tvaṃ, dabba, aivarūpaṃ kattā yathāyaṃ bhikkhunī āhā’’ti? ‘‘yathā maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā jānātī’’ti| tatiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sarasi tvaṃ, dabba, aivarūpaṃ kattā yathāyaṃ bhikkhunī āhā’’ti? ‘‘yathā maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā jānātī’’ti| ‘‘na khau, dabba, dabbā aivaṃ nibbaiṭhainti| sacai tayā kataṃ katanti vadaihi| sacai akataṃ akatanti vadaihī’’ti| ‘‘yatauhaṃ, bhantai, jātau nābhijānāmi supinantainapi maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivitā, pagaiva jāgarau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, maittiyaṃ bhikkhuniṃ nāsaitha| imai ca bhikkhū anuyuñ‍jathā’’ti| idaṃ vatvā bhagavā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

atha khau tai bhikkhū maittiyaṃ bhikkhuniṃ nāsaisuṃ| atha khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū tai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘māvusau, maittiyaṃ bhikkhuniṃ nāsaitha, na sā kiñ‍ci aparajjhati; amhaihi sā ussāhitā kupitaihi anattamanaihi cāvanādhippāyaihī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau, āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ  mal‍laputtaṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaithā’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ  …pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa sativaipul‍lappattassa sativinayaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau – ‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, dabbaina mal‍laputtaina saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā, aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā, vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā, uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā, añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā, aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘imai maṃ, bhantai, maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsainti| sauhaṃ, bhantai, sativaipul‍lappattau saṅghaṃ sativinayaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau – ‘imai maṃ, bhantai maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsainti| sauhaṃ  sauhaṃ bhantai (ka॰) sativaipul‍lappattau tatiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ sativinayaṃ yācāmī’ti| ‘‘byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

194. suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsainti| āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau sativaipul‍lappattau saṅghaṃ sativinayaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa sativaipul‍lappattassa sativinayaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsainti| āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau sativaipul‍lappattau saṅghaṃ sativinayaṃ yācati| saṅghau āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa sativaipul‍lappattassa sativinayaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa sativaipul‍lappattassa sativinayassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imai maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsainti| āyasmā dabbau mal‍laputtau sativaipul‍lappattau saṅghaṃ sativinayaṃ yācati| saṅghau āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa sativaipul‍lappattassa sativinayaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati āyasmatau  dabbassa mal‍laputtassa sativaipul‍lappattassa sativinayassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina āyasmatau dabbassa mal‍laputtassa sativaipul‍lappattassa  sativinayau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

195. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, dhammikāni sativinayassa dānāni| suddhau hauti bhikkhu anāpattikau, anuvadanti ca naṃ, yācati ca, tassa saṅghau sativinayaṃ daiti dhammaina samaggaina – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammikāni sativinayassa dānānī’’ti|

3. amūḷhavinayau

196. taina  khau pana samayaina gaggau bhikkhu ummattakau hauti, cittavipariyāsakatau| taina ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ hauti bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| bhikkhū gaggaṃ bhikkhuṃ ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina ajjhāciṇṇaina āpattiyā caudainti – ‘‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi| mūḷhaina mai aitaṃ kata’’nti| aivampi naṃ vuc‍camānā caudaintaiva – ‘‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’’ti? yai tai bhikkhū appicchā..pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū gaggaṃ bhikkhuṃ ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina ajjhāciṇṇaina āpattiyā caudaissanti – ‘‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti! sau aivaṃ vadaiti  – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi| mūḷhaina mai aitaṃ kata’nti| aivampi naṃ vuc‍camānā caudaintaiva – ‘‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’’ti? atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau gaggassa bhikkhunau amūḷhassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daitu| aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau – ‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, gaggaina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā, aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā, vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā, uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā, añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā, aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| maṃ bhikkhū ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina ajjhāciṇṇaina āpattiyā caudainti – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi| mūḷhaina mai aitaṃ kata’nti| aivampi maṃ vuc‍camānā caudaintaiva  – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sauhaṃ, bhantai, amūḷhau saṅghaṃ amūḷhavinayaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| maṃ bhikkhū ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina ajjhāciṇṇaina āpattiyā caudainti – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi mūḷhaina mai aitaṃ kata’nti| aivampi maṃ vuc‍camānā caudaintaiva – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sauhaṃ amūḷhau sauhaṃ bhantai amūḷhau (ka॰) tatiyampi, bhantai, saṅghaṃ amūḷhavinayaṃ yācāmī’ti| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

197. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ gaggau bhikkhu ummattakau ahausi cittavipariyāsakatau| taina ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| bhikkhū gaggaṃ bhikkhuṃ ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina ajjhāciṇṇaina āpattiyā caudainti – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ  bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi, mūḷhaina  mai aitaṃ kata’nti| aivampi naṃ vuc‍camānā caudaintaiva – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sau amūḷhau saṅghaṃ amūḷhavinayaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau gaggassa bhikkhunau amūḷhassa amūḷhavinayaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ gaggau bhikkhu ummattakau ahausi cittavipariyāsakatau| taina ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| bhikkhū gaggaṃ bhikkhuṃ ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina ajjhāciṇṇaina āpattiyā caudainti – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ  bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi mūḷhaina mai aitaṃ kata’nti| aivampi naṃ vuc‍camānā caudaintaiva – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sau amūḷhau saṅghaṃ amūḷhavinayaṃ yācati| saṅghau gaggassa bhikkhunau amūḷhassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati gaggassa bhikkhunau amūḷhassa amūḷhavinayassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina gaggassa bhikkhunau amūḷhassa amūḷhavinayau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

198. ‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, adhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni, tīṇi dhammikāni| kammāni tīṇi adhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni?

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hauti| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā vā, aikapuggalau vā – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sau saramānauva aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, sarāmi aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| tassa saṅghau amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti| adhammikaṃ amūḷhavinayassa dānaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hauti| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā vā, aikapuggalau vā – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sau saramānauva aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘sarāmi khau ahaṃ, āvusau, yathāsupinantainā’ti| tassa saṅghau amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti| adhammikaṃ amūḷhavinayassa dānaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hauti| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā  vā, aikapuggalau vā – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sau anummattakau ummattakālayaṃ karauti – ‘ahampi khau aivaṃ karaumi| tumhaipi aivaṃ karautha| mayhampi aitaṃ kappati| tumhākampaitaṃ kappatī’ti| tassa saṅghau amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti| adhammikaṃ amūḷhavinayassa dānaṃ| imāni tīṇi adhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni|

199. ‘‘katamāni  tīṇi dhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni?

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ummattakau hauti cittavipariyāsakatau| taina ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ hauti bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā vā, aikapuggalau vā – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sau assaramānauva aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, sarāmi aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| tassa saṅghau amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti| dhammikaṃ amūḷhavinayassa dānaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ummattakau hauti cittavipariyāsakatau| taina ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ hauti bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā vā, aikapuggalau vā – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sau assaramānauva aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘sarāmi khau ahaṃ, āvusau, yathā supinantainā’ti| tassa saṅghau amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti| dhammikaṃ amūḷhavinayassa dānaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ummattakau hauti cittavipariyāsakatau| taina ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ hauti bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā vā, aikapuggalau vā – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti? sau ummattakau ummattakālayaṃ karauti – ‘ahampi aivaṃ  karaumi| tumhaipi aivaṃ karautha| mayhampi aitaṃ kappati| tumhākampaitaṃ kappatī’ti| tassa saṅghau amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti| dhammikaṃ amūḷhavinayassa dānaṃ| ‘‘imāni tīṇi dhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānānī’’ti|

4. paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ

200. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū appaṭiñ‍ñāya bhikkhūnaṃ kammāni karaunti  – taj‍janīyampi, niyassampi, pabbājanīyampi, paṭisāraṇīyampi, ukkhaipanīyampi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū appaṭiñ‍ñāya bhikkhūnaṃ kammāni karissanti – taj‍janīyampi, niyassampi, pabbājanīyampi, paṭisāraṇīyampi, ukkhaipanīyampī’’ti! atha khau  tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, appaṭiñ‍ñāya bhikkhūnaṃ kammaṃ kātabbaṃ – taj‍janīyaṃ vā, niyassaṃ vā, pabbājanīyaṃ vā, paṭisāraṇīyaṃ vā, ukkhaipanīyaṃ vā| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

201. ‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, adhammikaṃ hauti paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, aivaṃ dhammikaṃ| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, adhammikaṃ hauti paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ?

‘‘bhikkhu pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā vā, aikapuggalau vā – ‘pārājikaṃ āyasmā ajjhāpan‍nau’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau, saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’ti| taṃ saṅghau saṅghādisaisaina kāraiti| adhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ|

‘‘bhikkhu pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā vā, aikapuggalau vā – ‘pārājikaṃ āyasmā ajjhāpan‍nau’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau, thul‍lac‍cayaṃ…pai॰… pācittiyaṃ…pai॰… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ…pai॰… duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰…  dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’ti| taṃ saṅghau dubbhāsitaina kāraiti| adhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ|

‘‘bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaṃ…pai॰… thul‍lac‍cayaṃ…pai॰… pācittiyaṃ…pai॰… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ…pai॰… duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā vā, aikapuggalau vā – ‘dubbhāsitaṃ āyasmā ajjhāpan‍nau’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau, pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’ti| taṃ saṅghau pārājikaina kāraiti| adhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ|

‘‘bhikkhu dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā vā, aikapuggalau  vā – ‘dubbhāsitaṃ āyasmā ajjhāpan‍nau’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau, saṅghādisaisaṃ…pai॰… thul‍lac‍cayaṃ…pai॰… pācittiyaṃ…pai॰… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ…pai॰… duk‍kaṭaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’ti| taṃ saṅghau duk‍kaṭaina kāraiti| adhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ| ‘‘aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, adhammikaṃ hauti paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammikaṃ hauti paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ? bhikkhu pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā vā, aikapuggalau vā – ‘pārājikaṃ āyasmā ajjhāpan‍nau’ti? sau  aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘āma, āvusau, pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’ti| taṃ saṅghau pārājikaina kāraiti| dhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ|

‘‘bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaṃ…pai॰… thul‍lac‍cayaṃ…pai॰… pācittiyaṃ…pai॰… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ…pai॰… duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti| tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā, sambahulā vā, aikapuggalau vā – ‘dubbhāsitaṃ āyasmā ajjhāpan‍nau’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘āma, āvusau, dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’ti| taṃ saṅghau dubbhāsitaina kāraiti| dhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, dhammikaṃ hauti paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇa’’nti|

5. yaibhuyyasikā

202. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū saṅghamajjhai bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti, na sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ yaibhuyyasikāya vūpasamaituṃ| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu salākaggāhāpakau samman‍nitabbau – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, gahitāgahituñ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau, yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

203. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ salākaggāhāpakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā  ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ salākaggāhāpakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau  khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau salākaggāhāpakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu salākaggāhāpakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

204. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, adhammikā salākaggāhā, dasa dhammikā dasa dhammikā salākaggāhā (ka॰)| katamai dasa adhammikā salākaggāhā? auramattakañ‍ca adhikaraṇaṃ hauti, na ca gatigataṃ hauti, na ca saritasāritaṃ hauti, jānāti adhammavādī bahutarāti, appaiva nāma adhammavādī bahutarā assūti, jānāti saṅghau bhij‍jissatīti, appaiva nāma saṅghau bhij‍jaiyyāti, adhammaina gaṇhanti, vaggā gaṇhanti, na ca yathādiṭṭhiyā gaṇhanti – imai dasa adhammikā salākaggāhā|

‘‘katamai dasa dhammikā salākaggāhā? na ca auramattakaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ hauti, gatigatañ‍ca hauti, saritasāritañ‍ca hauti, jānāti dhammavādī bahutarāti, appaiva nāma dhammavādī bahutarā assūti, jānāti saṅghau na bhij‍jissatīti, appaiva nāma saṅghau na bhij‍jaiyyāti, dhammaina gaṇhanti, samaggā gaṇhanti, yathādiṭṭhiyā ca gaṇhanti – imai dasa dhammikā salākaggāhā’’ti|

6. tassapāpiyasikā

205. taina khau pana samayaina upavāḷau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā  anuyuñ‍jiyamānau avajānitvā paṭijānāti, paṭijānitvā avajānāti, añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, sampajānamusā bhāsati| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma upavāḷau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jiyamānau avajānitvā paṭijānissati, paṭijānitvā avajānissati, añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarissati, sampajānamusā bhāsissatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau upavāḷassa bhikkhunau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karautu| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbaṃ| paṭhamaṃ  upavāḷau bhikkhu caudaitabbau, caudaitvā sāraitabbau, sāraitvā āpattiṃ āraupaitabbau, āpattiṃ āraupaitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

206. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ upavāḷau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jiyamānau avajānitvā paṭijānāti, paṭijānitvā avajānāti, añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, sampajānamusā ‘bhāsati| yadi  saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau upavāḷassa bhikkhunau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ upavāḷau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jiyamānau avajānitvā paṭijānāti, paṭijānitvā  avajānāti, añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, sampajānamusā bhāsati| saṅghau upavāḷassa bhikkhunau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti| yassāyasmatau khamati upavāḷassa bhikkhunau tassapāpiyasikākammassa karaṇaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘kataṃ saṅghaina upavāḷassa bhikkhunau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

207. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, dhammikāni tassapāpiyasikākammassa karaṇāni| asuci ca hauti, alaj‍jī ca, sānuvādau ca, tassa saṅghau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti dhammaina, samaggaina – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca dhammikāni tassapāpiyasikākammassa karaṇāni|

adhammakammadvādasakaṃ

208. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca| asammukhā kataṃ hauti, appaṭipucchākataṃ hauti, appaṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti…pai॰… adhammaina kataṃ hauti, vaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ adhammakammañ‍ca hauti, avinayakammañ‍ca, duvūpasantañ‍ca|

dhammakammadvādasakaṃ

209. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca| sammukhā kataṃ hauti, paṭipucchākataṃ hauti, paṭiñ‍ñāya kataṃ hauti…pai॰…  dhammaina kataṃ hauti, samaggaina kataṃ hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ dhammakammañ‍ca hauti, vinayakammañ‍ca, suvūpasantañ‍ca|

ākaṅkhamānachak‍kaṃ

210. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karaiyya| bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau  saṅghau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karaiyya| adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaihipi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘tiṇṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karaiyya| aikau bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau; aikau bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau; aikau gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karaiyya|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karaiyya| aikau adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, aikau ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, aikau atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai…pai॰…|

‘‘aparaisampi, bhikkhavai…pai॰… aikau buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, aikau saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati – imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ  karaiyya|

aṭṭhārasavattaṃ

211. ‘‘tassapāpiyasikākammakataina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sammā vattitabbaṃ| tatrāyaṃ sammāvattanā –

na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā…pai॰… na bhikkhūhi sampayaujaitabba’’nti| atha khau saṅghau upavāḷassa bhikkhunau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ akāsi|

7. tiṇavatthārakaṃ

212. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ hauti bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘amhākaṃ khau bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya kakkhaḷatāya vāḷatāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰) bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| kathaṃ nu khau  amhaihi paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ hauti bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ tatra cai bhikkhavai bhikkhūnaṃ (syā॰) aivaṃ hauti – ‘amhākaṃ khau bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ; sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyyā’ti, anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ tiṇavatthārakaina vūpasamaituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, vūpasamaitabbaṃ| sabbaihaiva aikajjhaṃ san‍nipatitabbaṃ, san‍nipatitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ  adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ tiṇavatthārakaina vūpasamaiyya, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyutta’’nti| ‘‘aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina sakau pakkhau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇantu mai āyasmantā| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya  vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| yadāyasmantānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ yā caiva āyasmantānaṃ āpatti, yā ca attanau āpatti, āyasmantānañ‍caiva atthāya, attanau ca atthāya, saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisaiyyaṃ, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyutta’’nti|

‘‘athāparaisaṃ aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina sakau pakkhau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇantu mai āyasmantā| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| yadāyasmantānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ yā caiva āyasmantānaṃ āpatti, yā ca attanau āpatti, āyasmantānañ‍caiva atthāya, attanau ca atthāya, saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisaiyyaṃ, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyutta’’nti|

213. ‘‘athāparaisaṃ aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| yadi  saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ yā caiva  imaisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ āpatti, yā ca attanau āpatti, imaisañ‍caiva āyasmantānaṃ atthāya, attanau ca  atthāya, saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisaiyyaṃ, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| ahaṃ yā caiva imaisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ āpatti, yā ca attanau āpatti, imaisañ‍caiva āyasmantānaṃ atthāya, attanau ca atthāya, saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisaimi, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ| yassāyasmatau khamati amhākaṃ imāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisanā, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘daisitā amhākaṃ imā āpattiyau saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā  gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

214. ‘‘athāparaisaṃ aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ  kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ yā caiva imaisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ āpatti, yā ca attanau āpatti, imaisañ‍caiva āyasmantānaṃ atthāya, attanau ca atthāya, saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisaiyyaṃ, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| ahaṃ yā caiva imaisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ āpatti, yā ca attanau āpatti, imaisañ‍caiva āyasmantānaṃ  atthāya, attanau ca atthāya, saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisaimi, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ| yassāyasmatau khamati amhākaṃ imāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisanā, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘daisitā amhākaṃ imā āpattiyau saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

‘‘aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, tai bhikkhū tāhi āpattīhi vuṭṭhitā haunti, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ, ṭhapaitvā diṭṭhāvikammaṃ, ṭhapaitvā yai na tattha hauntī’’ti|

8. adhikaraṇaṃ

215. taina khau pana samayaina (bhikkhūpi bhikkhūhi vivadanti,) ( ) natthi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) bhikkhūpi bhikkhunīhi vivadanti, bhikkhuniyaupi bhikkhūhi vivadanti, chan‍naupi bhikkhu bhikkhunīnaṃ anupakhaj‍ja bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ vivadati, bhikkhunīnaṃ pakkhaṃ gāhaiti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chan‍nau bhikkhu bhikkhunīnaṃ anupakhaj‍ja bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ vivadissati, bhikkhunīnaṃ pakkhaṃ gāhaissatīti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

pari॰ 275 ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇāni – vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca (ka॰)|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaṃ pari॰ 314? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ (ka॰) vivadanti – dhammauti vā adhammauti vā, vinayauti vā avinayauti vā, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti vā abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti vā, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti vā anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti vā, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti vā appañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti vā, āpattīti vā anāpattīti vā, lahukā āpattīti vā garukā āpattīti vā, sāvasaisā āpattīti vā anavasaisā āpattīti vā, duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā? yaṃ tattha  bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau  vivādau nānāvādau añ‍ñathāvādau vipac‍catāya vauhārau maidhagaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati vivādādhikaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ? pari॰ 315 idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ anuvadanti sīlavipattiyā vā ācāravipattiyā vā diṭṭhivipattiyā vā ājīvavipattiyā vā| yau tattha anuvādau anuvadanā anul‍lapanā anubhaṇanā anusampavaṅkatā abbhussahanatā anubalappadānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ? pañ‍capi āpattikkhandhā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, sattapi āpattikkhandhā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ? pari॰ 317 yā saṅghassa kic‍cayatā, karaṇīyatā, apalaukanakammaṃ, ñattikammaṃ, ñattidutiyakammaṃ, ñatticatutthakammaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ|
