216. ‘‘vivādādhikaraṇassa kiṃ mūlaṃ? cha vivādamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ| tīṇipi akusalamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, tīṇipi kusalamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ| pari॰ 272; a॰ ni॰ 2.35-36; ma॰ ni॰ 3.44 katamāni cha vivādamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī, sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi…pai॰… saṅghaipi…pai॰… sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī, sau saṅghai vivādaṃ janaiti| yau hauti vivādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha, tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha, tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa  āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti|

pari॰272 ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu makkhī hauti paḷāsī…pai॰… issukī hauti maccharī, saṭhau hauti māyāvī, pāpicchau hauti micchādiṭṭhī, sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī  duppaṭinissaggī, sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi…pai॰… saṅghaipi…pai॰… sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī, sau saṅghai vivādaṃ janaiti| yau hauti vivādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha, tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha, tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti| imāni cha vivādamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

‘‘katamāni tīṇi akusalamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū luddhacittā vivadanti, duṭṭhacittā vivadanti, mūḷhacittā vivadanti – dhammauti vā adhammauti vā, vinayauti vā avinayauti vā, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti vā abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti  vā, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti vā anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti vā, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti vā appañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti vā, āpattīti vā anāpattīti vā, lahukā āpattīti vā garukā āpattīti vā, sāvasaisā āpattīti vā anavasaisā āpattīti vā, duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā| imāni tīṇi akusalamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

‘‘katamāni  tīṇi kusalamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū aluddhacittā vivadanti, aduṭṭhacittā vivadanti, amūḷhacittā vivadanti  – dhammauti vā adhammauti vā…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā| imāni tīṇi kusalamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

217. pari॰ 272 ‘‘anuvādādhikaraṇassa kiṃ mūlaṃ? cha anuvādamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ| tīṇipi akusalamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, tīṇipi kusalamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa  mūlaṃ, kāyaupi anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, vācāpi anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ| ‘‘katamāni cha anuvādamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī, sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati  appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi…pai॰… saṅghaipi…pai॰… sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī, sau saṅghai anuvādaṃ janaiti| yau hauti anuvādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, anuvādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha, tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, anuvādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha, tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu makkhī hauti paḷāsī…pai॰… issukī hauti maccharī, saṭhau hauti māyāvī, pāpicchau hauti micchādiṭṭhī, sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī, sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sattharipi agāravau  viharati appatissau, dhammaipi…pai॰… saṅghaipi…pai॰… sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī, sau saṅghai anuvādaṃ janaiti| yau hauti anuvādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, anuvādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha, tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpañ‍cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, anuvādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha, tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, tassaiva pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa āyattiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti| imāni cha anuvādamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

‘‘katamāni  tīṇi akusalamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ luddhacittā anuvadanti, duṭṭhacittā anuvadanti, mūḷhacittā anuvadanti – sīlavipattiyā vā, ācāravipattiyā vā, diṭṭhivipattiyā vā, ājīvavipattiyā vā| imāni tīṇi akusalamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

‘‘katamāni tīṇi kusalamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ aluddhacittā anuvadanti, aduṭṭhacittā anuvadanti, amūḷhacittā anuvadanti – sīlavipattiyā vā, ācaravipattiyā  vā, diṭṭhivipattiyā vā, ājīvavipattiyā vā| imāni tīṇi kusalamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

‘‘katamau kāyau katamau ca kāyau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ? idhaikac‍cau dubbaṇṇau hauti, duddassikau, aukauṭimakau, bahvābādhau, kāṇau vā, kuṇī vā, khañ‍jau vā, pakkhahatau vā, yaina naṃ anuvadanti| ayaṃ kāyau anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

‘‘katamā vācā anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ? idhaikac‍cau dubbacau hauti, mammanau, aiḷagalavācau, yāya naṃ anuvadanti| ayaṃ vācā anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

218. ‘‘āpattādhikaraṇassa kiṃ mūlaṃ? cha āpattisamuṭṭhānā āpattādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ| atthāpatti kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau, na  cittatau| atthāpatti vācatau samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau, na cittatau| atthāpatti kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau| atthāpatti kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau| atthāpatti vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau| atthāpatti kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti| imai cha āpattisamuṭṭhānā āpattādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

219. ‘‘kic‍cādhikaraṇassa kiṃ mūlaṃ? kic‍cādhikaraṇassa aikaṃ mūlaṃ – saṅghau|

220. ‘‘vivādādhikaraṇaṃ  kusalaṃ, akusalaṃ, abyākataṃ| vivādādhikaraṇaṃ siyā kusalaṃ, siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ| tattha katamaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū kusalacittā vivadanti – dhammauti vā adhammauti vā…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti  vā aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā| yaṃ tattha bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau nānāvādau añ‍ñathāvādau vipac‍catāya vauhārau maidhagaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati vivādādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaṃ akusalaṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū akusalacittā vivadanti – dhammauti vā adhammauti vā…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā| yaṃ tattha bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau nānāvādau añ‍ñathāvādau vipac‍catāya vauhārau maidhagaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati vivādādhikaraṇaṃ akusalaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaṃ abyākataṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū abyākatacittā vivadanti – dhammauti vā adhammauti vā…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā| yaṃ tattha bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau nānāvādau añ‍ñathāvādau vipac‍catāya vauhārau maidhagaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati vivādādhikaraṇaṃ abyākataṃ|

221. ‘‘anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ  kusalaṃ, akusalaṃ, abyākataṃ| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ siyā kusalaṃ, siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ| tattha katamaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ kusalacittā anuvadanti – sīlavipattiyā vā, ācāravipattiyā vā, diṭṭhivipattiyā vā, ājīvavipattiyā vā| yau tattha anuvādau anuvadanā anul‍lapanā anubhaṇanā  anusampavaṅkatā abbhussahanatā anubalappadānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ akusalaṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ akusalacittā anuvadanti – sīlavipattiyā vā, ācāravipattiyā vā, diṭṭhivipattiyā vā, ājīvavipattiyā vā| yau tattha anuvādau anuvadanā anul‍lapanā anubhaṇanā anusampavaṅkatā abbhussahanatā anubalappadānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ akusalaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ abyākataṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ abyākatacittā anuvadanti – sīlavipattiyā vā, ācāravipattiyā vā, diṭṭhivipattiyā vā, ājīvavipattiyā vā| yau tattha anuvādau anuvadanā anul‍lapanā anubhaṇanā anusampavaṅkatā abbhussahanatā anubalappadānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ abyākataṃ|

222. ‘‘āpattādhikaraṇaṃ  kusalaṃ idaṃ padaṃ kaisuci natthi, akusalaṃ, abyākataṃ? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ; natthi āpattādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ| tattha  katamaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ akusalaṃ? yaṃ jānantau sañ‍jānantau caic‍ca abhivitaritvā vītik‍kamau – idaṃ vuc‍cati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ akusalaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ abyākataṃ? yaṃ ajānantau asañ‍jānantau acaic‍ca anabhivitaritvā vītik‍kamau – idaṃ vuc‍cati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ abyākataṃ|

223. ‘‘kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ, akusalaṃ, abyākataṃ? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ siyā kusalaṃ, siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ| tattha katamaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ? yaṃ saṅghau kusalacittau kammaṃ karauti – apalaukanakammaṃ, ñattikammaṃ, ñattidutiyakammaṃ, ñatticatutthakammaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ  akusalaṃ? yaṃ saṅghau akusalacittau kammaṃ karauti – apalaukanakammaṃ, ñattikammaṃ, ñattidutiyakammaṃ, ñatticatutthakammaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ akusalaṃ|

‘‘tattha  katamaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ abyākataṃ? yaṃ saṅghau abyākatacittau kammaṃ karauti – apalaukanakammaṃ, ñattikammaṃ, ñattidutiyakammaṃ, ñatticatutthakammaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ abyākataṃ|

224. pari॰ 355 ‘‘vivādau vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, vivādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ, adhikaraṇaṃ nau vivādau, adhikaraṇañ‍caiva vivādau ca| siyā vivādau vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, siyā vivādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ, siyā adhikaraṇaṃ nau  vivādau, siyā adhikaraṇañ‍caiva vivādau ca|

‘‘tattha katamau vivādau vivādādhikaraṇaṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū vivadanti – dhammauti vā adhammauti vā…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā| yaṃ tattha bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau nānāvādau añ‍ñathāvādau vipac‍catāya vauhārau maidhagaṃ – ayaṃ vivādau vivādādhikaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tattha  katamau vivādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ? mātāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi mātarā vivadati, pitāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi pitarā vivadati, bhātāpi bhātarā vivadati, bhātāpi bhaginiyā vivadati, bhaginīpi bhātarā vivadati, sahāyaupi sahāyaina vivadati – ayaṃ vivādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau vivādau? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ – idaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau vivādau|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva vivādau ca? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva vivādau ca|

225. pari॰ 356 ‘‘anuvādau anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ, adhikaraṇaṃ nau anuvādau, adhikaraṇañ‍caiva anuvādau ca| siyā anuvādau anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, siyā anuvādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ, siyā adhikaraṇaṃ nau anuvādau, siyā adhikaraṇañ‍caiva anuvādau ca|

‘‘tattha katamau anuvādau anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ anuvadanti – sīlavipattiyā vā, ācāravipattiyā vā, diṭṭhivipattiyā vā, ājīvavipattiyā vā| yau tattha anuvādau anuvadanā anul‍lapanā anubhaṇanā  anusampavaṅkatā abbhussahanatā anubalappadānaṃ – ayaṃ anuvādau anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamau anuvādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ? mātāpi puttaṃ anuvadati, puttaupi mātaraṃ anuvadati, pitāpi puttaṃ anuvadati, puttaupi pitaraṃ anuvadati, bhātāpi bhātaraṃ anuvadati, bhātāpi bhaginiṃ anuvadati, bhaginīpi bhātaraṃ anuvadati, sahāyaupi sahāyaṃ anuvadati – ayaṃ anuvādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau anuvādau? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, vivādādhikaraṇaṃ – idaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau anuvādau|

‘‘tattha  katamaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva anuvādau ca? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva anuvādau ca|

226. pari॰ 357 ‘‘āpatti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, āpatti nau adhikaraṇaṃ, adhikaraṇaṃ nau āpatti, adhikaraṇañ‍caiva āpatti ca| siyā āpatti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, siyā āpatti nau adhikaraṇaṃ, siyā adhikaraṇaṃ nau āpatti, siyā adhikaraṇañ‍caiva āpatti ca| ‘‘tattha katamaṃ  āpatti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ? pañ‍capi āpattikkhandhā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, sattapi āpattikkhandhā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ – ayaṃ āpatti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ āpatti nau adhikaraṇaṃ? sautāpatti samāpatti – ayaṃ āpatti nau adhikaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau āpatti? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ – idaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau āpatti|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva āpatti ca? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

227. pari॰ 358 ‘‘kic‍caṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍caṃ nau adhikaraṇaṃ, adhikaraṇaṃ nau kic‍caṃ, adhikaraṇañ‍caiva kic‍cañ‍ca| siyā kic‍caṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, siyā kic‍caṃ nau adhikaraṇaṃ, siyā adhikaraṇaṃ nau kic‍caṃ, siyā adhikaraṇañ‍caiva kic‍cañ‍ca|

‘‘tattha  katamaṃ kic‍caṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ? yā saṅghassa kic‍cayatā, karaṇīyatā, apalaukanakammaṃ, ñattikammaṃ, ñattidutiyakammaṃ, ñatticatutthakammaṃ – idaṃ kic‍caṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ kic‍caṃ nau adhikaraṇaṃ? ācariyakic‍caṃ, upajjhāyakic‍caṃ, samānupajjhāyakic‍caṃ, samānācariyakic‍caṃ – idaṃ kic‍caṃ nau adhikaraṇaṃ|

‘‘tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau kic‍caṃ? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ – idaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau kic‍caṃ|

‘‘tattha  katamaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva kic‍cañ‍ca? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva kic‍cañ‍ca|

9. adhikaraṇavūpasamanasamathau

sammukhāvinayau

228. pari॰ 292-293, 307 ādayau ‘‘vivādādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ dvīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca, yaibhuyyasikāya ca| siyā vivādādhikaraṇaṃ aikaṃ samathaṃ anāgamma yaibhuyyasikaṃ, aikaina samathaina samaiyya – sammukhāvinayaināti? siyātissa vacanīyaṃ| yathā kathaṃ viya? idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū vivadanti – dhammauti vā adhammauti vā, vinayauti vā avinayauti vā, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti vā abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti vā, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti vā anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti vā, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti vā apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti vā, āpattīti vā anāpattīti vā, lahukā āpattīti vā garukā āpattīti vā, sāvasaisā āpattīti vā anavasaisā āpattīti vā, duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā|

‘‘tai cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? saṅghasammukhatā, dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā| kā ca tattha saṅghasammukhatā? yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā na paṭik‍kausanti – ayaṃ tattha saṅghasammukhatā| kā ca  tattha dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā? yaina dhammaina yaina vinayaina yaina satthusāsanaina taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati – ayaṃ tattha dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā| kā ca tattha puggalasammukhatā? yau ca vivadati, yaina ca vivadati, ubhau atthapac‍catthikā sammukhībhūtā haunti – ayaṃ tattha puggalasammukhatā| aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ kārakau  uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ; chandadāyakau khīyati, khīyanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

229. ‘‘tai cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū na sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ tasmiṃ āvāsai vūpasamaituṃ, taihi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi, yasmiṃ āvāsai sambahulā bahutarā (sī॰ syā॰) bhikkhū, sau āvāsau gantabbau| tai cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū taṃ āvāsaṃ gacchantā antarāmaggai sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? saṅghasammukhatā, dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā| kā ca tattha saṅghasammukhatā? yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā na paṭik‍kausanti – ayaṃ tattha saṅghasammukhatā| kā ca tattha dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā? yaina dhammaina yaina vinayaina yaina satthusāsanaina taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati – ayaṃ tattha dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā| kā ca tattha puggalasammukhatā? yau ca vivadati, yaina ca vivadati, ubhau atthapac‍catthikā sammukhībhūtā haunti – ayaṃ tattha puggalasammukhatā| aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ kārakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ; chandadāyakau khīyati, khīyanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

230. ‘‘tai  cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū taṃ āvāsaṃ āgacchantā antarāmaggai na sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ, taihi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi, taṃ āvāsaṃ gantvā āvāsikā bhikkhū aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘idaṃ khau, āvusau, adhikaraṇaṃ aivaṃ jātaṃ, aivaṃ samuppan‍naṃ; sādhāyasmantā imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaintu dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina, yathayidaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ suvūpasantaṃ assā’ti|

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, āvāsikā bhikkhū vuḍḍhatarā haunti, āgantukā bhikkhū navakatarā, taihi, bhikkhavai, āvāsikaihi bhikkhūhi āgantukā bhikkhū aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘iṅgha tumhai, āyasmantau, muhuttaṃ aikamantaṃ hautha, yāva mayaṃ mantaimā’ti| sacai pana, bhikkhavai, āvāsikā bhikkhū navakatarā haunti, āgantukā bhikkhū vuḍḍhatarā, taihi, bhikkhavai, āvāsikaihi bhikkhūhi āgantukā bhikkhū aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘taina hi tumhai, āyasmantau, muhuttaṃ idhaiva tāva hautha, yāva mayaṃ mantaimā’ti|

‘‘sacai pana, bhikkhavai, āvāsikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mantayamānānaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘na mayaṃ sak‍kauma imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanainā’ti, na taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ āvāsikaihi bhikkhūhi sampaṭicchitabbaṃ| sacai pana, bhikkhavai, āvāsikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mantayamānānaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘sak‍kauma mayaṃ imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanainā’ti, taihi, bhikkhavai, āvāsikaihi  bhikkhūhi āgantukā bhikkhū aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘sacai tumhai, āyasmantau, amhākaṃ  imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ yathājātaṃ yathāsamuppan‍naṃ āraucaissatha, yathā ca mayaṃ imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaissāma dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina tathā suvūpasantaṃ  bhavissati| aivaṃ mayaṃ imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ sampaṭicchissāma| nau cai tumhai, āyasmantau, amhākaṃ imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ yathājātaṃ yathāsamuppan‍naṃ āraucaissatha, yathā ca mayaṃ imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaissāma dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina tathā na suvūpasantaṃ bhavissati, na mayaṃ imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ sampaṭicchissāmā’ti| aivaṃ supariggahitaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, katvā āvāsikaihi bhikkhūhi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ sampaṭicchitabbaṃ|

‘‘taihi, bhikkhavai, āgantukaihi bhikkhūhi āvāsikā bhikkhū aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘yathājātaṃ yathāsamuppan‍naṃ mayaṃ imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ āyasmantānaṃ āraucaissāma| sacai āyasmantā sak‍kaunti aittakaina vā aittakaina vā antaraina imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina tathā suvūpasantaṃ bhavissati| aivaṃ mayaṃ imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ āyasmantānaṃ niyyādaissāma| nau cai āyasmantā sak‍kaunti aittakaina vā aittakaina vā antaraina imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina tathā na suvūpasantaṃ bhavissati, na mayaṃ imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ āyasmantānaṃ niyyādaissāma| mayamaiva  imassa adhikaraṇassa sāminau bhavissāmā’ti| aivaṃ supariggahitaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, katvā āgantukaihi bhikkhūhi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ āvāsikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ niyyādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘tai cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? saṅghasammukhatā, dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā…pai॰… aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ kārakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ; chandadāyakau khīyati, khīyanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ubbāhikāyavūpasamanaṃ

231. ‘‘taihi cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi tasmiṃ adhikaraṇai vinicchiyamānai anantāni anaggāni (sī॰) caiva bhassāni jāyanti, na caikassa na caitassa (ka॰) bhāsitassa atthau viñ‍ñāyati, anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ ubbāhikāya vūpasamaituṃ| ‘‘dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau – sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpassa dhammā bahussutā haunti,  dhātā dhatā (sī॰ syā॰) vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā  diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā; ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau; vinayai khau pana ṭhitau chaikau (ka॰) hauti asaṃhīrau; paṭibalau hauti ubhau atthapac‍catthikai assāsaituṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ nijjhāpaituṃ paikkhaituṃ pasādaituṃ; adhikaraṇasamuppādavūpasamanakusalau hauti; adhikaraṇaṃ jānāti; adhikaraṇasamudayaṃ jānāti; adhikaraṇaniraudhaṃ jānāti; adhikaraṇaniraudhagāminipaṭipadaṃ jānāti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imaihi dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhikkhuṃ ubbāhikāya samman‍nituṃ| aivañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau, yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

232. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai vinicchiyamānai anantāni caiva bhassāni jāyanti, na caikassa bhāsitassa atthau viñ‍ñāyati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmañ‍ca itthan‍nāmañ‍ca bhikkhuṃ samman‍naiyya ubbāhikāya imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai vinicchiyamānai anantāni caiva bhassāni jāyanti, na caikassa bhāsitassa atthau viñ‍ñāyati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmañ‍ca itthan‍nāmañ‍ca bhikkhuṃ samman‍nati ubbāhikāya imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ  vūpasamaituṃ| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa ca itthan‍nāmassa ca bhikkhunau sammuti ubbāhikāya imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau ca itthan‍nāmau ca bhikkhu ubbāhikāya imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

‘‘tai cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ ubbāhikāya vūpasamaituṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā…pai॰… aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ kārakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

233. ‘‘taihi cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi tasmiṃ adhikaraṇai vinicchiyamānai tatrāssa bhikkhu dhammakathikau, tassa naiva suttaṃ āgataṃ hauti, nau suttavibhaṅgau, sau atthaṃ asal‍lakkhaintau  byañ‍janacchāyāya atthaṃ paṭibāhati, byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina tai bhikkhū ñāpaitabbā –

‘‘suṇantu mai āyasmantā| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu dhammakathikau| imassa naiva suttaṃ āgataṃ hauti, nau suttavibhaṅgau| sau atthaṃ asal‍lakkhaintau byañ‍janacchāyāya atthaṃ paṭibāhati| yadāyasmantānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ, itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ  vuṭṭhāpaitvā avasaisā imaṃ  adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaiyyāmāti|

‘‘tai cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā…pai॰… aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ kārakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

‘‘taihi cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi tasmiṃ adhikaraṇai vinicchiyamānai tatrāssa bhikkhu dhammakathikau, tassa suttañhi khau āgataṃ hauti, nau suttavibhaṅgau, sau atthaṃ asal‍lakkhaintau byañ‍janacchāyāya atthaṃ paṭibāhati, byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina tai bhikkhū ñāpaitabbā –

‘‘suṇantu mai āyasmantā| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu dhammakathikau| imassa suttañhi khau āgataṃ hauti, nau suttavibhaṅgau| sau atthaṃ asal‍lakkhaintau byañ‍janacchāyāya atthaṃ paṭibāhati| yadāyasmantānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ, itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā avasaisā imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaiyyāmāti|

‘‘tai cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū taṃ bhikkhuṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā…pai॰… aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ kārakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

yaibhuyyasikāvinayau

234. ‘‘tai cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū na sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ ubbāhikāya  vūpasamaituṃ, taihi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ saṅghassa niyyādaitabbaṃ – ‘na mayaṃ na ca mayaṃ (ka॰), bhantai, sak‍kauma  imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ ubbāhikāya vūpasamaituṃ, saṅghauva imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitū’ti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ yaibhuyyasikāya vūpasamaituṃ| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu salākaggāhāpakau samman‍nitabbau – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na  dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, gahitāgahitañ‍ca jānaiyya…pai॰… aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau, yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ salākaggāhāpakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ salākaggāhāpakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau salākaggāhāpakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu salākaggāhāpakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

‘‘taina salākaggāhāpakaina bhikkhunā salākā gāhaitabbā| yathā bahutarā bhikkhū dhammavādinau vadanti tathā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitabbaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina ca, yaibhuyyasikāya ca| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? saṅghasammukhatā, dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā| kā ca tattha saṅghasammukhatā? yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā na paṭik‍kausanti – ayaṃ tattha saṅghasammukhatā| kā ca tattha dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā? yaina dhammaina yaina vinayaina yaina satthusāsanaina taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati – ayaṃ tattha dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā| kā ca tattha puggalasammukhatā? yau ca vivadati, yaina ca vivadati, ubhau atthapac‍catthikā sammukhībhūtā haunti – ayaṃ tattha puggalasammukhatā| kā ca tattha yaibhuyyasikāya? yā yaibhuyyasikākammassa kiriyā karaṇaṃ upagamanaṃ ajjhupagamanaṃ adhivāsanā appaṭik‍kausanā – ayaṃ tattha yaibhuyyasikāya| aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ kārakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ; chandadāyakau khīyati, khīyanakaṃ pācittiya’’nti|

tividhasalākaggāhau

235. taina  khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyā aivaṃ jātaṃ aivaṃ samuppan‍naṃ adhikaraṇaṃ hauti| atha khau tai bhikkhū – asantuṭṭhā sāvatthiyā saṅghassa adhikaraṇavūpasamanaina – assausuṃ khau amukasmiṃ kira āvāsai sambahulā thairā viharanti bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā paṇḍitā viyattā maidhāvinau laj‍jinau kuk‍kuc‍cakā sikkhākāmā| tai cai thairā imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaiyyuṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina, aivamidaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ suvūpasantaṃ assāti| atha khau tai bhikkhū taṃ āvāsaṃ gantvā tai thairai aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, adhikaraṇaṃ aivaṃ jātaṃ, aivaṃ samuppan‍naṃ| sādhu, bhantai, thairā imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaintu dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina, yathayidaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ suvūpasantaṃ assā’’ti| atha khau tai thairā – yathā sāvatthiyā saṅghaina adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamitaṃ tathā suvūpasantanti yathā suvūpasantaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) – tathā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaisuṃ|

atha khau tai bhikkhū – asantuṭṭhā sāvatthiyā saṅghassa adhikaraṇavūpasamanaina, asantuṭṭhā sambahulānaṃ thairānaṃ adhikaraṇavūpasamanaina – assausuṃ khau amukasmiṃ kira āvāsai tayau thairā viharanti…pai॰… dvai thairā viharanti…pai॰… aikau thairau viharati bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| sau cai thairau imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaiyya dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina, aivamidaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ suvūpasantaṃ assāti| atha khau tai bhikkhū taṃ āvāsaṃ gantvā taṃ thairaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, adhikaraṇaṃ aivaṃ jātaṃ, aivaṃ samuppan‍naṃ| sādhu, bhantai, thairau imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitu dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina, yathayidaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ suvūpasantaṃ assā’’ti| atha khau sau thairau – yathā sāvatthiyā saṅghaina adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamitaṃ, yathā sambahulaihi thairaihi adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamitaṃ, yathā tīhi thairaihi adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamitaṃ, yathā dvīhi thairaihi adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamitaṃ, tathā suvūpasantanti – tathā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaisi|

atha  khau tai bhikkhū asantuṭṭhā sāvatthiyā saṅghassa adhikaraṇavūpasamanaina, asantuṭṭhā sambahulānaṃ thairānaṃ adhikaraṇavūpasamanaina, asantuṭṭhā tiṇṇaṃ thairānaṃ adhikaraṇavūpasamanaina, asantuṭṭhā dvin‍naṃ thairānaṃ adhikaraṇavūpasamanaina, asantuṭṭhā aikassa thairassa adhikaraṇavūpasamanaina, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰…  ‘‘nihatamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ santaṃ vūpasantaṃ suvūpasantaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sañ‍ñattiyā tayau salākaggāhai – gūḷhakaṃ, sakaṇṇajappakaṃ, vivaṭakaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, gūḷhakau salākaggāhau hauti? taina salākaggāhāpakaina bhikkhunā  salākāyau vaṇṇāvaṇṇāyau katvā aikamaikau bhikkhu upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ayaṃ aivaṃvādissa salākā, ayaṃ aivaṃvādissa salākā| yaṃ icchasi taṃ gaṇhāhī’ti| gahitai vattabbau – ‘mā ca kassaci dassaihī’ti| sacai jānāti – adhammavādī bahutarāti, duggahauti, pac‍cuk‍kaḍḍhitabbaṃ| sacai jānāti – dhammavādī bahutarāti, suggahauti, sāvaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, gūḷhakau salākaggāhau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sakaṇṇajappakau salākaggāhau hauti? taina salākaggāhāpakaina bhikkhunā aikamaikassa bhikkhunau upakaṇṇakai āraucaitabbaṃ – ‘ayaṃ aivaṃvādissa salākā, ayaṃ aivaṃvādissa salākā| yaṃ icchasi taṃ gaṇhāhī’ti| gahitai vattabbau – ‘mā ca kassaci āraucaihī’ti| sacai jānāti – adhammavādī bahutarāti, duggahauti, pac‍cuk‍kaḍḍhitabbaṃ| sacai jānāti – dhammavādī bahutarāti, suggahauti, sāvaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, sakaṇṇajappakau salākaggāhau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vivaṭakau salākaggāhau hauti? sacai jānāti – dhammavādī bahutarāti, vissaṭṭhainaiva vivaṭaina gāhaitabbau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, vivaṭakau salākaggāhau hauti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau salākaggāhā’’ti|

sativinayau

236. pari॰ 292-293, 307 ādayau ‘‘anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ  katihi samathaihi sammati? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ catūhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca, sativinayaina ca, amūḷhavinayaina ca, tassapāpiyasikāya ca| siyā anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ dvai samathai anāgamma – amūḷhavinayañ‍ca, tassapāpiyasikañ‍ca; dvīhi samathaihi sammaiyya – sammukhāvinayaina ca, sativinayaina cāti? siyātissa vacanīyaṃ| yathā kathaṃ viya? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsainti| tassa khau, bhikkhavai tassa khau taṃ bhikkhavai (syā॰ ka॰), bhikkhunau sativaipul‍lappattassa sativinayau dātabbau| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅga karitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘maṃ, bhantai, bhikkhū amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsainti| sauhaṃ, bhantai, sativaipul‍lappattau saṅghaṃ sativinayaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| byattainaṃ bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| bhikkhū itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsainti| sau sativaipul‍lappattau saṅghaṃ sativinayaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sativaipul‍lappattassa  sativinayaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| bhikkhū itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsainti| sau sativaipul‍lappattau saṅghaṃ sativinayaṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sativaipul‍lappattassa sativinayaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sativaipul‍lappattassa sativinayassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sativaipul‍lappattassa sativinayau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

‘‘idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? saṅghasammukhatā, dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā…pai॰… kā ca tattha puggalasammukhatā? yau ca anuvadati, yañ‍ca anuvadati, ubhau sammukhībhūtā haunti – ayaṃ tattha puggalasammukhatā| kiñ‍ca tattha sativinayasmiṃ? yā sativinayassa kammassa kiriyā karaṇaṃ upagamanaṃ ajjhupagamanaṃ adhivāsanā appaṭik‍kausanā – idaṃ tattha sativinayasmiṃ| aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ kārakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ; chandadāyakau khīyati, khīyanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

amūḷhavinayau

237. ‘‘siyā anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ dvai samathai anāgamma – sativinayañ‍ca, tassapāpiyasikañ‍ca; dvīhi samathaihi sammaiyya – sammukhāvinayaina ca, amūḷhavinayaina  cāti? siyātissa vacanīyaṃ| yathā kathaṃ viya? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ummattakau hauti cittavipariyāsakatau| taina ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ hauti bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| taṃ bhikkhū ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina ajjhāciṇṇaina āpattiyā caudainti – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi| mūḷhaina mai aitaṃ kata’nti| aivampi naṃ vuc‍camānā caudaintaiva – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| ‘‘tassa khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau amūḷhassa amūḷhavinayau dātabbau| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbau –

‘‘taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā…pai॰… aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| maṃ bhikkhū ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina ajjhāciṇṇaina āpattiyā caudainti – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi| mūḷhaina mai aitaṃ kata’nti| aivampi maṃ vuc‍camānā caudaintaiva – ‘saratāyasmā  aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| ‘sauhaṃ, bhantai, amūḷhau saṅghaṃ amūḷhavinayaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu ummattakau ahausi cittavipariyāsakatau| taina ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| taṃ bhikkhū ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina ajjhāciṇṇaina āpattiyā caudainti – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi| mūḷhaina mai aitaṃ kata’nti| aivampi naṃ vuc‍camānā caudaintaiva – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| sau amūḷhau saṅghaṃ amūḷhavinayaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau amūḷhassa amūḷhavinayaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu ummattakau ahausi cittavipariyāsakatau| taina ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| taṃ bhikkhū ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina ajjhāciṇṇaina āpattiyā caudainti – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, ummattakau ahausiṃ cittavipariyāsakatau| taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi| mūḷhaina mai aitaṃ kata’nti| aivampi naṃ vuc‍camānā caudaintaiva – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| sau amūḷhau saṅghaṃ amūḷhavinayaṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau amūḷhassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau amūḷhassa amūḷhavinayassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau amūḷhassa amūḷhavinayau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

‘‘idaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina ca, amūḷhavinayaina ca| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? saṅghasammukhatā, dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā…pai॰… kiñ‍ca tattha amūḷhavinayasmiṃ? yā amūḷhavinayassa kammassa kiriyā karaṇaṃ upagamanaṃ ajjhupagamanaṃ adhivāsanā appaṭik‍kausanā – idaṃ tattha amūḷhavinayasmiṃ| aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ kārakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ; chandadāyakau khīyati, khīyanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

tassapāpiyasikāvinayau

238. ‘‘siyā anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ dvai samathai anāgamma – sativinayañ‍ca, amūḷhavinayañ‍ca; dvīhi samathaihi sammaiyya – sammukhāvinayaina ca, tassapāpiyasikāya cāti? siyātissa vacanīyaṃ| yathā kathaṃ viya? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ saṅghamajjhai garukāya āpattiyā caudaiti – ‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā, pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, sarāmi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā, pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti| tamainaṃ sau nibbaiṭhaintaṃ  ativaiṭhaiti – ‘iṅghāyasmā sādhukamaiva jānāhi, yadi sarasi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā, pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, sarāmi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā, pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā| sarāmi ca khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivarūpiṃ appamattikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’ti| tamainaṃ sau nibbaiṭhaintaṃ ativaiṭhaiti – ‘iṅghāyasmā sādhukamaiva jānāhi, yadi sarasi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā, pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘imañhi nāmāhaṃ, āvusau, appamattikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā apuṭṭhau paṭijānissāmi| kiṃ panāhaṃ aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā, pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā, puṭṭhau na paṭijānissāmī’ti? sau aivaṃ vadaiti – ‘imañhi nāma tvaṃ, āvusau, appamattikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā apuṭṭhau na paṭijānissasi| kiṃ pana tvaṃ aivarūpiṃ  garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā, pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā, apuṭṭhau paṭijānissasi? iṅghāyasmā sādhukamaiva jānāhi, yadi sarasi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā, pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti| sau aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘sarāmi khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivarūpiṃ  garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā, pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā| davā mai aitaṃ vuttaṃ, ravā mai aitaṃ vuttaṃ – nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi aivarūpiṃ garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā, pārājikaṃ vā pārājikasāmantaṃ vā’ti| tassa khau, bhikkhavai tassa khau taṃ bhikkhavai (ka॰) tassa khvataṃ bhikkhavai (syā॰), bhikkhunau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ kātabbaṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai garukāya āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jiyamānau avajānitvā paṭijānāti, paṭijānitvā avajānāti, añ‍ñaināñ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, sampajānamusā bhāsati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai garukāya āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jiyamānau avajānitvā paṭijānāti, paṭijānitvā avajānāti, añ‍ñaināñ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, sampajānamusā bhāsati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ  karauti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tassapāpiyasikākammassa karaṇaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰…|

‘‘kataṃ saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

‘‘idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ  vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina ca, tassapāpiyasikāya ca| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? saṅghasammukhatā, dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā…pai॰… kā ca tattha tassapāpiyasikāya? yā tassapāpiyasikākammassa kiriyā karaṇaṃ upagamanaṃ ajjhupagamanaṃ adhivāsanā appaṭik‍kausanā – ayaṃ tattha tassapāpiyasikāya| aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ kārakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ; chandadāyakau khīyati, khīyanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ

239. pari॰ 295, 306, 308 ādayau ‘‘āpattādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, tiṇavatthārakaina  ca| siyā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ aikaṃ samathaṃ anāgamma – tiṇavatthārakaṃ, dvīhi samathaihi sammaiyya – sammukhāvinayaina ca, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina cāti? siyātissa vacanīyaṃ| yathā kathaṃ viya? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu lahukaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hauti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ  paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, āvusau, itthan‍nāmaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau; taṃ paṭidaisaimī’ti| taina vattabbau – ‘passasī’ti? ‘āma passāmī’ti| ‘āyatiṃ saṃvaraiyyāsī’ti|

‘‘idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina ca, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā…pai॰… kā ca tattha puggalasammukhatā? yau ca daisaiti, yassa ca daisaiti, ubhau sammukhībhūtā haunti – ayaṃ tattha puggalasammukhatā| kiñ‍ca tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇasmiṃ? yā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa kammassa kiriyā karaṇaṃ upagamanaṃ ajjhupagamanaṃ adhivāsanā appaṭik‍kausanā – idaṃ tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇasmiṃ| aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ paṭiggāhakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā sambahulai bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau; taṃ paṭidaisaimī’ti| byattaina bhikkhunā  paṭibalaina tai bhikkhū ñāpaitabbā –

‘‘suṇantu mai āyasmantā| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu āpattiṃ sarati, vivarati, uttāniṃ karauti daisaiti| yadāyasmantānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau āpattiṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyyanti| taina vattabbau – ‘passasī’ti? ‘āma  passāmī’ti| ‘āyatiṃ saṃvaraiyyāsī’ti|

‘‘idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina ca, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā…pai॰… kā ca tattha puggalasammukhatā? yau ca daisaiti, yassa ca daisaiti, ubhau sammukhībhūtā haunti  – ayaṃ tattha puggalasammukhatā| kiñ‍ca tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇasmiṃ? yā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa kammassa kiriyā karaṇaṃ upagamanaṃ ajjhupagamanaṃ adhivāsanā appaṭik‍kausanā – idaṃ tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇasmiṃ| aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ paṭiggāhakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ| aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, itthan‍nāmaṃ āpattiṃ āpan‍nau; taṃ paṭidaisaimī’ti| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu āpattiṃ sarati, vivarati, uttāniṃ karauti, daisaiti| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau āpattiṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyyanti| taina vattabbau – ‘passasī’ti? ‘āma passāmī’ti| ‘āyatiṃ saṃvaraiyyāsī’ti|

‘‘idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina ca, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? saṅghasammukhatā, dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā…pai॰… aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ paṭiggāhakau  uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ; chandadāyakau khīyati, khīyanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

tiṇavatthārakaṃ

240. ‘‘siyā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ aikaṃ samathaṃ anāgamma – paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, dvīhi samathaihi sammaiyya – sammukhāvinayaina ca, tiṇavatthārakaina cāti? siyātissa vacanīyaṃ| yathā kathaṃ viya? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ hauti bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| tatra cai bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘amhākaṃ khau bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyyā’ti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aivarūpaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ tiṇavatthārakaina vūpasamaituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, vūpasamaitabbaṃ| sabbaihaiva  aikajjhaṃ san‍nipatitabbaṃ, san‍nipatitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ tiṇavatthārakaina vūpasamaiyya, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttanti| ‘‘aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina sakau pakkhau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇantu mai āyasmantā| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| yadāyasmantānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ yā caiva āyasmantānaṃ āpatti, yā ca attanau āpatti, āyasmantānañ‍caiva atthāya, attanau ca atthāya, saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisaiyyaṃ, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttanti|

241. ‘‘athāparaisaṃ  aikataupakkhikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina sakau pakkhau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇantu mai āyasmantā| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| yadāyasmantānaṃ pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ yā caiva āyasmantānaṃ āpatti, yā ca attanau āpatti, āyasmantānañ‍caiva atthāya, attanau ca atthāya, saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisaiyyaṃ, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttanti|

242. ‘‘athāparaisaṃ aikataupakkhikānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ yā caiva imaisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ āpatti, yā ca attanau āpatti, imaisañ‍caiva āyasmantānaṃ atthāya, attanau ca atthāya, saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisaiyyaṃ, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ viharataṃ bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ| sacai mayaṃ imāhi āpattīhi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kāraissāma, siyāpi taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷattāya vāḷattāya bhaidāya saṃvattaiyya| ahaṃ yā caiva imaisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ āpatti, yā ca attanau āpatti, imaisañ‍caiva āyasmantānaṃ atthāya, attanau ca atthāya, saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisaimi, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ| yassāyasmatau khamati amhākaṃ imāsaṃ āpattīnaṃ saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina daisanā, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘daisitā  amhākaṃ imā āpattiyau saṅghamajjhai tiṇavatthārakaina, ṭhapaitvā thul‍lavaj‍jaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gihippaṭisaṃyuttaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

‘‘athāparaisaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

‘‘idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ| kaina vūpasantaṃ? sammukhāvinayaina ca, tiṇavatthārakaina ca| kiñ‍ca tattha sammukhāvinayasmiṃ? saṅghasammukhatā, dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā|

‘‘kā ca tattha saṅghasammukhatā? yāvatikā bhikkhū kammappattā, tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā na paṭik‍kausanti – ayaṃ tattha saṅghasammukhatā|

‘‘kā  ca tattha dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā? yaina dhammaina yaina vinayaina yaina satthusāsanaina taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati – ayaṃ tattha dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā|

‘‘kā ca tattha puggalasammukhatā? yau ca daisaiti, yassa ca daisaiti, ubhau sammukhībhūtā haunti – ayaṃ tattha puggalasammukhatā|

‘‘kiñ‍ca tattha tiṇavatthārakasmiṃ? yā tiṇavatthārakassa kammassa kiriyā karaṇaṃ upagamanaṃ ajjhupagamanaṃ adhivāsanā appaṭik‍kausanā – idaṃ tattha tiṇavatthārakasmiṃ| aivaṃ vūpasantaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇaṃ paṭiggāhakau uk‍kauṭaiti, uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ; chandadāyakau khīyati, khīyanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

pari॰ 295, 307, 308 ‘‘kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ aikaina samathaina sammati – sammukhāvinayainā’’ti|

samathakkhandhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|



5. khuddakavatthukkhandhakaṃ

khuddakavatthūni

243. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nahāyamānā rukkhai kāyaṃ ugghaṃsainti, ūrumpi bāhumpi urampi piṭṭhimpi| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā nahāyamānā rukkhai kāyaṃ ugghaṃsaissanti, ūrumpi bāhumpi urampi piṭṭhimpi, saiyyathāpi mal‍lamuṭṭhikā gāmamauddavā’’ti gāmapauddavā (sī॰), gāmapūtavā (syā॰)! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nahāyamānā rukkhai kāyaṃ ugghaṃsainti, ūrumpi bāhumpi urampi piṭṭhimpī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ maughapurisānaṃ ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā nahāyamānā rukkhai kāyaṃ ugghaṃsaissanti, ūrumpi bāhumpi urampi piṭṭhimpi? naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, nahāyamānaina bhikkhunā rukkhai kāyau ugghaṃsaitabbau| yau ugghaṃsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nahāyamānā thambhai kāyaṃ ugghaṃsainti, ūrumpi bāhumpi urampi piṭṭhimpi| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā nahāyamānā thambhai kāyaṃ ugghaṃsaissanti, ūrumpi bāhumpi urampi piṭṭhimpi, saiyyathāpi mal‍lamuṭṭhikā gāmamauddavā’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ  khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, nahāyamānaina bhikkhunā thambhai kāyau ugghaṃsaitabbau| yau ugghaṃsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nahāyamānā kuṭṭai kuḍḍai (sī॰ syā॰) kāyaṃ ugghaṃsainti, ūrumpi bāhumpi urampi piṭṭhimpi| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā  nahāyamānā kuṭṭai kāyaṃ ugghaṃsaissanti, ūrumpi bāhumpi urampi piṭṭhimpi, saiyyathāpi mal‍lamuṭṭhikā gāmamauddavā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, nahāyamānaina bhikkhunā kuṭṭai kāyau ugghaṃsaitabbau| yau ugghaṃsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū aṭṭānai aṭṭhānai (sī॰ syā॰) nahāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, aṭṭānai nahāyitabbaṃ| yau nahāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū gandhabbahatthakaina nahāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, gandhabbahatthakaina nahāyitabbaṃ| yau nahāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kuruvindakasuttiyā nahāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi  gihī kāmabhauginauti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, kuruvindakasuttiyā nahāyitabbaṃ| yau nahāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū viggayha parikammaṃ kārāpainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, viggayha parikammaṃ kārāpaitabbaṃ| yau kārāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mal‍lakaina nahāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mal‍lakaina nahāyitabbaṃ| yau nahāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

244. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau kacchuraugābādhau hauti| na tassa vinā mal‍lakaina phāsu hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānassa akatamal‍laka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu jarādubbalau nahāyamānau na sak‍kauti attanau kāyaṃ ugghaṃsaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uk‍kāsika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū piṭṭhiparikammaṃ kātuṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, puthupāṇika’’nti|

245. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū val‍likaṃ dhārainti…pai॰… pāmaṅgaṃ dhārainti…pai॰… kaṇṭhasuttakaṃ dhārainti…pai॰… kaṭisuttakaṃ dhārainti…pai॰… auvaṭṭikaṃ dhārainti…pai॰… kāyuraṃ dhārainti…pai॰… hatthābharaṇaṃ dhārainti…pai॰… aṅgulimuddikaṃ dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū val‍likaṃ dhārainti…pai॰… pāmaṅgaṃ dhārainti, kaṇṭhasuttakaṃ dhārainti, kaṭisuttakaṃ dhārainti, auvaṭṭikaṃ dhārainti, kāyuraṃ dhārainti, hatthābharaṇaṃ dhārainti, aṅgulimuddikaṃ dhāraintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, val‍likā dhāraitabbā…pai॰… na pāmaṅgau dhāraitabbau… na kaṇṭhasuttakaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ… na  kaṭisuttakaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ… na auvaṭṭikaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ… na kāyuraṃ dhāraitabbaṃ… na hatthābharaṇaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ… na aṅgulimuddikā  dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

246. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā  bhikkhū dīghai kaisai dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, dīghā kaisā  dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dumāsikaṃ vā duvaṅgulaṃ vā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kaucchaina kaisai ausaṇṭhainti ausaṇhainti (sī॰ syā॰) …pai॰… phaṇakaina kaisai ausaṇṭhainti, hatthaphaṇakaina kaisai ausaṇṭhainti, sitthatailakaina kaisai ausaṇṭhainti, udakatailakaina kaisai ausaṇṭhainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, kaucchaina kaisā ausaṇṭhaitabbā…pai॰… na sitthatailakaina kaisā ausaṇṭhaitabbā… na udakatailakaina kaisā ausaṇṭhaitabbā| yau ausaṇṭhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

247. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ādāsaipi udakapattaipi mukhanimittaṃ aulaukainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, ādāsai vā udakapattai vā mukhanimittaṃ aulaukaitabbaṃ| yau aulaukaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau mukhai vaṇau hauti| sau bhikkhū pucchi – ‘‘kīdisau mai, āvusau, vaṇau’’ti? bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘aidisau tai, āvusau vaṇau’’ti| sau na saddahati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ābādhappac‍cayā ādāsai vā udakapattai vā mukhanimittaṃ aulaukaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mukhaṃ ālimpanti…pai॰… mukhaṃ ummaddainti, mukhaṃ cuṇṇainti, manausilikāya mukhaṃ lañchainti, aṅgarāgaṃ karaunti, mukharāgaṃ karaunti, aṅgarāgamukharāgaṃ karaunti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mukhaṃ ālimpitabbaṃ…pai॰… na mukhaṃ ummadditabbaṃ, na mukhaṃ cuṇṇaitabbaṃ, na manausilikāya mukhaṃ lañchaitabbaṃ, na aṅgarāgau kātabbau, na mukharāgau kātabbau, na aṅgarāgamukharāgau kātabbau| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau cakkhuraugābādhau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ābādhappac‍cayā mukhaṃ ālimpitu’’nti|

248. taina  khau pana samayaina rājagahai giraggasamaj‍jau samaj‍jā (abhidhānaganthaisu) hauti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū giraggasamaj‍jaṃ dassanāya agamaṃsu| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā nac‍campi gītampi vāditampi dassanāya  gacchissanti, saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginau’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, nac‍caṃ vā gītaṃ vā vāditaṃ vā dassanāya gantabbaṃ| yau gacchaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

249. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū āyatakaina gītassaraina dhammaṃ gāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘yathaiva yathā ca (ka॰) mayaṃ gāyāma, aivamaivimai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā āyatakaina gītassaraina dhammaṃ gāyantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū āyatakaina gītassaraina dhammaṃ gāyissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā āyatakaina gītassaraina dhammaṃ gāyantassa| attanāpi tasmiṃ sarai sāraj‍jati, paraipi tasmiṃ sarai sāraj‍janti, gahapatikāpi ujjhāyanti, sarakuttimpi nikāmayamānassa  samādhissa bhaṅgau hauti, pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā āyatakaina gītassaraina dhammaṃ gāyantassa| na, bhikkhavai, āyatakaina gītassaraina dhammau gāyitabbau| yau gāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū sarabhañ‍ñai kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sarabhañ‍ña’’nti|

‘‘taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bāhiralaumiṃ bāhiyalaumiṃ (ka॰) uṇṇiṃ dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bāhiyalaumi uṇṇi dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

250. taina khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa ārāmai ambā phalinau haunti| rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisāraina anuñ‍ñātaṃ hauti – ‘‘yathāsukhaṃ ayyā ambaṃ paribhuñ‍jantū’’ti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhū taruṇañ‍ñaiva ambaṃ pātāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jiṃsu| rañ‍ñau ca māgadhassa  sainiyassa bimbisārassa ambaina atthau hauti| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau manussai āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gacchatha, bhaṇai, ārāmaṃ gantvā ambaṃ āharathā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ daivā’’ti khau tai manussā rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa paṭissutvā ārāmaṃ gantvā ārāmapālaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘daivassa, bhaṇai, ambaina atthau, ambaṃ daithā’’ti| ‘‘natthāyyā ambaṃ| taruṇañ‍ñaiva ambaṃ pātāpaitvā bhikkhū paribhuñ‍jiṃsū’’ti| atha khau tai manussā rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘suparibhuttaṃ, bhaṇai, ayyaihi ambaṃ, api ca bhagavatā mattā vaṇṇitā’’ti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā na mattaṃ jānitvā rañ‍ñau ambaṃ paribhuñ‍jissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, ambaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa pūgassa saṅghabhattaṃ hauti| sūpai ambapaisikāyau pakkhittā haunti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nappaṭiggaṇhanti| ‘‘paṭiggaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, paribhuñ‍jatha| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ambapaisika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa pūgassa saṅghabhattaṃ hauti| tai na pariyāpuṇiṃsu ambapaisikaṃ kātuṃ, bhattaggai sakalaihaiva ambaihi dainti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nappaṭiggaṇhanti| ‘‘paṭiggaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, paribhuñ‍jatha| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahi samaṇakappaihi phalaṃ paribhuñ‍jituṃ – aggiparicitaṃ, satthaparicitaṃ, nakhaparicitaṃ, abījaṃ, nibbattabījañ‍ñaiva nibbaṭṭabījaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) pañ‍camaṃ| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imaihi pañ‍cahi samaṇakappaihi phalaṃ paribhuñ‍jitu’’nti|

251. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ahinā daṭṭhau kālaṅkatau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na hi nūna sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imāni cattāri ahirājakulāni maittaina cittaina phari| sacai hi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imāni cattāri ahirājakulāni maittaina cittaina pharaiyya, na hi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ahinā daṭṭhau kālaṅkaraiyya| katamāni cattāri ahirājakulāni? virūpakkhaṃ ahirājakulaṃ, airāpathaṃ ahirājakulaṃ, chabyāputtaṃ ahirājakulaṃ, kaṇhāgautamaṃ ahirājakulaṃ| na hi nūna sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imāni cattāri ahirājakulāni maittaina cittaina phari| sacai hi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imāni cattāri ahirājakulāni maittaina cittaina pharaiyya, na hi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ahinā daṭṭhau kālaṅkaraiyya| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imāni cattāri ahirājakulāni maittaina cittaina pharituṃ, attaguttiyā attarakkhāya attaparittaṃ kātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbaṃ –

jā॰ 1.2.105-106; a॰ ni॰ 4.67 idaṃ vatthu āgataṃ ‘‘virūpakkhaihi mai maittaṃ, maittaṃ airāpathaihi mai|

chabyāputtaihi mai maittaṃ, maittaṃ kaṇhāgautamakaihi ca||

‘‘apādakaihi  mai maittaṃ, maittaṃ dvipādakaihi mai|

catuppadaihi mai maittaṃ, maittaṃ bahuppadaihi mai||

‘‘mā maṃ apādakau hiṃsi, mā maṃ hiṃsi dvipādakau|

mā maṃ catuppadau hiṃsi, mā maṃ hiṃsi bahuppadau||

‘‘sabbai sattā sabbai pāṇā, sabbai bhūtā ca kaivalā|

sabbai bhadrāni passantu, mā kiñ‍ci pāpamāgamā||

‘‘appamāṇau buddhau, appamāṇau dhammau,

appamāṇau saṅghau, pamāṇavantāni sarīsapāni siriṃsapāni (sī॰ syā॰)||

‘‘ahi vicchikā satapadī, uṇṇanābhi sarabū mūsikā|

katā mai rakkhā kataṃ mai parittaṃ, paṭik‍kamantu bhūtāni||

‘‘sauhaṃ  namau bhagavatau, namau sattan‍naṃ sammāsambuddhāna’’nti||

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu anabhiratiyā pīḷitau attanau aṅgajātaṃ chindi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘añ‍ñamhi sau, bhikkhavai, maughapurisau chaitabbamhi, añ‍ñaṃ chindi| na, bhikkhavai, attanau aṅgajātaṃ chaitabbaṃ| yau chindaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

252. taina khau pana samayaina rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa mahagghassa candanassa candanasārassa (sī॰ syā॰) candanagaṇṭhi uppan‍nā hauti| atha khau rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa  aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ imāya candanagaṇṭhiyā pattaṃ laikhāpaiyyaṃ| laikhañ‍ca mai paribhaugaṃ bhavissati, pattañ‍ca dānaṃ dassāmī’’ti| atha khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhi tāya candanagaṇṭhiyā pattaṃ laikhāpaitvā sik‍kāya uḍḍitvā vāhitvā (sī॰) vaiḷaggai ālaggaitvā vaiḷuparamparāya bandhitvā vāhitvā (syā॰) aivamāha – ‘‘yau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā arahā caiva iddhimā ca din‍naṃyaiva pattaṃ auharatū’’ti| atha khau pūraṇau  kassapau yaina rājagahakau saiṭṭhi tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā rājagahakaṃ saiṭṭhiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahañhi, gahapati, arahā caiva iddhimā ca, daihi mai patta’’nti| ‘‘sacai, bhantai, āyasmā arahā caiva iddhimā ca din‍naṃyaiva pattaṃ auharatū’’ti| atha khau makkhali gausālau… ajitau kaisakambalau… pakudhau kac‍cāyanau… sañ‍cayau bailaṭṭhaputtau sañ‍jayau bail‍laṭṭhiputtau (sī॰) … nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau nāthaputtau (sī॰) yaina rājagahakau saiṭṭhi tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā rājagahakaṃ saiṭṭhiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahañhi, gahapati, arahā caiva iddhimā ca, daihi mai patta’’nti| ‘‘sacai, bhantai, āyasmā arahā caiva iddhimā ca, din‍naṃyaiva pattaṃ auharatū’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau āyasmā  ca piṇḍaulabhāradvājau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pavisiṃsu| atha khau āyasmā piṇḍaulabhāradvājau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmā khau mahāmauggal‍lānau arahā caiva iddhimā ca| gacchāvusau, mauggal‍lāna, aitaṃ pattaṃ auhara| tuyhaisau pattau’’ti| ‘‘āyasmā khau bhāradvājau arahā caiva iddhimā ca| gacchāvusau, bhāradvāja, aitaṃ pattaṃ auhara| tuyhaisau pattau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā piṇḍaulabhāradvājau vaihāsaṃ abbhuggantvā taṃ pattaṃ gahaitvā tikkhattuṃ rājagahaṃ anupariyāyi|

taina  khau pana samayaina rājagahakau saiṭṭhi saputtadārau sakai nivaisanai ṭhitau hauti pañ‍jalikau  namassamānau – idhaiva, bhantai, ayyau bhāradvājau amhākaṃ nivaisanai patiṭṭhātūti| atha khau āyasmā piṇḍaulabhāradvājau rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa nivaisanai patiṭṭhāsi| atha  khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhi āyasmatau piṇḍaulabhāradvājassa hatthatau pattaṃ gahaitvā mahagghassa khādanīyassa pūraitvā āyasmatau piṇḍaulabhāradvājassa adāsi| atha khau āyasmā piṇḍaulabhāradvājau taṃ pattaṃ gahaitvā ārāmaṃ agamāsi| assausuṃ khau manussā – ayyaina kira piṇḍaulabhāradvājaina rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa pattau auhāritauti| tai ca manussā uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā āyasmantaṃ piṇḍaulabhāradvājaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhiṃsu|

assaisi khau bhagavā uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ; sutvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau sau, ānanda, uc‍cāsaddau mahāsaddau’’ti? ‘‘āyasmatā, bhantai, piṇḍaulabhāradvājaina rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa pattau auhāritau| assausuṃ khau, bhantai, manussā – ayyaina kira piṇḍaulabhāradvājaina rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa pattau auhāritauti| tai ca, bhantai, manussā uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā āyasmantaṃ piṇḍaulabhāradvājaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhā| sau aisau, bhantai, bhagavā uc‍cāsaddau mahāsaddau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ  piṇḍaulabhāradvājaṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tayā, bhāradvāja, rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa pattau auhāritau’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ  bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, bhāradvāja, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, bhāradvāja, chavassa dārupattassa kāraṇā gihīnaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaissasi! saiyyathāpi, bhāradvāja, mātugāmau chavassa māsakarūpassa kāraṇā kaupinaṃ dassaiti, aivamaiva khau tayā, bhāradvāja, chavassa dārupattassa kāraṇā gihīnaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassitaṃ| naitaṃ, bhāradvāja, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, gihīnaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaitabbaṃ| yau dassaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| bhindathaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, dārupattaṃ sakalikaṃ sakalikaṃ katvā, bhikkhūnaṃ añ‍janupapisanaṃ daitha| na ca, bhikkhavai, dārupattau dhāraitabbau| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacai pattai dhārainti sauvaṇṇamayaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, sauvaṇṇamayau pattau dhāraitabbau…pai॰… na rūpiyamayau  pattau dhāraitabbau… na maṇimayau pattau dhāraitabbau… na vaiḷuriyamayau pattau dhāraitabbau… na phalikamayau pattau dhāraitabbau… na kaṃsamayau pattau dhāraitabbau… na  kācamayau pattau dhāraitabbau… na tipumayau pattau dhāraitabbau… na sīsamayau pattau dhāraitabbau… na tambalauhamayau pattau dhāraitabbau| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dvai pattai – ayaupattaṃ, mattikāpatta’’nti|

253. taina khau pana samayaina pattamūlaṃ ghaṃsiyati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pattamaṇḍala’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacāni pattamaṇḍalāni dhārainti sauvaṇṇamayaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāvacāni pattamaṇḍalāni dhāraitabbāni| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dvai pattamaṇḍalāni – tipumayaṃ, sīsamaya’’nti| bahalāni maṇḍalāni na acchupiyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, likhitu’’nti| valī calī (ka॰) haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, makaradantakaṃ chinditu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū citrāni pattamaṇḍalāni dhārainti rūpakākiṇṇāni bhittikammakatāni| tāni rathikāyapi dassaintā āhiṇḍanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, citrāni pattamaṇḍalāni dhāraitabbāni rūpakākiṇṇāni bhittikammakatāni| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pakatimaṇḍala’’nti|

254. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū saudakaṃ pattaṃ paṭisāmainti| pattau dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, saudakau pattau paṭisāmaitabbau| yau paṭisāmaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, autāpaitvā pattaṃ paṭisāmaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū saudakaṃ saudakaṃ (ka॰) pattaṃ autāpainti| pattau duggandhau hauti| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, saudakau pattau autāpaitabbau| yau autāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vaudakaṃ katvā autāpaitvā pattaṃ paṭisāmaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū uṇhai pattaṃ nidahanti| pattassa vaṇṇau dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, uṇhai pattau nidahitabbau| yau nidahaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, muhuttaṃ uṇhai autāpaitvā pattaṃ paṭisāmaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā pattā ajjhaukāsai anādhārā nikkhittā haunti| vātamaṇḍalikāya āvaṭṭaitvā pattā bhij‍jiṃsu| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pattādhāraka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū miḍḍhantai pattaṃ nikkhipanti| paripatitvā parivaṭṭitvā (syā॰) pattau bhij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, miḍḍhantai pattau nikkhipitabbau| yau nikkhipaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū paribhaṇḍantai pattaṃ nikkhipanti| paripatitvā pattau bhij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, paribhaṇḍantai pattau nikkhipitabbau| yau nikkhipaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū chamāya pattaṃ nik‍kuj‍janti| auṭṭhau ghaṃsiyati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tiṇasanthāraka’’nti| tiṇasanthārakau upacikāhi khaj‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cauḷaka’’nti| cauḷakaṃ upacikāhi khaj‍jati| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pattamāḷaka’’nti| pattamāḷakau paripatitvā pattau bhij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pattakuṇḍaulika’’nti| pattakuṇḍaulikāya pattau ghaṃsiyati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pattathavika’’nti| aṃsabaddhakau na hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṃsabaddhakaṃ bandhanasuttaka’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū bhittikhilaipi nāgadantakaipi pattaṃ laggainti| paripatitvā pattau bhij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pattau laggaitabbau| yau laggaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū mañ‍cai pattaṃ nikkhipanti, satisammausā nisīdantā auttharitvā pattaṃ bhindainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mañ‍cai pattau nikkhipitabbau| yau nikkhipaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū pīṭhai pattaṃ nikkhipanti, satisammausā nisīdantā auttharitvā pattaṃ bhindainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pīṭhai pattau nikkhipitabbau| yau nikkhipaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū aṅkai pattaṃ nikkhipanti, satisammausā uṭṭhahanti| paripatitvā pattau bhij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, aṅkai pattau nikkhipitabbau| yau nikkhipaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū chattai pattaṃ nikkhipanti| vātamaṇḍalikāya chattaṃ ukkhipiyati paripatitvā pattau bhij‍jati| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, chattai pattau nikkhipitabbau| yau nikkhipaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

255. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū pattahatthā kavāṭaṃ paṇāmainti| kavāṭau āvaṭṭitvā pattau bhij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pattahatthaina kavāṭaṃ paṇāmaitabbaṃ kavāṭau paṇāmaitabbau (ka॰)| yau paṇāmaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū tumbakaṭāhai piṇḍāya caranti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi titthiyāti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, tumbakaṭāhai piṇḍāya caritabbaṃ| yau caraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ghaṭikaṭāhai  ghaṭikaṭāhaina (syā॰) piṇḍāya caranti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti  vipācainti – saiyyathāpi titthiyāti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, ghaṭikaṭāhai piṇḍāya caritabbaṃ| yau caraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sabbapaṃsukūlikau hauti| sau chavasīsassa pattaṃ dhāraiti| añ‍ñatarā itthī passitvā  bhītā vissaramakāsi – ‘‘abhuṃ mai pisācau vatāya’’nti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā chavasīsassa pattaṃ dhāraissanti, saiyyathāpi pisācil‍likā’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, chavasīsassa pattau dhāraitabbau| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, sabbapaṃsukūlikaina bhavitabbaṃ| yau bhavaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū calakānipi aṭṭhikānipi ucchiṭṭhaudakampi pattaina nīharanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘yasmiṃ yaivimai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā bhuñ‍janti sauva naisaṃ paṭiggahau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na bhikkhavai, calakāni vā aṭṭhikāni vā ucchiṭṭhaudakaṃ vā pattaina nīharitabbaṃ| yau nīharaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paṭiggaha’’nti|

256. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū hatthaina vipphāḷaitvā cīvaraṃ sibbainti| cīvaraṃ vilaumikaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, satthakaṃ namataka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa daṇḍasatthakaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, daṇḍasatthaka’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacai satthakadaṇḍai dhārainti sauvaṇṇamayaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāvacā satthakadaṇḍā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhimayaṃ dantamayaṃ visāṇamayaṃ naḷamayaṃ vaiḷumayaṃ kaṭṭhamayaṃ jatumayaṃ phalamayaṃ lauhamayaṃ saṅkhanābhimaya’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū kuk‍kuṭapattainapi vaiḷupaisikāyapi cīvaraṃ sibbainti| cīvaraṃ  dussibbitaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sūci’’nti| sūciyau kaṇṇakitāyau haunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sūcināḷika’’nti| sūcināḷikāyapi kaṇṇakitāyau haunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kiṇṇaina pūraitu’’nti| kiṇṇaipi kaṇṇakitāyau haunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sattuyā pūraitu’’nti| sattuyāpi kaṇṇakitāyau haunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saritaka’’nti| saritakaipi kaṇṇakitāyau haunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, madhusitthakaina sāraitu’’nti| saritakaṃ paribhij‍jati| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saritakasipāṭika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū tattha tattha khilaṃ nikkhaṇitvā sambandhitvā  cīvaraṃ sibbainti| cīvaraṃ vikaṇṇaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kathinaṃ kathinaraj‍juṃ kaṭhinaṃ kaṭhinaraj‍juṃ (sī॰ syā॰) tattha tattha aubandhitvā cīvaraṃ sibbaitu’’nti| visamai kathinaṃ pattharanti| kathinaṃ paribhij‍jati…pai॰… ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, visamai kathinaṃ pattharitabbaṃ| yau pattharaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

chamāya kathinaṃ pattharanti| kathinaṃ paṃsukitaṃ hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tiṇasanthāraka’’nti| kathinassa antau jīrati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, anuvātaṃ paribhaṇḍaṃ āraupaitu’’nti| kathinaṃ nappahauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, daṇḍakathinaṃ bidalakaṃ salākaṃ vinandhanaraj‍juṃ vinandhanasuttaṃ vinandhitvā cīvaraṃ sibbaitu’’nti| suttantarikāyau visamā haunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kaḷimbhaka’’nti| suttā vaṅkā haunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, maughasuttaka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū adhautaihi pādaihi kathinaṃ ak‍kamanti| kathinaṃ dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, adhautaihi pādaihi kathinaṃ ak‍kamitabbaṃ| yau ak‍kamaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū al‍laihi pādaihi kathinaṃ ak‍kamanti| kathinaṃ dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, al‍laihi pādaihi kathinaṃ ak‍kamitabbaṃ| yau ak‍kamaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū saupāhanā kathinaṃ ak‍kamanti| kathinaṃ dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, saupāhanaina kathinaṃ ak‍kamitabbaṃ| yau ak‍kamaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū cīvaraṃ sibbantā aṅguliyā paṭiggaṇhanti| aṅguliyau dukkhā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paṭiggaha’’nti|

257. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacai paṭiggahai dhārainti sauvaṇṇamayaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ| manussā  ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāvacā paṭiggahā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhimayaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhanābhimaya’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina sūciyaupi satthakāpi paṭiggahāpi nassanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āvaisanavitthaka’’nti| āvaisanavitthakai samākulā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paṭiggahathavika’’nti| aṃsabaddhakau na hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṃsabaddhakaṃ bandhanasuttaka’’nti|
