taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū abbhaukāsai cīvaraṃ sibbantā sītainapi uṇhainapi kilamanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kathinasālaṃ kathinamaṇḍapa’’nti| kathinasālā nīcavatthukā  hauti, udakaina autthariyyati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| cayau paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau saupānai – iṭṭhakāsaupānaṃ, silāsaupānaṃ, dārusaupāna’’nti| ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti| kathinasālāya tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā augumbaitvā (sī॰ syā॰) ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ – saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭikaṃ cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍juka’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū cīvaraṃ sibbaitvā tatthaiva kathinaṃ ujjhitvā pak‍kamanti, undūraihipi upacikāhipi khaj‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kathinaṃ saṅgharitu’’nti| kathinaṃ paribhij‍jati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gaughaṃsikāya kathinaṃ saṅgharitu’’nti| kathinaṃ vinivaiṭhiyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bandhanaraj‍ju’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū kuṭṭaipi thambhaipi kathinaṃ ussāpaitvā pak‍kamanti| paripatitvā kathinaṃ bhij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhittikhilai vā nāgadantai vā laggaitu’’nti|

258. atha khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina vaisālī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| taina khau pana samayaina  bhikkhū sūcikampi satthakampi bhaisaj‍jampi pattaina ādāya gacchanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhaisaj‍jatthavika’’nti| aṃsabaddhakau na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṃsabaddhakaṃ bandhanasuttaka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu upāhanāyau kāyabandhanaina bandhitvā gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| añ‍ñatarau upāsakau taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaintau upāhanāyau sīsaina ghaṭṭaiti| sau bhikkhu maṅku ahausi| atha khau sau bhikkhu ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upāhanatthavika’’nti| aṃsabaddhakau na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṃsabaddhakaṃ bandhanasuttaka’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina antarāmaggai udakaṃ akappiyaṃ hauti| parissāvanaṃ na hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parissāvana’’nti| cauḷakaṃ nappahauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kaṭacchuparissāvana’’nti| cauḷakaṃ nappahauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dhammakaraṇa’’nti dhammakaraṇaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), dhamakaraṇaṃ (ka॰)|

259. taina khau pana samayaina dvai bhikkhū kausalaisu janapadai addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nā haunti| aikau bhikkhu anācāraṃ ācarati| dutiyau bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā, āvusau, aivarūpaṃ akāsi| naitaṃ kappatī’’ti| sau tasmiṃ upanandhi| atha khau sau bhikkhu pipāsāya  pīḷitau upanaddhaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘daihi mai, āvusau, parissāvanaṃ, pānīyaṃ pivissāmī’’ti| upanaddhau bhikkhu na adāsi| sau bhikkhu pipāsāya pīḷitau kālamakāsi| atha khau sau bhikkhu ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, āvusau, parissāvanaṃ yāciyamānau na adāsī’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu parissāvanaṃ yāciyamānau na dassatī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhikkhu, parissāvanaṃ yāciyamānau na adāsī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā – ‘‘ananucchavikaṃ, maughapurisa, ananulaumikaṃ appatirūpaṃ assāmaṇakaṃ  akappiyaṃ akaraṇīyaṃ| kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, parissāvanaṃ yāciyamānau na dassasi| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na bhikkhavai, addhānamaggappaṭipan‍naina bhikkhunā parissāvanaṃ yāciyamānaina na dātabbaṃ| yau na dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na ca, bhikkhavai, aparissāvanakaina addhānau paṭipaj‍jitabbau| yau paṭipaj‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sacai na hauti parissāvanaṃ vā dhammakaraṇau vā, saṅghāṭikaṇṇaupi adhiṭṭhātabbau – iminā parissāvaitvā pivissāmī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina vaisālī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū navakammaṃ karaunti| parissāvanaṃ na sammati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, daṇḍaparissāvana’’nti| daṇḍaparissāvanaṃ na sammati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, auttharaka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū makasaihi ubbāḷhā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, makasakuṭika’’nti|

260. taina khau pana samayaina vaisāliyaṃ paṇītānaṃ bhattānaṃ bhattapaṭipāṭi aṭṭhitā hauti| bhikkhū paṇītāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā abhisan‍nakāyā  haunti bahvābādhā bavhābādhā (sī॰)| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau vaisāliṃ agamāsi kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| addasā khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhikkhū abhisan‍nakāyai bahvābādhai| disvāna yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitarahi, bhantai, bhikkhū abhisan‍nakāyā bahvābādhā| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ caṅkamañ‍ca jantāgharañ‍ca anujānātu| aivaṃ bhikkhū appābādhā bhavissantī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau jīvakau kaumārabhac‍cau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, caṅkamañ‍ca jantāgharañ‍cā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū  visamai caṅkamai caṅkamanti| pādā dukkhā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, samaṃ kātu’’nti| caṅkamau nīcavatthukau hauti| udakaina autthariyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| cayau  paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau saupānai – iṭṭhakāsaupānaṃ, silāsaupānaṃ, dārusaupāna’’nti| ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū caṅkamai caṅkamantā paripatanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, caṅkamanavaidika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ajjhaukāsai caṅkamantā sītainapi uṇhainapi kilamanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, caṅkamanasāla’’nti| caṅkamanasālāyaṃ tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ – saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭikaṃ cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍ju’’nti| jantāgharaṃ nīcavatthukaṃ hauti, udakaina autthariyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| cayau paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau saupānai – iṭṭhakāsaupānaṃ, silāsaupānaṃ, dārusaupāna’’nti| ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti| jantāgharassa kavāṭaṃ na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kavāṭaṃ piṭṭhasaṅghāṭaṃ pīṭhasaṃghāṭaṃ (ka॰) udukkhalikaṃ uttarapāsakaṃ aggaḷavaṭṭikaṃ kapisīsakaṃ sūcikaṃ ghaṭikaṃ tāḷacchiddaṃ āviñchanachiddaṃ āviñchanaraj‍ju’’nti| jantāgharassa kuṭṭapādau jīrati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, maṇḍalikaṃ kātu’’nti| jantāgharassa dhūmanaittaṃ na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dhūmanaitta’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū khuddakai jantāgharai majjhai aggiṭṭhānaṃ karaunti| upacārau na hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, khuddakai jantāgharai aikamantaṃ aggiṭṭhānaṃ kātuṃ, mahal‍lakai majjhai’’ti| jantāgharai aggi mukhaṃ ḍahati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mukhamattika’’nti| hatthai mattikaṃ taimainti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mattikādauṇika’’nti| mattikā duggandhā hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vāsaitu’’nti| jantāgharai aggi kāyaṃ ḍahati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakaṃ atiharitu’’nti| pātiyāpi pattainapi udakaṃ atiharanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakaṭṭhānaṃ, udakasarāvaka’’nti| jantāgharaṃ tiṇacchadanaṃ na saidaiti tiṇacchādanaina chādaiti (ka॰) …pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātu’’nti| jantāgharaṃ  cikkhal‍laṃ hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, santharituṃ tayau santharai – iṭṭhakāsantharaṃ, silāsantharaṃ, dārusanthara’’nti| cikkhal‍laṃyaiva hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dhauvitu’’nti| udakaṃ santiṭṭhati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakaniddhamana’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū jantāgharai chamāya  nisīdanti, gattāni kaṇḍūvanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, jantāgharapīṭha’’nti| taina khau pana samayaina jantāgharaṃ aparikkhittaṃ hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parikkhipituṃ tayau pākārai – iṭṭhakāpākāraṃ, silāpākāraṃ, dārupākāra’’nti| kauṭṭhakau na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kauṭṭhaka’’nti| kauṭṭhakau nīcavatthukau hauti, udakaina autthariyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| cayau paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau saupānai – iṭṭhakāsaupānaṃ, silāsaupānaṃ, dārusaupāna’’nti| ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti| kauṭṭhakassa kavāṭaṃ na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kavāṭaṃ piṭṭhasaṅghāṭaṃ udukkhalikaṃ uttarapāsakaṃ aggaḷavaṭṭiṃ kapisīsakaṃ sūcikaṃ ghaṭikaṃ tāḷacchiddaṃ āviñchanachiddaṃ āviñchanaraj‍ju’’nti| kauṭṭhakai tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ – saitavaṇṇaṃ  kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭikanti| parivaiṇaṃ cikkhal‍laṃ hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, marumbaṃ upakiritu’’nti| na pariyāpuṇanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, padarasilaṃ nikkhipitu’’nti| udakaṃ santiṭṭhati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakaniddhamana’’nti|

261. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū naggā naggaṃ abhivādainti…pai॰… naggā naggaṃ abhivādāpainti, naggā naggassa parikammaṃ karaunti, naggā naggassa parikammaṃ kārāpainti, naggā naggassa dainti, naggā paṭiggaṇhanti, naggā khādanti, naggā bhuñ‍janti, naggā sāyanti, naggā pivanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, naggaina idaṃ padaṃ katthaci natthi naggau abhivādaitabbau…pai॰… na naggaina abhivādaitabbaṃ… na naggaina idaṃ padaṃ katthaci natthi naggau abhivādāpaitabbau… na naggaina abhivādāpaitabbaṃ… na naggaina naggassa parikammaṃ  kātabbaṃ… na naggaina naggassa parikammaṃ kārāpaitabbaṃ… na naggaina naggassa dātabbaṃ… na naggaina paṭiggahaitabbaṃ… na naggaina khāditabbaṃ… na naggaina bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ… na  naggaina sāyitabbaṃ… na naggaina pātabbaṃ| yau pivaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū jantāgharai chamāya cīvaraṃ nikkhipanti| cīvaraṃ paṃsukitaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍ju’’nti| daivai vassantai cīvaraṃ auvassati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, jantāgharasāla’’nti| jantāgharasālā nīcavatthukā hauti, udakaina autthariyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| cayau paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ…pai॰… ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti| jantāgharasālāya tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ…pai॰… cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍ju’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū jantāgharaipi udakaipi parikammaṃ kātuṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tissau paṭicchādiyau – jantāgharapaṭicchādiṃ, udakapaṭicchādiṃ, vatthapaṭicchādi’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina jantāgharai udakaṃ na hauti| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udapāna’’nti| udapānassa kūlaṃ luj‍jati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| udapānau nīcavatthukau hauti, udakaina autthariyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| cayau paripatati…pai॰… ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti|

262. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū val‍likāyapi kāyabandhanainapi udakaṃ vāhainti vāhanti (ka॰)| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakavāhanaraj‍ju’’nti| hatthā dukkhā haunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tulaṃ karakaṭakaṃ cak‍kavaṭṭaka’’nti| bhājanā bahū bhij‍janti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau vārakai – lauhavārakaṃ, dāruvārakaṃ, cammakkhaṇḍa’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ajjhaukāsai udakaṃ vāhaintā sītainapi uṇhainapi kilamanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udapānasāla’’nti| udapānasālāya tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ – saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭikaṃ cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍ju’’nti| udapānau apārutau hauti, tiṇacuṇṇaihipi  paṃsukaihipi aukiriyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, apidhāna’’nti| udakabhājanaṃ na saṃvij‍jati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakadauṇiṃ udakakaṭāha’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ārāmai tahaṃ tahaṃ nahāyanti| ārāmau cikkhal‍lau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, candanika’’nti| candanikā pākaṭā hauti| bhikkhū hiriyanti nahāyituṃ…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parikkhipituṃ tayau pākārai – iṭṭhakāpākāraṃ, silāpākāraṃ, dārupākāra’’nti| candanikā cikkhal‍lā hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, santharituṃ tayau santharai – iṭṭhakāsantharaṃ, silāsantharaṃ, dārusanthara’’nti| udakaṃ santiṭṭhati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakaniddhamana’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ gattāni sītigatāni haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakapuñchaniṃ cauḷakainapi pac‍cuddharitu’’nti|

263. taina  khau pana samayaina  añ‍ñatarau upāsakau saṅghassa atthāya paukkharaṇiṃ kāraitukāmau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paukkharaṇi’’nti| paukkharaṇiyā kūlaṃ  luj‍jati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau saupānai – iṭṭhakāsaupānaṃ, silāsaupānaṃ, dārusaupāna’’nti| ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti| paukkharaṇiyā udakaṃ purāṇaṃ hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakamātikaṃ udakaniddhamana’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghassa atthāya nil‍laikhaṃ jantāgharaṃ kattukāmau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, nil‍laikhaṃ jantāghara’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū cātumāsaṃ nisīdanaina vippavasanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, cātumāsaṃ nisīdanaina vippavasitabbaṃ| yau vippavasaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

264. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū pupphābhikiṇṇaisu sayanaisu sayanti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, pupphābhikiṇṇaisu sayanaisu sayitabbaṃ| yau sayaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina manussā gandhampi mālampi ādāya ārāmaṃ āgacchanti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na paṭiggaṇhanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gandhaṃ gahaitvā kavāṭai pañ‍caṅgulikaṃ dātuṃ, pupphaṃ gahaitvā vihārai aikamantaṃ nikkhipitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa namatakaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, namataka’’nti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘namatakaṃ adhiṭṭhātabbaṃ nu khau udāhu vikappaitabba’’nti…pai॰… ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, namatakaṃ adhiṭṭhātabbaṃ, na vikappaitabba’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū āsittakūpadhānai bhuñ‍janti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, āsittakūpadhānai bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau bhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| sau bhuñ‍jamānau na sak‍kauti hatthaina pattaṃ sandhāraituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, maḷaurika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū aikabhājanaipi bhuñ‍janti…pai॰…  aikathālakaipi pivanti, aikamañ‍caipi tuvaṭṭainti, aikattharaṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti, aikapāvuraṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti, aikattharaṇapāvuraṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti| manussā ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, aikabhājanai bhuñ‍jitabbaṃ…pai॰… na aikathālakai pātabbaṃ… na aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭitabbaṃ… na aikattharaṇā na aikattharaṇai tuvaṭṭitabbaṃ… na aikapāvuraṇā na aikapāvuraṇai tuvaṭṭitabbaṃ… na aikattharaṇapāvuraṇā na aikattharaṇapāvuraṇai (syā॰) tuvaṭṭitabbaṃ| yau tuvaṭṭaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

265. taina khau pana samayaina vaḍḍhau licchavī maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sahāyau hauti| atha khau vaḍḍhau licchavī yaina maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘vandāmi ayyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū nālapiṃsu| dutiyampi khau vaḍḍhau licchavī maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘vandāmi ayyā’’ti| dutiyampi khau maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū nālapiṃsu| tatiyampi khau vaḍḍhau licchavī maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘vandāmi ayyā’’ti| tatiyampi khau maittiyabhūmajakā  bhikkhū nālapiṃsu| ‘‘kyāhaṃ ayyānaṃ aparajjhāmi, kissa maṃ ayyā nālapantī’’ti? ‘‘tathā hi pana tvaṃ, āvusau vaḍḍha, amhai dabbaina mal‍laputtaina vihaiṭhiyamānai ajjhupaikkhasī’’ti| ‘‘kyāhaṃ ayyā karaumī’’ti? ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, āvusau vaḍḍha, icchaiyyāsi, aj‍jaiva bhagavā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ nāsāpaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘kyāhaṃ ayyā karaumi, kiṃ mayā sak‍kā kātu’’nti? ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, āvusau vaḍḍha, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkama, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, nacchan‍naṃ nappatirūpaṃ| yāyaṃ, bhantai, disā abhayā anītikā anupaddavā sāyaṃ disā sabhayā saītikā saupaddavā; yatau nivātaṃ tatau savātaṃ; udakaṃ mañ‍ñai ādittaṃ; ayyaina mai dabbaina mal‍laputtaina pajāpati dūsitā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ ayyā’’ti khau vaḍḍhau licchavī maittiyabhūmajakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vaḍḍhau licchavī  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, nacchan‍naṃ nappatirūpaṃ| yāyaṃ, bhantai, disā abhayā anītikā anupaddavā sāyaṃ disā sabhayā saītikā saupaddavā; yatau nivātaṃ tatau savātaṃ; udakaṃ mañ‍ñai ādittaṃ; ayyaina mai dabbaina mal‍laputtaina pajāpati dūsitā’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘sarasi  tvaṃ, dabba, aivarūpaṃ kattā yathāyaṃ vaḍḍhau āhā’’ti? ‘‘yathā maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā jānātī’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā…pai॰… tatiyampi khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sarasi tvaṃ, dabba, aivarūpaṃ kattā yathāyaṃ vaḍḍhau āhā’’ti? ‘‘yathā maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā jānātī’’ti| ‘‘na khau, dabba, dabbā aivaṃ nibbaiṭhainti| sacai tayā kataṃ, katanti vadaihi; sacai akataṃ, akatanti vadaihī’’ti| ‘‘yatau ahaṃ, bhantai, jātau nābhijānāmi supinantainapi maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivitā, pagaiva jāgarau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattaṃ nik‍kuj‍jatu, asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina karautu|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa upāsakassa pattau nik‍kuj‍jitabbau – bhikkhūnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhūnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhūnaṃ avāsāya anāvāsāya (syā॰) parisak‍kati, bhikkhū ak‍kausati paribhāsati, bhikkhū bhikkhūhi bhaidaiti, buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imaihi aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa upāsakassa pattaṃ nik‍kuj‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, nik‍kuj‍jitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā  paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

266. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| vaḍḍhau licchavī āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattaṃ nik‍kuj‍jaiyya, asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina karaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| vaḍḍhau licchavī āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti| saṅghau vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattaṃ nik‍kuj‍jati, asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina karauti| yassāyasmatau khamati vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattassa nik‍kuj‍janā, asambhaugaṃ saṅghaina karaṇaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘nik‍kuj‍jitau saṅghaina vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattau, asambhaugau saṅghaina| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina vaḍḍhassa licchavissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā vaḍḍhaṃ licchaviṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saṅghaina tai, āvusau vaḍḍha, pattau nik‍kuj‍jitau| asambhaugausi saṅghainā’’ti| atha  khau vaḍḍhau licchavī – saṅghaina kira mai pattau nik‍kuj‍jitau, asambhaugaumhi kira saṅghaināti – tatthaiva mucchitau papatau| atha khau vaḍḍhassa licchavissa  mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā vaḍḍhaṃ licchaviṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau vaḍḍha, mā sauci, mā paridaivi| mayaṃ bhagavantaṃ pasādaissāma bhikkhusaṅghañ‍cā’’ti|

atha khau vaḍḍhau licchavī saputtadārau samittāmac‍cau sañātisālauhitau al‍lavatthau al‍lakaisau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ac‍cayau maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yauhaṃ ayyaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaisiṃ| tassa mai, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti| ‘‘taggha tvaṃ, āvusau vaḍḍha, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaisi| yatau ca khau tvaṃ, āvusau vaḍḍha, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarausi, taṃ tai mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| vuḍḍhihaisā, āvusau vaḍḍha, ariyassa vinayai yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti, āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattaṃ uk‍kuj‍jatu, sambhaugaṃ saṅghaina karautu|

‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa upāsakassa pattau uk‍kuj‍jitabbau – na bhikkhūnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, na bhikkhūnaṃ  anatthāya parisak‍kati, na bhikkhūnaṃ avāsāya parisak‍kati, na bhikkhū ak‍kausati paribhāsati, na bhikkhū bhikkhūhi bhaidaiti, na buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, na dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, na saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, imaihi aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa upāsakassa pattaṃ uk‍kuj‍jituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, uk‍kuj‍jitabbau| taina, bhikkhavai, vaḍḍhaina licchavinā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘saṅghaina mai, bhantai, pattau  nik‍kuj‍jitau, asambhaugaumhi saṅghaina| sauhaṃ, bhantai, sammā vattāmi, laumaṃ pātaimi, naitthāraṃ vattāmi, saṅghaṃ pattuk‍kuj‍janaṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbau| tatiyampi yācitabbau| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau  ñāpaitabbau –

267. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghaina vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattau nik‍kuj‍jitau, asambhaugau saṅghaina| sau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, saṅghaṃ pattuk‍kuj‍janaṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattaṃ uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, sambhaugaṃ saṅghaina karaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghaina vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattau nik‍kuj‍jitau, asambhaugau saṅghaina| sau sammā vattati, laumaṃ pātaiti, naitthāraṃ vattati, saṅghaṃ pattuk‍kuj‍janaṃ yācati| saṅghau vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattaṃ uk‍kuj‍jati, sambhaugaṃ saṅghaina karauti| yassāyasmatau khamati vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattassa uk‍kuj‍janā, sambhaugaṃ saṅghaina karaṇaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘uk‍kuj‍jitau saṅghaina vaḍḍhassa licchavissa pattau, sambhaugau saṅghaina| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

268. atha khau bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina bhaggā taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina bhaggā tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai suṃsumāragirai (sī॰ syā॰), saṃsumāragirai (ka॰) bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina baudhissa rājakumārassa kaukanadau kaukanudau (ka॰) nāma pāsādau acirakāritau hauti, anajjhāvutthau samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā kainaci vā manussabhūtaina| atha khau baudhi rājakumārau sañ‍jikāputtaṃ māṇavaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, samma sañ‍cikāputta, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkama, upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vanda; appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ puccha – ‘baudhi, bhantai, rājakumārau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ  lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ  pucchatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘adhivāsaitu kira, bhantai, bhagavā baudhissa rājakumārassa svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhau’’ti khau sañ‍cikāputtau māṇavau baudhissa rājakumārassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sañ‍cikāputtau māṇavau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘baudhi khau rājakumārau bhautau gautamassa pādai sirasā vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchati| aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘adhivāsaitu kira bhavaṃ gautamau baudhissa rājakumārassa svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau sañ‍cikāputtau māṇavau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina baudhi rājakumārau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā  baudhiṃ rājakumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘avaucumha khau mayaṃ bhautau vacanaina taṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ – ‘baudhi khau rājakumārau bhautau gautamassa pādai sirasā vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchati| aivañ‍ca vadaiti – adhivāsaitu kira bhavaṃ gautamau baudhissa rājakumārassa svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’ti| adhivutthañ‍ca pana samaṇaina gautamainā’’ti|

atha khau baudhi rājakumārau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaṃ  khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā, kaukanadañ‍ca pāsādaṃ audātaihi dussaihi santharāpaitvā yāva pacchimasaupānakaḷaivarā, sañ‍cikāputtaṃ māṇavaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, samma sañ‍cikāputta, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkama, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucaihi – ‘kālau, bhantai niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhau’’ti khau sañ‍cikāputtau māṇavau baudhissa rājakumārassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina baudhissa rājakumārassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina baudhi rājakumārau bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitau hauti, bhagavantaṃ āgamayamānau| addasā khau baudhi rājakumārau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva  āgacchantaṃ| disvāna tatau pac‍cuggantvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā puraikkhatvā yaina kaukanadau pāsādau tainupasaṅkami| atha khau bhagavā pacchimasaupānakaḷaivaraṃ nissāya aṭṭhāsi| atha khau baudhi rājakumārau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ak‍kamatu, bhantai, bhagavā dussāni, ak‍kamatu sugatau dussāni, yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai  bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau baudhi rājakumārau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ak‍kamatu, bhantai, bhagavā dussāni, ak‍kamatu  sugatau dussāni, yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ apalaukaisi| atha khau āyasmā ānandau baudhiṃ rājakumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saṃharantu, rājakumāra, dussāni| na bhagavā cailapaṭikaṃ cailapattikaṃ (sī॰) ak‍kamissati pacchimaṃ janataṃ tathāgatau anukampatī’’ti|

atha khau baudhi rājakumārau dussāni saṃharāpaitvā uparikaukanadai pāsādai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi| atha khau bhagavā kaukanadaṃ pāsādaṃ abhiruhitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau baudhi rājakumārau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā, bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ, aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau baudhiṃ rājakumāraṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā  samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, cailapaṭikā ak‍kamitabbā| yau ak‍kamaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī apagatagabbhā bhikkhū nimantaitvā dussaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘ak‍kamatha, bhantai, dussa’’nti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na ak‍kamanti| ‘‘ak‍kamatha, bhantai, dussaṃ maṅgalatthāyā’’ti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na ak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau sā itthī ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyā maṅgalatthāya yāciyamānā cailappaṭikaṃ na ak‍kamissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassā itthiyā ujjhāyantiyā khiyyantiyā vipācaintiyā| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘gihī, bhikkhavai, maṅgalikā| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gihīnaṃ maṅgalatthāya yāciyamānaina cailappaṭikaṃ ak‍kamitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū dhautapādakaṃ ak‍kamituṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dhautapādakaṃ ak‍kamitu’’nti|

dutiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

269. atha  khau bhagavā bhaggaisu yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina sāvatthi taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sāvatthi tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau visākhā migāramātā ghaṭakañ‍ca katakañ‍ca sammaj‍janiñ‍ca ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau visākhā migāramātā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu mai, bhantai, bhagavā ghaṭakañ‍ca katakañ‍ca sammaj‍janiñ‍ca, yaṃ  mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā ghaṭakañ‍ca sammaj‍janiñ‍ca| na bhagavā katakaṃ paṭiggahaisi| atha khau bhagavā visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau visākhā migāramātā bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau  bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ghaṭakañ‍ca sammaj‍janiñ‍ca| na, bhikkhavai, katakaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tissau pādaghaṃsaniyau – sakkharaṃ, kathalaṃ, samuddaphaiṇaka’’nti|

atha khau visākhā migāramātā vidhūpanañ‍ca tālavaṇṭañ‍ca tālavaṇḍañ‍ca (ka॰) ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau visākhā migāramātā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu mai, bhantai, bhagavā vidhūpanañ‍ca tālavaṇṭañ‍ca, yaṃ mama assa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā vidhūpanañ‍ca tālavaṇṭañ‍ca|

atha  khau bhagavā visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi…pai॰… padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ  kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vidhūpanañ‍ca tālavaṇṭañ‍cā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa makasabījanī uppan‍nā hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, makasabījani’’nti| cāmaribījanī uppan‍nā hauti| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, cāmaribījanī dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tissau bījaniyau – vākamayaṃ, usīramayaṃ, maurapiñchamaya’’nti|

270. taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa chattaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, chatta’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū chattappaggahitā chattaṃ paggahaitvā (ka॰) āhiṇḍanti| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau upāsakau sambahulaihi ājīvakasāvakaihi saddhiṃ uyyānaṃ agamāsi| addasāsuṃ khau tai ājīvakasāvakā chabbaggiyai bhikkhū dūratauva chattappaggahitai āgacchantai| disvāna taṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aitai khau, ayyā ayyau (ka॰), tumhākaṃ bhadantā chattappaggahitā āgacchanti, saiyyathāpi gaṇakamahāmattā’’ti| ‘‘nāyyā aitai bhikkhū, paribbājakā’’ti| ‘bhikkhū na bhikkhū’ti abbhutaṃ akaṃsu| atha khau sau upāsakau upagatai sañ‍jānitvā ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā chattappaggahitā  āhiṇḍissantī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa upāsakassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, chattaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| tassa bhikkhunau vinā chattaṃ na phāsu hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānassa chattaṃ dhāraitu’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū – gilānassaiva bhagavatā chattaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ nau agilānassāti – ārāmai ārāmūpacārai chattaṃ dhāraituṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, agilānainapi ārāmai ārāmūpacārai chattaṃ dhāraitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu sik‍kāya pattaṃ uṭṭitvā daṇḍai ālaggitvā vikālai añ‍ñataraina gāmadvāraina atik‍kamati| manussā – ‘aisayyau caurau gacchati, asissa vij‍jautalatī’ti anupatitvā  gahaitvā sañ‍jānitvā muñ‍ciṃsu| atha khau sau bhikkhu ārāmaṃ gantvā  bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, āvusau, daṇḍasik‍kaṃ dhāraisī’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu daṇḍasik‍kaṃ dhāraissasī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, daṇḍasik‍kā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti, na sak‍kauti vinā daṇḍaina āhiṇḍituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānassa bhikkhunau daṇḍasammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| taina gilānaina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, gilānau; na sak‍kaumi vinā daṇḍaina āhiṇḍituṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ daṇḍasammutiṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau  ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu gilānau, na sak‍kauti  vinā daṇḍaina āhiṇḍituṃ| sau saṅghaṃ daṇḍasammutiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau daṇḍasammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu gilānau, na sak‍kauti vinā daṇḍaina āhiṇḍituṃ| sau saṅghaṃ daṇḍasammutiṃ yācati| saṅghau  itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau daṇḍasammutiṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau daṇḍasammutiyā dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau daṇḍasammuti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

271. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti, na sak‍kauti vinā sik‍kāya pattaṃ pariharituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānassa bhikkhunau sik‍kāsammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| taina gilānaina bhikkhunā  saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, gilānau; na sak‍kaumi vinā sik‍kāya pattaṃ pariharituṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ sik‍kāsammutiṃ yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu gilānau, na sak‍kauti vinā sik‍kāya pattaṃ pariharituṃ| sau saṅghaṃ sik‍kāsammutiṃ yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sik‍kāsammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu gilānau, na sak‍kauti vinā sik‍kāya pattaṃ pariharituṃ| sau saṅghaṃ sik‍kāsammutiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sik‍kāsammutiṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sik‍kāsammutiyā dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sik‍kāsammuti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

272. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti, na sak‍kauti vinā daṇḍaina āhiṇḍituṃ, na sak‍kauti vinā sik‍kāya pattaṃ pariharituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānassa bhikkhunau daṇḍasik‍kāsammutiṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbā| taina gilānaina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā vuḍḍhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘ahaṃ, bhantai, gilānau; na sak‍kaumi vinā daṇḍaina āhiṇḍituṃ; na sak‍kaumi vinā sik‍kāya pattaṃ pariharituṃ| sauhaṃ, bhantai, saṅghaṃ daṇḍasik‍kāsammutiṃ  yācāmī’ti| dutiyampi yācitabbā| tatiyampi yācitabbā| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu gilānau, na sak‍kauti vinā daṇḍaina āhiṇḍituṃ, na sak‍kauti vinā sik‍kāya pattaṃ pariharituṃ| sau saṅghaṃ daṇḍasik‍kāsammutiṃ  yācati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau daṇḍasik‍kāsammutiṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu gilānau na sak‍kauti vinā daṇḍaina āhiṇḍituṃ, na sak‍kauti vinā sik‍kāya pattaṃ pariharituṃ| sau saṅghaṃ daṇḍasik‍kāsammutiṃ yācati| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau daṇḍasik‍kāsammutiṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau daṇḍasik‍kāsammutiyā dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau daṇḍasik‍kāsammuti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

273. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu raumanthakau raumaṭṭhakau (ka॰) hauti| sau raumanthitvā raumanthitvā ajjhauharati| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘vikālāyaṃ kathaṃ hi nāma vikālāyaṃ(ka॰) bhikkhu bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jatī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘aisau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aciraṃgauyauniyā  cutau| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, raumanthakassa raumanthanaṃ| na ca, bhikkhavai, bahimukhadvāraṃ nīharitvā ajjhauharitabbaṃ| yau ajjhauharaiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa pūgassa saṅghabhattaṃ hauti| bhattaggai bahusitthāni pakiriyiṃsu vippakirīyiṃsu (sī॰), parikiriṃsu (syā॰)| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā audanai diyyamānai na sak‍kac‍caṃ paṭiggahaissanti, aikamaikaṃ sitthaṃ kammasataina niṭṭhāyatī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū  taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ diyyamānaṃ patati, taṃ sāmaṃ gahaitvā paribhuñ‍jituṃ| paric‍cattaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, dāyakaihī’’ti|

274. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu dīghaihi nakhaihi piṇḍāya carati| añ‍ñatarā itthī passitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi, bhantai, maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, bhagini, naitaṃ kappatī’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, bhantai, nappaṭisaivissasi, idānāhaṃ attanau nakhaihi gattāni vilikhitvā kuppaṃ karissāmi – ayaṃ maṃ bhikkhu vippakarautī’’ti| ‘‘pajānāhi  tvaṃ, bhaginī’’ti| atha khau sā itthī  attanau nakhaihi gattāni vilikhitvā kuppaṃ akāsi – ayaṃ maṃ bhikkhu vippakarautīti| manussā upadhāvitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aggahaisuṃ| addasāsuṃ khau tai manussā tassā itthiyā nakhai chavimpi lauhitampi| disvāna – imissāyaiva itthiyā idaṃ kammaṃ, akārakau bhikkhūti – taṃ bhikkhuṃ muñ‍ciṃsu| atha khau sau bhikkhu ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, āvusau, dīghai nakhai dhāraisī’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhu dīghai nakhai dhāraissasī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, dīghā nakhā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū nakhainapi nakhaṃ chindanti, mukhainapi nakhaṃ chindanti, kuṭṭaipi ghaṃsanti| aṅguliyau dukkhā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, nakhacchaidana’’nti| salauhitaṃ nakhaṃ chindanti| aṅguliyau dukkhā haunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, maṃsappamāṇaina nakhaṃ chinditu’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū vīsatimaṭṭhaṃ vīsatimaṭṭaṃ (sī॰) kārāpainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, vīsatimaṭṭhaṃ kārāpaitabbaṃ| yau kārāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, malamattaṃ apakaḍḍhitu’’nti|

275. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ kaisā dīghā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘ussahanti pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kaisai auraupaitu’’nti? ‘‘ussahanti bhagavā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā…pai॰… bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, khuraṃ khurasilaṃ khurasipāṭikaṃ namatakaṃ sabbaṃ khurabhaṇḍa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū massuṃ kappāpainti…pai॰… massuṃ vaḍḍhāpainti… gaulaumikaṃ kārāpainti… caturassakaṃ kārāpainti… parimukhaṃ kārāpainti… aḍḍhadukaṃ aḍḍhurakaṃ (sī॰), aḍḍharukaṃ (syā॰) kārāpainti… dāṭhikaṃ ṭhapainti… sambādhai laumaṃ saṃharāpainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, massu kappāpaitabbaṃ…pai॰… na massu vaḍḍhāpaitabbaṃ… na gaulaumikaṃ kārāpaitabbaṃ… na caturassakaṃ kārāpaitabbaṃ… na parimukhaṃ kārāpaitabbaṃ… na aḍḍhadukaṃ kārāpaitabbaṃ… na dāṭhikā ṭhapaitabbā… na sambādhai laumaṃ saṃharāpaitabbaṃ| yau saṃharāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau sambādhai vaṇau hauti| bhaisaj‍jaṃ na santiṭṭhati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ābādhappac‍cayā sambādhai laumaṃ saṃharāpaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kattarikāya kaisai chaidāpainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, kattarikāya kaisā chaidāpaitabbā| yau chaidāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau sīsai vaṇau hauti, na sak‍kauti khuraina kaisai auraupaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ābādhappac‍cayā kattarikāya kaisai chaidāpaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū dīghāni nāsikālaumāni dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi pisācil‍likāti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, dīghaṃ nāsikālaumaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū sakkharikāyapi madhusitthakainapi nāsikālaumaṃ gāhāpainti| nāsikā dukkhā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṇḍāsa’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū palitaṃ gāhāpainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, palitaṃ gāhāpaitabbaṃ| yau gāhāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

276. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau kaṇṇagūthakaihi kaṇṇā thakitā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kaṇṇamalaharaṇi’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacā kaṇṇamalaharaṇiyau dhārainti sauvaṇṇamayaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāvacā kaṇṇamalaharaṇiyau dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhimayaṃ dantamayaṃ visāṇamayaṃ naḷamayaṃ vaiḷumayaṃ kaṭṭhamayaṃ jatumayaṃ phalamayaṃ lauhamayaṃ saṅkhanābhimaya’’nti|

277. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bahuṃ lauhabhaṇḍaṃ kaṃsabhaṇḍaṃ san‍nicayaṃ karaunti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā bahuṃ lauhabhaṇḍaṃ kaṃsabhaṇḍaṃ san‍nicayaṃ karissanti, saiyyathāpi  kaṃsapattharikā’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bahuṃ lauhabhaṇḍaṃ kaṃsabhaṇḍaṃ san‍nicayau kātabbau| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū añ‍janimpi añ‍janisalākampi kaṇṇamalaharaṇimpi bandhanamattampi kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, añ‍janiṃ añ‍janisalākaṃ kaṇṇamalaharaṇiṃ bandhanamatta’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū saṅghāṭipal‍latthikāya nisīdanti| saṅghāṭiyā pattā luj‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, saṅghāṭipal‍latthikāya nisīditabbaṃ| yau nisīdaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| tassa vinā āyaugaina āyaugā (sī॰ syā॰) na phāsu hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āyauga’’nti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau āyaugau kātabbau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tantakaṃ vaimaṃ kavaṭaṃ vaimakaṃ vaṭṭaṃ (sī॰), vaimakaṃ vaṭaṃ (syā॰) salākaṃ sabbaṃ tantabhaṇḍaka’’nti|

278. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu akāyabandhanau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| tassa rathikāya antaravāsakau pabhassittha| manussā uk‍kuṭṭhimakaṃsu| sau bhikkhu maṅku  ahausi| atha khau sau bhikkhu ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, akāyabandhanaina gāmau pavisitabbau| yau pavisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kāyabandhana’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacāni kāyabandhanāni dhārainti – kalābukaṃ, daiḍḍubhakaṃ, murajaṃ, maddavīṇaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāvacāni kāyabandhanāni dhāraitabbāni – kalābukaṃ, daiḍḍubhakaṃ, murajaṃ, maddavīṇaṃ| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dvai kāyabandhanāni – paṭṭikaṃ, sūkarantaka’’nti| kāyabandhanassa dasā  jīranti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, murajaṃ maddavīṇa’’nti| kāyabandhanassa antau jīrati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saubhaṇaṃ guṇaka’’nti| kāyabandhanassa pavanantau jīrati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vidha’’nti vīthanti (sī॰ syā॰)|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacai vidhai dhārainti sauvaṇṇamayaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti  vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāvacā vidhā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhimayaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhanābhimayaṃ suttamaya’’nti|

279. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ānandau lahukā saṅghāṭiyau pārupitvā gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| vātamaṇḍalikāya saṅghāṭiyau ukkhipiyiṃsu| atha khau āyasmā ānandau ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gaṇṭhikaṃ pāsaka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāvacā gaṇṭhikāyau dhārainti sauvaṇṇamayaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāvacā gaṇṭhikā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhimayaṃ dantamayaṃ visāṇamayaṃ naḷamayaṃ vaiḷumayaṃ kaṭṭhamayaṃ jatumayaṃ phalamayaṃ lauhamayaṃ saṅkhanābhimayaṃ suttamaya’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū gaṇṭhikampi pāsakampi cīvarai  appainti| cīvaraṃ jīrati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gaṇṭhikaphalakaṃ pāsakaphalaka’’nti| gaṇṭhikaphalakampi  pāsakaphalakampi antai appainti| kauṭṭau kauṇau (sī॰ syā॰) vivariyati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gaṇṭhikaphalakaṃ antai appaituṃ; pāsakaphalakaṃ sattaṅgulaṃ vā aṭṭhaṅgulaṃ vā augāhaitvā appaitu’’nti|

280. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū gihinivatthaṃ nivāsainti – hatthisauṇḍakaṃ, macchavāḷakaṃ, catukaṇṇakaṃ, tālavaṇṭakaṃ, satavalikaṃ| manussā ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, gihinivatthaṃ nivāsaitabbaṃ – hatthisauṇḍakaṃ, macchavāḷakaṃ, catukaṇṇakaṃ, tālavaṇṭakaṃ, satavalikaṃ| yau nivāsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū gihipārutaṃ pārupanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, gihipārutaṃ pārupitabbaṃ| yau pārupaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū saṃvail‍liyaṃ nivāsainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi  rañ‍ñau muṇḍavaṭṭīti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, saṃvail‍liyaṃ nivāsaitabbaṃ| yau nivāsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

281. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ubhataukājaṃ haranti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi rañ‍ñau muṇḍavaṭṭīti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, ubhataukājaṃ haritabbaṃ| yau haraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikataukājaṃ antarākājaṃ sīsabhāraṃ khandhabhāraṃ kaṭibhāraṃ aulambaka’’nti|

282. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū dantakaṭṭhaṃ na khādanti| mukhaṃ duggandhaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ādīnavā dantakaṭṭhassa akhādanai| acakkhussaṃ, mukhaṃ duggandhaṃ hauti, rasaharaṇiyau na visujjhanti, pittaṃ saimhaṃ bhattaṃ pariyaunandhati, bhattamassa nacchādaiti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ādīnavā dantakaṭṭhassa akhādanai|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, ānisaṃsā dantakaṭṭhassa khādanai| cakkhussaṃ, mukhaṃ na duggandhaṃ hauti, rasaharaṇiyau visujjhanti, pittaṃ saimhaṃ bhattaṃ na pariyaunandhati, bhattamassa chādaiti  – imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā dantakaṭṭhassa khādanai| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dantakaṭṭha’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū dīghāni dantakaṭṭhāni khādanti, taihaiva sāmaṇairaṃ ākauṭainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, dīghaṃ dantakaṭṭhaṃ khāditabbaṃ| yau khādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhaṅgulaparamaṃ dantakaṭṭhaṃ, na ca taina sāmaṇairau ākauṭaitabbau| yau ākauṭaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau atimaṭāhakaṃ dantakaṭṭhaṃ khādantassa kaṇṭhai vilaggaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, atimaṭāhakaṃ dantakaṭṭhaṃ khāditabbaṃ| yau khādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, caturaṅgulapacchimaṃ caturaṅgulaṃ pacchimaṃ (ka॰) dantakaṭṭha’’nti|

283. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū dāyaṃ ālimpainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi davaḍāhakāti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, dāyau ālimpitabbau| yau ālimpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina vihārā tiṇagahanā haunti, davaḍāhai ḍayhamānai vihārā ḍayhanti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti paṭaggiṃ  dātuṃ, parittaṃ kātuṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, davaḍāhai ḍayhamānai paṭaggiṃ dātuṃ, parittaṃ kātu’’nti|

284. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū rukkhaṃ abhiruhanti, rukkhā rukkhaṃ saṅkamanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi mak‍kaṭāti| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, rukkhau abhiruhitabbau| yau abhiruhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau kausalaisu janapadai sāvatthiṃ gacchantassa antarāmaggai hatthī pariyuṭṭhāti| atha khau sau bhikkhu rukkhamūlaṃ upadhāvitvā kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau rukkhaṃ na abhiruhi| sau hatthī añ‍ñaina agamāsi| atha khau sau bhikkhu sāvatthiṃ gantvā bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sati karaṇīyai paurisaṃ rukkhaṃ abhiruhituṃ āpadāsu yāvadattha’’nti|

285. taina  khau pana samayaina yamaiḷakaikuṭā nāma yamaiḷutaikulā nāma (sī॰), maiṭṭhakaukuṭṭhā nāma (syā॰) bhikkhū dvai bhātikā haunti brāhmaṇajātikā kalyāṇavācā kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇā| tai yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau  tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aitarahi, bhantai, bhikkhū nānānāmā nānāgauttā nānājac‍cā nānākulā pabbajitā| tai sakāya niruttiyā buddhavacanaṃ dūsainti| handa mayaṃ, bhantai, buddhavacanaṃ chandasau āraupaimā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tumhai, maughapurisā, aivaṃ vakkhatha – ‘handa mayaṃ, bhantai, buddhavacanaṃ chandasau āraupaimā’ti| naitaṃ, maughapurisā, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, buddhavacanaṃ chandasau āraupaitabbaṃ| yau āraupaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sakāya niruttiyā buddhavacanaṃ pariyāpuṇitu’’nti|

286. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū laukāyataṃ pariyāpuṇanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘api nu khau, bhikkhavai, laukāyatai sāradassāvī imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ viruḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nauhaitaṃ bhantai’’| ‘‘imasmiṃ vā pana dhammavinayai sāradassāvī laukāyataṃ pariyāpuṇaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nauhaitaṃ bhantai’’| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, laukāyataṃ pariyāpuṇitabbaṃ| yau pariyāpuṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū laukāyataṃ vācainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti  vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, laukāyataṃ vācaitabbaṃ| yau vācaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

287. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, tiracchānavij‍jā pariyāpuṇitabbā| yau pariyāpuṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tiracchānavij‍jaṃ vācainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, tiracchānavij‍jā vācaitabbā| yau vācaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

288. taina khau pana  samayaina bhagavā mahatiyā parisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaintau khipi| bhikkhū – ‘jīvatu, bhantai, bhagavā; jīvatu sugatau’ti – uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ akaṃsu| taina saddaina dhammakathā antarā ahausi| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘api nu khau, bhikkhavai, khipitai ‘jīvā’ti vuttau vuttai (ka॰) tappac‍cayā jīvaiyya vā  maraiyya vā’’ti? ‘‘nauhaitaṃ bhantai’’| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, khipitai ‘jīvā’ti vattabbau| yau vadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina manussā bhikkhūnaṃ khipitai ‘jīvatha bhantai’ti vadanti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nālapanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā ‘jīvatha bhantai’ti vuc‍camānā nālapissantī’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘gihī, bhikkhavai, maṅgalikā| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gihīnaṃ ‘jīvatha bhantai’ti vuc‍camānaina ‘ciraṃ jīvā’ti vattu’’nti|

289. taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā mahatiyā parisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaintau nisin‍nau hauti| añ‍ñataraina bhikkhunā lasuṇaṃ khāyitaṃ hauti| sau – mā bhikkhū byābādhiṃsūti – aikamantaṃ nisīdi| addasā khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ| disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau’’ti? ‘‘aitaina, bhantai, bhikkhunā lasuṇaṃ khāyitaṃ| sau – mā bhikkhū byābādhiṃsūti – aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau’’ti| ‘‘api nu khau, bhikkhavai bhikkhavai bhikkhunā (syā॰), taṃ khāditabbaṃ, yaṃ khāditvā aivarūpāya dhammakathāya paribāhiyau assā’’ti? ‘‘nauhaitaṃ bhantai’’| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, lasuṇaṃ khāditabbaṃ| yau khādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmatau sāriputtassa udaravātābādhau hauti| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pubbai tai, āvusau sāriputta, udaravātābādhau kaina phāsu hautī’’ti? ‘‘lasuṇaina mai, āvusau’’ti atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca ‘‘pubbai khau mai āvusau mauggal‍lāna udaravātābādhau lasuṇaina phāsu hautī’’ti (ka॰)| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ābādhappac‍cayā lasuṇaṃ khāditu’’nti|

290. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ārāmai tahaṃ tahaṃ passāvaṃ karaunti| ārāmau dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikamantaṃ passāvaṃ kātu’’nti| ārāmau duggandhau hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, passāvakumbhi’’nti| dukkhaṃ nisin‍nā passāvaṃ karaunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, passāvapāduka’’nti| passāvapādukā pākaṭā haunti| bhikkhū hiriyanti passāvaṃ kātuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parikkhipituṃ tayau pākārai – iṭṭhakāpākāraṃ, silāpākāraṃ, dārupākāra’’nti| passāvakumbhī apārutā duggandhā hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, apidhāna’’nti|

291. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ārāmai tahaṃ tahaṃ vac‍caṃ karaunti| ārāmau dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikamantaṃ vac‍caṃ kātu’’nti| ārāmau duggandhau hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vac‍cakūpa’’nti| vac‍cakūpassa kūlaṃ luj‍jati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| vac‍cakūpau nīcavatthukau hauti, udakaina autthariyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| cayau paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau saupānai – iṭṭhakāsaupānaṃ, silāsaupānaṃ, dārusaupāna’’nti| ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti| antai nisin‍nā vac‍caṃ karauntā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, santharitvā majjhai chiddaṃ katvā vac‍caṃ kātu’’nti| dukkhaṃ nisin‍nā vac‍caṃ karaunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vac‍capāduka’’nti| bahiddhā passāvaṃ  karaunti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, passāvadauṇika’’nti| avalaikhanakaṭṭhaṃ na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, avalaikhanakaṭṭha’’nti| avalaikhanapiṭharau na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, avalaikhanapiṭhara’’nti| vac‍cakūpau apārutau duggandhau hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, apidhāna’’nti| ajjhaukāsai vac‍caṃ karauntā sītainapi uṇhainapi kilamanti…pai॰…  ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vac‍cakuṭi’’nti| vac‍cakuṭiyā kavāṭaṃ na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kavāṭaṃ piṭṭhasaṅghāṭaṃ udukkhalikaṃ uttarapāsakaṃ aggaḷavaṭṭiṃ kapisīsakaṃ sūcikaṃ ghaṭikaṃ tāḷacchiddaṃ āviñchanacchiddaṃ āviñchanaraj‍ju’’nti| vac‍cakuṭiyā tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ – saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭikaṃ cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍ju’’nti|

292. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu jarādubbalau vac‍caṃ katvā vuṭṭhahantau  paripatati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aulambaka’’nti| vac‍cakuṭi aparikkhittā hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parikkhipituṃ tayau pākārai – iṭṭhakāpākāraṃ, silāpākāraṃ, dārupākāra’’nti| kauṭṭhakau na hauti…pai॰… (‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kauṭṭhaka’’nti| kauṭṭhakassa kavāṭaṃ na  hauti) (anujānāmi bhikkhavai kauṭṭhakanti| kauṭṭhakau nīcavatthukau hauti…pai॰… anujānāmi bhikkhavai uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātunti| cayau paripatati…pai॰… anujānāmi bhikkhavai cinituṃ tayau cayai iṭṭhakācayaṃ silācayaṃ dārucayanti| ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… anujānāmi bhikkhavai tayau saupānai iṭṭhakāsaupānaṃ silāsaupānaṃ dārusaupānanti| ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… anujānāmi bhikkhavai ālambanabāhanti| kauṭṭhakassa kavāṭaṃ na hauti|) (syā॰ kaṃ॰) …pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kavāṭaṃ piṭṭhasaṅghāṭaṃ udukkhalikaṃ uttarapāsakaṃ aggaḷavaṭṭiṃ kapisīsakaṃ sūcikaṃ ghaṭikaṃ tāḷacchiddaṃ āviñchanacchiddaṃ āviñchanaraj‍ju’’nti| kauṭṭhakai tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ – saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭika’’nti| parivaiṇaṃ cikkhal‍laṃ hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, marumbaṃ pakiritu’’nti| na pariyāpuṇanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, padarasilaṃ paṭṭasilaṃ (ka॰) nikkhipitu’’nti| udakaṃ santiṭṭhati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakaniddhamana’’nti| ācamanakumbhī na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ācamanakumbhi’’nti| ācamanasarāvakau  na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ācamanasarāvaka’’nti| dukkhaṃ nisin‍nā ācamainti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ācamanapāduka’’nti| ācamanapādukā pākaṭā haunti, bhikkhū hiriyanti ācamaituṃ…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parikkhipituṃ tayau pākārai  – iṭṭhakāpākāraṃ, silāpākāraṃ, dārupākāra’’nti| ācamanakumbhī apārutā hauti, tiṇacuṇṇaihipi paṃsukaihipi aukiriyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, apidhāna’’nti|

293. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū aivarūpaṃ anācāraṃ ācaranti – mālāvacchaṃ raupaintipi raupāpaintipi, siñ‍cantipi siñ‍cāpaintipi, aucinantipi aucināpaintipi, ganthaintipi ganthāpaintipi, aikatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, ubhatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ karauntipi  kārāpaintipi, mañ‍jarikaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, vidhūtikaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, vaṭaṃsakaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, āvaiḷaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi, uracchadaṃ karauntipi kārāpaintipi| tai kulitthīnaṃ kuladhītānaṃ kulakumārīnaṃ kulasuṇhānaṃ kuladāsīnaṃ aikatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, ubhatauvaṇṭikamālaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, mañ‍jarikaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, vidhūtikaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, vaṭaṃsakaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, āvaiḷaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi, uracchadaṃ harantipi harāpaintipi| tai kulitthīhi kuladhītāhi kulakumārīhi kulasuṇhāhi kuladāsīhi saddhiṃ aikabhājanaipi bhuñ‍janti, aikathālakaipi pivanti, aikāsanaipi nisīdanti, aikamañ‍caipi tuvaṭṭainti, aikattharaṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti, aikapāvuraṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti, aikattharaṇapāvuraṇāpi tuvaṭṭainti, vikālaipi bhuñ‍janti, maj‍jampi pivanti, mālāgandhavilaipanampi dhārainti, nac‍cantipi, gāyantipi, vādaintipi, lāsaintipi; nac‍cantiyāpi nac‍canti, nac‍cantiyāpi gāyanti, nac‍cantiyāpi vādainti, nac‍cantiyāpi lāsainti…pai॰… lāsaintiyāpi nac‍canti, lāsaintiyāpi gāyanti, lāsaintiyāpi vādainti, lāsaintiyāpi lāsainti; aṭṭhapadaipi kīḷanti, dasapadaipi kīḷanti, ākāsaipi kīḷanti, parihārapathaipi kīḷanti, santikāyapi kīḷanti, khalikāyapi kīḷanti, ghaṭikāyapi kīḷanti, salākahatthainapi kīḷanti, akkhainapi kīḷanti, paṅgacīrainapi kīḷanti, vaṅkakainapi kīḷanti, maukkhacikāyapi kīḷanti, ciṅgulakainapi kīḷanti, pattāḷhakainapi kīḷanti, rathakainapi kīḷanti, dhanukainapi kīḷanti, akkharikāyapi kīḷanti, manaisikāyapi kīḷanti, yathāvaj‍jainapi kīḷanti; hatthismimpi sikkhanti, assasmimpi sikkhanti, rathasmimpi sikkhanti, dhanusmimpi  sikkhanti; tharusmimpi sikkhanti; hatthissapi puratau dhāvanti, assassapi puratau dhāvanti, rathassapi puratau dhāvantipi ādhāvantipi; ussaiḷaintipi, apphauṭaintipi, nibbujjhantipi, muṭṭhīhipi yujjhanti; raṅgamajjhaipi saṅghāṭiṃ pattharitvā nac‍cakiṃ aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘idha, bhagini, nac‍cassū’’ti; nalāṭikampi dainti; vividhampi anācāraṃ ācaranti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, vividhaṃ anācāraṃ ācaritabbaṃ| yau ācaraiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmantai uruvailakassapai pabbajitai saṅghassa bahuṃ lauhabhaṇḍaṃ dārubhaṇḍaṃ mattikābhaṇḍaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau bhagavatā lauhabhaṇḍaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kiṃ ananuñ‍ñātaṃ; kiṃ dārubhaṇḍaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kiṃ ananuñ‍ñātaṃ; kiṃ mattikābhaṇḍaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kiṃ ananuñ‍ñāta’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ṭhapaitvā paharaṇiṃ sabbaṃ lauhabhaṇḍaṃ, ṭhapaitvā āsandiṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ  dārupattaṃ dārupādukaṃ sabbaṃ dārubhaṇḍaṃ, ṭhapaitvā katakañ‍ca kumbhakārikañ‍ca sabbaṃ mattikābhaṇḍa’’nti|

khuddakavatthukkhandhakau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

rukkhai  thambhai ca kuṭṭai ca, aṭṭānai gandhasuttiyā|

vigayha mal‍lakau kacchu, jarā ca puthupāṇikā||

val‍likāpi ca pāmaṅgau, kaṇṭhasuttaṃ na dhārayai|

kaṭi auvaṭṭi kāyuraṃ, hatthābharaṇamuddikā||

dīghai kaucchai phaṇai hatthai, sitthā udakatailakai|

ādāsudapattavaṇā, ālaipaummaddacuṇṇanā||

lañchainti aṅgarāgañ‍ca, mukharāgaṃ tadūbhayaṃ|

cakkhuraugaṃ giraggañ‍ca, āyataṃ sarabāhiraṃ||

ambapaisisakalaihi, ahicchindi ca candanaṃ|

uc‍cāvacā pattamūlā, suvaṇṇau bahalā valī||

citrā  dussati duggandhau, uṇhai bhij‍jiṃsu miḍḍhiyā|

paribhaṇḍaṃ tiṇaṃ cauḷaṃ, mālaṃ kuṇḍaulikāya ca||

thavikā  aṃsabaddhañ‍ca, tathā bandhanasuttakā|

khilai mañ‍cai ca pīṭhai ca, aṅkai chattai paṇāmanā||

tumbaghaṭichavasīsaṃ, calakāni paṭiggahau|

vipphālidaṇḍasauvaṇṇaṃ, pattai paisi ca nāḷikā||

kiṇṇasattu saritañ‍ca, madhusitthaṃ sipāṭikaṃ|

vikaṇṇaṃ bandhivisamaṃ, chamājirapahauti ca||

kaḷimbhaṃ  maughasuttañ‍ca, adhautal‍laṃ upāhanā|

aṅgulai paṭiggahañ‍ca, vitthakaṃ thavikabaddhakā||

ajjhaukāsai nīcavatthu, cayau cāpi vihañ‍ñarai|

paripati tiṇacuṇṇaṃ, ul‍littaavalittakaṃ||

saitaṃ kāḷakavaṇṇañ‍ca, parikammañ‍ca gairukaṃ|

mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ, makaradantakapāṭikaṃ||

cīvaravaṃsaṃ raj‍juñ‍ca, anuñ‍ñāsi vināyakau|

ujjhitvā pak‍kamanti ca, kathinaṃ paribhij‍jati||

vinivaiṭhiyati kuṭṭaipi, pattainādāya gaccharai|

thavikā bandhasuttañ‍ca, bandhitvā ca upāhanā||

upāhanatthavikañ‍ca, aṃsabaddhañ‍ca suttakaṃ|

udakākappiyaṃ maggai, parissāvanacauḷakaṃ||

dhammakaraṇaṃ dvai bhikkhū, vaisāliṃ agamā muni|

daṇḍaṃ auttharakaṃ tattha, anuñ‍ñāsi parissāvanaṃ||

makasaihi  paṇītaina, bahvābādhā ca jīvakau|

caṅkamanajantāgharaṃ, visamai nīcavatthukau||

tayau  cayai vihañ‍ñanti, saupānālambavaidikaṃ|

ajjhaukāsai tiṇacuṇṇaṃ, ul‍littaavalittakaṃ||

saitakaṃ  kāḷavaṇṇañ‍ca, parikammañ‍ca gairukaṃ|

mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ, makaradantakapāṭikaṃ||

vaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍juñ‍ca, uc‍cañ‍ca vatthukaṃ karai|

cayau saupānabāhañ‍ca, kavāṭaṃ piṭṭhasaṅghāṭaṃ||

udukkhaluttarapāsakaṃ, vaṭṭiñ‍ca kapisīsakaṃ|

sūcighaṭitāḷacchiddaṃ, āviñchanañ‍ca raj‍jukaṃ||

maṇḍalaṃ dhūmanaittañ‍ca, majjhai ca mukhamattikā|

dauṇi duggandhā dahati, udakaṭṭhānaṃ sarāvakaṃ||

na saidaiti ca cikkhal‍laṃ, dhauvi niddhamanaṃ karai|

pīṭhañ‍ca kauṭṭhakai kammaṃ, marumbā silā niddhamanaṃ||

naggā chamāya vassantai, paṭicchādī tayau tahiṃ|

udapānaṃ luj‍jati nīcaṃ, val‍liyā kāyabandhanai||

tulaṃ kaṭakaṭaṃ cak‍kaṃ, bahū bhij‍janti bhājanā|

lauhadārucammakhaṇḍaṃ, sālātiṇāpidhāni ca||

dauṇicandani pākāraṃ, cikkhal‍laṃ niddhamanaina ca|

sītigataṃ paukkharaṇiṃ, purāṇañ‍ca nil‍laikhaṇaṃ||

cātumāsaṃ  sayanti ca, namatakañ‍ca gandhapupphaṃ (syā॰) nadhiṭṭhahai|

āsittakaṃ  maḷaurikaṃ, bhuñ‍jantaikaṃ tuvaṭṭaiyyuṃ||

vaḍḍhau baudhi na ak‍kami, ghaṭaṃ katakasammaj‍jani|

sakkharaṃ kathalañ‍caiva, phaiṇakaṃ pādaghaṃsanī||

vidhūpanaṃ tālavaṇṭaṃ, makasañ‍cāpi cāmarī|

chattaṃ vinā ca ārāmai, tayau sik‍kāya sammuti||

raumasitthā nakhā dīghā, chindantaṅgulikā dukkhā|

salauhitaṃ pamāṇañ‍ca, vīsati dīghakaisatā||

khuraṃ silaṃ sipāṭikaṃ, namatakaṃ khurabhaṇḍakaṃ|

massuṃ kappainti vaḍḍhainti, gaulaumicaturassakaṃ||

parimukhaṃ  aḍḍhadukañ‍ca, dāṭhisambādhasaṃharai|

ābādhā kattarivaṇau, dīghaṃ sakkharikāya ca||

palitaṃ thakitaṃ uc‍cā, lauhabhaṇḍañ‍janī saha|

pal‍latthikañ‍ca āyaugau, vaṭaṃ salākabandhanaṃ||

kalābukaṃ daiḍḍubhakaṃ, murajaṃ maddavīṇakaṃ|

paṭṭikā sūkarantañ‍ca, dasā murajavaiṇikā|

antau saubhaṃ guṇakañ‍ca, pavanantaupi jīrati||

gaṇṭhikā uc‍cāvacañ‍ca, phalakantaipi augāhai|

gihivatthaṃ hatthisauṇḍaṃ, macchakaṃ catukaṇṇakaṃ||

tālavaṇṭaṃ satavali, gihipārutapārupaṃ|

saṃvail‍li  ubhataukājaṃ, dantakaṭṭhaṃ ākauṭanai||

kaṇṭhai  vilaggaṃ dāyañ‍ca, paṭaggi rukkhahatthinā|

yamaiḷai laukāyatakaṃ, pariyāpuṇiṃsu vācayuṃ||

tiracchānakathā  vij‍jā, khipi maṅgalaṃ khādi ca|

vātābādhau dussati ca, duggandhau dukkhapādukā||

hiriyanti pāruduggandhau, tahaṃ tahaṃ karaunti ca|

duggandhau kūpaṃ luj‍janti, uc‍cavatthu cayaina ca||

saupānālambanabāhā antai, dukkhañ‍ca pādukā|

bahiddhā dauṇi kaṭṭhañ‍ca, piṭharau ca apārutau||

vac‍cakuṭiṃ kavāṭañ‍ca, piṭṭhasaṅghāṭamaiva ca|

udukkhaluttarapāsau, vaṭṭiñ‍ca kapisīsakaṃ||

sūcighaṭitāḷacchiddaṃ, āviñchanacchiddamaiva ca|

raj‍ju ul‍littāvalittaṃ, saitavaṇṇañ‍ca kāḷakaṃ||

mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ, makaraṃ pañ‍capaṭikaṃ|

cīvaravaṃsaṃ raj‍juñ‍ca, jarādubbalapākāraṃ||

kauṭṭhakai cāpi tathaiva, marumbaṃ padarasilā|

santiṭṭhati niddhamanaṃ, kumbhiñ‍cāpi sarāvakaṃ||

dukkhaṃ  hiri apidhānaṃ, anācārañ‍ca ācaruṃ|

lauhabhaṇḍaṃ  anuñ‍ñāsi, ṭhapayitvā paharaṇiṃ||

ṭhapayitvā sandipal‍laṅkaṃ, dārupattañ‍ca pādukaṃ|

sabbaṃ dārumayaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ, anuñ‍ñāsi mahāmuni||

katakaṃ  kumbhakārañ‍ca, ṭhapayitvā tathāgatau|

sabbampi mattikābhaṇḍaṃ, anuñ‍ñāsi anukampakau||

yassa vatthussa niddaisau, purimaina yadi samaṃ|

tampi saṃkhittaṃ uddānai, nayatau taṃ vijāniyā||

aivaṃ dasasatā vatthū, vinayai khuddakavatthukai|

saddhammaṭṭhitikau caiva, paisalānañ‍canuggahau||

susikkhitau vinayadharau, hitacittau supaisalau|

padīpakaraṇau dhīrau, pūjārahau bahussutauti||

khuddakavatthukkhandhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



6. saināsanakkhandhakaṃ

1. paṭhamabhāṇavārau

vihārānujānanaṃ

294. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ saināsanaṃ apañ‍ñattaṃ hauti| tai ca bhikkhū tahaṃ tahaṃ viharanti – arañ‍ñai, rukkhamūlai, pabbatai, kandarāyaṃ, giriguhāyaṃ, susānai, vanapatthai, ajjhaukāsai, palālapuñ‍jai| tai kālassaiva tatau tatau upanikkhamanti – arañ‍ñā rukkhamūlā pabbatā kandarā giriguhā susānā vanapatthā ajjhaukāsā palālapuñ‍jā, pāsādikaina abhik‍kantaina paṭik‍kantaina, ālaukitaina vilaukitaina, samiñ‍jitaina pasāritaina, aukkhittacakkhū, iriyāpathasampan‍nā| taina khau pana samayaina rājagahakau saiṭṭhī saiṭṭhi (ka॰) kālassaiva uyyānaṃ agamāsi| addasā khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī tai bhikkhū kālassaiva tatau tatau upanikkhamantai – arañ‍ñā rukkhamūlā pabbatā kandarā giriguhā susānā vanapatthā ajjhaukāsā palālapuñ‍jā, pāsādikaina abhik‍kantaina paṭik‍kantaina, ālaukitaina vilaukitaina, samiñ‍jitaina pasāritaina, aukkhittacakkhū, iriyāpathasampan‍nai| disvānassa cittaṃ pasīdi| atha khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘sacāhaṃ, bhantai, vihārai kārāpaiyyaṃ, vasaiyyātha mai vihāraisū’’ti? ‘‘na khau, gahapati, bhagavatā vihārā  anuñ‍ñātā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ paṭipucchitvā mama āraucaiyyāthā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ gahapatī’’ti khau tai bhikkhū rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘rājagahakau, bhantai, saiṭṭhī vihārai kārāpaitukāmau| kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, amhaihi bhantai (sī॰ ka॰) paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai  aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca laiṇāni pañ‍ca saināsanāni (syā॰) – vihāraṃ, aḍḍhayaugaṃ, pāsādaṃ, hammiyaṃ, guha’’nti|

atha  khau tai  bhikkhū yaina rājagahakau saiṭṭhī tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā rājagahakaṃ saiṭṭhiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘anuñ‍ñātā khau, gahapati, bhagavatā vihārā; yassadāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī aikāhainaiva saṭṭhivihārai patiṭṭhāpaisi| atha khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī tai saṭṭhivihārai pariyausāpaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha  khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā, bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ, aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitai mai, bhantai, saṭṭhivihārā puñ‍ñatthikaina saggatthikaina kārāpitā| kathāhaṃ, bhantai, taisu vihāraisu paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti? ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, gahapati, tai saṭṭhivihārai āgatānāgatassa cātuddisassa saṅghassa patiṭṭhāpaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī bhagavatau paṭissutvā tai saṭṭhivihārai āgatānāgatassa cātuddisassa saṅghassa patiṭṭhāpaisi|

295. atha khau bhagavā rājagahakaṃ saiṭṭhiṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumaudi –

‘‘sītaṃ uṇhaṃ paṭihanti paṭihanati (ka॰), tatau vāḷamigāni ca|

sarīsapai ca makasai, sisirai cāpi vuṭṭhiyau||

‘‘tatau  vātātapau ghaurai, sañ‍jātau vātātapai ghaurai, sañ‍jātai (ka॰ saddanīti) paṭihañ‍ñati|

laiṇatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca, jhāyituñ‍ca vipassituṃ||

‘‘vihāradānaṃ saṅghassa, aggaṃ buddhaina buddhaihi (syā॰) vaṇṇitaṃ|

tasmā hi paṇḍitau, pausau sampassaṃ atthamattanau||

‘‘vihārai kārayai rammai, vāsayaittha bahussutai|

taisaṃ  an‍nañ‍ca pānañ‍ca, vatthasaināsanāni ca|

dadaiyya ujubhūtaisu, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

‘‘tai tassa dhammaṃ daisainti, sabbadukkhāpanūdanaṃ|

yaṃ sau dhammaṃ idhañ‍ñāya, parinibbāti anāsavau’’ti||

atha khau bhagavā rājagahakaṃ saiṭṭhiṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

296. assausuṃ khau manussā – ‘‘bhagavatā kira vihārā anuñ‍ñātā’’ti sak‍kac‍caṃ tai sak‍kac‍caṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) vihārai kārāpainti| tai vihārā akavāṭakā haunti; ahīpi vicchikāpi satapadiyaupi pavisanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kavāṭa’’nti| bhittichiddaṃ karitvā val‍liyāpi raj‍juyāpi kavāṭaṃ bandhanti| undūraihipi upacikāhipi khaj‍janti| khayitabandhanāni kavāṭāni patanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, piṭṭhasaṅghāṭaṃ udukkhalikaṃ uttarapāsaka’’nti| kavāṭā na phusīyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āviñchanacchiddaṃ āviñchanaraj‍ju’’nti| kavāṭā na thakiyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aggaḷavaṭṭiṃ kapisīsakaṃ sūcikaṃ ghaṭika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū na sak‍kaunti kavāṭaṃ apāpurituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tāḷacchiddaṃ| tīṇi tāḷāni – lauhatāḷaṃ, kaṭṭhatāḷaṃ, visāṇatāḷa’’nti| yaihi yaipi (sī॰ syā॰) tai ugghāṭaitvā pavisanti visāṇatāḷaṃ, yaihi tai ugghāṭaitvā pavisantīti (ka॰), vihārā aguttā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yantakaṃ sūcika’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina vihārā tiṇacchadanā haunti; sītakālai sītā, uṇhakālai uṇhā| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina vihārā avātapānakā haunti acakkhussā duggandhā| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tīṇi vātapānāni – vaidikāvātapānaṃ, jālavātapānaṃ, salākavātapāna’’nti| vātapānantarikāya kāḷakāpi vagguliyaupi pavisanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vātapānacak‍kalika’’nti| cak‍kalikantarikāyapi kāḷakāpi vagguliyaupi pavisanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vātapānakavāṭakaṃ vātapānabhisika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū chamāyaṃ sayanti| gattānipi  cīvarānipi paṃsukitāni haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tiṇasanthāraka’’nti| tiṇasanthārakau undūraihipi  upacikāhipi khaj‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, miḍḍhi’’nti mīḍhanti (sī॰), miḍhinti (syā॰)| miḍḍhiyā gattāni dukkhā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bidalamañ‍caka’’nti|

mañ‍capīṭhādianujānanaṃ

297. taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa sausānikau masārakau mañ‍cau uppan‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, masārakaṃ mañ‍ca’’nti| masārakaṃ pīṭhaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, masārakaṃ pīṭha’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa sausānikau bundikābaddhau mañ‍cau uppan‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bundikābaddhaṃ mañ‍ca’’nti| bundikābaddhaṃ pīṭhaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bundikābaddhaṃ pīṭha’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa sausānikau kuḷīrapādakau mañ‍cau uppan‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kuḷīrapādakaṃ  mañ‍ca’’nti| kuḷīrapādakaṃ pīṭhaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kuḷīrapādakaṃ pīṭha’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa sausānikau āhac‍capādakau mañ‍cau uppan‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ aurācaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍ca’’nti| āhac‍capādakaṃ pīṭhaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āhac‍capādakaṃ pīṭha’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa āsandikau uppan‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āsandika’’nti| uc‍cakau āsandikau uppan‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cakampi āsandika’’nti| sattaṅgau uppan‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sattaṅga’’nti| uc‍cakau sattaṅgau uppan‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cakampi sattaṅga’’nti| bhaddapīṭhaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhaddapīṭha’’nti| pīṭhikā uppan‍nā hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pīṭhika’’nti| aiḷakapādakaṃ pīṭhaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aiḷakapādakaṃ pīṭha’’nti| āmalakavaṭṭikaṃ āmalakavaṇṭikaṃ (syā॰) pīṭhaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āmalakavaṭṭikaṃ pīṭha’’nti| phalakaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, phalaka’’nti| kaucchaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kauccha’’nti| palālapīṭhaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, palālapīṭha’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cai mañ‍cai sayanti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cai mañ‍cai sayitabbaṃ| yau sayaiyya, āpatti  duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu nīcai mañ‍cai sayantau ahinā daṭṭhau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mañ‍capaṭipādaka’’nti|
