taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cai mañ‍capaṭipādakai dhārainti, saha mañ‍capaṭipādakaihi pavaidhainti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, uc‍cā mañ‍capaṭipādakā dhāraitabbā| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhaṅgulaparamaṃ mañ‍capaṭipādaka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa suttaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, suttaṃ mañ‍caṃ vaiṭhaitu’’nti vaitunti (sī॰)| aṅgāni bahusuttaṃ pariyādiyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṅgai vijjhitvā aṭṭhapadakaṃ vaiṭhaitu’’nti| cauḷakaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cimilikaṃ kātu’’nti| tūlikā uppan‍nā hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vijaṭaitvā bibbauhanaṃ bimbauhanaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ bimba + auhanaṃ) kātuṃ| tīṇi tūlāni – rukkhatūlaṃ, latātūlaṃ, pauṭakitūla’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū addhakāyikāni aḍḍhakāyikāni (ka॰) bibbauhanāni dhārainti| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, aḍḍhakāyikāni bibbauhanāni dhāraitabbāni| yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sīsappamāṇaṃ bibbauhanaṃ kātu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina rājagahai giraggasamaj‍jau hauti| manussā mahāmattānaṃ atthāya bhisiyau paṭiyādainti – uṇṇabhisiṃ, cauḷabhisiṃ, vākabhisiṃ, tiṇabhisiṃ, paṇṇabhisiṃ| tai vītivattai samaj‍jai chaviṃ uppāṭaitvā haranti| addasāsuṃ khau bhikkhū samaj‍jaṭṭhānai bahuṃ uṇṇampi cauḷakampi vākampi tiṇampi paṇṇampi chaṭṭitaṃ| disvāna  bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca bhisiyau – uṇṇabhisiṃ, cauḷabhisiṃ, vākabhisiṃ, tiṇabhisiṃ, paṇṇabhisi’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa saināsanaparikkhārikaṃ dussaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhisiṃ aunandhitu’’nti aunaddhituṃ (syā॰)|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū mañ‍cabhisiṃ pīṭhai santharanti, pīṭhabhisiṃ mañ‍cai santharanti| bhisiyau paribhij‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aunaddhamañ‍caṃ aunaddhamañ‍caṃ (ka॰) aivamuparipi aunaddhapīṭha’’nti| ul‍laukaṃ akaritvā  santharanti, haiṭṭhatau nipatanti nipaṭanti (ka॰), nipphaṭanti (sī॰), nippāṭainti (syā॰) …pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ul‍laukaṃ karitvā santharitvā bhisiṃ aunandhitu’’nti| chaviṃ uppāṭaitvā haranti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, phausitu’’nti| harantiyaiva…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhattikamma’’nti| harantiyaiva…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, hatthabhattikamma’’nti| harantiyaiva…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, hatthabhatti’’nti|

saitavaṇṇādianujānanaṃ

298. taina khau pana samayaina titthiyānaṃ saiyyāyau saitavaṇṇā haunti, kāḷavaṇṇakatā bhūmi, gairukaparikammakatā bhitti| bahū manussā saiyyāpaikkhakā gacchanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vihārai saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikamma’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina pharusāya bhittiyā saitavaṇṇau na nipatati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, thusapiṇḍaṃ datvā pāṇikāya paṭibāhaitvā saitavaṇṇaṃ nipātaitu’’nti| saitavaṇṇau anibandhanīyau hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṇhamattikaṃ datvā pāṇikāya paṭibāhaitvā saitavaṇṇaṃ nipātaitu’’nti| saitavaṇṇau anibandhanīyau hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ik‍kāsaṃ piṭṭhamadda’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina pharusāya bhittiyā gairukā na nipatati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, thusapiṇḍaṃ datvā pāṇikāya paṭibāhaitvā gairukaṃ nipātaitu’’nti| gairukā anibandhanīyā hauti  …pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kuṇḍakamattikaṃ datvā pāṇikāya paṭibāhaitvā gairukaṃ nipātaitu’’nti| gairukā anibandhanīyā hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sāsapakuṭṭaṃ sitthatailaka’’nti| ac‍cussan‍naṃ hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cauḷakaina pac‍cuddharitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina pharusāya bhūmiyā kāḷavaṇṇau na nipatati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, thusapiṇḍaṃ datvā pāṇikāya paṭibāhaitvā kāḷavaṇṇaṃ nipātaitu’’nti| kāḷavaṇṇau anibandhanīyau hauti…pai॰…  ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gaṇḍumattikaṃ  datvā pāṇikāya paṭibāhaitvā kāḷavaṇṇaṃ nipātaitu’’nti| kāḷavaṇṇau anibandhanīyau hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ik‍kāsaṃ kasāva’’nti|

paṭibhānacittapaṭikkhaipaṃ

299. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū vihārai paṭibhānacittaṃ kārāpainti – itthirūpakaṃ purisarūpakaṃ| manussā vihāracārikaṃ āhiṇḍantā passitvā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihī  kāmabhauginauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, paṭibhānacittaṃ kārāpaitabbaṃ – itthirūpakaṃ purisarūpakaṃ| yau kārāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭika’’nti|

iṭṭhakācayādianujānanaṃ

300. taina khau pana samayaina vihārā nīcavatthukā haunti, udakaina autthariyyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| cayau paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau saupānai – iṭṭhakāsaupānaṃ, silāsaupānaṃ, dārusaupāna’’nti| ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina vihārā āḷakamandā haunti| bhikkhū hiriyanti nipaj‍jituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tiraukaraṇi’’nti| tiraukaraṇiṃ ukkhipitvā aulaukainti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aḍḍhakuṭṭaka’’nti| aḍḍhakuṭṭakā uparitau aulaukainti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau gabbhai – sivikāgabbhaṃ, nāḷikāgabbhaṃ, hammiyagabbha’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū khuddakai vihārai majjhai gabbhaṃ karaunti| upacārau na hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, khuddakai vihārai aikamantaṃ gabbhaṃ kātuṃ, mahal‍lakai majjhai’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina vihārassa kuṭṭapādau jīrati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kulaṅkapādaka’’nti kuḷuṅkapādakanti (sī॰)| vihārassa kuṭṭau auvassati…pai॰…‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parittāṇakiṭikaṃ uddasudha’’nti uddhāsudhanti (syā॰)|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau tiṇacchadanā ahi khandhai patati| sau bhītau vissaramakāsi| bhikkhū upadhāvitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, āvusau, vissaramakāsī’’ti? atha khau sau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vitāna’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū mañ‍capādaipi pīṭhapādaipi thavikāyau laggainti| undūraihipi upacikāhipi khaj‍janti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhittikhilaṃ nāgadantaka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū mañ‍caipi pīṭhaipi cīvaraṃ nikkhipanti| cīvaraṃ paribhij‍jiti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vihārai cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍ju’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina vihārā anāḷindakā haunti appaṭissaraṇā| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āḷindaṃ paghanaṃ pakuṭṭaṃ pakuḍḍaṃ (sī॰) ausāraka’’nti| āḷindā pākaṭā haunti| bhikkhū hiriyanti nipaj‍jituṃ…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṃsaraṇakiṭikaṃ ugghāṭanakiṭika’’nti|

upaṭṭhānasālāanujānanaṃ

301. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ajjhaukāsai bhattavissaggaṃ karauntā sītainapi uṇhainapi kilamanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upaṭṭhānasāla’’nti| upaṭṭhānasālā nīcavatthukā hauti, udakaina autthariyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| cayau paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau saupānai – iṭṭhakāsaupānaṃ, silāsaupānaṃ, dārusaupāna’’nti| ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰…  ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti| upaṭṭhānasālāya tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā  ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ – saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭikaṃ cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍ju’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū ajjhaukāsai chamāya cīvaraṃ pattharanti| cīvaraṃ paṃsukitaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ajjhaukāsai cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍ju’’nti| pānīyaṃ autappati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pānīyasālaṃ pānīyamaṇḍapa’’nti| pānīyasālā nīcavatthukā hauti, udakaina autthariyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| cayau paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau saupānai – iṭṭhakāsaupānaṃ, silāsaupānaṃ, dārusaupāna’’nti| ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti| pānīyasālāya tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ – saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭikaṃ cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍ju’’nti| pānīyabhājanaṃ na saṃvij‍jati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pānīyasaṅkhaṃ pānīyasarāvaka’’nti|

pākārādianujānanaṃ

302. taina  khau pana samayaina vihārā aparikkhittā haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parikkhipituṃ tayau pākārai – iṭṭhakāpākāraṃ, silāpākāraṃ, dārupākāra’’nti| kauṭṭhakau na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kauṭṭhaka’’nti| kauṭṭhakau nīcavatthukau hauti, udakaina autthariyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| kauṭṭhakassa kavāṭaṃ na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kavāṭaṃ piṭṭhasaṅghāṭaṃ udukkhalikaṃ uttarapāsakaṃ aggaḷavaṭṭiṃ kapisīsakaṃ sūcikaṃ ghaṭikaṃ tāḷacchiddaṃ  āviñchanacchiddaṃ āviñchanaraj‍ju’’nti| kauṭṭhakai tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ – saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina parivaiṇaṃ cikkhal‍laṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, marumbaṃ upakiritu’’nti| na pariyāpuṇanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, padarasilaṃ nikkhipitu’’nti| udakaṃ santiṭṭhati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakaniddhamana’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū parivaiṇai tahaṃ tahaṃ aggiṭṭhānaṃ karaunti| parivaiṇaṃ uk‍lāpaṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikamantaṃ aggisālaṃ kātu’’nti| aggisālā nīcavatthukā hauti, udakaina autthariyyati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uc‍cavatthukaṃ kātu’’nti| cayau paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cinituṃ tayau cayai – iṭṭhakācayaṃ, silācayaṃ, dārucaya’’nti| ārauhantā vihañ‍ñanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tayau saupānai – iṭṭhakāsaupānaṃ, silāsaupānaṃ, dārusaupāna’’nti| ārauhantā paripatanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ālambanabāha’’nti| aggisālāya kavāṭaṃ na hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kavāṭaṃ piṭṭhasaṅghāṭaṃ udukkhalikaṃ uttarapāsakaṃ aggaḷavaṭṭiṃ kapisīsakaṃ sūcikaṃ ghaṭikaṃ tāḷacchiddaṃ āviñchanacchiddaṃ āviñchanaraj‍ju’’nti| aggisālāya tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ – saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭikaṃ cīvaravaṃsaṃ cīvararaj‍ju’’nti|

ārāmaparikkhaipaanujānanaṃ

303. taina khau pana samayaina ārāmau aparikkhittau hauti| ajakāpi pasukāpi uparaupai vihaiṭhainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parikkhipituṃ tayau vāṭai – vaiḷuvāṭaṃ, kaṇḍakavāṭaṃ vaṭai vaiḷuvaṭaṃ kaṇḍakavaṭaṃ (syā॰), parikha’’nti| kauṭṭhakau na hauti| tathaiva ajakāpi pasukāpi uparaupai vihaiṭhainti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kauṭṭhakaṃ apaisiṃ yamakakavāṭaṃ tauraṇaṃ paligha’’nti| kauṭṭhakai tiṇacuṇṇaṃ paripatati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, augumphaitvā ul‍littāvalittaṃ kātuṃ – saitavaṇṇaṃ kāḷavaṇṇaṃ gairukaparikammaṃ mālākammaṃ  latākammaṃ makaradantakaṃ pañ‍capaṭika’’nti| ārāmau cikkhal‍lau hauti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, marumbaṃ upakiritu’’nti| na pariyāpuṇanti…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, padarasilaṃ nikkhipitu’’nti| udakaṃ santiṭṭhati…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, udakaniddhamana’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau saṅghassa atthāya sudhāmattikālaipanaṃ pāsādaṃ kāraitukāmau hauti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau bhagavatā  chadanaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kiṃ ananuñ‍ñāta’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca chadanāni – iṭṭhakāchadanaṃ, silāchadanaṃ, sudhāchadanaṃ, tiṇacchadanaṃ, paṇṇacchadana’’nti|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

2. dutiyabhāṇavārau

anāthapiṇḍikavatthu

304. taina khau pana samayaina anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa bhaginipatikau hauti| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati rājagahaṃ agamāsi kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| taina khau pana samayaina rājagahakaina saiṭṭhinā svātanāya buddhappamukhau saṅghau nimantitau hauti| atha khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī dāsai ca kammakārai kammakarai (sī॰ syā॰) ca āṇāpaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhaṇai, kālassaiva uṭṭhāya yāguyau pacatha, bhattāni pacatha, sūpāni sampādaitha, uttaribhaṅgāni sampādaithā’’ti| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘pubbai khvāyaṃ  gahapati mayi āgatai sabbakic‍cāni nikkhipitvā mamañ‍ñaiva saddhiṃ paṭisammaudati| saudānāyaṃ vikkhittarūpau dāsai ca kammakārai ca āṇāpaisi – ‘taina hi, bhaṇai, kālassaiva uṭṭhāya yāguyau pacatha, bhattāni pacatha, sūpāni sampādaitha, uttaribhaṅgāni  sampādaithā’ti| kiṃ nu khau imassa gahapatissa āvāhau vā bhavissati, vivāhau vā bhavissati, mahāyañ‍ñau vā pac‍cupaṭṭhitau, rājā vā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau nimantitau svātanāya saddhiṃ balakāyainā’’ti?

atha khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī dāsai ca kammakārai ca āṇāpaitvā yaina anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā anāthapiṇḍikaina gahapatinā saddhiṃ paṭisammauditvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau rājagahakaṃ saiṭṭhiṃ anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati aitadavauca – ‘‘pubbai khau tvaṃ, gahapati, mayi āgatai sabbakic‍cāni nikkhipitvā mamañ‍ñaiva saddhiṃ paṭisammaudasi| saudāni tvaṃ vikkhittarūpau dāsai ca kammakārai ca āṇāpaisi – ‘taina hi, bhaṇai, kālassaiva uṭṭhāya yāguyau pacatha, bhattāni pacatha, sūpāni sampādaitha, uttaribhaṅgāni sampādaithā’ti| kiṃ nu khau tai, gahapati, āvāhau vā bhavissati, vivāhau vā bhavissati, mahāyañ‍ñau vā pac‍cupaṭṭhitau, rājā vā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau nimantitau svātanāya saddhiṃ  balakāyainā’’ti? ‘‘na mai, gahapati, āvāhau  vā bhavissati, nāpi vivāhau vā bhavissati, nāpi rājā vā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau nimantitau svātanāya saddhiṃ balakāyaina; api ca mai mahāyañ‍ñau pac‍cupaṭṭhitau; svātanāya buddhappamukhau saṅghau nimantitau’’ti| ‘‘buddhauti tvaṃ, gahapati, vadaisī’’ti? ‘‘buddhau tyāhaṃ, gahapati, vadāmī’’ti| ‘‘buddhauti tvaṃ, gahapati, vadaisī’’ti? ‘‘buddhau tyāhaṃ, gahapati, vadāmī’’ti| ‘‘buddhauti tvaṃ, gahapati, vadaisī’’ti? ‘‘buddhau tyāhaṃ, gahapati, vadāmī’’ti| ‘‘ghausaupi khau aisau, gahapati, dul‍labhau laukasmiṃ yadidaṃ – buddhau buddhauti| sak‍kā nu khau, gahapati, imaṃ kālaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti? ‘‘akālau khau, gahapati, imaṃ kālaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ| svaidāni tvaṃ kālaina taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissasi arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati – svaidānāhaṃ kālaina taṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamissāmi arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhanti – buddhagatāya satiyā nipaj‍jitvā rattiyā sudaṃ tikkhattuṃ vuṭṭhāsi pabhātaṃ mañ‍ñamānau|

305. saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.242 atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina sivakadvāraṃ sīvadvāraṃ (sī॰), sītavanadvāraṃ (syā॰) tainupasaṅkami| amanussā dvāraṃ vivariṃsu| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa  nagaramhā nikkhantassa ālaukau antaradhāyi, andhakārau  pāturahausi, bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau  udapādi; tatauva puna nivattitukāmau ahausi| atha khau sivakau sīvakau (sī॰ syā॰) yakkhau antarahitau saddamanussāvaisi –

‘‘sataṃ hatthī sataṃ assā, sataṃ assatarīrathā|

sataṃ kañ‍ñāsahassāni, āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalā|

aikassa padavītihārassa, kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ sauḷasinti (sī॰ ka॰)||

‘‘abhik‍kama gahapati abhik‍kama gahapati|

abhik‍kantaṃ tai saiyyau nau paṭik‍kanta’’nti||

atha khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa andhakārau antaradhāyi, ālaukau pāturahausi| yaṃ ahausi bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau sau paṭippassambhi| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau…pai॰… anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa ālaukau antaradhāyi, andhakārau pāturahausi, bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ  laumahaṃsau udapādi, tatauva puna nivattitukāmau ahausi| tatiyampi khau sivakau yakkhau antarahitau saddamanussāvaisi –

‘‘sataṃ hatthī sataṃ assā, sataṃ assatarīrathā|

sataṃ kañ‍ñāsahassāni, āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalā|

aikassa padavītihārassa, kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ||

‘‘abhik‍kama gahapati abhik‍kama gahapati,

abhik‍kantaṃ tai saiyyau nau paṭik‍kanta’’nti||

tatiyampi khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa andhakārau antaradhāyi, ālaukau pāturahausi, yaṃ ahausi bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau, sau paṭippassambhi| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina sītavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya ajjhaukāsai caṅkamati| addasā khau bhagavā anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna caṅkamā aurauhitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi sudattā’’ti| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati – nāmaina maṃ bhagavā ālapatīti – haṭṭhau udaggau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhantai, bhagavā sukhaṃ sayitthā’’ti?

saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.242 ‘‘sabbadā vai sukhaṃ saiti, brāhmaṇau parinibbutau|

yau na limpati kāmaisu, sītibhūtau nirūpadhi||

‘‘sabbā āsattiyau chaitvā, vinaiyya hadayai daraṃ|

upasantau sukhaṃ saiti, santiṃ pappuyya caitasā’’ti caitasauti (sī॰ syā॰)||

atha khau bhagavā anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa anupubbiṃ kathaṃ ānupubbikathaṃ (sī॰) kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ, kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā  bhagavā añ‍ñāsi anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ kal‍lacittaṃ muducittaṃ vinīvaraṇacittaṃ udaggacittaṃ pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā taṃ pakāsaisi  – dukkhaṃ, samudayaṃ, niraudhaṃ, maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ  apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya, aivamaiva anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ bhantai! saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti – aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammañ‍ca, bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gataṃ| adhivāsaitu ca mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| assausi  khau  rājagahakau saiṭṭhī – ‘‘anāthapiṇḍikaina kira gahapatinā svātanāya buddhappamukhau saṅghau nimantitau’’ti|

306. atha khau rājagahakau saiṭṭhī anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tayā kira, gahapati, svātanāya buddhappamukhau saṅghau nimantitau| tvañ‍cāsi āgantukau| daimi tai, gahapati, vaiyyāyikaṃ yaina tvaṃ buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa bhattaṃ karaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, gahapati atthi mai vaiyyāyikaṃ yaināhaṃ buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa bhattaṃ karissāmī’’ti|

assausi khau rājagahakau naigamau – ‘‘anāthapiṇḍikaina kira gahapatinā svātanāya buddhappamukhau saṅghau nimantitau’’ti| atha khau rājagahakau naigamau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tayā kira, gahapati, svātanāya buddhappamukhau saṅghau nimantitau| tvañ‍cāsi āgantukau| daimi tai, gahapati, vaiyyāyikaṃ yaina tvaṃ buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa bhattaṃ karaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ ayya; atthi mai vaiyyāyikaṃ yaināhaṃ buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa bhattaṃ karissāmī’’ti|

assausi khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau – ‘‘anāthapiṇḍikaina kira gahapatinā svātanāya buddhappamukhau saṅghau nimantitau’’ti| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ  gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tayā kira, gahapati, svātanāya buddhappamukhau saṅghau nimantitau| tvañ‍cāsi āgantukau| daimi tai, gahapati, vaiyyāyikaṃ yaina tvaṃ buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa bhattaṃ karaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ  daiva; atthi mai vaiyyāyikaṃ yaināhaṃ buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa bhattaṃ karissāmī’’ti|

atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa nivaisanai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā  bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina rājagahakassa saiṭṭhissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā, bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ, aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ vassāvāsaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| ‘‘suñ‍ñāgārai khau, gahapati, tathāgatā abhiramantī’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ bhagavā, añ‍ñātaṃ sugatā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

307. taina khau pana samayaina anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati bahumittau hauti bahusahāyau ādaiyyavācau| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati rājagahai taṃ karaṇīyaṃ tīraitvā yaina sāvatthi taina pak‍kāmi| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati antarāmaggai manussai āṇāpaisi – ‘‘ārāmai, ayyā, karautha, vihārai patiṭṭhāpaitha, dānāni paṭṭhapaitha| buddhau laukai uppan‍nau| sau ca mayā bhagavā nimantitau iminā maggaina āgacchissatī’’ti| atha khau tai manussā anāthapiṇḍikaina gahapatinā uyyaujitā ārāmai akaṃsu, vihārai patiṭṭhāpaisuṃ, dānāni paṭṭhapaisuṃ|

atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati sāvatthiṃ gantvā samantā sāvatthiṃ anuvilaukaisi – ‘‘kattha nu khau bhagavā viharaiyya? yaṃ assa gāmatau naiva atidūrai na ac‍cāsan‍nai, gamanāgamanasampan‍naṃ, atthikānaṃ atthikānaṃ manussānaṃ abhik‍kamanīyaṃ, divā appākiṇṇaṃ, rattiṃ appasaddaṃ, appanigghausaṃ, vijanavātaṃ, manussarāhassaiyyakaṃ, paṭisal‍lānasāruppa’’nti|

addasā  khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati jaitassa kumārassa rājakumārassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) uyyānaṃ – gāmatau naiva atidūrai na ac‍cāsan‍nai, gamanāgamanasampan‍naṃ, atthikānaṃ atthikānaṃ manussānaṃ abhik‍kamanīyaṃ, divā appākiṇṇaṃ, rattiṃ appasaddaṃ, appanigghausaṃ, vijanavātaṃ, manussarāhassaiyyakaṃ, paṭisal‍lānasāruppaṃ| disvāna yaina jaitau kumārau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā jaitaṃ kumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘daihi mai, ayyaputta, uyyānaṃ ārāmaṃ kātu’’nti kaituṃ (vajīrabuddhiṭīkāyaṃ)| ‘‘adaiyyau, gahapati, ārāmau api kauṭisantharainā’’ti| ‘‘gahitau, ayyaputta, ārāmau’’ti| ‘‘na, gahapati, gahitau ārāmau’’ti| gahitau na gahitauti vauhārikai mahāmattai pucchiṃsu| mahāmattā aivamāhaṃsu  – ‘‘yatau tayā, ayyaputta, agghau katau, gahitau ārāmau’’ti| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau  gahapati sakaṭaihi hirañ‍ñaṃ nibbāhāpaitvā jaitavanaṃ kauṭisantharaṃ santharāpaisi| sakiṃ nīhaṭaṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ thaukassa aukāsassa kauṭṭhakasāmantā nappahauti| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati manussai āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gacchatha, bhaṇai, hirañ‍ñaṃ āharatha; imaṃ aukāsaṃ santharissāmā’’ti|

atha khau jaitassa kumārassa aitadahausi – ‘‘na khau idaṃ aurakaṃ bhavissati, yathāyaṃ gahapati tāva bahuṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ paric‍cajatī’’ti| anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, gahapati; mā taṃ aukāsaṃ santharāpaisi| daihi mai aitaṃ aukāsaṃ| mamaitaṃ dānaṃ bhavissatī’’ti| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati – ayaṃ khau jaitau kumārau abhiñ‍ñātau ñātamanussau; mahatthikau khau pana aivarūpānaṃ ñātamanussānaṃ imasmiṃ dhammavinayai pasādauti – taṃ aukāsaṃ jaitassa kumārassa pādāsi adāsi (syā॰)| atha khau jaitau kumārau tasmiṃ aukāsai kauṭṭhakaṃ māpaisi|

atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati jaitavanai vihārai kārāpaisi, parivaiṇāni kārāpaisi, kauṭṭhakai kārāpaisi, upaṭṭhānasālāyau kārāpaisi, aggisālāyau kārāpaisi, kappiyakuṭiyau kārāpaisi, vac‍cakuṭiyau kārāpaisi, caṅkamai kārāpaisi, caṅkamanasālāyau kārāpaisi, udapānai kārāpaisi, udapānasālāyau kārāpaisi, jantāgharai kārāpaisi, jantāgharasālāyau kārāpaisi, paukkharaṇiyau kārāpaisi, maṇḍapai  kārāpaisi|

navakammadānaṃ

308. atha khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina vaisālī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina vaisālī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina manussā sak‍kac‍caṃ navakammaṃ  karaunti| yaipi bhikkhū navakammaṃ adhiṭṭhainti taipi sak‍kac‍caṃ upaṭṭhainti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina| atha khau añ‍ñatarassa daliddassa tun‍navāyassa aitadahausi – ‘‘na khau idaṃ aurakaṃ bhavissati, yathayimai manussā sak‍kac‍caṃ navakammaṃ karaunti; yaṃnūnāhampi navakammaṃ karaiyya’’nti| atha khau sau daliddau tun‍navāyau sāmaṃ cikkhal‍laṃ madditvā iṭṭhakāyau cinitvā kuṭṭaṃ uṭṭhāpaisi| taina akusalakaina citā vaṅkā bhitti paripati| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau sau daliddau tun‍navāyau sāmaṃ cikkhal‍laṃ madditvā iṭṭhakāyau cinitvā kuṭṭaṃ uṭṭhāpaisi| taina akusalakaina citā vaṅkā bhitti paripati| atha khau sau daliddau tun‍navāyau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘yai imaisaṃ samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ  dainti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ, tai imai auvadanti anusāsanti, taisañ‍ca navakammaṃ adhiṭṭhainti| ahaṃ  panamhi daliddau| na maṃ kauci auvadati vā anusāsati vā navakammaṃ vā adhiṭṭhaitī’’ti| assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa daliddassa tun‍navāyassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, navakammaṃ dātuṃ| navakammikau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ussuk‍kaṃ āpaj‍jissati – ‘kinti nu khau vihārau khippaṃ pariyausānaṃ gacchaiyyā’ti; khaṇḍaṃ phul‍laṃ paṭisaṅkharissati| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, dātabbaṃ| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau, yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

309. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa gahapatinau vihāraṃ itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau navakammaṃ dadaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmassa gahapatinau vihāraṃ itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau navakammaṃ daiti| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa gahapatinau vihāraṃ itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau navakammassa dānaṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘din‍nau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmassa gahapatinau vihārau itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau navakammaṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

aggāsanādianujānanaṃ

310. atha  khau bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina sāvatthi taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  antaivāsikā bhikkhū buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa puratau puratau gantvā vihārai pariggaṇhanti, saiyyāyau pariggaṇhanti – idaṃ amhākaṃ upajjhāyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ ācariyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ bhavissatī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau gantvā vihāraisu pariggahitaisu, saiyyāsu pariggahitāsu, saiyyaṃ alabhamānau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi| atha khau bhagavā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya uk‍kāsi| āyasmāpi sāriputtau uk‍kāsi| ‘‘kau aitthā’’ti? ‘‘ahaṃ, bhagavā, sāriputtau’’ti| ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, sāriputtaṃ, idha nisin‍nau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ antaivāsikā bhikkhū buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa puratau puratau gantvā vihārai pariggaṇhanti, saiyyāyau pariggaṇhanti – idaṃ amhākaṃ upajjhāyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ ācariyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa puratau puratau gantvā vihārai pariggahaissanti, saiyyāyau pariggahaissanti – idaṃ amhākaṃ upajjhāyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ ācariyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ bhavissatīti! naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ  vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kau, bhikkhavai, arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti?

aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, khattiyakulā pabbajitau sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, brāhmaṇakulā pabbajitau sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, gahapatikulā pabbajitau sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai  bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, suttantikau sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, vinayadharau sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ  aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, dhammakathikau sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhī sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, dutiyassa jhānassa lābhī sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, tatiyassa jhānassa lābhī sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, catutthassa jhānassa lābhī sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, sautāpan‍nau sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, sakadāgāmī…pai॰… yau, bhagavā, anāgāmī…pai॰… yau, bhagavā, arahā sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, taivij‍jau sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti| aikac‍cai bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yau, bhagavā, chaḷabhiñ‍ñau sau arahati aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍa’’nti|

311. atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, himavantapadaisai himavantapassai (sī॰ syā॰) mahānigraudhau ahausi| taṃ tayau sahāyā upanissāya vihariṃsu – tittirau ca, mak‍kaṭau ca, hatthināgau ca| tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ agāravā appatissā asabhāgavuttikā viharanti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ sahāyānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ahau nūna mayaṃ jānaiyyāma yaṃ amhākaṃ jātiyā mahantataraṃ taṃ mayaṃ sak‍karaiyyāma garuṃ karaiyyāma mānaiyyāma pūjaiyyāma, tassa ca mayaṃ auvādai tiṭṭhaiyyāmā’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, tittirau ca mak‍kaṭau ca hatthināgaṃ pucchiṃsu – ‘tvaṃ, samma, kiṃ paurāṇaṃ sarasī’ti? ‘yadāhaṃ, sammā, pautau haumi, imaṃ nigraudhaṃ antarā  satthīnaṃ antarāsatthikaṃ (sī॰) karitvā atik‍kamāmi, aggaṅkurakaṃ mai udaraṃ chupati| imāhaṃ, sammā, paurāṇaṃ sarāmī’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, tittirau ca hatthināgau ca mak‍kaṭaṃ pucchiṃsu – ‘tvaṃ, samma, kiṃ paurāṇaṃ sarasī’ti? ‘yadāhaṃ, sammā, chāpau haumi, chamāyaṃ nisīditvā imassa nigraudhassa aggaṅkurakaṃ khādāmi| imāhaṃ, sammā, paurāṇaṃ sarāmī’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, mak‍kaṭau ca hatthināgau ca tittiraṃ pucchiṃsu – ‘tvaṃ, samma, kiṃ paurāṇaṃ sarasī’ti? ‘amukasmiṃ, sammā, aukāsai mahānigraudhau ahausi| tatau ahaṃ phalaṃ bhakkhitvā imasmiṃ aukāsai vac‍caṃ akāsiṃ; tassāyaṃ nigraudhau jātau| tadāhaṃ, sammā, jātiyā mahantatarau’ti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, mak‍kaṭau ca hatthināgau ca tittiraṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘tvaṃ, samma, amhākaṃ jātiyā mahantatarau| taṃ mayaṃ sak‍karissāma garuṃ karissāma mānaissāma pūjaissāma, tuyhañ‍ca mayaṃ auvādai patiṭṭhissāmā’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, tittirau mak‍kaṭañ‍ca hatthināgañ‍ca pañ‍casu sīlaisu samādapaisi, attanā ca pañ‍casu sīlaisu samādāya vattati| tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sagāravā sappatissā sabhāgavuttikā viharitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jiṃsu| aivaṃ khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, tittiriyaṃ nāma brahmacariyaṃ ahausi|

jā॰ 1.1.37 ‘‘yai vuḍḍhamapacāyanti, narā dhammassa kauvidā|

diṭṭhai dhammai ca pāsaṃsā, samparāyai ca suggatī’’ti||

‘‘tai hi nāma, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sagāravā sappatissā sabhāgavuttikā viharissanti| idha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, saubhaitha yaṃ tumhai aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitā samānā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ agāravā appatissā asabhāgavuttikā viharaiyyātha? naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yathāvuḍḍhaṃ abhivādanaṃ, pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ, añ‍jalikammaṃ, sāmīcikammaṃ, aggāsanaṃ, aggaudakaṃ, aggapiṇḍaṃ| na ca, bhikkhavai, saṅghikaṃ yathāvuḍḍhaṃ paṭibāhitabbaṃ| yau paṭibāhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

avandiyādipuggalā

312. ‘‘dasayimai, bhikkhavai, avandiyā – purai upasampan‍naina pacchā upasampan‍nau avandiyau, anupasampan‍nau avandiyau, nānāsaṃvāsakau vuḍḍhatarau adhammavādī avandiyau, mātugāmau avandiyau, paṇḍakau avandiyau, pārivāsikau avandiyau, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahau avandiyau, mānattārahau avandiyau, mānattacārikau avandiyau, abbhānārahau avandiyau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, dasa avandiyā|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, vandiyā – pacchā upasampan‍naina purai upasampan‍nau vandiyau, nānāsaṃvāsakau vuḍḍhatarau dhammavādī vandiyau, sadaivakai bhikkhavai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vandiyau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau vandiyā’’ti|

āsanappaṭibāhanapaṭikkhaipaṃ

313. taina  khau pana samayaina manussā saṅghaṃ uddissa maṇḍapai paṭiyādainti, santharai paṭiyādainti, aukāsai paṭiyādainti| chabbaggiyānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ antaivāsikā bhikkhū – ‘saṅghikañ‍ñaiva bhagavatā yathāvuḍḍhaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, nau uddissakata’nti buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa puratau puratau gantvā maṇḍapaipi pariggaṇhanti, santharaipi pariggaṇhanti, aukāsaipi pariggaṇhanti – idaṃ amhākaṃ upajjhāyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ ācariyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ bhavissatīti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau gantvā maṇḍapaisu pariggahitaisu, santharaisu pariggahitaisu, aukāsaisu pariggahitaisu, aukāsaṃ alabhamānau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi| atha khau bhagavā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya uk‍kāsi| āyasmāpi sāriputtau uk‍kāsi| ‘‘kau aitthā’’ti? ‘‘ahaṃ, bhagavā, sāriputtau’’ti| ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, sāriputta, idha nisin‍nau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ san‍nipātāpaitvā bhikkhū paṭipucchi – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ antaivāsikā bhikkhū – ‘saṅghikañ‍ñaiva bhagavatā yathāvuḍḍhaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, nau uddissakata’nti buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa puratau puratau gantvā  maṇḍapai pariggaṇhanti, santharai pariggaṇhanti, aukāsai pariggaṇhanti – idaṃ amhākaṃ upajjhāyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ ācariyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, uddissakatampi yathāvuḍḍhaṃ paṭibāhaitabbaṃ| yau  paṭibāhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

gihivikataanujānanaṃ

314. taina khau pana samayaina manussā bhattaggai antaragharai uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanāni pañ‍ñapainti, saiyyathidaṃ – āsandiṃ, pal‍laṅkaṃ, gaunakaṃ, cittakaṃ, paṭikaṃ, paṭalikaṃ, tūlikaṃ, vikatikaṃ, uddalaumiṃ, aikantalaumiṃ, kaṭṭissaṃ, kausaiyyaṃ kausaiyyaṃ kambalaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), kuttakaṃ, hatthattharaṃ, assattharaṃ, rathattharaṃ, ajinapavaiṇiṃ, kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇaṃ, sauttaracchadaṃ, ubhataulauhitakūpadhānaṃ| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nābhinisīdanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ṭhapaitvā tīṇi – āsandiṃ, pal‍laṅkaṃ, tūlikaṃ – gihivikataṃ gihivikaṭaṃ (sī॰ ka॰), avasaisaṃ gihivikaṭaṃ (syā॰) abhinisīdituṃ, natvaiva abhinipaj‍jitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina manussā bhattaggai antaragharai tūlaunaddhaṃ mañ‍campi pīṭhampi pañ‍ñapainti| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā nābhinisīdanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gihivikataṃ abhinisīdituṃ, natvaiva abhinipaj‍jitu’’nti|

jaitavanavihārānumaudanā

315. atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sāvatthi tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati  jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaitvā sampavāraitvā, bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ, aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathāhaṃ, bhantai, jaitavanai paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti? ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, gahapati, jaitavanaṃ āgatānāgatassa cātuddisassa saṅghassa patiṭṭhapaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati bhagavatau paṭissutvā jaitavanaṃ āgatānāgatassa cātuddisassa saṅghassa patiṭṭhāpaisi|

atha khau bhagavā anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumaudi –

‘‘sītaṃ uṇhaṃ paṭihanti, tatau vāḷamigāni ca|

sarīsapai  ca makasai, sisirai cāpi vuṭṭhiyau||

‘‘tatau vātātapau ghaurau, sañ‍jātau paṭihañ‍ñati|

laiṇatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca, jhāyituñ‍ca vipassituṃ||

‘‘vihāradānaṃ  saṅghassa, aggaṃ buddhaina vaṇṇitaṃ|

tasmā hi paṇḍitau pausau, sampassaṃ atthamattanau||

‘‘vihārai kārayai rammai, vāsayaittha bahussutai|

taisaṃ an‍nañ‍ca pānañ‍ca, vatthasaināsanāni ca|

dadaiyya ujubhūtaisu, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

‘‘tai tassa dhammaṃ daisainti, sabbadukkhāpanūdanaṃ|

yaṃ sau dhammaṃ idhañ‍ñāya, parinibbāti anāsavau’’ti||

atha  khau bhagavā anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ imāhi gāthāhi anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi|

āsanappaṭibāhanādi

316. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa ājīvakasāvakassa mahāmattassa saṅghabhattaṃ hauti| āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau pacchā āgantvā vippakatabhaujanaṃ ānantarikaṃ bhikkhuṃ vuṭṭhāpaisi| bhattaggaṃ kaulāhalaṃ ahausi| atha khau sau mahāmattau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā pacchā āgantvā vippakatabhaujanaṃ ānantarikaṃ bhikkhuṃ vuṭṭhāpaissanti! bhattaggaṃ kaulāhalaṃ ahausi| nanu nāma labbhā añ‍ñatrāpi nisin‍naina yāvadatthaṃ  bhuñ‍jitu’’nti? assausuṃ khau bhikkhū tassa mahāmattassa ujjhāyantassa khiyyantassa vipācaintassa| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau pacchā āgantvā vippakatabhaujanaṃ ānantarikaṃ bhikkhuṃ vuṭṭhāpaissati! bhattaggaṃ kaulāhalaṃ ahausī’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, pacchā āgantvā vippakatabhaujanaṃ ānantarikaṃ bhikkhuṃ vuṭṭhāpaisi, bhattaggaṃ kaulāhalaṃ ahausī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, pacchā āgantvā vippakatabhaujanaṃ ānantarikaṃ bhikkhuṃ vuṭṭhāpaissasi? bhattaggaṃ kaulāhalaṃ ahausi| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, vippakatabhaujanau bhaujanau ānantarikau (syā॰) bhikkhu vuṭṭhāpaitabbau| yau vuṭṭhāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| sacai vuṭṭhāpaiti, pavāritau ca hauti, ‘gaccha udakaṃ āharā’ti vattabbau| aivañ‍caitaṃ  labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, sādhukaṃ sitthāni gilitvā vuḍḍhatarassa bhikkhunau āsanaṃ dātabbaṃ| na tvaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, kainaci pariyāyaina vuḍḍhatarassa bhikkhunau āsanaṃ paṭibāhitabbanti vadāmi| yau paṭibāhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassāti’’|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū gilānai bhikkhū vuṭṭhāpainti| gilānā aivaṃ vadainti – ‘‘na mayaṃ, āvusau, sak‍kauma vuṭṭhātuṃ, gilānāmhā’’ti| ‘‘mayaṃ āyasmantai vuṭṭhāpaissāmā’’ti pariggahaitvā vuṭṭhāpaitvā ṭhitakai muñ‍canti| gilānā mucchitā papatanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, gilānau vuṭṭhāpaitabbau| yau vuṭṭhāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū  ‘gilānā mayamhā avuṭṭhāpanīyā’ti varasaiyyāyau palibuddhainti palibundhanti (ka॰)| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānassa patirūpaṃ saiyyaṃ dātu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū laisakappaina saināsanaṃ paṭibāhanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, laisakappaina saināsanaṃ paṭibāhitabbaṃ| yau paṭibāhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

pāci॰ 122 taina khau pana samayaina sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū añ‍ñataraṃ pac‍cantimaṃ mahāvihāraṃ paṭisaṅkharaunti – ‘idha mayaṃ vassaṃ vasissāmā’ti| addasaṃsu addasāsuṃ (ka॰) khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū vihāraṃ añ‍ñataraṃ vihāraṃ (ka॰) paṭisaṅkharauntai| disvāna aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘imai, āvusau, sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū vihāraṃ paṭisaṅkharaunti| handa nai vuṭṭhāpaissāmā’’ti| aikac‍cai  aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘āgamaithāvusau, yāva paṭisaṅkharaunti, paṭisaṅkhatai vuṭṭhāpaissāmā’’ti| atha khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘uṭṭhaithāvusau, amhākaṃ vihārau pāpuṇātī’’ti| ‘‘nanu, āvusau, paṭikac‍caiva ācikkhitabbaṃ? mayañ‍cañ‍ñaṃ paṭisaṅkharaiyyāmā’’ti| ‘‘nanu, āvusau, saṅghikau vihārau’’ti? ‘‘āmāvusau, saṅghikau vihārau’’ti| ‘‘uṭṭhaithāvusau, amhākaṃ vihārau pāpuṇātī’’ti| ‘‘mahal‍lakau, āvusau, vihārau; tumhaipi vasatha, mayampi vasissāmā’’ti| ‘‘uṭṭhaithāvusau, amhākaṃ vihārau pāpuṇātī’’ti kupitā anattamanā gīvāyaṃ gahaitvā nik‍kaḍḍhanti| tai nik‍kaḍḍhiyamānā raudanti| bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kissa tumhai, āvusau, raudathā’’ti? ‘‘imai, āvusau, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā  anattamanā amhai saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhantī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā bhikkhū saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, tumhai bhikkhavai, kupitā anattamanā saṅghikā vihārā bhikkhū nik‍kaḍḍhathā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, kupitaina  anattamanaina bhikkhu saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhitabbau| yau nik‍kaḍḍhaiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saināsanaṃ gāhaitu’’nti|

saināsanaggāhāpakasammuti

317. atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘kaina nu khau saināsanaṃ gāhaitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ …pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhikkhuṃ saināsanaggāhāpakaṃ samman‍nituṃ – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, gahitāgahitañ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau –

‘‘paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau, yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ saināsanaggāhāpakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ saināsanaggāhāpakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau saināsanaggāhāpakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu saināsanaggāhāpakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

318. atha khau saināsanaggāhāpakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau saināsanaṃ gāhaitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ bhikkhū gaṇaituṃ, bhikkhū gaṇaitvā saiyyā gaṇaituṃ, saiyyā gaṇaitvā saiyyaggaina gāhaitu’’nti| saiyyaggaina gāhaintā saiyyā ussārayiṃsu…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vihāraggaina gāhaitu’’nti| vihāraggaina gāhaintā vihārā ussārayiṃsu…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, parivaiṇaggaina gāhaitu’’nti| parivaiṇaggaina gāhaintā parivaiṇā ussārayiṃsu…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, anubhāgampi dātuṃ| gahitai anubhāgai añ‍ñau bhikkhu āgacchati, na akāmā dātabbau’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū nissīmai ṭhitassa saināsanaṃ gāhainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, nissīmai ṭhitassa saināsanaṃ gāhaitabbaṃ| yau gāhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū saināsanaṃ gahaitvā sabbakālaṃ paṭibāhanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, saināsanaṃ gahaitvā sabbakālaṃ paṭibāhaitabbaṃ| yau paṭibāhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vassānaṃ taimāsaṃ  paṭibāhituṃ, utukālaṃ pana na paṭibāhitu’’nti|

atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kati nu khau saināsanaggāhā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘tayau mai, bhikkhavai, saināsanaggāhā – purimakau, pacchimakau, antarāmuttakau| aparaj‍jugatāya āsāḷhiyā purimakau gāhaitabbau| māsagatāya āsāḷhiyā pacchimakau gāhaitabbau| aparaj‍jugatāya pavāraṇāya āyatiṃ vassāvāsatthāya antarāmuttakau gāhaitabbau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau saināsanaggāhā’’ti|

dutiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

3. tatiyabhāṇavārau

319. taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau sāvatthiyaṃ saināsanaṃ gahaitvā añ‍ñataraṃ gāmakāvāsaṃ agamāsi| tatthapi saināsanaṃ aggahaisi| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ, āvusau, āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau bhaṇḍanakārakau kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau| sacāyaṃ idha vassaṃ vasissati, sabbaiva mayaṃ na phāsu bhavissāma| handa naṃ pucchāmā’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ  upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘nanu tayā, āvusau upananda, sāvatthiyaṃ saināsanaṃ gahita’’nti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, āvusau upananda, aikau dvai paṭibāhasī’’ti? ‘‘idhadānāhaṃ āvusau, muñ‍cāmi; tattha gaṇhāmī’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau  aikau dvai paṭibāhaissatī’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, upananda, aikau dvai paṭibāhasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, aikau dvai paṭibāhissasi? tattha tayā, maughapurisa, gahitaṃ idha muttaṃ, idha tayā gahitaṃ tatra muttaṃ| aivaṃ khau tvaṃ, maughapurisa, ubhayattha paribāhirau| naitaṃ, maughapurisa, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, aikaina dvai paṭibāhaitabbā| yau paṭibāhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

320. pāci॰ 438 taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ anaikapariyāyaina vinayakathaṃ kathaiti, vinayassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, vinayapariyattiyā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ādissa ādissa āyasmatau upālissa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavā khau anaikapariyāyaina vinayakathaṃ kathaiti, vinayassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, vinayapariyattiyā vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, ādissa ādissa āyasmatau upālissa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| handa mayaṃ, āvusau, āyasmatau upālissa santikai vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇāmā’’ti| taidha tai ca (syā॰ ka॰) bahū bhikkhū thairā ca navā ca majjhimā ca āyasmatau upālissa  santikai vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇanti| āyasmā upāli ṭhitakauva uddisati thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ gāravaina| thairāpi bhikkhū ṭhitakāva uddisāpainti dhammagāravaina| tattha thairā caiva bhikkhū kilamanti, āyasmā ca upāli kilamati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, navakaina bhikkhunā  uddisantaina samakai vā āsanai nisīdituṃ, uc‍catarai vā dhammagāravaina; thairaina bhikkhunā uddisāpaintaina samakai vā āsanai nisīdituṃ, nīcatarai vā dhammagāravainā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bahū bhikkhū āyasmatau upālissa santikai ṭhitakā uddaisaṃ paṭimānaintā kilamanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, samānāsanikaihi saha nisīditu’’nti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau samānāsanikau hautī’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tivassantaraina saha nisīditu’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū samānāsanikā mañ‍cai aikamañ‍cai (syā॰) nisīditvā mañ‍caṃ bhindiṃsu, pīṭhai aikapīṭhai (syā॰) nisīditvā pīṭhaṃ bhindiṃsu| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tivaggassa  mañ‍caṃ, tivaggassa pīṭha’’nti| tivaggaupi mañ‍cai nisīditvā mañ‍caṃ bhindi, pīṭhai nisīditvā pīṭhaṃ bhindi…pai॰… ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, duvaggassa mañ‍caṃ, duvaggassa pīṭha’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū asamānāsanikaihi saha dīghāsanai nisīdituṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ṭhapaitvā paṇḍakaṃ, mātugāmaṃ, ubhataubyañ‍janakaṃ, asamānāsanikaihi saha dīghāsanai nisīditu’’nti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kittakaṃ pacchimaṃ nu khau dīghāsanaṃ hautī’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ tiṇṇaṃ pahauti, aittakaṃ pacchimaṃ dīghāsana’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina visākhā migāramātā saṅghassa atthāya sāḷindaṃ pāsādaṃ kārāpaitukāmā hauti hatthinakhakaṃ| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau bhagavatā pāsādaparibhaugau anuñ‍ñātau kiṃ ananuñ‍ñātau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sabbaṃ pāsādaparibhauga’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa ayyikā kālaṅkatā hauti| tassa kālaṅkiriyāya saṅghassa bahuṃ akappiyabhaṇḍaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti, saiyyathidaṃ – āsandi, pal‍laṅkau, gaunakau, cittakau, paṭikā, paṭalikā, tūlikā, vikatikā, uddalaumī, aikantalaumī, kaṭṭissaṃ, kausaiyyaṃ, kuttakaṃ, hatthattharaṃ, assattharaṃ, rathattharaṃ, ajinappavaiṇi, kadalimigappavarapac‍cattharaṇaṃ, sauttaracchadaṃ, ubhataulauhitakūpadhānaṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āsandiyā pādai chinditvā  paribhuñ‍jituṃ, pal‍laṅkassa vāḷai bhinditvā paribhuñ‍jituṃ, tūlikaṃ vijaṭaitvā bibbauhanaṃ kātuṃ, avasaisaṃ bhūmattharaṇaṃ summattharaṇaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) kātu’’nti|

avissaj‍jiyavatthu

321. taina  khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyā avidūrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ gāmakāvāsai āvāsikā bhikkhū upaddutā haunti āgantukagamikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaintā| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘aitarahi khau mayaṃ, āvusau, upaddutā āgantukagamikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaintā| handa mayaṃ, āvusau, sabbaṃ saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ aikassa daima| tassa  santakaṃ paribhuñ‍jissāmā’’ti| tai sabbaṃ saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ aikassa adaṃsu| āgantukā bhikkhū tai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘amhākaṃ, āvusau, saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaithā’’ti| ‘‘natthāvusau, saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ; sabbaṃ amhaihi aikassa din‍na’’nti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau, saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ vissaj‍jaithā’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhū saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ vissaj‍jaissantī’’ti vissaj‍jissantīti (ka॰)! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ vissaj‍jaintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ vissaj‍jaissanti? naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, avissaj‍jiyāni, na vissaj‍jaitabbāni na vissaj‍jitabbāni (ka॰), saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vissaj‍jitānipi avissaj‍jitāni haunti| yau vissaj‍jaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| katamāni pañ‍ca? ārāmau, ārāmavatthu – idaṃ paṭhamaṃ avissaj‍jiyaṃ, na vissaj‍jaitabbaṃ, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vissaj‍jitampi avissaj‍jitaṃ hauti| yau vissaj‍jaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

‘‘vihārau, vihāravatthu – idaṃ dutiyaṃ avissaj‍jiyaṃ, na vissaj‍jaitabbaṃ, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggal‍laina vā| vissaj‍jitampi avissaj‍jitaṃ hauti| yau vissaj‍jaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

‘‘mañ‍cau, pīṭhaṃ, bhisi, bibbauhanaṃ – idaṃ tatiyaṃ avissaj‍jiyaṃ, na vissaj‍jaitabbaṃ, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vissaj‍jitampi avissaj‍jitaṃ hauti| yau vissaj‍jaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

‘‘lauhakumbhī, lauhabhāṇakaṃ, lauhavārakau, lauhakaṭāhaṃ, vāsi, parasu pharasu (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), kuṭhārī kudhārī (ka॰), kudālau, nikhādanaṃ – idaṃ catutthaṃ avissaj‍jiyaṃ, na vissaj‍jaitabbaṃ, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vissaj‍jitampi avissaj‍jitaṃ hauti| yau vissaj‍jaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

‘‘val‍li, vaiḷu, muñ‍jaṃ, pabbajaṃ  babbajaṃ (sī॰), tiṇaṃ, mattikā, dārubhaṇḍaṃ, mattikābhaṇḍaṃ – idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ  avissaj‍jiyaṃ, na vissaj‍jaitabbaṃ, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vissaj‍jitampi avissaj‍jitaṃ hauti| yau vissaj‍jaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca avissaj‍jiyāni, na vissaj‍jaitabbāni, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vissaj‍jitānipi avissaj‍jitāni haunti| yau vissaj‍jaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

avaibhaṅgiyavatthu

322. atha khau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā  yaina kīṭāgiri taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sāriputtamauggal‍lānaihi ca| assausuṃ khau assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū – ‘‘bhagavā kira kīṭāgiriṃ āgacchati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sāriputtamauggal‍lānaihi ca’’| ‘‘handa mayaṃ, āvusau, sabbaṃ saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ bhājaima| pāpicchā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā; na mayaṃ taisaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmā’’ti, tai sabbaṃ saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ bhājaisuṃ| atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina kīṭāgiri tadavasari| atha khau bhagavā sambahulai bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘gacchatha tumhai, bhikkhavai; assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaitha – ‘bhagavā, āvusau, āgacchati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sāriputtamauggal‍lānaihi ca| bhagavatau ca, āvusau, saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitha, bhikkhusaṅghassa ca, sāriputtamauggal‍lānānañ‍cā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘bhagavā, āvusau, āgacchati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sāriputtamauggal‍lānaihi ca| bhagavatau ca, āvusau, saināsanaṃ  pañ‍ñapaitha, bhikkhusaṅghassa ca, sāriputtamauggal‍lānānañ‍cā’’ti| ‘‘natthāvusau, saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ| sabbaṃ amhaihi bhājitaṃ| svāgataṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau| yasmiṃ vihārai bhagavā icchissati tasmiṃ vihārai vasissati| pāpicchā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā| na mayaṃ taisaṃ saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau, saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ bhājitthā’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ bhājaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… ‘‘kathañhi nāma  tai, bhikkhavai, maughapurisā saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ bhājaissanti? naitaṃ, bhikkhavai, appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, avaibhaṅgiyāni avaibhaṅgikāni (ka॰), na vibhajitabbāni, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina  vā| vibhattānipi avibhattāni haunti| yau vibhajaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| katamāni pañ‍ca? ārāmau, ārāmavatthu – idaṃ paṭhamaṃ avaibhaṅgiyaṃ, na vibhajitabbaṃ, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vibhattampi avibhattaṃ hauti| yau vibhajaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

‘‘vihārau, vihāravatthu – idaṃ dutiyaṃ avaibhaṅgiyaṃ, na vibhajitabbaṃ, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vibhattampi avibhattaṃ hauti| yau vibhajaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

‘‘mañ‍cau, pīṭhaṃ, bhisi, bibbauhanaṃ – idaṃ tatiyaṃ avaibhaṅgiyaṃ, na vibhajitabbaṃ, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vibhattampi avibhattaṃ hauti| yau vibhajaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

‘‘lauhakumbhī, lauhabhāṇakaṃ, lauhavārakau, lauhakaṭāhaṃ, vāsī, parasu, kuṭhārī, kudālau, nikhādanaṃ – idaṃ catutthaṃ avaibhaṅgiyaṃ, na vibhajitabbaṃ, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vibhattampi avibhattaṃ hauti| yau vibhajaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa|

‘‘val‍lī, vaiḷu, muñ‍jaṃ, pabbajaṃ, tiṇaṃ, mattikā, dārubhaṇḍaṃ, mattikābhaṇḍaṃ – idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ avaibhaṅgiyaṃ, na vibhajitabbaṃ, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vibhattampi  avibhattaṃ hauti| yau vibhajaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca avaibhaṅgiyāni, na vibhajitabbāni, saṅghaina vā gaṇaina vā puggalaina vā| vibhattānipi avibhattāni haunti| yau vibhajaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassā’’ti|

navakammadānakathā

323. atha  khau bhagavā kīṭāgirismiṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina āḷavī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina āḷavī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai| taina khau pana samayaina āḷavakā āḷavikā (syā॰ ka॰) bhikkhū aivarūpāni navakammāni  dainti – piṇḍanikkhaipanamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; kuṭṭalaipanamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; dvāraṭṭhapanamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; aggaḷavaṭṭikaraṇamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; ālaukasandhikaraṇamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; saitavaṇṇakaraṇamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; kāḷavaṇṇakaraṇamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; gairukaparikammakaraṇamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; chādanamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; bandhanamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; bhaṇḍikāṭṭhapanamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; khaṇḍaphul‍lapaṭisaṅkharaṇamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; paribhaṇḍakaraṇamattainapi navakammaṃ dainti; vīsativassikampi navakammaṃ dainti; tiṃsavassikampi navakammaṃ dainti; yāvajīvikampi navakammaṃ dainti; dhūmakālikampi pariyausitaṃ vihāraṃ navakammaṃ dainti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āḷavakā bhikkhū aivarūpāni navakammāni dassanti – piṇḍanikkhaipanamattainapi navakammaṃ dassanti; kuṭṭalaipanamattainapi… dvāraṭṭhapanamattainapi … aggaḷavaṭṭikaraṇamattainapi… ālaukasandhikaraṇamattainapi… saitavaṇṇakaraṇamattainapi… kāḷavaṇṇakaraṇamattainapi… gairukaparikammakaraṇamattainapi… chādainamattainapi… bandhanamattainapi… bhaṇḍikāḷapanamattainapi… khaṇḍaphul‍lapaṭisaṅkharaṇamattainapi… paribhaṇḍakaraṇamattainapi… visativassikampi… tiṃsavassikampi… yāvajīvikampi… dhūmakālikampi pariyausitaṃ vihāraṃ navakammaṃ dassantī’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, piṇḍanikkhaipanamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ; na kuṭṭalaipanamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na  dvāraṭṭhapanamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na aggaḷavaṭṭikaraṇamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na ālaukasandhikaraṇamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na saitavaṇṇakaraṇamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na kāḷavaṇṇakaraṇamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na gairukaparikammakaraṇamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na chādainamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na bandhanamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na bhaṇḍikāḷapanamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na khaṇḍaphul‍lapaṭisaṅkharaṇamattaina navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na paribhaṇḍakaraṇamattaina  navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na visativassikaṃ navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na tiṃsavassikaṃ navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na yāvajīvikaṃ navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| na dhūmakālikampi pariyausitaṃ vihāraṃ navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| yau dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, akataṃ vā vippakataṃ vā navakammaṃ dātuṃ, khuddakai vihārai kammaṃ aulaukaitvā chappañ‍cavassikaṃ navakammaṃ dātuṃ, aḍḍhayaugai kammaṃ aulaukaitvā sattaṭṭhavassikaṃ navakammaṃ dātuṃ, mahal‍lakai vihārai pāsādai vā kammaṃ aulaukaitvā dasadvādasavassikaṃ navakammaṃ dātu’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū sabbai vihārai navakammaṃ dainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, sabbai vihārai navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| yau dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū aikassa dvai dainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, aikassa dvai dātabbā| yau dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū navakammaṃ gahaitvā añ‍ñaṃ vāsainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, navakammaṃ  gahaitvā añ‍ñau vāsaitabbau| yau vāsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū navakammaṃ gahaitvā saṅghikaṃ  paṭibāhainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, navakammaṃ gahaitvā saṅghikaṃ paṭibāhitabbaṃ| yau paṭibāhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikaṃ varasaiyyaṃ gahaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū nissīmai ṭhitassa navakammaṃ dainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, nissīmai ṭhitassa navakammaṃ dātabbaṃ| yau dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū navakammaṃ gahaitvā sabbakālaṃ paṭibāhanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, navakammaṃ gahaitvā sabbakālaṃ paṭibāhitabbaṃ| yau paṭibāhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, vassānaṃ taimāsaṃ paṭibāhituṃ, utukālaṃ pana na paṭibāhitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū navakammaṃ gahaitvā pak‍kamantipi, vibbhamantipi, kālampi karaunti; sāmaṇairāpi paṭijānanti; sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakāpi paṭijānanti; antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakāpi paṭijānanti; ummattakāpi paṭijānanti; khittacittāpi paṭijānanti; vaidanāṭṭāpi paṭijānanti; āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakāpi paṭijānanti; āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakāpi paṭijānanti; pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakāpi paṭijānanti; paṇḍakāpi paṭijānanti; thaiyyasaṃvāsakāpi paṭijānanti; titthiyapak‍kantakāpi 

paṭijānanti; tiracchānagatāpi paṭijānanti; mātughātakāpi paṭijānanti; pitughātakāpi paṭijānanti; arahantaghātakāpi paṭijānanti; bhikkhunidūsakāpi paṭijānanti; saṅghabhaidakāpi paṭijānanti; lauhituppādakāpi paṭijānanti; ubhataubyañ‍janakāpi paṭijānanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu navakammaṃ gahaitvā pak‍kamati – mā saṅghassa hāyīti añ‍ñassa dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu navakammaṃ gahaitvā vibbhamati…pai॰… kālaṅkarauti, sāmaṇairau paṭijānāti, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakau paṭijānāti, antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakau paṭijānāti, ummattakau paṭijānāti, khittacittau paṭijānāti, vaidanāṭṭau paṭijānāti, āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti, āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti, paṇḍakau paṭijānāti, thaiyyasaṃvāsakau paṭijānāti, titthiyapak‍kantakau paṭijānāti, tiracchānagatau paṭijānāti, mātughātakau paṭijānāti, pitughātakau paṭijānāti, arahantaghātakau paṭijānāti, bhikkhunīdūsakau paṭijānāti, saṅghabhaidakau paṭijānāti, lauhituppādakau paṭijānāti, ubhataubyañ‍janakau paṭijānāti – mā saṅghassa hāyīti añ‍ñassa dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu navakammaṃ gahaitvā vippakatai pak‍kamati – mā saṅghassa hāyīti añ‍ñassa dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu navakammaṃ gahaitvā vippakatai vibbhamati…pai॰… ubhataubyañ‍janakau paṭijānāti – mā saṅghassa hāyīti  añ‍ñassa dātabbaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu navakammaṃ gahaitvā pariyausitai pak‍kamati – tassaivaitaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu navakammaṃ gahaitvā pariyausitai vibbhamati…pai॰… kālaṅkarauti, sāmaṇairau paṭijānāti, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakau paṭijānāti, antimavatthuṃ ajjhāpan‍nakau paṭijānāti – saṅghau sāmī|

‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu navakammaṃ gahaitvā pariyausitai ummattakau paṭijānāti, khittacittau paṭijānāti, vaidanāṭṭau paṭijānāti, āpattiyā adassanai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti, āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhittakau paṭijānāti, pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai  ukkhittakau paṭijānāti – tassaivaitaṃ|

‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu navakammaṃ gahaitvā pariyausitai paṇḍakau paṭijānāti, thaiyyasaṃvāsakau paṭijānāti, titthiyapak‍kantakau paṭijānāti, tiracchānagatau paṭijānāti, mātughātakau paṭijānāti, pitughātakau paṭijānāti, arahantaghātakau paṭijānāti, bhikkhunīdūsakau paṭijānāti, saṅghabhaidakau paṭijānāti, lauhituppādakau paṭijānāti, ubhataubyañ‍janakau paṭijānāti – saṅghau sāmī’’ti|

añ‍ñatraparibhaugapaṭikkhaipādi

324. pārā॰ 157 taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū añ‍ñatarassa upāsakassa vihāraparibhaugaṃ saināsanaṃ añ‍ñatra paribhuñ‍janti| atha khau sau upāsakau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā añ‍ñatra  paribhaugaṃ añ‍ñatra paribhuñ‍jissantī’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatra paribhaugau añ‍ñatra paribhuñ‍jitabbau| yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

pārā॰ 157 taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū upausathaggampi san‍nisaj‍jampi harituṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā chamāya nisīdanti| gattānipi cīvarānipi paṃsukitāni haunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tāvakālikaṃ haritu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa mahāvihārau undriyati| bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā saināsanaṃ nātiharanti nābhiharanti (ka॰)| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, guttatthāya haritu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa saināsanaparikkhārikau mahagghau kambalau uppan‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, phātikammatthāya parivattaitu’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina saṅghassa saināsanaparikkhārikaṃ mahagghaṃ dussaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, phātikammatthāya parivattaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa acchacammaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pādapuñchaniṃ kātu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa cak‍kalikaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pādapuñchaniṃ kātu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa cauḷakaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pādapuñchaniṃ kātu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū adhautaihi pādaihi saināsanaṃ ak‍kamanti| saināsanaṃ dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, adhautaihi pādaihi saināsanaṃ ak‍kamitabbaṃ| yau ak‍kamaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū al‍laihi pādaihi saināsanaṃ ak‍kamanti| saināsanaṃ dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, al‍laihi pādaihi saināsanaṃ ak‍kamitabbaṃ| yau ak‍kamaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū saupāhanā saināsanaṃ ak‍kamanti| saināsanaṃ dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, saupāhanaina saināsanaṃ ak‍kamitabbaṃ| yau ak‍kamaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū parikammakatāya bhūmiyā niṭṭhubhanti| vaṇṇau dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, parikammakatāya bhūmiyā niṭṭhubhitabbaṃ| yau niṭṭhubhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, khaiḷamal‍laka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina mañ‍capādāpi pīṭhapādāpi parikammakataṃ bhūmiṃ vilikhanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, cauḷakaina palivaiṭhaitu’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū parikammakataṃ bhittiṃ apassainti| vaṇṇau dussati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, parikammakatā bhitti apassaitabbā| yau apassaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, apassainaphalaka’’nti| apassainaphalakaṃ haiṭṭhatau bhūmiṃ vilikhati, uparitau bhittiñ‍ca| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, haiṭṭhatau ca uparitau ca cauḷakaina palivaiṭhaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū dhautapādakā nipaj‍jituṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pac‍cattharitvā nipaj‍jitu’’nti|

saṅghabhattādianujānanaṃ

325. atha khau bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina rājagahaṃ taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina rājagahaṃ tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina  khau pana samayaina rājagahaṃ dubbhikkhaṃ hauti| manussā na sak‍kaunti saṅghabhattaṃ kātuṃ; icchanti uddaisabhattaṃ nimantanaṃ salākabhattaṃ pakkhikaṃ upausathikaṃ pāṭipadikaṃ kātuṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṅghabhattaṃ uddaisabhattaṃ nimantanaṃ salākabhattaṃ pakkhikaṃ upausathikaṃ pāṭipadika’’nti|

bhattuddaisakasammuti

326. taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū attanau varabhattāni gahaitvā lāmakāni bhattāni bhikkhūnaṃ dainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhikkhuṃ bhattuddaisakaṃ  samman‍nituṃ – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, uddiṭṭhānuddiṭṭhañ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau, yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ bhattuddaisakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ bhattuddaisakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau  khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau bhattuddaisakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu bhattuddaisakau| khamati  saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha khau bhattuddaisakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau bhattaṃ uddisitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, salākāya vā paṭṭikāya vā paṭikāya vā (syā॰) upanibandhitvā aupuñ‍jitvā bhattaṃ uddisitu’’nti|

saināsanapañ‍ñāpakādisammuti

327. taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa saināsanapañ‍ñāpakau na hauti…pai॰… bhaṇḍāgārikau na hauti…pai॰… cīvarappaṭiggāhakau na hauti…pai॰… cīvarabhājakau na hauti…pai॰… yāgubhājakau na hauti…pai॰… phalabhājakau na hauti…pai॰… khaj‍jakabhājakau na hauti| khaj‍jakaṃ abhājiyamānaṃ nassati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhikkhuṃ khaj‍jakabhājakaṃ samman‍nituṃ – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, bhājitābhājitañ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau, yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ khaj‍jakabhājakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ khaj‍jakabhājakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau khaj‍jakabhājakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu khaj‍jakabhājakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

appamattakavissaj‍jakasammuti

328. taina  khau pana samayaina saṅghassa bhaṇḍāgārai appamattakau parikkhārau uppan‍nau ussan‍nau (syā॰) hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ  bhikkhuṃ appamattakavissaj‍jakaṃ samman‍nituṃ – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, vissaj‍jitāvissaj‍jitañ‍ca jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau, yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ appamattakavissaj‍jakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ appamattakavissaj‍jakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau appamattakavissaj‍jakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu appamattakavissaj‍jakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taina  appamattakavissaj‍jakaina bhikkhunā aikā aikaikā (sī॰) sūci dātabbā, satthakaṃ dātabbaṃ, upāhanā dātabbā, kāyabandhanaṃ dātabbaṃ, aṃsabandhakau dātabbau, parissāvanaṃ dātabbaṃ, dhammakaraṇau dātabbau, kusi dātabbā, aḍḍhakusi dātabbā, maṇḍalaṃ dātabbaṃ, aḍḍhamaṇḍalaṃ dātabbaṃ, anuvātau dātabbau, paribhaṇḍaṃ dātabbaṃ| sacai hauti saṅghassa sappi vā tailaṃ vā madhu vā phāṇitaṃ vā, sakiṃ paṭisāyituṃ dātabbaṃ| sacai punapi atthau hauti, punapi dātabbaṃ|

sāṭiyaggāhāpakādisammuti

329. taina khau pana samayaina saṅghassa sāṭiyaggāhāpakau na hauti…pai॰… pattaggāhāpakau na hauti…pai॰… ārāmikapaisakau na hauti…pai॰… sāmaṇairapaisakau na hauti| sāmaṇairā apaisiyamānā kammaṃ na karaunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ bhikkhuṃ sāmaṇairapaisakaṃ samman‍nituṃ – yau na chandāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na dausāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchaiyya, paisitāpaisitañ‍ca  jānaiyya| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhu yācitabbau, yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau  itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ sāmaṇairapaisakaṃ samman‍naiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuṃ sāmaṇairapaisakaṃ samman‍nati| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmassa bhikkhunau sāmaṇairapaisakassa sammuti, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu sāmaṇairapaisakau| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

tatiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

saināsanakkhandhakau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

vihāraṃ  buddhasaiṭṭhaina, apañ‍ñattaṃ tadā ahu|

tahaṃ tahaṃ nikkhamanti, vāsā tai jinasāvakā||

saiṭṭhī gahapati disvā, bhikkhūnaṃ idamabravi|

kārāpaiyyaṃ vasaiyyātha, paṭipucchiṃsu nāyakaṃ||

vihāraṃ aḍḍhayaugañ‍ca, pāsādaṃ hammiyaṃ guhaṃ|

pañ‍calaiṇaṃ anuñ‍ñāsi, vihārai saiṭṭhi kārayi||

janau vihāraṃ kāraiti, akavāṭaṃ asaṃvutaṃ|

kavāṭaṃ piṭṭhasaṅghāṭaṃ, udukkhalañ‍ca uttari||

āviñchanacchiddaṃ  raj‍juṃ, vaṭṭiñ‍ca kapisīsakaṃ|

sūcighaṭitāḷacchiddaṃ, lauhakaṭṭhavisāṇakaṃ||

yantakaṃ sūcikañ‍caiva, chadanaṃ ul‍littāvalittaṃ|

vaidijālasalākañ‍ca, cak‍kali santharaina ca||

miḍḍhi bidalamañ‍cañ‍ca, sausānikamasārakau|

bundikuḷirapādañ‍ca, āhac‍cāsandi uc‍cakai||

sattaṅgau ca bhaddapīṭhaṃ, pīṭhakaiḷakapādakaṃ|

āmalāphalakā kaucchā, palālapīṭhamaiva ca||

uc‍cāhipaṭipādakā, aṭṭhaṅguli ca pādakā|

suttaṃ aṭṭhapadaṃ cauḷaṃ, tūlikaṃ aḍḍhakāyikaṃ||

giraggau bhisiyau cāpi, dussaṃ saināsanampi ca|

aunaddhaṃ haiṭṭhā patati, uppāṭaitvā haranti ca||

bhattiñ‍ca hatthabhattiñ‍ca, anuñ‍ñāsi tathāgatau|

titthiyā vihārai cāpi, thusaṃ saṇhañ‍ca mattikā||

ik‍kāsaṃ pāṇikaṃ kuṇḍaṃ, sāsapaṃ sitthatailakaṃ|

ussan‍nai pac‍cuddharituṃ, pharusaṃ gaṇḍumattikaṃ||

ik‍kāsaṃ  paṭibhānañ‍ca, nīcā cayau ca āruhaṃ|

paripatanti āḷakā, aḍḍhakuṭṭaṃ tayau puna||

khuddakai  kuṭṭapādau ca, auvassati saraṃ khilaṃ|

cīvaravaṃsaṃ raj‍juñ‍ca, āḷindaṃ kiṭikaina ca||

ālambanaṃ  tiṇacuṇṇaṃ, haiṭṭhāmaggai nayaṃ karai|

ajjhaukāsai autappati, sālaṃ haiṭṭhā ca bhājanaṃ||

vihārau kauṭṭhakau caiva, parivaiṇaggisālakaṃ|

ārāmai ca puna kauṭṭhai, haiṭṭhañ‍ñaiva nayaṃ karai||

sudhaṃ anāthapiṇḍi ca, saddhau sītavanaṃ agā|

diṭṭhadhammau nimantaisi, saha saṅghaina nāyakaṃ||

āṇāpaisantarāmaggai, ārāmaṃ kārayī gaṇau|

vaisāliyaṃ navakammaṃ, puratau ca pariggahi||

kau arahati bhattaggai, tittirañ‍ca avandiyā|

pariggahitantaragharā, tūlau sāvatthi ausari||

patiṭṭhāpaisi ārāmaṃ, bhattaggai ca kaulāhalaṃ|

gilānā varasaiyyā ca, laisā sattarasā tahiṃ||

kaina nu khau kathaṃ nu khau, vihāraggaina bhājayi|

parivaiṇaṃ  anubhāgañ‍ca, akāmā bhāgaṃ nau dadai||

nissīmaṃ sabbakālañ‍ca, gāhā saināsanai tayau|

upanandau ca vaṇṇaisi, ṭhitakā samakāsanā||

samānāsanikā bhindiṃsu, tivaggā ca duvaggikaṃ|

asamānāsanikā dīghaṃ, sāḷindaṃ paribhuñ‍jituṃ||

ayyikā ca avidūrai, bhājitañ‍ca kīṭāgirai|

āḷavī  piṇḍakakuṭṭaihi, dvāraaggaḷavaṭṭikā||

ālaukasaitakāḷañ‍ca, gairuchādanabandhanā|

bhaṇḍikhaṇḍaparibhaṇḍaṃ, vīsa tiṃsā ca kālikā||

ausitai akataṃ vippaṃ, khuddai chappañ‍cavassikaṃ|

aḍḍhayaugai ca sattaṭṭha, mahal‍lai dasa dvādasa||

sabbaṃ vihāraṃ aikassa, añ‍ñaṃ vāsainti saṅghikaṃ|

nissīmaṃ sabbakālañ‍ca, pak‍kami vibbhamanti ca||

kālañ‍ca  sāmaṇairañ‍ca, sikkhāpac‍cakkhaantimaṃ|

ummattakhittacittā ca, vaidanāpattidassanā||

appaṭikammadiṭṭhiyā, paṇḍakā thaiyyatitthiyā|

tiracchānamātupitu, arahantā ca dūsakā||

bhaidakā lauhituppādā, ubhatau cāpi byañ‍janakā|

mā saṅghassa parihāyi, kammaṃ añ‍ñassa dātavai||

vippakatai ca añ‍ñassa, katai tassaiva pak‍kamai|

vibbhamati  kālaṅkatau, sāmaṇairau ca jāyati||

pac‍cakkhātau ca sikkhāya, antimajjhāpan‍nakau yadi|

saṅghauva sāmikau hauti, ummattakhittavaidanā||

adassanāppaṭikammai, diṭṭhi tassaiva hauti taṃ|

paṇḍakau thaiyyatitthī ca, tiracchānamātupaittikaṃ||

ghātakau dūsakau cāpi, bhaidalauhitabyañ‍janā|

paṭijānāti yadi sau, saṅghauva hauti sāmikau||

harantañ‍ñatra  kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, undriyati ca kambalaṃ|

dussañ‍ca cammacak‍kalī, cauḷakaṃ ak‍kamanti ca||

al‍lā upāhanāniṭṭhu, likhanti apassainti ca|

apassainaṃ likhataiva, dhautapac‍cattharaina ca||

rājagahai na sak‍kaunti, lāmakaṃ bhattuddaisakaṃ|

kathaṃ nu khau pañ‍ñāpakaṃ, bhaṇḍāgārikasammuti||

paṭiggāhabhājakau cāpi, yāgu ca phalabhājakau|

khaj‍jakabhājakau caiva, appamattakavissaj‍jai||

sāṭiyaggāhāpakau caiva, tathaiva pattaggāhakau|

ārāmikasāmaṇaira, paisakassa ca sammuti||

sabbābhibhū laukavidū, hitacittau vināyakau|

laiṇatthañ‍ca sukhatthañ‍ca, jhāyituñ‍ca vipassitunti||

saināsanakkhandhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



7. saṅghabhaidakakkhandhakaṃ

1. paṭhamabhāṇavārau

chasakyapabbaj‍jākathā

330. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā anupiyāyaṃ viharati, anupiyaṃ nāma mal‍lānaṃ nigamau| taina khau pana samayaina abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā sakyakumārā bhagavantaṃ pabbajitaṃ anupabbajanti| taina khau pana samayaina mahānāmau ca sak‍kau anuruddhau ca sak‍kau dvaibhātikā haunti| anuruddhau sak‍kau sukhumālau hauti| tassa tayau pāsādā haunti – aikau haimantikau, aikau gimhikau, aikau vassikau| sau vassikai pāsādai cattārau māsai vassikai pāsādai vassikai cattārau māsai (sī॰) nippurisaihi tūriyaihi paricārayamānau paricāriyamānau (ka॰) na haiṭṭhāpāsādaṃ aurauhati| atha khau mahānāmassa sak‍kassa aitadahausi – ‘‘aitarahi khau abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā sakyakumārā bhagavantaṃ pabbajitaṃ anupabbajanti| amhākañ‍ca pana kulā natthi kauci agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ vā pabbajaiyyaṃ, anuruddhau vā’’ti| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina anuruddhau sak‍kau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā anuruddhaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitarahi, tāta anuruddha, abhiñ‍ñātā abhiñ‍ñātā sakyakumārā bhagavantaṃ pabbajitaṃ anupabbajanti| amhākañ‍ca pana kulā natthi kauci agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| taina  hi tvaṃ vā pabbaja, ahaṃ vā pabbajissāmī’’ti| ‘‘ahaṃ khau sukhumālau, nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ| tvaṃ pabbajāhī’’ti| ‘‘aihi khau tai, tāta anuruddha, gharāvāsatthaṃ anusāsissāmi| paṭhamaṃ khaittaṃ kasāpaitabbaṃ| kasāpaitvā vapāpaitabbaṃ| vapāpaitvā udakaṃ abhinaitabbaṃ| udakaṃ abhinaitvā udakaṃ nin‍naitabbaṃ| udakaṃ nin‍naitvā niddhāpaitabbaṃ| niddhāpaitvā niḍḍahaitabbaṃ, niḍḍahaitvā (sī॰) lavāpaitabbaṃ| lavāpaitvā ubbāhāpaitabbaṃ| ubbāhāpaitvā puñ‍jaṃ kārāpaitabbaṃ| puñ‍jaṃ kārāpaitvā maddāpaitabbaṃ| maddāpaitvā palālāni uddharāpaitabbāni| palālāni uddharāpaitvā  bhusikā uddharāpaitabbā| bhusikaṃ uddharāpaitvā aupunāpaitabbaṃ auphunāpaitabbaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), auphuṇāpaitabbaṃ (yaujanā)| aupunāpaitvā atiharāpaitabbaṃ| atiharāpaitvā  āyatimpi vassaṃ aivamaiva kātabbaṃ, āyatimpi vassaṃ aivamaiva kātabba’’nti| ‘‘na kammā khīyanti?
