na kammānaṃ antau pañ‍ñāyati? kadā kammā khīyissanti? kadā kammānaṃ antau pañ‍ñāyissati? kadā mayaṃ appaussuk‍kā pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitā samaṅgībhūtā paricāraissāmā’’ti? ‘‘na hi, tāta anuruddha, kammā khīyanti| na kammānaṃ antau pañ‍ñāyati| akhīṇaiva kammai pitarau ca pitāmahā ca  kālaṅkatā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi tvañ‍ñaiva gharāvāsatthaina upajānāhi| ahaṃ agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissāmī’’ti|

atha khau anuruddhau sak‍kau yaina mātā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā mātaraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, amma, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ| anujānāhi maṃ agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai anuruddhassa sak‍kassa mātā anuruddhaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘tumhai khau mai, tāta anuruddha, dvai puttā piyā manāpā appaṭikūlā| maraṇainapi vau akāmakā vinā bhavissāmi| kiṃ panāhaṃ tumhai jīvantai anujānissāmi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti? dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau anuruddhau sak‍kau mātaraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, amma, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajituṃ| anujānāhi maṃ agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāyā’’ti imassa anantaraṃ kaisuci pautthakaisu aivampi pāṭhau dissati –- ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai anuruddhassa sak‍kassa mātā aivamāha ‘sacai tāta anuruddha bhaddiyau sakyarājā sakyānaṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraiti agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, aivaṃ tvampi pabbajāhī’’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina bhaddiyau sakyarājā sakyānaṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraiti| sau ca anuruddhassa sak‍kassa sahāyau hauti| atha khau anuruddhassa sak‍kassa mātā – ‘ayaṃ khau bhaddiyau sakyarājā sakyānaṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraiti; anuruddhassa sak‍kassa sahāyau; sau na ussahati agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitu’nti – anuruddhaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai, tāta anuruddha, bhaddiyau sakyarājā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, aivaṃ tvampi pabbajāhī’’ti| atha khau anuruddhau sak‍kau yaina  bhaddiyau sakyarājā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhaddiyaṃ sakyarājānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mama khau, samma, pabbaj‍jā tava paṭibaddhā’’ti| ‘‘sacai tai, samma, pabbaj‍jā mama paṭibaddhā vā appaṭibaddhā vā sā hautu, ahaṃ tayā; yathā sukhaṃ pabbajāhī’’ti| ‘‘aihi, samma, ubhau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissāmā’’ti| ‘‘nāhaṃ, samma, sak‍kaumi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitunti| yaṃ tai sak‍kā añ‍ñaṃ mayā  kātuṃ, kyāhaṃ tyāhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) karissāmi| tvaṃ pabbajāhī’’ti| ‘‘mātā khau maṃ, samma, aivamāha – ‘sacai, tāta anuruddha, bhaddiyau sakyarājā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, aivaṃ tvampi pabbajāhī’’’ti| ‘‘bhāsitā khau pana tai, samma, aisā vācā| sacai tai, samma, pabbaj‍jā mama paṭibaddhā vā, appaṭibaddhā vā sā hautu, ahaṃ tayā; yathā sukhaṃ pabbajāhī’’ti| ‘‘aihi, samma, ubhau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissāmā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina manussā sac‍cavādinau haunti, sac‍capaṭiñ‍ñā| atha khau bhaddiyau sakyarājā anuruddhaṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamaihi, samma, sattavassāni| sattan‍naṃ vassānaṃ ac‍cayaina ubhau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aticiraṃ, samma, sattavassāni| nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi sattavassāni āgamaitu’’nti| ‘‘āgamaihi, samma, chavassāni…pai॰… pañ‍cavassāni… cattāri vassāni… tīṇi vassāni… dvai vassāni… aikaṃ vassaṃ| aikassa vassassa  ac‍cayaina ubhau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aticiraṃ, samma, aikavassaṃ| nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi aikaṃ vassaṃ āgamaitu’’nti| ‘‘āgamaihi, samma, sattamāsai| sattan‍naṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina ubhaupi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aticiraṃ, samma, sattamāsā| nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi sattamāsai āgamaitu’’nti| ‘‘āgamaihi, samma, cha māsai…pai॰… pañ‍ca māsai… cattārau māsai… tayau māsai… dvai māsai… aikaṃ māsaṃ… aḍḍhamāsaṃ| aḍḍhamāsassa ac‍cayaina ubhaupi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aticiraṃ, samma, aḍḍhamāsau| nāhaṃ sak‍kaumi aḍḍhamāsaṃ āgamaitu’’nti| ‘‘āgamaihi, samma, sattāhaṃ yāvāhaṃ puttai ca bhātarau ca raj‍jaṃ niyyādaimī’’ti| ‘‘na ciraṃ, samma, sattāhau, āgamaissāmī’’ti|

331. atha khau bhaddiyau ca sakyarājā anuruddhau ca ānandau ca bhagu ca kimilau ca daivadattau ca, upālikappakaina sattamā, yathā purai caturaṅginiyā saināya uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyanti, aivamaiva caturaṅginiyā saināya niyyiṃsu| tai dūraṃ gantvā sainaṃ nivattāpaitvā paravisayaṃ auk‍kamitvā ābharaṇaṃ aumuñ‍citvā uttarāsaṅgaina bhaṇḍikaṃ bandhitvā upāliṃ kappakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘handa, bhaṇai upāli, nivattassu; alaṃ tai aittakaṃ jīvikāyā’’ti| atha khau upālissa kappakassa nivattantassa aitadahausi – ‘‘caṇḍā  khau sākiyā; iminā kumārā nippātitāti ghātāpaiyyumpi maṃ| imai  hi nāma sakyakumārā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissanti| kimaṅga kimaṅga (sī॰) panāha’’nti| bhaṇḍikaṃ muñ‍citvā taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ rukkhai ālaggaitvā ‘yau passati, din‍naṃyaiva haratū’ti  vatvā yaina tai sakyakumārā tainupasaṅkami| addasāsuṃ khau tai sakyakumārā upāliṃ kappakaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna upāliṃ kappakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa, bhaṇai upāli, nivattaisī’’ti? ‘‘idha mai, ayyaputtā, nivattantassa aitadahausi – ‘caṇḍā khau sākiyā; iminā kumārā nippātitāti ghātāpaiyyumpi maṃ| imai hi nāma sakyakumārā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajissanti| kimaṅga panāha’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, ayyaputtā, bhaṇḍikaṃ muñ‍citvā taṃ bhaṇḍaṃ rukkhai ālaggaitvā ‘yau passati, din‍nañ‍ñaiva haratū’ti vatvā tataumhi paṭinivattau’’ti| ‘‘suṭṭhu, bhaṇai upāli, akāsi yampi na nivattau yaṃ nivattau (sī॰), yaṃ pana nivattau (syā॰)| caṇḍā khau sākiyā; iminā kumārā nippātitāti ghātāpaiyyumpi ta’’nti|

atha  khau sakyakumārā upāliṃ kappakaṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai sakyakumārā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘mayaṃ, bhantai, sākiyā nāma mānassinau| ayaṃ, bhantai, upāli kappakau amhākaṃ dīgharattaṃ paricārakau| imaṃ bhagavā paṭhamaṃ pabbājaitu| imassa mayaṃ abhivādanapac‍cuṭṭhānaañ‍jalikammasāmīcikammaṃ karissāma| aivaṃ amhākaṃ sākiyānaṃ sākiyamānau nimmānāyissatī’’ti nimmādayissatīti (sī॰), nimmāniyissatīti (syā॰)|

atha khau bhagavā upāliṃ kappakaṃ paṭhamaṃ pabbājaisi, pacchā tai sakyakumārai| atha khau āyasmā bhaddiyau tainaiva antaravassaina tissau vij‍jā sacchākāsi| āyasmā anuruddhau dibbacakkhuṃ uppādaisi| āyasmā ānandau sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchākāsi| daivadattau pauthuj‍janikaṃ iddhiṃ abhinipphādaisi|

332. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā bhaddiyau arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’’nti| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, bhaddiyau arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’nti| nissaṃsayaṃ khau, bhantai, āyasmā bhaddiyau anabhiratauva brahmacariyaṃ carati| taṃyaiva vā purimaṃ raj‍jasukhaṃ samanussarantau arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi  – ‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina bhaddiyaṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaihi – ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau bhaddiya, āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā bhaddiyau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ bhaddiyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau bhaddiya, āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā bhaddiyau tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ bhaddiyaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhaddiya, arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, bhaddiya, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau arañ‍ñagataupi  rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘ahau sukhaṃ ahau sukha’’’nti? ‘‘pubbai mai, bhantai, rañ‍ñau sataupi antaupi antaipurai rakkhā susaṃvihitā hauti, bahipi antaipurai rakkhā susaṃvihitā hauti, antaupi nagarai rakkhā susaṃvihitā hauti, bahipi nagarai rakkhā susaṃvihitā hauti, antaupi janapadai rakkhā susaṃvihitā hauti, bahipi janapadai rakkhā susaṃvihitā hauti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ  rakkhitaupi gaupitaupi santau bhītau ubbiggau ussaṅkī utrastau viharāmi| aitarahi khau pana ahaṃ aikau, bhantai, arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhītau anubbiggau anussaṅkī anutrastau appaussuk‍kau pan‍nalaumau paradattavuttau migabhūtaina caitasā viharāmīti| imaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaimi – ‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’’’nti| atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

udā॰ 20 ‘‘yassantaratau  na santi kaupā, iti bhavābhavatañ‍ca vītivattau|

taṃ vigatabhayaṃ sukhiṃ asaukaṃ, daivā nānubhavanti dassanāyā’’ti||

daivadattavatthu

333. atha khau bhagavā anupiyāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina kausambī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina kausambī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha khau daivadattassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘kaṃ nu khau ahaṃ pasādaiyyaṃ, yasmiṃ mai  pasan‍nai bahulābhasak‍kārau uppaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? atha khau daivadattassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau ajātasattu kumārau taruṇau caiva  āyatiṃ bhaddau ca| yaṃnūnāhaṃ ajātasattuṃ kumāraṃ pasādaiyyaṃ| tasmiṃ mai pasan‍nai bahulābhasak‍kārau uppaj‍jissatī’’ti|

atha khau daivadattau saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina rājagahaṃ taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina rājagahaṃ tadavasari| atha khau daivadattau sakavaṇṇaṃ paṭisaṃharitvā kumārakavaṇṇaṃ abhinimminitvā ahimaikhalikāya ajātasattussa kumārassa ucchaṅgai uc‍caṅkai (syā॰) pāturahausi| atha khau ajātasattu kumārau bhītau ahausi, ubbiggau ussaṅkī utrastau| atha khau daivadattau ajātasattuṃ kumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhāyasi maṃ tvaṃ kumārā’’ti? ‘‘āma, bhāyāmi| kausi tva’’nti? ‘‘ahaṃ daivadattau’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, bhantai, ayyau daivadattau, iṅgha  sakainaiva vaṇṇaina pātubhavassū’’ti| atha khau daivadattau kumārakavaṇṇaṃ paṭisaṃharitvā saṅghāṭipattacīvaradharau ajātasattussa kumārassa puratau aṭṭhāsi| atha khau ajātasattu kumārau daivadattassa iminā iddhipāṭihāriyaina abhippasan‍nau pañ‍cahi rathasataihi sāyaṃ pātaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ gacchati, pañ‍ca ca thālipākasatāni bhattābhihārau abhiharīyati| atha khau daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtassa pariyādin‍nacittassa aivarūpaṃ icchāgataṃ  uppaj‍ji – ‘‘ahaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmī’’ti| saha cittuppādāva daivadattau tassā iddhiyā parihāyi|

a॰ ni॰ 5.100 taina khau pana samayaina kakudhau nāma kauḷiyaputtau, āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa upaṭṭhākau, adhunā kālaṅkatau añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau| tassa aivarūpau attabhāvappaṭilābhau hauti – saiyyathāpi nāma dvai vā tīṇi vā māgadhakāni māgadhikāni (syā॰) gāmakkhaittāni| sau taina attabhāvappaṭilābhaina naiva attānaṃ na paraṃ byābādhaiti| atha khau kakudhau daivaputtau yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau kakudhau daivaputtau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘daivadattassa, bhantai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtassa pariyādin‍nacittassa pariyādiṇṇacittassa (ka॰) aivarūpaṃ icchāgataṃ uppaj‍ji – ‘ahaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmī’ti| saha cittuppādāva bhantai, daivadattau tassā iddhiyā parihīnau’’ti| idamavauca kakudhau daivaputtau| idaṃ vatvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaiva antaradhāyi|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kakudhau nāma, bhantai, kauḷiyaputtau mama upaṭṭhākau adhunā kālaṅkatau añ‍ñataraṃ manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nau| tassa aivarūpau attabhāvappaṭilābhau – saiyyathāpi nāma dvai vā tīṇi vā māgadhakāni gāmakkhaittāni| sau taina attabhāvappaṭilābhaina naiva attānaṃ na paraṃ byābādhaiti| atha khau, bhantai, kakudhau daivaputtau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau, bhantai, kakudhau daivaputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘daivadattassa, bhantai, lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina abhibhūtassa pariyādin‍nacittassa aivarūpaṃ icchāgataṃ uppaj‍ji – ahaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmīti| saha cittuppādāva bhantai, daivadattau tassā iddhiyā parihīnau’ti| idamavauca, bhantai, kakudhau daivaputtau| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaiva antaradhāyī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ  pana tai, mauggal‍lāna, kakudhau daivaputtau caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau? yaṃ kiñ‍ci kakudhau daivaputtau bhāsati sabbaṃ taṃ tathaiva hauti, nau añ‍ñathā’’ti? ‘‘caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau ca mai, bhantai, kakudhau daivaputtau| yaṃ kiñ‍ci kakudhau daivaputtau bhāsati sabbaṃ taṃ tathaiva hauti, nau añ‍ñathā’’ti| ‘‘rakkhassaitaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, vācaṃ| rakkhassaitaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, vācaṃ| idāni sau maughapurisau attanāva attānaṃ pātukarissati|

pañ‍casatthukathā

334. a॰ ni॰ 5.100 ‘‘pañ‍cimai, mauggal‍lāna, satthārau santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? ‘‘idha, mauggal‍lāna, aikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhasīlau samānau ‘parisuddhasīlaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai sīlaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhasīlau samānau ‘parisuddhasīlaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai sīlaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ naṃ mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma? samman‍nati khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina – yaṃ tumau karissati, tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, satthāraṃ sāvakā sīlatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi sīlatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsati pac‍cāsiṃsati (sī॰ syā॰)|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, idhaikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhājīvau samānau ‘parisuddhājīvaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhau mai ājīvau pariyaudātau asaṃkiliṭṭhau’ti ca| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhājīvau samānau ‘parisuddhājīvaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhau mai ājīvau pariyaudātau asaṃkiliṭṭhau’ti  ca| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ na mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma? samman‍nati  khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina – yaṃ tumau  karissati, tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, satthāraṃ sāvakā ājīvatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi ājīvatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, idhaikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhadhammadaisanau samānau ‘parisuddhadhammadaisanaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhā mai dhammadaisanā pariyaudātā asaṃkiliṭṭhā’ti ca| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhadhammadaisanau samānau ‘parisuddhadhammadaisanaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhā mai dhammadaisanā pariyaudātā asaṃkiliṭṭhā’ti ca| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ naṃ mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma? samman‍nati khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina – yaṃ tumau karissati, tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, satthāraṃ sāvakā dhammadaisanatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi dhammadaisanatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, idhaikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhavaiyyākaraṇau samānau ‘parisuddhavaiyyākaraṇaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai vaiyyākaraṇaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhavaiyyākaraṇau samānau  ‘parisuddhavaiyyākaraṇaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai vaiyyākaraṇaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ naṃ mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma? samman‍nati khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina – yaṃ tumau karissati, tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, satthāraṃ sāvakā vaiyyākaraṇatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi vaiyyākaraṇatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, idhaikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhañāṇadassanau samānau ‘parisuddhañāṇadassanaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai ñāṇadassanaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhañāṇadassanau samānau ‘parisuddhañāṇadassanaumhī’ti  paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai ñāṇadassanaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ naṃ mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma? samman‍nati khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina – yaṃ tumau karissati, tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, mauggal‍lāna, satthāraṃ sāvakā ñāṇadassanatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi ñāṇadassanatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsatīti| imai khau, mauggal‍lāna, pañ‍ca  satthārau santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ|

‘‘ahaṃ khau pana, mauggal‍lāna, parisuddhasīlau samānau ‘parisuddhasīlaumhī’ti paṭijānāmi  ‘parisuddhaṃ mai sīlaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| na ca maṃ sāvakā sīlatau rakkhanti; na cāhaṃ sāvakaihi sīlatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsāmi| parisuddhājīvau samānau…pai॰… parisuddhadhammadaisanau samānau…pai॰… parisuddhavaiyyākaraṇau samānau…pai॰… parisuddhañāṇadassanau samānau ‘parisuddhañāṇadassanaumhī’ti paṭijānāmi ‘parisuddhaṃ mai ñāṇadassanaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| na ca maṃ sāvakā ñāṇadassanatau rakkhanti; na cāhaṃ sāvakaihi ñāṇadassanatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsāmī’’ti|

335. atha khau bhagavā kausambiyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina rājagahaṃ taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina rājagahaṃ tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘daivadattassa, bhantai, ajātasattu kumārau pañ‍cahi rathasataihi sāyaṃ pātaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ gacchati; pañ‍ca ca thālipākasatāni bhattābhihārau abhiharīyatī’’ti| ‘‘mā, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ pihayittha| yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa ajātasattu kumārau pañ‍cahi rathasataihi sāyaṃ pātaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ gamissati, pañ‍ca ca thālipākasatāni bhattābhihārau abhiharīyissati, hāniyaiva, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau vuḍḍhi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, caṇḍassa kuk‍kurassa nāsāya pittaṃ bhindaiyyuṃ, aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kurau bhiyyausaumattāya caṇḍatarau assa, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yāvakīvañ‍ca daivadattassa ajātasattu kumārau pañ‍cahi rathasataihi sāyaṃ pātaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ gamissati, pañ‍ca ca thālipākasatāni bhattābhihārau abhiharīyissati, hāniyaiva, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa pāṭikaṅkhā kusalaisu dhammaisu, nau vuḍḍhi|

saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.184; a॰ ni॰ 4.68 ‘‘attavadhāya, bhikkhavai, daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kadalī attavadhāya phalaṃ daiti, parābhavāya phalaṃ daiti, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, vaiḷu attavadhāya phalaṃ daiti, parābhavāya phalaṃ daiti, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, naḷau attavadhāya phalaṃ daiti, parābhavāya phalaṃ daiti, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, assatarī attavadhāya gabbhaṃ gaṇhāti, parābhavāya gabbhaṃ gaṇhāti, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, attavadhāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādi, parābhavāya daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārasilaukau udapādī’’ti|

saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.183, 1.2.184, naitti॰ 90 ‘‘phalaṃ vai kadaliṃ hanti, phalaṃ vaiḷuṃ phalaṃ naḷaṃ|

sak‍kārau kāpurisaṃ hanti, gabbhau assatariṃ yathā’’ti||

paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

2. dutiyabhāṇavārau

pakāsanīyakammaṃ

336. taina  khau pana samayaina bhagavā mahatiyā parisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaintau nisin‍nau hauti sarājikāya parisāya| atha khau daivadattau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘jiṇṇau dāni, bhantai, bhagavā vuḍḍhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| appaussuk‍kau dāni, bhantai, bhagavā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ anuyuttau viharatu, mamaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ nissaj‍jatu| ahaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, daivadatta, mā tai ruc‍ci bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharitu’’nti| dutiyampi khau daivadattau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau daivadattau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘jiṇṇau dāni, bhantai, bhagavā vuḍḍhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau| appaussuk‍kau dāni, bhantai, bhagavā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ anuyuttau viharatu, mamaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ nissaj‍jatu| ahaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pariharissāmī’’ti| ‘‘sāriputtamauggal‍lānānampi  khau ahaṃ, daivadatta, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ na nissaj‍jaiyyaṃ, kiṃ pana tuyhaṃ chavassa khaiḷāsakassā’’ti! atha khau daivadattau – sarājikāya  maṃ bhagavā parisāya khaiḷāsakavādaina apasādaiti, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaiva uk‍kaṃsatīti – kupitau anattamanau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| ayañ‍carahi daivadattassa bhagavati paṭhamau āghātau ahausi|

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau daivadattassa rājagahai pakāsanīyaṃ kammaṃ karautu – ‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati| yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’ti| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, kātabbaṃ| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

337. ‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ saṅghau daivadattassa rājagahai pakāsanīyaṃ kammaṃ karaiyya – ‘‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati| yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina  vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’ti| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau daivadattassa rājagahai  pakāsanīyaṃ kammaṃ karauti – ‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati| yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’ti| yassāyasmatau khamati daivadattassa rājagahai pakāsanīyaṃ kammassa karaṇaṃ – ‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati, yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’ti – sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘kataṃ saṅghaina daivadattassa rājagahai pakāsanīyaṃ kammaṃ – ‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati| yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’ti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

338. atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, sāriputta, daivadattaṃ  rājagahai pakāsaihī’’ti| ‘‘pubbai mayā, bhantai, daivadattassa rājagahai vaṇṇau bhāsitau – ‘mahiddhikau gaudhiputtau, mahānubhāvau gaudhiputtau’ti| kathāhaṃ, bhantai, daivadattaṃ rājagahai pakāsaimī’’ti? ‘‘nanu tayā, sāriputta, bhūtauyaiva daivadattassa rājagahai vaṇṇau bhāsitau – ‘mahiddhikau gaudhiputtau, mahānubhāvau gaudhiputtau’’’ ti? ‘‘aivaṃ  bhantai’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, sāriputta, bhūtaṃyaiva daivadattaṃ rājagahai pakāsaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, saṅghau sāriputtaṃ samman‍natu daivadattaṃ rājagahai pakāsaituṃ – ‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati| yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’ti| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbau| paṭhamaṃ sāriputtau  yācitabbau| yācitvā byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ samman‍naiyya  daivadattaṃ rājagahai pakāsaituṃ – ‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati| yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’ti| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| saṅghau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ samman‍nati daivadattaṃ rājagahai pakāsaituṃ – ‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati, yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’ti| yassāyasmatau khamati, āyasmatau sāriputtassa sammuti  daivadattaṃ rājagahai pakāsaituṃ – ‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati, yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’ti – sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatau saṅghaina āyasmā sāriputtau daivadattaṃ rājagahai pakāsaituṃ – ‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati| yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’ti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

sammatau  ca āyasmā sāriputtau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ rājagahaṃ pavisitvā daivadattaṃ rājagahai pakāsaisi – ‘‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati| yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’’ti| tattha yai tai manussā assaddhā appasan‍nā dubbuddhinau, tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘usūyakā imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā daivadattassa lābhasak‍kāraṃ usūyantī’’ti| yai pana tai manussā saddhā pasan‍nā paṇḍitā byattā buddhimantau, tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘na khau idaṃ aurakaṃ bhavissati yathā bhagavā daivadattaṃ rājagahai pakāsāpaitī’’ti|

ajātasattukumāravatthu

339. atha  khau daivadattau yaina ajātasattu kumārau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā ajātasattuṃ kumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pubbai khau, kumāra, manussā dīghāyukā, aitarahi appāyukā| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ tvaṃ kumārauva samānau kālaṃ karaiyyāsi| taina hi tvaṃ, kumāra, pitaraṃ hantvā rājā hauhi| ahaṃ bhagavantaṃ hantvā buddhau bhavissāmī’’ti|

atha khau ajātasattu kumārau – ayyau khau daivadattau mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau, jānaiyyāsi ayyau daivadattauti – ūruyā pautthanikaṃ bandhitvā divā divassa divā divasassa (ka॰) bhītau ubbiggau ussaṅkī utrastau sahasā antaipuraṃ pāvisi| addasāsuṃ khau antaipurai upacārakā mahāmattā ajātasattuṃ kumāraṃ divā divassa bhītaṃ ubbiggaṃ ussaṅkiṃ utrastaṃ sahasā antaipuraṃ pavisantaṃ; disvāna aggahaisuṃ| tai vicinantā ūruyā pautthanikaṃ baddhaṃ bandhaṃ (ka॰) disvāna ajātasattuṃ kumāraṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kiṃ tvaṃ, kumāra, kattukāmausī’’ti? ‘‘pitaramhi hantukāmau’’ti| ‘‘kaināsi ussāhitau’’ti? ‘‘ayyaina daivadattainā’’ti| aikac‍cai mahāmattā aivaṃ matiṃ akaṃsu – ‘‘kumārau ca hantabbau, daivadattau ca, sabbai ca bhikkhū hantabbā’’ti| aikac‍cai mahāmattā aivaṃ matiṃ akaṃsu – ‘‘na bhikkhū hantabbā| na bhikkhū kiñ‍ci aparajjhanti| kumārau ca hantabbau, daivadattau cā’’ti| aikac‍cai mahāmattā aivaṃ matiṃ akaṃsu – ‘‘na kumārau ca hantabbau, na daivadattau| na bhikkhū  hantabbā| rañ‍ñau āraucaitabbaṃ| yathā rājā vakkhati tathā karissāmā’’ti|

atha khau tai mahāmattā ajātasattuṃ kumāraṃ ādāya yaina rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā rañ‍ñau māgadhassa sainiyassa bimbisārassa aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘kathaṃ, bhaṇai, mahāmattaihi mati katā’’ti? ‘‘aikac‍cai, daiva, mahāmattā aivaṃ matiṃ akaṃsu – ‘kumārau ca hantabbau, daivadattau ca, sabbai ca bhikkhū hantabbā’ti| aikac‍cai mahāmattā aivaṃ matiṃ akaṃsu – ‘na bhikkhū hantabbā| na bhikkhū kiñ‍ci aparajjhanti| kumārau ca hantabbau, daivadattau cā’ti| aikac‍cai mahāmattā aivaṃ matiṃ akaṃsu – ‘na kumārau ca hantabbau, na daivadattau| na bhikkhū hantabbā| rañ‍ñau  āraucaitabbaṃ| yathā rājā vakkhati tathā karissāmā’’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ, bhaṇai, karissati buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā? nanu bhagavatā paṭikac‍caiva daivadattau rājagahai pakāsāpitau – ‘pubbai daivadattassa añ‍ñā pakati ahausi, idāni añ‍ñā pakati| yaṃ daivadattau karaiyya kāyaina vācāya, na taina buddhau vā dhammau vā saṅghau vā daṭṭhabbau, daivadattauva taina daṭṭhabbau’’ti? tattha yai tai mahāmattā aivaṃ matiṃ akaṃsu – ‘kumārau ca hantabbau daivadattau ca; sabbai ca bhikkhū hantabbā’ti; tai aṭṭhānai akāsi| yai tai mahāmattā aivaṃ matiṃ akaṃsu – ‘na bhikkhū hantabbā; na bhikkhū kiñ‍ci aparajjhanti; kumārau ca hantabbau daivadattau cā’ti; tai nīcai ṭhānai ṭhapaisi| yai  tai mahāmattā aivaṃ matiṃ akaṃsu – ‘na kumārau ca hantabbau, na daivadattau; na bhikkhū hantabbā; rañ‍ñau āraucaitabbaṃ; yathā rājā vakkhati tathā karissāmā’ti; tai uc‍cai ṭhānai ṭhapaisi| atha khau rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau ajātasattuṃ kumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa maṃ tvaṃ, kumāra, hantukāmausī’’ti? ‘‘raj‍jaināmhi, daiva, atthikau’’ti| ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, kumāra, raj‍jaina atthikau, aitaṃ tai raj‍ja’’nti ajātasattussa kumārassa raj‍jaṃ niyyādaisi|

abhimārapaisanaṃ

340. atha khau daivadattau yaina ajātasattu kumārau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā ajātasattuṃ kumāraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘purisai, mahārāja, āṇāpaihi, yai samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ jīvitā vauraupaissantī’’ti| atha khau ajātasattu kumārau manussai āṇāpaisi – ‘‘yathā, bhaṇai, ayyau daivadattau āha tathā karaiyyāthā’’ti| atha khau daivadattau aikaṃ purisaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gacchāvusau, amukasmiṃ aukāsai samaṇau gautamau viharati| taṃ jīvitā vauraupaitvā iminā maggaina āgacchā’’ti| tasmiṃ maggai dvai purisai ṭhapaisi – ‘‘yau iminā maggaina aikau purisau āgacchati, taṃ jīvitā vauraupaitvā iminā maggaina āgacchathā’’ti| tasmiṃ maggai cattārau purisai ṭhapaisi – ‘‘yai iminā maggaina  dvai purisā āgacchanti, tai jīvitā vauraupaitvā iminā maggaina āgacchathā’’ti| tasmiṃ maggai aṭṭha purisai ṭhapaisi – ‘‘yai iminā maggaina cattārau purisā āgacchanti, tai jīvitā  vauraupaitvā iminā maggaina  āgacchathā’’ti| tasmiṃ maggai sauḷasa  purisai ṭhapaisi – ‘‘yai iminā maggaina aṭṭha purisā āgacchanti, tai jīvitā vauraupaitvā āgacchathā’’ti|

atha khau sau aikau purisau asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau avidūrai bhītau ubbiggau ussaṅkī utrastau patthaddhaina kāyaina aṭṭhāsi| addasā khau bhagavā taṃ purisaṃ bhītaṃ ubbiggaṃ ussaṅkiṃ utrastaṃ patthaddhaina kāyaina ṭhitaṃ| disvāna taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihāvusau, mā bhāyī’’ti| atha khau sau purisau asicammaṃ aikamantaṃ karitvā dhanukalāpaṃ nikkhipitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ac‍cayau maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yauhaṃ duṭṭhacittau vadhakacittau idhūpasaṅkantau| tassa mai, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti| ‘‘taggha tvaṃ, āvusau, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ duṭṭhacittau vadhakacittau idhūpasaṅkantau| yatau ca khau tvaṃ, āvusau, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarausi, taṃ tai mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| vuḍḍhi haisā, āvusau, ariyassa vinayai – yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti, āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā tassa purisassa anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ saggakathaṃ, kāmānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ pakāsaisi| yadā taṃ bhagavā añ‍ñāsi kal‍lacittaṃ, muducittaṃ, vinīvaraṇacittaṃ, udaggacittaṃ, pasan‍nacittaṃ, atha yā buddhānaṃ sāmuk‍kaṃsikā dhammadaisanā taṃ pakāsaisi – dukkhaṃ, samudayaṃ, niraudhaṃ, maggaṃ| saiyyathāpi nāma suddhaṃ vatthaṃ apagatakāḷakaṃ sammadaiva rajanaṃ paṭiggahaiyya, aivamaiva tassa purisassa tasmiṃyaiva āsanai virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti| atha khau sau purisau diṭṭhadhammau pattadhammau viditadhammau pariyaugāḷhadhammau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai| saiyyathāpi, bhantai, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa  vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti – aivamaivaṃ bhagavatā anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ saraṇaṃ  gacchāmi, dhammañ‍ca, bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ  gata’’nti| atha khau bhagavā taṃ purisaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘mā khau tvaṃ, āvusau, iminā maggaina gaccha, iminā maggaina gacchāhī’’ti añ‍ñaina maggaina uyyaujaisi|

atha khau tai dvai purisā – kiṃ nu khau sau aikau purisau ciraina āgacchatīti – paṭipathaṃ gacchantā addasaṃsu bhagavantaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ| disvāna yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| taisaṃ bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi…pai॰… aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰… upāsakai nau bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tai purisai aitadavauca – ‘‘mā khau tumhai, āvusau, iminā maggaina gacchittha, iminā maggaina gacchathā’’ti añ‍ñaina maggaina uyyaujaisi|

atha khau tai cattārau purisā…pai॰… atha khau tai aṭṭha purisā…pai॰… atha khau tai sauḷasa purisā – kiṃ nu khau tai aṭṭha purisā ciraina āgacchantīti – paṭipathaṃ gacchantā addasāsuṃ bhagavantaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ| disvāna yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| taisaṃ bhagavā anupubbiṃ kathaṃ kathaisi, saiyyathidaṃ – dānakathaṃ…pai॰… aparappac‍cayā satthusāsanai bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhantai…pai॰…  upāsakai nau bhagavā dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tai purisai aitadavauca – ‘‘mā khau tumhai, āvusau, iminā maggaina gacchittha, iminā maggaina gacchathā’’ti añ‍ñaina maggaina uyyaujaisi|

atha khau sau aikau purisau yaina daivadattau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā daivadattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhantai, sak‍kaumi taṃ bhagavantaṃ jīvitā vauraupaituṃ; mahiddhikau sau bhagavā, mahānubhāvau’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau; mā tvaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ jīvitā vauraupaisi| ahamaiva samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ jīvitā vauraupaissāmī’’ti|

lauhituppādakakammaṃ

341. taina  khau pana samayaina bhagavā gijjhakūṭassa pabbatassa chāyāyaṃ caṅkamati| atha khau daivadattau gijjhakūṭaṃ pabbataṃ āruhitvā mahatiṃ silaṃ pavijjhi – imāya samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ jīvitā vauraupaissāmīti| dvai pabbatakūṭā samāgantvā taṃ silaṃ sampaṭicchiṃsu| tatau papatikā uppatitvā bhagavatau pādai ruhiraṃ uppādaisi| atha khau bhagavā uddhaṃ ul‍laukaitvā daivadattaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘bahuṃ tayā, maughapurisa, apuñ‍ñaṃ pasutaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ duṭṭhacittau vadhakacittau tathāgatassa ruhiraṃ uppādaisī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘idaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivadattaina paṭhamaṃ ānantariyaṃ kammaṃ upacitaṃ, yaṃ duṭṭhacittaina vadhakacittaina tathāgatassa ruhiraṃ uppādita’’nti|

assausuṃ  khau bhikkhū – ‘‘daivadattaina kira bhagavatau vadhau payuttau’’ti| tai ca bhikkhū bhagavatau vihārassa paritau paritau caṅkamanti uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā sajjhāyaṃ  karauntā, bhagavatau rakkhāvaraṇaguttiyā| assausi khau bhagavā uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ sajjhāyasaddaṃ| sutvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau sau, ānanda, uc‍cāsaddau mahāsaddau sajjhāyasaddau’’ti? ‘‘assausuṃ khau, bhantai, bhikkhū – ‘daivadattaina kira bhagavatau vadhau payuttau’ti| tai ca taidha (sī॰), bhantai, bhikkhū bhagavatau vihārassa paritau paritau caṅkamanti uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā sajjhāyaṃ karauntā, bhagavatau rakkhāvaraṇaguttiyā| sau aisau, bhagavā, uc‍cāsaddau mahāsaddau sajjhāyasaddau’’ti| ‘‘taina hānanda, mama vacanaina tai bhikkhū āmantaihi – satthā āyasmantai āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, anavakāsau, yaṃ parūpak‍kamaina tathāgataṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya| anupak‍kamaina, bhikkhavai, tathāgatā parinibbāyanti|

cūḷava॰ 334; a॰ ni॰ 5.100 ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, satthārau santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhasīlau samānau ‘parisuddhasīlaumhī’ti paṭijānāti  ‘parisuddhaṃ mai sīlaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhasīlau samānau ‘parisuddhasīlaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai sīlaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ naṃ mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma? samman‍nati khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina – yaṃ tumau karissati, tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, satthāraṃ sāvakā sīlatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi sīlatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau satthā aparisuddhaājīvau samānau…pai॰… aparisuddhadhammadaisanau samānau…pai॰… aparisuddhavaiyyākaraṇau samānau…pai॰…  aparisuddhañāṇadassanau samānau ‘parisuddhañāṇadassanaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai ñāṇadassanaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| tamainaṃ sāvakā aivaṃ jānanti – ‘ayaṃ khau bhavaṃ satthā aparisuddhañāṇadassanau samānau ‘parisuddhañāṇadassanaumhī’ti paṭijānāti ‘parisuddhaṃ mai ñāṇadassanaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| mayañ‍caiva khau pana gihīnaṃ āraucaiyyāma, nāssassa manāpaṃ| yaṃ khau panassa amanāpaṃ, kathaṃ na mayaṃ taina samudācaraiyyāma? samman‍nati khau pana cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina – yaṃ tumau karissati, tumauva taina pañ‍ñāyissatī’ti| aivarūpaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, satthāraṃ sāvakā ñāṇadassanatau rakkhanti; aivarūpau ca pana satthā sāvakaihi ñāṇadassanatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsati| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca satthārau santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| ‘‘ahaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, parisuddhasīlau samānau ‘parisuddhasīlaumhī’ti paṭijānāmi ‘parisuddhaṃ mai sīlaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’nti ca| na ca maṃ sāvakā sīlatau rakkhanti; na cāhaṃ sāvakaihi sīlatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsāmi| ahaṃ khau pana  bhikkhavai parisuddhājīvau samānau…pai॰…  parisuddhadhammadaisanau samānau…pai॰… parisuddhavaiyyākaraṇau samānau…pai॰… parisuddhañāṇadassanau samānau ‘‘parisuddhañāṇadassanaumhī’’ti paṭijānāmi ‘‘parisuddhaṃ mai ñāṇadassanaṃ pariyaudātaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭha’’nti ca, na ca maṃ sāvakā ñāṇadassanatau rakkhanti, na cāhaṃ sāvakaihi ñāṇadassanatau rakkhaṃ pac‍cāsīsāmi| ‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, anavakāsau, yaṃ parūpak‍kamaina tathāgataṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya| anupak‍kamaina, bhikkhavai, tathāgatā parinibbāyanti| gacchatha tumhai, bhikkhavai, yathāvihāraṃ| arakkhiyā, bhikkhavai, tathāgatā’’ti|

nāḷāgiripaisanaṃ

342. taina khau pana samayaina rājagahai nāḷāgiri nāma hatthī caṇḍau hauti, manussaghātakau| atha khau daivadattau rājagahaṃ pavisitvā hatthisālaṃ gantvā hatthibhaṇḍai aitadavauca – ‘‘mayaṃ khau, bhaṇai, rājañātakā nāma paṭibalā nīcaṭṭhāniyaṃ uc‍caṭṭhānai ṭhapaituṃ, bhattampi vaitanampi vaḍḍhāpaituṃ| taina hi, bhaṇai, yadā samaṇau gautamau imaṃ racchaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti, tadā imaṃ nāḷāgiriṃ hatthiṃ muñ‍caitvā imaṃ racchaṃ paṭipādaithā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau tai  hatthibhaṇḍā daivadattassa pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sambahulaihi  bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau bhagavā taṃ racchaṃ paṭipaj‍ji| addasāsuṃ khau tai hatthibhaṇḍā bhagavantaṃ taṃ racchaṃ paṭipan‍naṃ| disvāna nāḷāgiriṃ hatthiṃ muñ‍citvā taṃ racchaṃ paṭipādaisuṃ| addasā khau nāḷāgiri hatthī bhagavantaṃ dūratauva  āgacchantaṃ| disvāna sauṇḍaṃ ussāpaitvā pahaṭṭhakaṇṇavālau yaina bhagavā taina abhidhāvi| addasāsuṃ khau tai bhikkhū nāḷāgiriṃ hatthiṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, nāḷāgiri hatthī caṇḍau manussaghātakau imaṃ racchaṃ paṭipan‍nau| paṭik‍kamatu, bhantai, bhagavā; paṭik‍kamatu sugatau’’ti| ‘‘āgacchatha, bhikkhavai, mā bhāyittha| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, anavakāsau, yaṃ parūpak‍kamaina tathāgataṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya| anupak‍kamaina, bhikkhavai, tathāgatā parinibbāyantī’’ti| dutiyampi khau tai bhikkhū…pai॰… tatiyampi khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, nāḷāgiri hatthī caṇḍau manussaghātakau imaṃ racchaṃ paṭipan‍nau| paṭik‍kamatu, bhantai, bhagavā; paṭik‍kamatu sugatau’’ti| ‘‘āgacchatha, bhikkhavai, mā bhāyittha| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, anavakāsau, yaṃ parūpak‍kamaina tathāgataṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya| anupak‍kamaina, bhikkhavai, tathāgatā parinibbāyantī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina manussā pāsādaisupi hammiyaisupi chadanaisupi āruḷhā acchanti| tattha yai tai manussā assaddhā appasan‍nā dubbuddhinau, tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘abhirūpau vata, bhau abhirūpau vata bhau gautamau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), mahāsamaṇau nāgaina vihaiṭhīyissatī’’ti| yai pana tai manussā  saddhā pasan‍nā paṇḍitā byattā buddhimantau, tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘nacirassaṃ vata, bhau, nāgau nāgaina saṅgāmaissatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā nāḷāgiriṃ hatthiṃ maittaina cittaina phari| atha khau nāḷāgiri hatthī bhagavatau bhagavatā (sī॰) maittaina cittaina phuṭṭhau phuṭau (ka॰) sauṇḍaṃ auraupaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau puratau aṭṭhāsi| atha khau bhagavā dakkhiṇaina hatthaina nāḷāgirissa hatthissa kumbhaṃ parāmasantau nāḷāgiriṃ hatthiṃ imāhi gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘mā kuñ‍jara nāgamāsadau, dukkhañhi kuñ‍jara nāgamāsadau|

na hi nāgahatassa kuñ‍jara sugati, hauti itau paraṃ yatau||

‘‘mā ca madau mā ca pamādau, na hi pamattā sugatiṃ vajanti tai|

tvañ‍ñaiva tathā karissasi, yaina tvaṃ sugatiṃ gamissasī’’ti||

atha  khau nāḷāgiri hatthī sauṇḍāya bhagavatau pādapaṃsūni gahaitvā uparimuddhani ākiritvā paṭikuṭiyauva paṭikuṭitau paṭisak‍ki (sī॰ syā॰) ausak‍ki, yāva bhagavantaṃ addakkhi| atha khau nāḷāgiri hatthī hatthisālaṃ gantvā sakai ṭhānai aṭṭhāsi| tathā dantau ca pana nāḷāgiri  hatthī ahausi| taina  khau pana samayaina manussā imaṃ gāthaṃ gāyanti –

ma॰ ni॰ 2.352; thairagā॰ 878 ‘‘daṇḍainaikai damayanti, aṅkusaihi kasāhi ca|

adaṇḍaina asatthaina, nāgau dantau mahaisinā’’ti||

manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘yāva pāpau ayaṃ daivadattau, alakkhikau, yatra hi nāma samaṇassa gautamassa aivaṃmahiddhikassa aivaṃ mahānubhāvassa vadhāya parak‍kamissatī’’ti| daivadattassa lābhasak‍kārau parihāyi| bhagavatau ca lābhasak‍kārau abhivaḍḍhi|

pañ‍cavatthuyācanakathā

343. pāci॰ 209 taina  khau pana samayaina daivadattau parihīnalābhasak‍kārau saparisau kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇā sakyaputtiyā kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissanti! kassa sampan‍naṃ na manāpaṃ, kassa sāduṃ na ruc‍catī’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – kathañhi nāma daivadattau saparisau kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jissatī’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, daivadatta, saparisau kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ kulaisu tikabhaujanaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi tayau atthavasai paṭic‍ca – dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalāna niggahāya; paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya, mā pāpicchā pakkhaṃ nissāya saṅghaṃ bhindaiyyunti; kulānuddayāya kulānudayatāya (sī॰ syā॰) ca| gaṇabhaujanai yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

pārā॰ 409 atha khau daivadattau yaina kaukālikau kaṭamaudakatissakau kaṭamaurakatissakau (sī॰ syā॰) khaṇḍadaiviyā puttau samuddadattau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā kaukālikaṃ kaṭamaudakatissakaṃ khaṇḍadaiviyā puttaṃ samuddadattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitha, mayaṃ, āvusau, samaṇassa gautamassa saṅghabhaidaṃ karissāma cak‍kabhaida’’nti| aivaṃ  vuttai kaukālikau daivadattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘samaṇau khau, āvusau, gautamau mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau| kathaṃ mayaṃ samaṇassa gautamassa saṅghabhaidaṃ karissāma cak‍kabhaida’’nti? ‘‘aitha, mayaṃ, āvusau, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ca vatthūni yācissāma – ‘bhagavā, bhantai, anaikapariyāyaina appicchassa santuṭṭhassa sal‍laikhassa  dhutassa pāsādikassa apacayassa vīriyārambhassa vaṇṇavādī| imāni, bhantai, pañ‍ca vatthūni anaikapariyāyaina appicchatāya santuṭṭhiyā sal‍laikhāya dhutatāya pāsādikatāya apacayāya vīriyārambhāya saṃvattanti| sādhu, bhantai, bhikkhū yāvajīvaṃ ārañ‍ñikā assu; yau gāmantaṃ  ausaraiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ piṇḍapātikā assu; yau nimantanaṃ sādiyaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ paṃsukūlikā  assu; yau gahapaticīvaraṃ sādiyaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ rukkhamūlikā assu; yau chan‍naṃ upagacchaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ macchamaṃsaṃ na khādaiyyuṃ; yau macchamaṃsaṃ khādaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyyā’ti| imāni pañ‍ca vatthūni samaṇau gautamau nānujānissati| tai mayaṃ imaihi pañ‍cahi vatthūhi janaṃ sañ‍ñāpaissāmā’’ti| ‘‘sak‍kā khau, āvusau, imaihi pañ‍cahi vatthūhi samaṇassa gautamassa saṅghabhaidau kātuṃ cak‍kabhaidau| lūkhappasan‍nā hi, āvusau, manussā’’ti|

atha khau daivadattau saparisau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau daivadattau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavā, bhantai, anaikapariyāyaina appicchassa santuṭṭhassa sal‍laikhassa dhutassa pāsādikassa apacayassa vīriyārambhassa vaṇṇavādī| imāni, bhantai, pañ‍ca vatthūni anaikapariyāyaina appicchatāya santuṭṭhiyā sal‍laikhāya dhutatāya pāsādikatāya apacayāya vīriyārambhāya saṃvattanti| sādhu, bhantai, bhikkhū yāvajīvaṃ ārañ‍ñikā assu; yau gāmantaṃ ausaraiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ piṇḍapātikā assu; yau nimantanaṃ sādiyaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ paṃsukūlikā assu; yau gahapaticīvaraṃ  sādiyaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ rukkhamūlikā assu; yau chan‍naṃ upagacchaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya| yāvajīvaṃ macchamaṃsaṃ na khādaiyyuṃ; yau macchamaṃsaṃ khādaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, daivadatta| yau icchati, ārañ‍ñikau hautu; yau icchati, gāmantai viharatu| yau icchati, piṇḍapātikau hautu; yau icchati, nimantanaṃ sādiyatu| yau icchati, paṃsukūlikau hautu; yau icchati, gahapaticīvaraṃ sādiyatu| aṭṭhamāsai khau mayā, daivadatta, rukkhamūlasaināsanaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ; tikauṭiparisuddhaṃ macchamaṃsaṃ – adiṭṭhaṃ, assutaṃ, aparisaṅkita’’nti| atha khau daivadattau – na bhagavā  imāni pañ‍ca vatthūni anujānātīti – haṭṭhau udaggau saparisau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau daivadattau saparisau rājagahaṃ pavisitvā pañ‍cahi vatthūhi janaṃ sañ‍ñāpaisi – ‘‘mayaṃ, āvusau, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ca vatthūni  yācimhā – ‘bhagavā, bhantai, anaikapariyāyaina appicchassa…pai॰… vīriyārambhassa vaṇṇavādī| imāni, bhantai, pañ‍ca vatthūni anaikapariyāyaina appicchatāya…pai॰… vīriyārambhāya saṃvattanti| sādhu, bhantai, bhikkhū yāvajīvaṃ ārañ‍ñikā assu; yau gāmantaṃ ausaraiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya…pai॰… yāvajīvaṃ macchamaṃsaṃ na khādaiyyuṃ; yau macchamaṃsaṃ khādaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyyā’ti| imāni pañ‍ca vatthūni samaṇau gautamau nānujānāti| tai mayaṃ imaihi pañ‍cahi vatthūhi  samādāya vattāmā’’ti|

tattha yai tai manussā assaddhā appasan‍nā dubbuddhinau, tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘imai khau samaṇā sakyaputtiyā dhutā sal‍laikhavuttinau| samaṇau pana gautamau bāhul‍likau bāhul‍lāya caitaitī’’ti| yai pana tai manussā saddhā  pasan‍nā paṇḍitā byattā buddhimantau, tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma daivadattau bhagavatau saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamissati cak‍kabhaidāyā’’ti! assausuṃ khau bhikkhū taisaṃ manussānaṃ ujjhāyantānaṃ khiyyantānaṃ vipācaintānaṃ| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma daivadattau saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamissati cak‍kabhaidāyā’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, daivadatta, saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamasi cak‍kabhaidāyā’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, daivadatta| mā tai ruc‍ci saṅghabhaidau| garukau khau, daivadatta, saṅghabhaidau| yau khau, daivadatta, samaggaṃ saṅghaṃ bhindati, kappaṭṭhikaṃ kappaṭṭhitikaṃ (syā॰) kibbisaṃ pasavati, kappaṃ nirayamhi pac‍cati| yau ca khau, daivadatta, bhin‍naṃ saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ karauti, brahmaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati, kappaṃ saggamhi maudati| alaṃ, daivadatta| mā tai ruc‍ci saṅghabhaidau| garukau khau, daivadatta, saṅghabhaidau’’ti|

udā॰ 48 atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| addasā khau daivadattau āyasmantaṃ  ānandaṃ rājagahai piṇḍāya carantaṃ| disvāna yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aj‍jataggaidānāhaṃ, āvusau ānanda, añ‍ñatraiva bhagavatā, añ‍ñatraiva bhikkhusaṅghā, upausathaṃ karissāmi saṅghakammaṃ karissāmī’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmā ānandau rājagahai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātappaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| addasā khau maṃ, bhantai, daivadattau rājagahai piṇḍāya carantaṃ| disvāna yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘aj‍jataggaidānāhaṃ, āvusau ānanda, añ‍ñatraiva bhagavatā, añ‍ñatraiva bhikkhusaṅghā, upausathaṃ karissāmi saṅghakammaṃ karissāmī’ti| aj‍jataggai, bhantai, daivadattau saṅghaṃ bhindissatī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

udā॰ 48 ‘‘sukaraṃ sādhunā sādhuṃ, sādhuṃ pāpaina duk‍karaṃ|

pāpaṃ pāpaina sukaraṃ, pāpamariyaihi duk‍kara’’nti||

dutiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

3. tatiyabhāṇavārau

saṅghabhaidakathā

344. atha  khau daivadattau tadahupausathai uṭṭhāyāsanā salākaṃ gāhaisi – ‘‘mayaṃ, āvusau, samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ca vatthūni yācimhā  – ‘bhagavā, bhantai, anaikapariyāyaina appicchassa…pai॰… vīriyārambhassa vaṇṇavādī| imāni, bhantai, pañ‍ca vatthūni anaikapariyāyaina appicchatāya…pai॰… vīriyārambhāya saṃvattanti| sādhu, bhantai, bhikkhū yāvajīvaṃ ārañ‍ñikā assu; yau gāmantaṃ ausaraiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyya…pai॰… yāvajīvaṃ macchamaṃsaṃ na khādaiyyuṃ; yau macchamaṃsaṃ khādaiyya, vaj‍jaṃ naṃ phusaiyyā’ti| imāni pañ‍ca vatthūni samaṇau gautamau nānujānāti| tai mayaṃ imaihi pañ‍cahi vatthūhi samādāya vattāma| yassāyasmatau imāni pañ‍ca vatthūni khamanti, sau salākaṃ gaṇhātū’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā pañ‍camattāni bhikkhusatāni navakā caiva  haunti appakatañ‍ñunau ca| tai – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsana’nti – salākaṃ gaṇhiṃsu| atha khau daivadattau saṅghaṃ bhinditvā pañ‍camattāni bhikkhusatāni ādāya yaina gayāsīsaṃ taina pak‍kāmi| atha khau sāriputtamauggal‍lānā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘daivadattau, bhantai, saṅghaṃ bhinditvā pañ‍camattāni bhikkhusatāni ādāya yaina gayāsīsaṃ taina pak‍kantau’’ti| ‘‘na hi nāma tumhākaṃ, sāriputtā, taisu  navakaisu bhikkhūsu kāruñ‍ñampi bhavissati? gacchatha tumhai, sāriputtā, purā tai bhikkhū anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jantī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau sāriputtamauggal‍lānā bhagavatau paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina gayāsīsaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavatau avidūrai raudamānau ṭhitau hauti| atha khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, bhikkhu, raudasī’’ti? ‘‘yaipi tai, bhantai, bhagavatau aggasāvakā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā taipi daivadattassa santikai gacchanti daivadattassa dhammaṃ raucaintā’’ti| ‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhu, anavakāsau, yaṃ sāriputtamauggal‍lānā daivadattassa dhammaṃ raucaiyyuṃ, api ca tai gatā bhikkhūnaṃ sañ‍ñattiyā’’ti bhikkhusañ‍ñattiyāti (sī॰ syā॰), bhikkhū sañ‍ñattiyā (ka॰)|

345. taina khau pana samayaina daivadattau mahatiyā parisāya parivuttau dhammaṃ daisaintau nisin‍nau hauti| addasā khau daivadattau sāriputtamauggal‍lānai dūratauva āgacchantai| disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha, bhikkhavai, yāva svākkhātau mayā dhammau, yaipi tai samaṇassa gautamassa aggasāvakā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā taipi mama santikai āgacchanti| mama dhammaṃ raucaintā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai kaukālikau daivadattaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā, āvusau daivadatta, sāriputtamauggal‍lānai vissasi| pāpicchā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau| svāgataṃ taisaṃ yatau mai dhammaṃ raucaintī’’ti|

atha khau daivadattau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ upaḍḍhāsanaina nimantaisi – ‘‘aihāvusau sāriputta, idha nisīdāhī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ āvusau’’ti khau āyasmā  sāriputtau añ‍ñataraṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| āyasmāpi khau mahāmauggal‍lānau añ‍ñataraṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| atha khau daivadattau bahudaiva rattiṃ bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ ajjhaisi – ‘‘vigatathinamiddhau  khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhusaṅghau| paṭibhātu taṃ, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammī kathā, piṭṭhi mai āgilāyati, tamahaṃ āyamissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā sāriputtau daivadattassa pac‍cassausi| atha khau daivadattau catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā dakkhiṇaina passaina saiyyaṃ kappaisi| tassa kilamantassa muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa muhuttakainaiva niddā auk‍kami|

atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau ādaisanāpāṭihāriyānusāsaniyā bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya auvadi anusāsi| āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau iddhipāṭihāriyānusāsaniyā bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya auvadi anusāsi| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ āyasmatā sāriputtaina ādaisanāpāṭihāriyānusāsaniyā āyasmatā ca mahāmauggal‍lānaina iddhipāṭihāriyānusāsaniyā  auvadiyamānānaṃ anusāsiyamānānaṃ virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi – yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti|

atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘gacchāma mayaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatau santikai| yau tassa bhagavatau dhammaṃ raucaisi sau āgacchatū’’ti| atha khau sāriputtamauggal‍lānā tāni pañ‍cabhikkhusatāni ādāya yaina vaiḷuvanaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| atha khau kaukālikau daivadattaṃ uṭṭhāpaisi – ‘‘uṭṭhaihi, āvusau daivadatta, nītā tai bhikkhū sāriputtamauggal‍lānaihi| nanu tvaṃ, āvusau daivadatta, mayā vuttau – ‘mā, āvusau daivadatta, sāriputtamauggal‍lānai vissāsi| pāpicchā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā’’’ti? atha khau daivadattassa tatthaiva uṇhaṃ lauhitaṃ mukhatau uggañchi|

atha khau sāriputtamauggal‍lānā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdisuṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, bhaidakānuvattakā bhikkhū puna upasampaj‍jaiyyu’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, sāriputta| mā tai ruc‍ci bhaidakānuvattakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ puna upasampadā| taina hi tvaṃ, sāriputta, bhaidakānuvattakai bhikkhū thul‍lac‍cayaṃ daisāpaihi| kathaṃ pana tai, sāriputta, daivadattau paṭipaj‍jī’’ti? ‘‘yathaiva, bhantai, bhagavā bahudaiva rattiṃ bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā maṃ ajjhaisati – ‘vigatathinamiddhau khau, sāriputta, bhikkhusaṅghau; paṭibhātu taṃ, sāriputta, bhikkhūnaṃ dhammī kathā, piṭṭhi mai āgilāyati, tamahaṃ āyamissāmī’ti, aivamaiva khau, bhantai, daivadattau paṭipaj‍jī’’ti|

346. atha  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, arañ‍ñāyatanai mahāsarasī| taṃ nāgā upanissāya vihariṃsu| tai taṃ sarasiṃ augāhaitvā, sauṇḍāya bhisamuḷālaṃ abbuhitvā, suvikkhālitaṃ vikkhālaitvā, akaddamaṃ saṅkhāditvā, ajjhauharanti| taisaṃ taṃ vaṇṇāya caiva hauti, balāya ca| na ca tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchanti, maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| taisaṃyaiva khau pana, bhikkhavai, mahānāgānaṃ anusikkhamānā taruṇā bhiṅkacchāpā| tai taṃ sarasiṃ augāhaitvā, sauṇḍāya bhisamuḷālaṃ abbuhitvā, na suvikkhālitaṃ vikkhālaitvā, sakaddamaṃ saṅkhāditvā, ajjhauharanti| taisaṃ taṃ naiva vaṇṇāya hauti, na balāya| tataunidānañ‍ca maraṇaṃ vā nigacchanti, maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, daivadattau mamānukrubbaṃ mamānukubbaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) kapaṇau marissatīti|

‘‘mahāvarāhassa mahiṃ vikrubbatau vikubbatau (sī॰ syā॰), bhisaṃ ghasānassa ghasamānassa (ka॰) nadīsu jaggatau|

bhiṅkauva  paṅkaṃ abhibhakkhayitvā, mamānukrubbaṃ kapaṇau marissatī’’ti||

347. a॰ ni॰ 8.16 ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahati| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sautā ca hauti, sāvaitā ca, uggahaitā ca, dhāraitā ca, viñ‍ñātā ca, viñ‍ñāpaitā ca, kusalau ca sahitāsahitassa, nau ca kalahakārakau – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahati|

a॰ ni॰ 8.16 ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau sāriputtau dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahati| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? idha, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau sautā ca hauti, sāvaitā ca, uggahaitā ca, dhāraitā ca, viñ‍ñātā ca, viñ‍ñāpaitā ca, kusalau ca sahitāsahitassa, nau ca kalahakārakau – imaihi  khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau sāriputtau dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahatīti|

a॰ ni॰ 8.16 ‘‘yau vai na byathati byādhati (sī॰ syā॰) patvā, parisaṃ uggavādiniṃ|

na ca hāpaiti vacanaṃ, na ca chādaiti sāsanaṃ||

‘‘asandiddhau ca akkhāti akkhātā (ka॰), pucchitau ca na kuppati|

sa vai tādisakau bhikkhu, dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahatī’’ti||

348. a॰ ni॰ 8.7 ‘‘aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, asaddhammaihi abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau  nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| katamaihi aṭṭhahi? lābhaina, bhikkhavai, abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau; alābhaina, bhikkhavai…pai॰… yasaina, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ayasaina, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sak‍kāraina, bhikkhavai…pai॰… asak‍kāraina, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpicchatāya, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpamittatāya bhikkhavai, abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhahi asaddhammaihi abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau|

349. ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya? yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ anabhibhuyya viharatau uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya  viharatau aivaṃsatai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| yaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ  pāpamittataṃ anabhibhuyya viharatau uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharatau aivaṃsatai āsavā vighātapariḷāhā na haunti| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharaiyya| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, uppan‍naṃ lābhaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharissāma, uppan‍naṃ alābhaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ yasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ ayasaṃ… uppan‍naṃ sak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ asak‍kāraṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpicchataṃ… uppan‍naṃ pāpamittataṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharissāmāti; aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbanti|

350. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, asaddhammaihi  abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| katamaihi tīhi? pāpicchatā, pāpamittatā, auramattakaina visaisādhigamaina antarā vausānaṃ āpādi – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi asaddhammaihi  abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchauti|

‘‘mā jātu kauci laukasmiṃ, pāpicchau udapaj‍jatha|

tadamināpi  jānātha, pāpicchānaṃ yathāgati||

‘‘paṇḍitauti samañ‍ñātau, bhāvitattauti sammatau|

jalaṃva yasasā aṭṭhā, daivadattauti mai sutaṃ||

‘‘sau pamādaṃ anuciṇṇau, āsaj‍ja naṃ tathāgataṃ|

avīcinirayaṃ pattau, catudvāraṃ bhayānakaṃ||

‘‘aduṭṭhassa hi yau dubbhai, pāpakammaṃ akrubbatau|

tamaiva pāpaṃ phusati, duṭṭhacittaṃ anādaraṃ||

‘‘samuddaṃ visakumbhaina, yau mañ‍ñaiyya padūsituṃ padussituṃ (ka॰)|

na sau taina padūsaiyya, bhaismā hi udadhī mahā||

‘‘aivamaiva  tathāgataṃ, yau vādainupahiṃsati|

samaggataṃ sammāgataṃ (sī॰), samagataṃ (syā॰) santacittaṃ, vādau tamhi na rūhati||

‘‘tādisaṃ mittaṃ krubbaitha kubbaitha (sī॰ syā॰), tañ‍ca saivaitha paṇḍitau|

yassa maggānugau bhikkhu, khayaṃ dukkhassa pāpuṇai’’ti||

upālipañhā

351. atha khau āyasmā upāli yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saṅgharāji saṅgharājīti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, saṅgharāji hauti, nau ca saṅghabhaidau? kittāvatā ca pana saṅgharāji caiva hauti  saṅghabhaidau cā’’ti?

‘‘aikatau, upāli, aikau hauti, aikatau dvai, catutthau anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| aivampi khau, upāli, saṅgharāji hauti, nau ca saṅghabhaidau| aikatau, upāli, dvai haunti, aikatau dvai, pañ‍camau anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| aivampi khau, upāli, saṅgharāji hauti, nau ca saṅghabhaidau| aikatau, upāli, dvai haunti, aikatau tayau, chaṭṭhau anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| aivampi khau, upāli, saṅgharāji hauti, nau ca saṅghabhaidau| aikatau, upāli, tayau haunti, aikatau tayau, sattamau anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| aivampi khau, upāli, saṅgharāji hauti, nau ca saṅghabhaidau| aikatau, upāli, tayau haunti, aikatau cattārau, aṭṭhamau anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| aivampi khau, upāli, saṅgharāji hauti, nau ca saṅghabhaidau| aikatau, upāli, cattārau haunti, aikatau cattārau, navamau anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| aivaṃ  khau, upāli, saṅgharāji caiva hauti saṅghabhaidau ca| navan‍naṃ vā, upāli, atiraikanavan‍naṃ vā saṅgharāji caiva hauti saṅghabhaidau ca| na khau, upāli, bhikkhunī saṅghaṃ bhindati, api ca bhaidāya parak‍kamati, na sikkhamānā saṅghaṃ bhindati…pai॰… na sāmaṇairau saṅghaṃ bhindati, na sāmaṇairī saṅghaṃ bhindati, na upāsakau saṅghaṃ bhindati, na upāsikā saṅghaṃ bhindati, api ca bhaidāya parak‍kamati| bhikkhu khau, upāli, pakatattau, samānasaṃvāsakau, samānasīmāyaṃ ṭhitau, saṅghaṃ bhindatī’’ti|

352. a॰ ni॰ 10.37 ‘‘saṅghabhaidau saṅghabhaidauti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, saṅghau bhin‍nau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhupāli, bhikkhū adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpainti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpainti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpainti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpainti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, anāpattiṃ āpattīti dīpainti, āpattiṃ anāpattīti  dīpainti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ garukā āpattīti dīpainti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ lahukā āpattīti dīpainti, sāvasaisaṃ āpattiṃ anavasaisā  āpattīti dīpainti, anavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ sāvasaisā āpattīti dīpainti, duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhulā āpattīti dīpainti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpainti| tai imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi apakassanti, avapakassanti, āvainiṃ āvaiṇi (sī॰), āvaiṇikaṃ (syā॰) upausathaṃ karaunti, āvainiṃ pavāraṇaṃ karaunti, āvainiṃ saṅghakammaṃ karaunti| aittāvatā khau, upāli, saṅghau bhin‍nau hautī’’ti|

353.  a॰ ni॰ 10.37 ‘‘saṅghasāmaggī saṅghasāmaggīti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, saṅghau samaggau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idhupāli, bhikkhū adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpainti, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpainti, avinayaṃ avinayauti dīpainti, vinayaṃ vinayauti  dīpainti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpainti, anāpattiṃ anāpattīti dīpainti, āpattiṃ āpattīti dīpainti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ lahukā āpattīti dīpainti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ garukā āpattīti dīpainti, sāvasaisaṃ āpattiṃ sāvasaisā āpattīti dīpainti, anavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ anavasaisā āpattīti dīpainti, duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpainti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpainti| tai imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi na apakassanti, na avapakassanti, na āvainiṃ upausathaṃ karaunti, na āvainiṃ pavāraṇaṃ karaunti, na āvainiṃ saṅghakammaṃ karaunti| aittāvatā khau, upāli, saṅghau samaggau hautī’’ti|

354. ‘‘samaggaṃ pana, bhantai, saṅghaṃ bhinditvā kiṃ sau pasavatī’’ti? ‘‘samaggaṃ khau, upāli, saṅghaṃ bhinditvā kappaṭṭhikaṃ kibbisaṃ pasavati, kappaṃ nirayamhi pac‍catī’’ti|

itivu॰ 18; a॰ ni॰ 10.39 ‘‘āpāyikau  nairayikau, kappaṭṭhau saṅghabhaidakau|

vaggaratau adhammaṭṭhau, yaugakkhaimā padhaṃsati|

saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ bhinditvā, kappaṃ nirayamhi pac‍catī’’ti||

‘‘bhin‍naṃ  pana, bhantai, saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ katvā kiṃ sau pasavatī’’ti? ‘‘bhin‍naṃ khau, upāli, saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ katvā brahmaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati, kappaṃ saggamhi maudatī’’ti|

ittivu॰ 18; a॰ ni॰ 10.40 ‘‘sukhā saṅghassa sāmaggī, samaggānañ‍ca anuggahau|

samaggaratau dhammaṭṭhau, yaugakkhaimā na dhaṃsati|

saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ katvāna, kappaṃ saggamhi maudatī’’ti||

355. pari॰ 459 ‘‘siyā nu khau, bhantai, saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau, nairayikau, kappaṭṭhau, ataikicchau’’ti? ‘‘siyā, upāli, saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau, nairayikau, kappaṭṭhau, ataikicchau’’ti|

pari॰ 459 ‘‘siyā  siyā nu khau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) pana, bhantai, saṅghabhaidakau  na āpāyikau, na nairayikau, na kappaṭṭhau, na ataikicchau’’ti? ‘‘siyā, upāli, saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau, na nairayikau, na kappaṭṭhau, na ataikicchau’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau, nairayikau, kappaṭṭhau, ataikicchau’’ti? ‘‘idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti| tasmiṃ adhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai adhammadiṭṭhi, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, vinidhāya khantiṃ, vinidhāya ruciṃ, vinidhāya bhāvaṃ, anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| ayampi khau, upāli, saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau, nairayikau, kappaṭṭhau, ataikicchau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti| tasmiṃ adhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai dhammadiṭṭhi, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, vinidhāya khantiṃ, vinidhāya ruciṃ, vinidhāya bhāvaṃ, anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| ayampi khau, upāli, saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau, nairayikau, kappaṭṭhau, ataikicchau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti| tasmiṃ adhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai vaimatikau, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, vinidhāya khantiṃ, vinidhāya ruciṃ, vinidhāya bhāvaṃ, anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ  raucaithā’ti| ayampi khau, upāli, saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau, nairayikau, kappaṭṭhau, ataikicchau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti| tasmiṃ dhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai  adhammadiṭṭhi…pai॰… (tasmiṃ dhammadiṭṭhi bhaidai dhammadiṭṭhi) ( ) syāmapautthakai natthi, vimativinaudanīṭīkāya samaiti| tasmiṃ dhammadiṭṭhi bhaidai vaimatikau| tasmiṃ vaimatikau bhaidai adhammadiṭṭhi| tasmiṃ vaimatikau bhaidai dhammadiṭṭhi| tasmiṃ vaimatikau bhaidai vaimatikau vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, vinidhāya khantiṃ, vinidhāya ruciṃ, vinidhāya bhāvaṃ, anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| ayampi khau, upāli, saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau, nairayikau, kappaṭṭhau, ataikicchau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, upāli, bhikkhu dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti…pai॰… avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti… vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti… abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti… bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti… anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti… āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti… apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti… pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti… anāpattiṃ āpattīti dīpaiti… āpattiṃ anāpattīti dīpaiti… lahukaṃ āpattiṃ garukā āpattīti dīpaiti… garukaṃ āpattiṃ lahukā āpattīti dīpaiti… sāvasaisaṃ āpattiṃ anavasaisā āpattīti dīpaiti… anavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ sāvasaisā āpattīti dīpaiti… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti… aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti… tasmiṃ adhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai adhammadiṭṭhi…pai॰… tasmiṃ adhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai dhammadiṭṭhi … tasmiṃ adhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai vaimatikau… tasmiṃ dhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai adhammadiṭṭhi… (tasmiṃ dhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai dhammadiṭṭhi) ( ) syāmapautthakai natthi … tasmiṃ dhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai vaimatikau… tasmiṃ vaimatikau, bhaidai adhammadiṭṭhi… tasmiṃ vaimatikau, bhaidai dhammadiṭṭhi… tasmiṃ vaimatikau, bhaidai vaimatikau, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, vinidhāya khantiṃ, vinidhāya ruciṃ, vinidhāya bhāvaṃ, anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| ayampi khau, upāli, saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau, nairayikau, kappaṭṭhau, ataikicchau’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau, na nairayikau, na kappaṭṭhau, na ataikicchau’’ti? ‘‘idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti| tasmiṃ dhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai dhammadiṭṭhi, avinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, avinidhāya khantiṃ, avinidhāya ruciṃ, avinidhāya bhāvaṃ, anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| ayampi khau, upāli, saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau, na nairayikau, na kappaṭṭhau, na ataikicchau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, upāli, bhikkhu dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti| tasmiṃ dhammadiṭṭhi, bhaidai dhammadiṭṭhi, avinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, avinidhāya khantiṃ, avinidhāya ruciṃ, avinidhāya bhāvaṃ, anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti – ‘ayaṃ dhammau, ayaṃ vinayau, idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, imaṃ gaṇhatha, imaṃ raucaithā’ti| ayampi khau, upāli, saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau, na nairayikau, na kappaṭṭhau, na ataikicchau’’ti|

tatiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

saṅghabhaidakakkhandhakau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

anupiyai abhiñ‍ñātā, sukhumālau na icchati|

kasā vapā abhi nin‍nai, niddhā lāvai ca ubbahai||

puñ‍jamaddapalālañ‍ca, bhusaauphuṇanīharai|

āyatimpi na khīyanti, pitarau ca pitāmahā||

bhaddiyau anuruddhau ca, ānandau bhagu kimilau|

sakyamānau ca kausambiṃ, parihāyi kakudhaina ca||

pakāsaisi pitunau ca, purisai silaṃ nāḷāgiriṃ|

tikapañ‍cagarukau khau, bhindi thul‍lac‍cayaina ca|

tayau aṭṭha puna tīṇi, rāji bhaidā siyā nu khauti||

saṅghabhaidakakkhandhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



8. vattakkhandhakaṃ

1. āgantukavattakathā

356. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āgantukā bhikkhū saupāhanāpi ārāmaṃ pavisanti, chattapaggahitāpi ārāmaṃ pavisanti, auguṇṭhitāpi ārāmaṃ pavisanti, sīsaipi cīvaraṃ karitvā ārāmaṃ pavisanti, pānīyainapi pādai dhauvanti, vuḍḍhataraipi āvāsikai bhikkhū na abhivādainti, napi saināsanaṃ pucchanti| añ‍ñataraupi āgantukau bhikkhu anajjhāvuṭṭhaṃ vihāraṃ ghaṭikaṃ ugghāṭaitvā kavāṭaṃ paṇāmaitvā sahasā pāvisi| tassa uparipiṭṭhitau uparipiṭṭhatau (?) ahi khandhai papati| sau bhītau vissaramakāsi| bhikkhū upadhāvitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, āvusau, vissaramakāsī’’ti? atha khau sau bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma āgantukā bhikkhū saupāhanāpi ārāmaṃ pavisissanti, chattapaggahitāpi ārāmaṃ pavisissanti, auguṇṭhitāpi ārāmaṃ pavisissanti, sīsaipi cīvaraṃ karitvā ārāmaṃ pavisissanti, pānīyainapi pādai dhauvissanti, vuḍḍhataraipi āvāsikai bhikkhū na abhivādaissanti, napi saināsanaṃ pucchissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ  kira, bhikkhavai, ‘‘āgantukā bhikkhū saupāhanāpi ārāmaṃ pavisanti, chattapaggahitāpi ārāmaṃ pavisanti, auguṇṭhitāpi ārāmaṃ pavisanti, sīsaipi cīvaraṃ karitvā ārāmaṃ pavisanti, pānīyainipi pādai dhauvanti, vuḍḍhataraipi āvāsikai bhikkhū na abhivādainti, napi saināsanaṃ pucchantīti| sac‍caṃ bhagavāti| vigarahi buddhau bhagavā…pai॰… kathañhi nāma bhikkhavai āgantukā bhikkhū saupāhanāpi ārāmaṃ pavisissanti, chattapaggahitāpi ārāmaṃ pavisissanti, auguṇṭhitāpi ārāmaṃ pavisissanti, sīsaipi cīvaraṃ karitvā ārāmaṃ pavisissanti, pānīyainapi pādai dhauvissanti, vuḍḍhataraipi āvāsikai  bhikkhū na abhivādaissanti, napi saināsanaṃ pucchissanti, naitaṃ bhikkhavai appasan‍nānaṃ vā pasādāya…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

357. ‘‘taina  hi, bhikkhavai, āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi yathā āgantukaihi bhikkhūhi sammā vattitabbaṃ| āgantukaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā ‘idāni ārāmaṃ pavisissāmī’ti upāhanā aumuñ‍citvā  nīcaṃ katvā papphauṭaitvā gahaitvā chattaṃ apanāmaitvā sīsaṃ vivaritvā sīsai cīvaraṃ vivaritvā cīvaraṃ (ka॰) khandhai katvā sādhukaṃ ataramānaina ārāmau pavisitabbau| ārāmaṃ pavisantaina sal‍lakkhaitabbaṃ – ‘kattha  āvāsikā bhikkhū paṭik‍kamantī’ti? yattha āvāsikā bhikkhū paṭik‍kamanti – upaṭṭhānasālāya vā maṇḍapai vā rukkhamūlai vā – tattha gantvā aikamantaṃ pattau nikkhipitabbau; aikamantaṃ cīvaraṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ; patirūpaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā nisīditabbaṃ; pānīyaṃ pucchitabbaṃ, paribhaujanīyaṃ pucchitabbaṃ – ‘katamaṃ pānīyaṃ, katamaṃ paribhaujanīya’nti? sacai pānīyaina atthau hauti, pānīyaṃ gahaitvā pātabbaṃ| sacai paribhaujanīyaina atthau hauti, paribhaujanīyaṃ gahaitvā pādā dhauvitabbā| pādai dhauvantaina aikaina hatthaina udakaṃ āsiñ‍citabbaṃ, aikaina hatthaina pādā dhauvitabbā| tainaiva udakaṃ āsiñ‍citabbaṃ yaina hatthaina udakaṃ āsiñ‍citabbaṃ (syā॰) na tainaiva hatthaina pādā dhauvitabbā| upāhanapuñchanacauḷakaṃ pucchitvā upāhanā puñchitabbā| upāhanā puñchantaina paṭhamaṃ sukkhaina cauḷakaina puñchitabbā, pacchā al‍laina| upāhanāpuñchanacauḷakaṃ dhauvitvā pīḷaitvā (syā॰) aikamantaṃ vissaj‍jaitabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai āvāsikau bhikkhu vuḍḍhau hauti, abhivādaitabbau| sacai navakau hauti, abhivādāpaitabbau| saināsanaṃ pucchitabbaṃ – ‘katamaṃ mai saināsanaṃ pāpuṇātī’ti? ajjhāvuṭṭhaṃ vā anajjhāvuṭṭhaṃ vā pucchitabbaṃ, gaucarau pucchitabbau, agaucarau pucchitabbau, saikkhasammatāni saikhasammatāni (ka॰) kulāni pucchitabbāni, vac‍caṭṭhānaṃ pucchitabbaṃ, passāvaṭṭhānaṃ pucchitabbaṃ, pānīyaṃ pucchitabbaṃ, paribhaujanīyaṃ pucchitabbaṃ, kattaradaṇḍau pucchitabbau, saṅghassa katikasaṇṭhānaṃ pucchitabbaṃ – ‘kaṃ kālaṃ pavisitabbaṃ, kaṃ kālaṃ nikkhamitabba’nti? sacai vihārau anajjhāvuṭṭhau hauti, kavāṭaṃ ākauṭaitvā muhuttaṃ āgamaitvā ghaṭikaṃ ugghāṭaitvā kavāṭaṃ paṇāmaitvā bahi ṭhitaina nil‍laukaitabbau|

‘‘sacai  vihārau uk‍lāpau hauti, mañ‍cai vā mañ‍cau āraupitau hauti, pīṭhai vā pīṭhaṃ āraupitaṃ hauti, saināsanaṃ upari puñ‍jīkataṃ puñ‍jakitaṃ (ka॰) hauti, sacai ussahati, saudhaitabbau| mahāva॰ 66-67 (thaukaṃ visadisaṃ) vihāraṃ saudhaintaina  paṭhamaṃ bhūmattharaṇaṃ nīharitvā aikamantaṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ; mañ‍capaṭipādakā nīharitvā aikamantaṃ nikkhipitabbā; bhisibibbauhanaṃ nīharitvā aikamantaṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ; nisīdanapac‍cattharaṇaṃ nīharitvā aikamantaṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ; mañ‍cau nīcaṃ katvā sādhukaṃ appaṭighaṃsantaina, asaṅghaṭṭaintaina kavāṭapiṭṭhaṃ, nīharitvā aikamantaṃ  nikkhipitabbau; pīṭhaṃ nīcaṃ katvā sādhukaṃ appaṭighaṃsantaina, asaṅghaṭṭaintaina kavāṭapiṭṭhaṃ, nīharitvā aikamantaṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ; khaiḷamal‍lakau nīharitvā aikamantaṃ nikkhipitabbau; apassainaphalakaṃ nīharitvā aikamantaṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ| sacai vihārai santānakaṃ hauti, ul‍laukā paṭhamaṃ auhāraitabbaṃ, ālaukasandhikaṇṇabhāgā pamaj‍jitabbā| sacai gairukaparikammakatā bhitti kaṇṇakitā hauti, cauḷakaṃ taimaitvā pīḷaitvā pamaj‍jitabbā| sacai kāḷavaṇṇakatā bhūmi kaṇṇakitā hauti, cauḷakaṃ taimaitvā pīḷaitvā  pamaj‍jitabbā| sacai akatā hauti bhūmi, udakaina paripphausitvā sammaj‍jitabbā – mā vihārau rajaina uhañ‍ñīti ūhañ‍ñīti (sī॰ syā॰)| saṅkāraṃ vicinitvā aikamantaṃ chaḍḍaitabbaṃ|

‘‘bhūmattharaṇaṃ autāpaitvā saudhaitvā papphauṭaitvā atiharitvā yathāṭhānai yathāpañ‍ñattaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), yathābhāgaṃ (ka॰) pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ| mañ‍capaṭipādakā autāpaitvā pamaj‍jitvā atiharitvā yathāṭhānai yathābhāgaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) ṭhapaitabbā| mañ‍cau autāpaitvā saudhaitvā papphauṭaitvā nīcaṃ katvā sādhukaṃ appaṭighaṃsantaina, asaṅghaṭṭaintaina kavāṭapiṭṭhaṃ, atiharitvā yathāṭhānai yathābhāgaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) pañ‍ñapaitabbau| pīṭhaṃ autāpaitvā saudhaitvā papphauṭaitvā nīcaṃ katvā sādhukaṃ appaṭighaṃsantaina, asaṅghaṭṭaintaina kavāṭapiṭṭhaṃ, atiharitvā yathāṭhānai yathābhāgaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ| bhisibibbauhanaṃ autāpaitvā saudhaitvā papphauṭaitvā atiharitvā yathābhāgaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ| nisīdanapac‍cattharaṇaṃ autāpaitvā saudhaitvā papphauṭaitvā atiharitvā yathābhāgaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ| khaiḷamal‍lakau autāpaitvā pamaj‍jitvā atiharitvā yathābhāgaṃ ṭhapaitabbau| apassainaphalakaṃ autāpaitvā pamaj‍jitvā atiharitvā yathābhāgaṃ ṭhapaitabbaṃ| pattacīvaraṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ| pattaṃ nikkhipantaina  aikaina hatthaina pattaṃ gahaitvā aikaina hatthaina haiṭṭhāmañ‍caṃ vā haiṭṭhāpīṭhaṃ vā parāmasitvā  pattau nikkhipitabbau| na ca anantarahitāya bhūmiyā pattau nikkhipitabbau| cīvaraṃ nikkhipantaina aikaina hatthaina cīvaraṃ gahaitvā aikaina hatthaina cīvaravaṃsaṃ vā cīvararaj‍juṃ vā pamaj‍jitvā pāratau antaṃ auratau bhaugaṃ katvā cīvaraṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai puratthimā sarajā vātā vāyanti, puratthimā vātapānā thakaitabbā| sacai pacchimā sarajā vātā vāyanti, pacchimā vātapānā thakaitabbā| sacai uttarā sarajā vātā vāyanti, uttarā vātapānā thakaitabbā| sacai dakkhiṇā sarajā vātā vāyanti, dakkhiṇā vātapānā thakaitabbā| sacai sītakālau hauti, divā vātapānā vivaritabbā, rattiṃ thakaitabbā| sacai uṇhakālau hauti, divā vātapānā thakaitabbā, rattiṃ vivaritabbā|

‘‘sacai parivaiṇaṃ uk‍lāpaṃ  hauti, parivaiṇaṃ sammaj‍jitabbaṃ| sacai kauṭṭhakau uk‍lāpau hauti, kauṭṭhakau sammaj‍jitabbau| sacai upaṭṭhānasālā uk‍lāpā hauti, upaṭṭhānasālā sammaj‍jitabbā| sacai aggisālā uk‍lāpā hauti, aggisālā sammaj‍jitabbā| sacai vac‍cakuṭi uk‍lāpā hauti, vac‍cakuṭi sammaj‍jitabbā| sacai pānīyaṃ na hauti, pānīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitabbaṃ| sacai paribhaujanīyaṃ na hauti, paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitabbaṃ| sacai ācamanakumbhiyā udakaṃ na hauti, ācamanakumbhiyā udakaṃ āsiñ‍citabbaṃ| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ yathā āgantukaihi bhikkhūhi sammā vattitabba’’nti|

2. āvāsikavattakathā
