383. udā॰ 45; a॰ ni॰ 8.20 taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātu pāsādai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā nikkhantai paṭhamai yāmai uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantā, bhantai, ratti, nikkhantau paṭhamau yāmau, ciranisin‍nau bhikkhusaṅghau| uddisatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimaukkha’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā nikkhantai majjhimai yāmai uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantā, bhantai, ratti, nikkhantau majjhimau yāmau, ciranisin‍nau bhikkhusaṅghau| uddisatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimaukkha’’nti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi| tatiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā nikkhantai pacchimai yāmai uddhastai aruṇai nandimukhiyā rattiyā uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantā, bhantai, ratti, nikkhantau pacchimau yāmau, uddhastaṃ aruṇaṃ nandimukhi ratti, ciranisin‍nau bhikkhusaṅghau| uddisatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimaukkha’’nti| ‘‘aparisuddhā, ānanda, parisā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kaṃ nu khau bhagavā puggalaṃ sandhāya aivamāha – ‘aparisuddhā, ānanda, parisā’’’ti? atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau sabbāvantaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca manasākāsi| addasā khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau taṃ puggalaṃ dussīlaṃ pāpadhammaṃ asucisaṅkassarasamācāraṃ paṭicchan‍nakammantaṃ assamaṇaṃ samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñaṃ abrahmacāriṃ brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñaṃ antaupūtiṃ avassutaṃ kasambujātaṃ kasambukajātaṃ (ka॰) majjhai bhikkhusaṅghassa nisin‍naṃ| disvāna yaina sau puggalau  tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā taṃ puggalaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘uṭṭhaihi, āvusau, diṭṭhausi bhagavatā; natthi tai bhikkhūhi  saddhiṃ saṃvāsau’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai sau puggalau tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau taṃ puggalaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘uṭṭhaihi, āvusau, diṭṭhausi bhagavatā; natthi tai bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvāsau’’ti| dutiyampi khau sau puggalau tuṇhī ahausi| tatiyampi khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau taṃ puggalaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘uṭṭhaihi, āvusau, diṭṭhausi bhagavatā; natthi tai bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvāsau’’ti| tatiyampi khau sau puggalau tuṇhī ahausi| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau taṃ puggalaṃ bāhāyaṃ gahaitvā  bahidvārakauṭṭhakā nikkhāmaitvā sūcighaṭikaṃ datvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nikkhāmitau sau, bhantai, puggalau mayā; suddhā parisā; uddisatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimaukkha’’nti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, abbhutaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, yāva bāhāgahaṇāpi nāma sau maughapurisau āgamaissatī’’ti! atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

2. mahāsamuddaiaṭṭhacchariyaṃ

384. udā॰ 45; a॰ ni॰ 8.19 ‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti| katamai aṭṭha?

‘‘mahāsamuddau, bhikkhavai, anupubbanin‍nau anupubbapauṇau anupubbapabbhārau na āyatakainaiva papātau| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau anupubbanin‍nau anupubbapauṇau anupubbapabbhārau na āyatakainaiva papātau – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau ṭhitadhammau vailaṃ nātivattati| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau ṭhitadhammau vailaṃ nātivattati – ayaṃ ayampi (syā॰), bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai dutiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau na mataina kuṇapaina saṃvasati| yaṃ hauti mahāsamuddai mataṃ kuṇapaṃ, taṃ khippañ‍ñaiva tīraṃ vāhaiti, thalaṃ ussāraiti| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau na mataina kuṇapaina saṃvasati, yaṃ hauti mahāsamuddai mataṃ kuṇapaṃ, taṃ khippañ‍ñaiva tīraṃ  vāhaiti, thalaṃ ussāraiti – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai tatiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yā kāci mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, tā mahāsamuddaṃ pattā jahanti purimāni nāmagauttāni, mahāsamuddau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchanti| yampi, bhikkhavai, yākāci mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, tā mahāsamuddaṃ pattā jahanti purimāni nāmagauttāni, mahāsamuddau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchanti – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai catutthau  acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, yā ca yākāci (syā॰) laukai savantiyau mahāsamuddaṃ appainti, yā ca antalikkhā dhārā papatanti, na taina mahāsamuddassa ūnattaṃ vā pūrattaṃ vā pañ‍ñāyati| yampi, bhikkhavai, yā ca laukai savantiyau mahāsamuddaṃ appainti, yā ca  antalikkhā dhārā papatanti, na taina mahāsamuddassa ūnattaṃ vā pūrattaṃ vā pañ‍ñāyati – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai pañ‍camau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau aikarasau lauṇarasau| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau aikarasau lauṇarasau – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai chaṭṭhau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau bahuratanau bahūtaratanau (ka॰) anaikaratanau| tatrimāni ratanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – muttā, maṇi, vaiḷuriyau, saṅkhau, silā, pavāḷaṃ, rajataṃ, jātarūpaṃ, lauhitakau, masāragal‍laṃ| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau bahuratanau anaikaratanau, tatrimāni ratanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – muttā, maṇi, vaiḷuriyau, saṅkhau, silā, pavāḷaṃ, rajataṃ, jātarūpaṃ, lauhitakau, masāragal‍laṃ – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai sattamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau| tatrimai bhūtā – timi, timiṅgalau, timitimiṅgalau timirapiṅgalau (sī॰), timitimiṅgalau mahātimiṅgalau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), asurā, nāgā, gandhabbā| santi mahāsamuddai yaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, dviyaujanasatikāpi  attabhāvā, tiyaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, catuyaujanasatikāpi  attabhāvā, pañ‍cayaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā| yampi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau, tatrimai bhūtā – timi, timiṅgalau, timitimiṅgalau, asurā, nāgā, gandhabbā; santi mahāsamuddai, yaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā…pai॰… pañ‍cayaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai aṭṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhiramanti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddai aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā asurā mahāsamuddai abhirama’’nti|

3. imasmiṃdhammavinayaiaṭṭhacchariyaṃ

385. ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti| katamai aṭṭha?

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau anupubbanin‍nau anupubbapauṇau anupubbapabbhārau na āyatakainaiva papātau; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā na āyatakainaiva añ‍ñāpaṭivaidhau| yampi, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai anupubbasikkhā anupubbakiriyā anupubbapaṭipadā na āyatakainaiva añ‍ñāpaṭivaidhau – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau ṭhitadhammau vailaṃ nātivattati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ mayā sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, taṃ mama sāvakā jīvitahaitupi nātik‍kamanti| yampi, bhikkhavai, mayā mama sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ  pañ‍ñattaṃ, taṃ  mama sāvakā jīvitahaitupi nātik‍kamanti – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai dutiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau na mataina kuṇapaina saṃvasati, yaṃ hauti mahāsamuddai mataṃ kuṇapaṃ taṃ khippamaiva tīraṃ vāhaiti, thalaṃ ussāraiti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yau sau puggalau dussīlau pāpadhammau asucisaṅkassarasamācārau paṭicchan‍nakammantau assamaṇau samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñau abrahmacārī brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñau antaupūti avassutau kasambujātau, na taina saṅghau saṃvasati, khippamaiva naṃ san‍nipatitvā ukkhipati, kiñ‍cāpi khau sau hauti majjhai bhikkhusaṅghassa  nisin‍nau| atha khau sau ārakāva saṅghamhā, saṅghau ca taina| yampi, bhikkhavai, yau sau puggalau dussīlau pāpadhammau asucisaṅkassarasamācārau paṭicchan‍nakammantau assamaṇau samaṇapaṭiñ‍ñau abrahmacārī brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñau antaupūti avassutau kasambujātau, na taina saṅghau saṃvasati, khippamaiva naṃ san‍nipūtitvā ukkhipati, kiñ‍cāpi khau sau hauti majjhai bhikkhusaṅghassa nisin‍nau, atha khau sau ārakāva saṅghamhā, saṅghau ca taina – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai tatiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kāci mahānadiyau, saiyyathidaṃ – gaṅgā, yamunā, aciravatī, sarabhū, mahī, tā mahāsamuddaṃ pattā jahanti purimāni nāmagauttāni, mahāsamuddau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cattāraumai vaṇṇā – khattiyā, brāhmaṇā, vaissā, suddā| tai tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā pabbajitā (sī॰) jahanti purimāni nāmagauttāni, samaṇā sakyaputtiyā tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchanti| yampi, bhikkhavai, cattāraumai vaṇṇā khattiyā brāhmaṇā vaissā suddā, tai tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā jahanti purimāni nāmagauttāni, samaṇā sakyaputtiyā tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchanti – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai catutthau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā ca laukai savantiyau mahāsamuddaṃ appainti, yā ca antalikkhā dhārā papatanti, na taina mahāsamuddassa ūnattaṃ vā pūrattaṃ vā pañ‍ñāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bahū caipi bhikkhū anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyanti, na taina nibbānadhātuyā ūnattaṃ vā pūrattaṃ vā pañ‍ñāyati| yampi, bhikkhavai, bahū caipi bhikkhū anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyanti, na taina nibbānadhātuyā ūnattaṃ vā pūrattaṃ vā pañ‍ñāyati – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai pañ‍camau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau aikarasau lauṇarasau, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ dhammavinayau aikarasau vimuttirasau| yampi, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ dhammavinayau aikarasau vimuttirasau – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai chaṭṭhau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau bahuratanau anaikaratanau, tatrimāni ratanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – muttā, maṇi, vaiḷuriyau, saṅkhau, silā, pavāḷaṃ, rajataṃ, jātarūpaṃ, lauhitakau, masāragal‍laṃ; aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ dhammavinayau bahuratanau anaikaratanau| tatrimāni ratanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgā, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| yampi, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ dhammavinayau bahuratanau anaikaratanau, tatrimāni ratanāni, saiyyathidaṃ – cattārau satipaṭṭhānā…pai॰… ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai sattamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahāsamuddau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau, tatrimai bhūtā – timi, timiṅgalau, timitimiṅgalau, asurā, nāgā, gandhabbā, santi mahāsamuddai yaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, dviyaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, tiyaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, catuyaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā, pañ‍cayaujanasatikāpi attabhāvā; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ dhammavinayau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau| tatrimai bhūtā – sautāpan‍nau, sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau; sakadāgāmī, sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya  paṭipan‍nau; anāgāmī, anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau; arahā, arahattaphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau| yampi, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ dhammavinayau mahataṃ bhūtānaṃ āvāsau, tatrimai bhūtā – sautāpan‍nau, sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau…pai॰… arahā, arahataphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai aṭṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, yaṃ disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramanti| ‘‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, yai disvā disvā bhikkhū imasmiṃ dhammavinayai abhiramantī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

udā॰ 45 ‘‘chan‍namativassati suchan‍namativassati (ka॰), vivaṭaṃ nātivassati|

tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraitha, aivaṃ taṃ nātivassatī’’ti||

4. pātimaukkhasavanārahau

386. atha  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘nadānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, itau  paraṃ upausathaṃ karissāmi, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisissāmi| tumhaivadāni, bhikkhavai, itau paraṃ upausathaṃ karaiyyātha, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisaiyyātha| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, anavakāsau yaṃ tathāgatau aparisuddhāya parisāya upausathaṃ karaiyya, pātimaukkhaṃ uddisaiyya| na ca, bhikkhavai na ca bhikkhavai bhikkhunā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), sāpattikaina pātimaukkhaṃ sautabbaṃ| yau suṇaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, yau sāpattikau pātimaukkhaṃ suṇāti, tassa pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, ṭhapaitabbaṃ| tadahupausathai cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai  udāharitabbaṃ –

‘suṇātu mai bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau puggalau sāpattikau, tassa pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaimi, na tasmiṃ sammukhībhūtai pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabba’nti ṭhapitaṃ hauti pātimaukkha’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ‘nāmhai kauci jānātī’ti sāpattikāva pātimaukkhaṃ suṇanti| thairā bhikkhū paracittavidunau bhikkhūnaṃ āraucainti – ‘‘itthan‍nāmau ca itthan‍nāmau ca, āvusau, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ‘nāmhai kauci jānātī’ti sāpattikāva pātimaukkhaṃ suṇantī’’ti| assausuṃ khau chabbaggiyā bhikkhū – ‘‘thairā kira bhikkhū paracittavidunau amhai bhikkhūnaṃ āraucainti – itthan‍nāmau ca itthan‍nāmau ca, āvusau, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ‘nāmhai kauci  jānātī’ti sāpattikāva pātimaukkhaṃ suṇantī’’ti|

tai – puramhākaṃ paisalā bhikkhū pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaintīti – paṭikac‍caiva suddhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anāpattikānaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapainti| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhū suddhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anāpattikānaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰… ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, bhikkhavai, chabbaggiyā bhikkhū suddhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anāpattikānaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaintī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ bhagavā’’ti…pai॰… vigarahitvā…pai॰… dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, suddhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anāpattikānaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaitabbaṃ| yau ṭhapaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa’’|

5. dhammikādhammikapātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ

387. ‘‘aikaṃ, bhikkhavai, adhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ, aikaṃ dhammikaṃ; pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ aikaṃ dhammikaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), dvai adhammikāni; pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni, dvai dhammikāni; tīṇi adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni, tīṇi dhammikāni, cattāri adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni, cattāri dhammikāni; pañ‍ca adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni, pañ‍ca dhammikāni; cha adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni, cha dhammikāni; satta adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni, satta dhammikāni; aṭṭha adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni, aṭṭha dhammikāni; nava adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni, nava dhammikāni; dasa adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni, dasa dhammikāni|

‘‘katamaṃ  aikaṃ adhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ? amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti – idaṃ aikaṃ adhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ| katamaṃ aikaṃ dhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ? samūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti – idaṃ aikaṃ dhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ|

‘‘katamāni dvai adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā  pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, amūlikāya ācāravipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti – imāni dvai adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| katamāni dvai dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? samūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, samūlikāya ācāravipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti – imāni dvai dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

‘‘katamāni tīṇi adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, amūlikāya ācāravipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, amūlikāya diṭṭhivipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti – imāni tīṇi adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| katamāni tīṇi dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? samūlikāya  sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, samūlikāya ācāravipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, samūlikāya diṭṭhivipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti – imāni tīṇi dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

‘‘katamāni cattāri adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, amūlikāya ācāravipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, amūlikāya diṭṭhivipattiyā  pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, amūlikāya ājīvavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti – imāni cattāri adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| katamāni cattāri dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? samūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, samūlikāya ācāravipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, samūlikāya diṭṭhivipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, samūlikāya ājīvavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti – imāni cattāri dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

‘‘katamāni pañ‍ca adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? amūlakaina pārājikaina pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina…pai॰… amūlakaina pācittiyaina… amūlakaina pāṭidaisanīyaina… amūlakaina duk‍kaṭaina pātimaukkhaṃ  ṭhapaiti – imāni pañ‍ca adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? samūlakaina pārājikaina pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, samūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina…pai॰…  pācittiyaina… pāṭidaisanīyaina… duk‍kaṭaina pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti – imāni pañ‍ca dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

‘‘katamāni cha adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya, amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti katāya; amūlikāya ācāravipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya, amūlikāya ācāravipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti katāya; amūlikāya diṭṭhivipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya, amūlikāya diṭṭhivipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti katāya – imāni cha adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| katamāni cha dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? samūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya, samūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti katāya; samūlikāya ācāravipattiyā…pai॰… diṭṭhivipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya…pai॰… katāya – imāni cha dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

‘‘katamāni satta adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? amūlakaina pārājikaina pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina…pai॰…  thul‍lac‍cayaina pācittiyaina pāṭidaisanīyaina duk‍kaṭaina dubbhāsitaina pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti – imāni satta adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| katamāni satta dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? samūlakaina pārājikaina pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti, samūlakaina  saṅghādisaisaina…pai॰… thul‍lac‍cayaina pācittiyaina pāṭidaisanīyaina duk‍kaṭaina dubbhāsitaina pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti – imāni satta dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

‘‘katamāni aṭṭha adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya, amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti katāya; amūlikāya ācāravipattiyā …pai॰… diṭṭhivipattiyā…pai॰… ājīvavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya…pai॰… katāya – imāni aṭṭha adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| katamāni  aṭṭha dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? samūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya, samūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti  katāya; samūlikāya ācāravipattiyā…pai॰… diṭṭhivipattiyā…pai॰… ājīvavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya…pai॰… katāya| imāni aṭṭha dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

‘‘katamāni nava adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya, amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti katāya, amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti katākatāya; amūlikāya ācāravipattiyā…pai॰… diṭṭhivipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya…pai॰… katāya…pai॰… katākatāya imāni nava adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| katamāni nava dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? samūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya, samūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ  ṭhapaiti katāya, samūlikāya sīlavipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti katākatāya; samūlikāya ācāravipattiyā…pai॰… diṭṭhivipattiyā pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti akatāya…pai॰… katāya – katākatāya…pai॰… imāni nava dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

‘‘katamāni dasa adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? na pārājikau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti, na pārājikakathā vippakatā hauti; na sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti, na sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakathā vippakatā hauti; dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ upaiti, na dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ pac‍cādiyati, na dhammikāya sāmaggiyā pac‍cādānakathā vippakatā hauti; na sīlavipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti, na ācāravipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti, na diṭṭhivipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti – imāni dasa adhammikāni  pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| katamāni dasa dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni? pārājikau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti, pārājikakathā vippakatā hauti; sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakathā vippakatā hauti; dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ na upaiti, dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ  pac‍cādiyati, dhammikāya sāmaggiyā pac‍cādānakathā vippakatā hauti; sīlavipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti, ācāravipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti, diṭṭhivipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti – imāni dasa dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

6. dhammikapātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ

388. ‘‘kathaṃ  pārājikau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, yaihi ākāraihi yaihi liṅgaihi yaihi nimittaihi pārājikassa dhammassa ajjhāpatti hauti, taihi ākāraihi taihi liṅgaihi taihi nimittaihi bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantaṃ| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati pārājikaṃ  dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantaṃ, api ca añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’ti| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantaṃ, nāpi nāpi ca (ka॰) añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’ti, apica sauva bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘ahaṃ, āvusau, pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau’ti| ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu taina diṭṭhaina taina sutaina tāya parisaṅkāya tadahupausathai cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharaiyya –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau puggalau pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau, tassa pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaimi, na tasmiṃ sammukhībhūtai  pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbanti – dhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ|

389. ‘‘bhikkhussa pātimaukkhai ṭhapitai parisā vuṭṭhāti, dasan‍naṃ antarāyānaṃ añ‍ñataraina añ‍ñataraina antarāyaina (syā॰ kaṃ॰) – rājantarāyaina vā, caurantarāyaina vā, agyantarāyaina vā, udakantarāyaina vā, manussantarāyaina vā, amanussantarāyaina vā, vāḷantarāyaina vā, sarīsapantarāyaina vā, jīvitantarāyaina  vā, brahmacariyantarāyaina vā| ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tasmiṃ vā āvāsai, añ‍ñasmiṃ vā āvāsai, tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharaiyya –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmassa puggalassa pārājikakathā vippakatā, taṃ vatthu avinicchitaṃ| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau taṃ vatthuṃ vinicchinaiyyāti|

‘‘aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, tadahupausathai cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharitabbaṃ –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmassa puggalassa pārājikakathā vippakatā, taṃ vatthu avinicchitaṃ| tassa pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaimi, na tasmiṃ sammukhībhūtai pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbanti – dhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ|

390. ‘‘kathaṃ sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaihi ākāraihi yaihi liṅgaihi yaihi  nimittaihi sikkhā pac‍cakkhātā hauti, taihi ākāraihi taihi liṅgaihi taihi nimittaihi bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhantaṃ| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhantaṃ, apica añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmaina, āvusau, bhikkhunā sikkhā pac‍cakkhātā’ti| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhantaṃ, nāpi añ‍ñau  bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmaina, āvusau, bhikkhunā sikkhā pac‍cakkhātā’ti, apica sauva bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘mayā, āvusau, sikkhā pac‍cakkhātā’ti| ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu taina diṭṭhaina taina sutaina tāya parisaṅkāya tadahupausathai cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharaiyya –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmaina puggalaina sikkhā pac‍cakkhātā, tassa pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaimi, na tasmiṃ sammukhībhūtai pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbanti – dhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ|

391. ‘‘bhikkhussa pātimaukkhai ṭhapitai parisā vuṭṭhāti, dasan‍naṃ antarāyānaṃ añ‍ñataraina – rājantarāyaina vā  …pai॰… brahmacariyantarāyaina vā, ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tasmiṃ vā āvāsai, añ‍ñasmiṃ vā āvāsai, tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharaiyya –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmassa puggalassa sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakathā sikkhāpac‍cakkhātakathā (sī॰) vippakatā, taṃ vatthu avinicchitaṃ| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau taṃ vatthuṃ vinicchinaiyyāti|

‘‘aivañ‍caitaṃ  labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, tadahupausathai cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharitabbaṃ –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmassa puggalassa sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātakathā vippakatā, taṃ vatthu avinicchitaṃ| tassa pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaimi, na tasmiṃ sammukhībhūtai pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbanti – dhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ|

392. ‘‘kathaṃ dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ na upaiti? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaihi ākāraihi yaihi liṅgaihi yaihi nimittaihi dhammikāya sāmaggiyānupagamanaṃ hauti, taihi ākāraihi taihi liṅgaihi taihi nimittaihi bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ na upaintaṃ| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ na upaintaṃ, api ca añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ na upaitī’ti| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ na upaintaṃ, nāpi añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ na upaitī’ti, api ca sauva bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘ahaṃ, āvusau, dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ na upaimī’ti| ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu taina diṭṭhaina taina sutaina tāya parisaṅkāya tadahupausathai cātuddasai vā  pan‍narasai vā tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharaiyya –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau puggalau dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ na upaiti, tassa pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaimi, na tasmiṃ sammukhībhūtai pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbanti – dhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ|

393. ‘‘kathaṃ dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ pac‍cādiyati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaihi ākāraihi yaihi liṅgaihi yaihi nimittaihi dhammikāya sāmaggiyā pac‍cādānaṃ hauti, taihi ākāraihi  taihi liṅgaihi taihi nimittaihi bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ pac‍cādiyantaṃ na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ pac‍cādiyantaṃ, api ca añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ pac‍cādiyatī’ti| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ pac‍cādiyantaṃ, nāpi añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ pac‍cādiyatī’ti, api ca sauva bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘ahaṃ, āvusau, dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ pac‍cādiyāmī’ti| ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu taina diṭṭhaina taina sutaina tāya parisaṅkāya tadahupausathai cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharaiyya –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau puggalau dhammikaṃ sāmaggiṃ pac‍cādiyati, tassa pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaimi, na tasmiṃ sammukhībhūtai pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbanti – dhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ|

394. ‘‘bhikkhussa pātimaukkhai ṭhapitai parisā vuṭṭhāti, dasan‍naṃ antarāyānaṃ añ‍ñataraina – rājantarāyaina vā…pai॰… brahmacariyantarāyaina  vā| ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tasmiṃ vā āvāsai, añ‍ñasmiṃ vā āvāsai, tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharaiyya –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmassa puggalassa dhammikāya sāmaggiyā pac‍cādānakathā vippakatā, taṃ vatthu avinicchitaṃ| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau taṃ vatthuṃ vinicchinaiyyāti|

‘‘aivañ‍caitaṃ labhaitha, ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai labhaitha, tadahupausathai cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharitabbaṃ –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmassa puggalassa dhammikāya sāmaggiyā pac‍cādānakathā vippakatā, taṃ vatthu avinicchitaṃ| tassa pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaimi, na tasmiṃ sammukhībhūtai pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbanti – dhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ|

395. ‘‘kathaṃ  sīlavipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaihi ākāraihi yaihi liṅgaihi yaihi nimittaihi sīlavipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti, taihi ākāraihi taihi liṅgaihi taihi nimittaihi bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati sīlavipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaṃ| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati sīlavipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaṃ, api ca añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu sīlavipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau’ti| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati sīlavipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaṃ, nāpi añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu  sīlavipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau’ti, api ca sauva bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘ahaṃ, āvusau, sīlavipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaumhī’ti| ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu taina diṭṭhaina taina sutaina tāya parisaṅkāya tadahupausathai cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharaiyya –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau puggalau sīlavipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau, diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) tassa pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaimi, na tasmiṃ sammukhībhūtai pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbanti – dhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ|

396. ‘‘kathaṃ ācāravipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaihi ākāraihi yaihi liṅgaihi yaihi nimittaihi ācāravipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti taihi ākāraihi taihi liṅgaihi taihi nimittaihi bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati ācāravipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaṃ| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati ācāravipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaṃ, api ca añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu ācāravipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau’ti| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati ācāravipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaṃ, nāpi añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu ācāravipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau’ti, api ca sauva bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘ahaṃ, āvusau, ācāravipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaumhī’ti| ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu taina diṭṭhaina taina sutaina tāya  parisaṅkāya tadahupausathai cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharaiyya –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau puggalau ācāravipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau, tassa  pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaimi, na tasmiṃ sammukhībhūtai pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbanti – dhammikaṃ pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ|

397. ‘‘kathaṃ diṭṭhivipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti? idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaihi ākāraihi yaihi liṅgaihi yaihi nimittaihi diṭṭhivipattiyā  diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau hauti, taihi ākāraihi taihi liṅgaihi taihi nimittaihi bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati diṭṭhivipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaṃ| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati diṭṭhivipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaṃ, api ca añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu diṭṭhivipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau’ti| na haiva khau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati diṭṭhivipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaṃ, nāpi añ‍ñau bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘itthan‍nāmau, āvusau, bhikkhu diṭṭhivipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau’ti, api ca sauva bhikkhu bhikkhussa āraucaiti – ‘ahaṃ, āvusau, diṭṭhivipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaumhī’ti| ākaṅkhamānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu taina diṭṭhaina taina sutaina tāya parisaṅkāya tadahupausathai cātuddasai vā pan‍narasai vā tasmiṃ puggalai sammukhībhūtai saṅghamajjhai udāharaiyya –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmau puggalau diṭṭhivipattiyā diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitau, tassa  pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaimi, na tasmiṃ sammukhībhūtai pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbanti – dhammikaṃ  pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ|

‘‘imāni dasa dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanānī’’ti|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

7. attādānaaṅgaṃ

398. atha khau āyasmā upāli yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘attādānaṃ ādātukāmaina, bhantai, bhikkhunā katamaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ kataṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ (ka॰) attādānaṃ ādātabba’’nti?

pari॰ 440 ‘‘attādānaṃ  ādātukāmaina, upāli, bhikkhunā pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ| attādānaṃ ādātukāmaina, upāli, bhikkhunā aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātukāmau, kālau nu khau imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātuṃ udāhu nau’ti? sacai, upāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘akālau imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātuṃ, nau kālau’ti, na taṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai panupāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘kālau imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātuṃ, nau akālau’ti, tainupāli, bhikkhunā uttari pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātukāmau, bhūtaṃ nu khau idaṃ attādānaṃ udāhu nau’ti? sacai, upāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhūtaṃ idaṃ attādānaṃ, nau bhūta’nti, na taṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai panupāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau  aivaṃ jānāti – ‘bhūtaṃ idaṃ attādānaṃ, nau abhūta’nti, tainupāli, bhikkhunā uttari pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātukāmau, atthasañhitaṃ nu khau idaṃ attādānaṃ udāhu nau’ti? sacai, upāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘anatthasañhitaṃ idaṃ attādānaṃ, nau atthasañhita’nti, na taṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai panupāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘atthasañhitaṃ idaṃ attādānaṃ, nau anatthasañhita’nti, tainupāli, bhikkhunā uttari pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau ahaṃ attādānaṃ ādiyamānau labhissāmi sandiṭṭhai sambhattai bhikkhū dhammatau vinayatau pakkhai udāhu nau’ti? sacai, upāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘imaṃ khau ahaṃ attādānaṃ ādiyamānau na labhissāmi sandiṭṭhai sambhattai bhikkhū dhammatau vinayatau pakkhai’ti, na taṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai panupāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘imaṃ khau ahaṃ  attādānaṃ ādiyamānau labhissāmi sandiṭṭhai sambhattai bhikkhū dhammatau vinayatau pakkhai’ti, tainupāli, bhikkhunā uttari pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai attādānaṃ ādiyatau bhavissati saṅghassa tataunidānaṃ bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau saṅghabhaidau saṅgharāji saṅghavavatthānaṃ saṅghanānākaraṇaṃ udāhu nau’ti? sacai upāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘imaṃ khau mai attādānaṃ  ādiyatau bhavissati saṅghassa tataunidānaṃ bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau saṅghabhaidau saṅgharāji saṅghavavatthānaṃ  saṅghanānākaraṇa’nti, na taṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ| sacai panupāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘imaṃ khau mai attādānaṃ ādiyatau na bhavissati saṅghassa tataunidānaṃ bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau  vivādau saṅghabhaidau saṅgharāji saṅghavavatthānaṃ saṅghanānākaraṇa’nti, ādātabbaṃ taṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ| aivaṃ pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ khau, upāli, attādānaṃ ādin‍naṃ, pacchāpi avippaṭisārakaraṃ bhavissatī’ti|

8. caudakainapac‍cavaikkhitabbadhammā

399. pari॰ 436 (a॰ ni॰ 10.44 thaukaṃ visadisaṃ) ‘‘caudakaina, bhantai, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina kati dhammai ajjhattaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti? ‘‘caudakaina, upāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā parau caudaitabbau|

‘‘caudakaina, upāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘parisuddhakāyasamācārau nu khaumhi, parisuddhainamhi kāyasamācāraina saman‍nāgatau – acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina? saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti? nau cai, upāli, bhikkhu parisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti, parisuddhaina kāyasamācāraina saman‍nāgatau – acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha tāva āyasmā kāyikaṃ sikkhassū’ti| itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, caudakaina bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘parisuddhavacīsamācārau nu khaumhi, parisuddhainamhi vacīsamācāraina saman‍nāgatau – acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina? saṃvij‍jati nu  khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti? nau cai, upāli, bhikkhu parisuddhavacīsamācārau hauti, parisuddhaina vacīsamācāraina saman‍nāgatau – acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha tāva āyasmā vācasikaṃ sikkhassū’ti| itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, caudakaina bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘maittaṃ nu khau mai cittaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ sabrahmacārīsu anāghātaṃ, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti? nau cai, upāli, bhikkhunau maittacittaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu anāghātaṃ, tassa  bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha tāva āyasmā sabrahmacārīsu maittacittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaihī’ti| itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, caudakaina bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘bahussutau nu khaumhi, sutadharau, sutasan‍nic‍cayau? yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā, sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpā mai dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā? saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti? nau cai, upāli, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti, sutadharau, sutasan‍nic‍cayau; yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā, sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ  parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpassa dhammā na bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha tāva āyasmā āgamaṃ pariyāpuṇassū’ti| itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, caudakaina bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘ubhayāni khau mai pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti, suvibhattāni, suppavattīni, suvinicchitāni – suttasau, anubyañ‍janasau? saṃviñ‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti? nau cai, upāli, bhikkhunau ubhayāni pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti, suvibhattāni, suppavattīni, suvinicchitāni – suttasau, anubyañ‍janasau, ‘idaṃ panāvusau, kattha vuttaṃ bhagavatā’ti, iti puṭṭhau na sampāyati na sampādayati (ka॰), tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha tāva āyasmā, vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇassū’ti| itissa bhavanti vattārau| caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti|

9. caudakainaupaṭṭhāpaitabbadhammā

400. ‘‘caudakaina, bhantai, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina kati dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti? ‘‘caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā parau caudaitabbau – kālaina vakkhāmi, nau akālaina; bhūtaina vakkhāmi, nau  abhūtaina; saṇhaina  vakkhāmi, nau pharusaina; atthasaṃhitaina vakkhāmi, nau anatthasaṃhitaina; maittacittau  vakkhāmi, nau dausantarauti| caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti|

10. caudakacuditakapaṭisaṃyuttakathā

401. ‘‘adhammacaudakassa, bhantai, bhikkhunau katīhākāraihi vippaṭisārau  upadahātabbau’’ti? ‘‘adhammacaudakassa, upāli, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahākāraihi vippaṭisārau upadahātabbau – akālaināyasmā caudaisi, nau kālaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; abhūtaināyasmā caudaisi, nau bhūtaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; pharusaināyasmā caudaisi, nau saṇhaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; anatthasaṃhitaināyasmā caudaisi, nau atthasaṃhitaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; dausantarau āyasmā caudaisi, nau maittacittau, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāyāti| adhammacaudakassa, upāli, bhikkhunau imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi vippaṭisārau upadahātabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā na añ‍ñaupi bhikkhu abhūtaina caudaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘adhammacuditassa pana, bhantai, bhikkhunau katihākāraihi avippaṭisārau upadahātabbau’’ti? ‘‘adhammacuditassa, upāli, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahākāraihi avippaṭisārau upadahātabbau – akālaināyasmā cuditau, nau kālaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; abhūtaināyasmā cuditau, nau bhūtaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; pharusaināyasmā cuditau, nau saṇhaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; anatthasaṃhitaināyasmā cuditau, nau atthasaṃhitaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; dausantaraināyasmā cuditau, nau maittacittaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāyāti| adhammacuditassa, upāli, bhikkhunau imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi avippaṭisārau upadahātabbau’’ti|

‘‘dhammacaudakassa, bhantai, bhikkhunau katihākāraihi avippaṭisārau upadahātabbau’’ti? ‘‘dhammacaudakassa, upāli, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahākāraihi avippaṭisārau upadahātabbau – kālaināyasmā caudaisi, nau akālaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; bhūtaināyasmā caudaisi, nau abhūtaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; saṇhaināyasmā caudaisi, nau pharusaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; atthasaṃhitaināyasmā caudaisi, nau anatthasaṃhitaina, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāya; maittacittau āyasmā caudaisi, nau dausantarau, alaṃ tai avippaṭisārāyāti| dhammacaudakassa, upāli, bhikkhunau imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi avippaṭisārau upadahātabbau| taṃ kissa haitu? yathā añ‍ñaupi bhikkhu bhūtaina caudaitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘dhammacuditassa  pana, bhantai, bhikkhunau katihākāraihi vippaṭisārau upadahātabbau’’ti? ‘‘dhammacuditassa, upāli, bhikkhunau pañ‍cahākāraihi vippaṭisārau upadahātabbau – kālaināyasmā cuditau, nau akālaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; bhūtaināyasmā cuditau, nau abhūtaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; saṇhaināyasmā cuditau, nau pharusaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; atthasaṃhitaināyasmā cuditau, nau anatthasaṃhitaina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāya; maittacittaināyasmā cuditau, nau dausantaraina, alaṃ tai vippaṭisārāyāti| dhammacuditassa, upāli, bhikkhunau imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi vippaṭisārau upadahātabbau’’ti|

pari॰ 438 ‘‘caudakaina, bhantai, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina kati dhammai ajjhattaṃ manasi karitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti? ‘‘caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ manasi karitvā parau caudaitabbau – kāruñ‍ñatā, hitaisitā, anukampitā, anukampatā (ka॰) āpattivuṭṭhānatā, vinayapuraikkhāratāti| caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ  manasi karitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti|

‘‘cuditaina pana, bhantai, bhikkhunā katisu dhammaisu patiṭṭhātabba’’nti? ‘‘cuditainupāli, bhikkhunā dvīsu dhammaisu patiṭṭhātabbaṃ – sac‍cai ca akuppai cā’’ti|

dutiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanakkhandhakau navamau|

imamhi khandhakai vatthū tiṃsa|

tassuddānaṃ –

upausathai  yāvatikaṃ, pāpabhikkhu na nikkhami|

mauggal‍lānaina nicchuddhau, acchairā jinasāsanai||

nin‍naunupubbasikkhā ca, ṭhitadhammau nātik‍kamma|

kuṇapukkhipati saṅghau, savantiyau jahanti ca||

savanti parinibbanti, aikarasa vimutti ca|

bahu dhammavinayaupi, bhūtaṭṭhāriyapuggalā||

samuddaṃ  upamaṃ katvā, vācaisi ṭhāpaisi (ka॰) sāsanai guṇaṃ|

upausathai pātimaukkhaṃ, na amhai kauci jānāti||

paṭikac‍caiva ujjhanti, aikau dvai tīṇi cattāri|

pañ‍ca cha satta aṭṭhāni, navā ca dasamāni ca||

sīla-ācāra-diṭṭhi ca, ājīvaṃ catubhāgikai|

pārājikañ‍ca saṅghādi, pācitti pāṭidaisani||

duk‍kaṭaṃ pañ‍cabhāgaisu, sīlācāravipatti ca|

akatāya katāya ca, chabhāgaisu yathāvidhi||

pārājikañ‍ca saṅghādi, thul‍laṃ pācittiyaina ca|

pāṭidaisaniyañ‍caiva, duk‍kaṭañ‍ca dubbhāsitaṃ||

sīlācāravipatti ca, diṭṭhiājīvavipatti|

yā ca aṭṭhā katākatai, tainaitā sīlācāradiṭṭhiyā||

akatāya katāyāpi, katākatāyamaiva ca|

aivaṃ navavidhā vuttā, yathābhūtaina ñāyatau||

pārājikau vippakatā, pac‍cakkhātau tathaiva ca|

upaiti pac‍cādiyati, pac‍cādānakathā ca yā||

sīlācāravipatti ca, tathā diṭṭhivipattiyā|

diṭṭhasutaparisaṅkitaṃ, dasadhā taṃ vijānātha||

bhikkhu  vipassati bhikkhuṃ, añ‍ñau cāraucayāti taṃ|

sau yaiva tassa akkhāti vipassañ‍ñau cāraucati| taṃ suddhaiva tassa akkhāti (ka॰), pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaiti sau||

vuṭṭhāti  antarāyaina, rājacauraggudakā ca|

manussaamanussā ca, vāḷasarīsapā jīvibrahmaṃ||

dasan‍namañ‍ñataraina, tasmiṃ añ‍ñataraisu vā|

dhammikādhammikā  caiva, yathā maggaina jānātha||

kālabhūtatthasaṃhitaṃ, labhissāmi bhavissati|

kāyavācasikā maittā, bāhusac‍caṃ ubhayāni||

kālabhūtaina saṇhaina, atthamaittaina caudayai|

vippaṭisāradhammaina, tathā vācā tathaivāpi (syā॰) vinaudayai||

dhammacaudacuditassa, vinaudaiti vippaṭisārau|

karuṇā hitānukampi, vuṭṭhānapuraikkhāratau||

caudakassa paṭipatti, sambuddhaina pakāsitā|

sac‍cai caiva akuppai ca, cuditassaiva dhammatāti||

pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanakkhandhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



10. bhikkhunikkhandhakaṃ

1. paṭhamabhāṇavārau

mahāpajāpatigautamīvatthu

402. a॰ ni॰ 8.51 idaṃ vatthu āgataṃ taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau mahāpajāpati mahāpajāpatī (sī॰ syā॰) gautamī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau mahāpajāpati gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, labhaiyya mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, gautami, mā tai ruc‍ci mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jā’’ti| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau mahapajāpati gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, labhaiyya mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, gautami, mā tai ruc‍ci mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jā’’ti| atha khau mahāpajāpati gautamī – na bhagavā anujānāti mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍janti – dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau bhagavā kapilavatthusmiṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina vaisālī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina vaisālī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai  kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau mahāpajāpati gautamī kaisai chaidāpaitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā sambahulāhi sākiyānīhi saddhiṃ yaina vaisālī taina pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina yaina vaisālī mahāvanaṃ kūṭāgārasālā tainupasaṅkami| atha khau mahāpajāpati gautamī sūnaihi pādaihi rajaukiṇṇaina gattaina dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā  bahidvārakauṭṭhakai aṭṭhāsi| addasā khau āyasmā  ānandau mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ sūnaihi pādaihi rajaukiṇṇaina gattaina dukkhiṃ dummanaṃ assumukhiṃ rudamānaṃ bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitaṃ| disvāna mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘kissa tvaṃ, gautami, sūnaihi pādaihi rajaukiṇṇaina gattaina dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitā’’ti? ‘‘tathā hi pana, bhantai ānanda, na bhagavā anujānāti mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, gautami, muhuttaṃ idhaiva tāva hauhi, yāvāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ yācāmi mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aisā, bhantai, mahāpajāpati gautamī sūnaihi pādaihi rajaukiṇṇaina gattaina dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitā – na bhagavā  anujānāti mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍janti| sādhu, bhantai, labhaiyya mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, ānanda, mā tai ruc‍ci mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jā’’ti| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, labhaiyya mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| ‘‘alaṃ, ānanda, mā tai ruc‍ci mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau a॰ ni॰ 8.51 ‘‘atha khau āyasmatau ānandassa aitadahausī’’ti āgataṃ – ‘‘na bhagavā anujānāti mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ; yaṃnūnāhaṃ añ‍ñainapi pariyāyaina bhagavantaṃ yācaiyyaṃ mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti| atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhabbau nu khau, bhantai, mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā sautāpattiphalaṃ vā sakadāgāmiphalaṃ sakidāgāmiphalaṃ (syā॰) vā anāgāmiphalaṃ vā arahattaphalaṃ  vā sacchikātu’’nti? ‘‘bhabbau, ānanda, mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā sautāpattiphalampi sakadāgāmiphalampi  anāgāmiphalampi arahattaphalampi sacchikātu’’nti| ‘‘sacai, bhantai, bhabbau mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā sautāpattiphalampi sakadāgāmiphalampi  anāgāmiphalampi arahattaphalampi sacchikātuṃ; bahūpakārā, bhantai, mahāpajāpati gautamī bhagavatau mātucchā āpādikā, pausikā, khīrassa dāyikā, bhagavantaṃ janaittiyā kālaṅkatāya thañ‍ñaṃ pāyaisi; sādhu, bhantai, labhaiyya mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍ja’’nti|

aṭṭhagarudhammā

403. ‘‘sacai, ānanda, mahāpajāpatī gautamī aṭṭha garudhammai paṭiggaṇhāti, sāvassā| hautu upasampadā|

pāci॰ 149 ‘‘vassasatūpasampan‍nāya bhikkhuniyā tadahupasampan‍nassa bhikkhunau abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ kātabbaṃ| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā garuṃkatvā (ka॰) mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘na bhikkhuniyā abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ vasitabbaṃ| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘anvaddhamāsaṃ bhikkhuniyā bhikkhusaṅghatau dvai dhammā pac‍cāsīsitabbā – upausathapucchakañ‍ca, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘vassaṃvuṭṭhāya bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai  tīhi ṭhānaihi pavāraitabbaṃ – diṭṭhaina vā, sutaina vā, parisaṅkāya vā| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘garudhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nāya bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai pakkhamānattaṃ caritabbaṃ| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya sikkhamānāya ubhatausaṅghai upasampadā pariyaisitabbā| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘na  bhikkhuniyā kainaci pariyāyaina bhikkhu ak‍kausitabbau paribhāsitabbau| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘aj‍jataggai auvaṭau bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūsu vacanapathau, anauvaṭau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīsu vacanapathau| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘sacai, ānanda, mahāpajāpati gautamī imai aṭṭha garudhammai paṭiggaṇhāti, sāvassā hautu upasampadā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau santikai aṭṭha garudhammai uggahaitvā yaina mahāpajāpati gautamī tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, gautami, aṭṭha garudhammai paṭiggaṇhaiyyāsi, sāva tai bhavissati upasampadā|

‘‘vassasatūpasampan‍nāya bhikkhuniyā tadahupasampan‍nassa bhikkhunau abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ kātabbaṃ| ayampi  dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘na bhikkhuniyā abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ vasitabbaṃ| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘anvaddhamāsaṃ bhikkhuniyā bhikkhusaṅghatau dvai dhammā pac‍cāsīsitabbā – upausathapucchakañ‍ca, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘vassaṃvuṭṭhāya bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai tīhi ṭhānaihi pavāraitabbaṃ – diṭṭhaina vā, sutaina vā, parisaṅkāya vā| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘garudhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nāya bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai pakkhamānattaṃ caritabbaṃ| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘dvai  vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhāya sikkhamānāya ubhatausaṅghai upasampadā pariyaisitabbā| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘na  bhikkhuniyā kainaci pariyāyaina bhikkhu ak‍kausitabbau paribhāsitabbau| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘aj‍jataggai auvaṭau bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūsu vacanapathau, anauvaṭau bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīsu vacanapathau| ayampi dhammau sak‍katvā garukatvā mānaitvā pūjaitvā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyau|

‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, gautami, imai aṭṭha garudhammai paṭiggaṇhaiyyāsi, sāva tai bhavissati  upasampadā’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai ānanda, itthī vā purisau vā daharau, yuvā, maṇḍanakajātikau sīsaṃnahātau uppalamālaṃ vā vassikamālaṃ vā atimuttakamālaṃ  adhimattakamālaṃ (syā॰) vā labhitvā ubhauhi hatthaihi paṭiggahaitvā uttamaṅgai sirasmiṃ patiṭṭhāpaiyya; aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, bhantai, ānanda imai aṭṭha garudhammai paṭiggaṇhāmi yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyai’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭiggahitā, bhantai, mahāpajāpatiyā gautamiyā aṭṭha garudhammā, upasampan‍nā bhagavatau mātucchā’’ti|

‘‘sacai, ānanda, nālabhissa mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ, ciraṭṭhitikaṃ, ānanda, brahmacariyaṃ abhavissa, vassasahassaṃ saddhammau tiṭṭhaiyya| yatau ca khau, ānanda, mātugāmau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau, na dāni, ānanda, brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ bhavissati| pañ‍caiva dāni, ānanda, vassasatāni saddhammau ṭhassati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, yāni kānici kulāni  bahutthikāni bahuitthikāni (sī॰ syā॰) appapurisakāni, tāni suppadhaṃsiyāni  haunti cauraihi kumbhathainakaihi; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yasmiṃ dhammavinayai labhati mātugāmau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ, na taṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sampan‍nai sālikkhaittai saitaṭṭikā nāma raugajāti nipatati, aivaṃ taṃ sālikkhaittaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yasmiṃ dhammavinayai labhati mātugāmau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ, na taṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sampan‍nai ucchukkhaittai mañ‍jiṭṭhikā mañ‍jaiṭṭhikā (sī॰ syā॰) nāma raugajāti nipatati, aivaṃ taṃ ucchukkhaittaṃ na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, yasmiṃ dhammavinayai labhati mātugāmau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ, na taṃ brahmacariyaṃ ciraṭṭhitikaṃ hauti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, ānanda, purisau mahatau taḷākassa paṭikac‍caiva āḷiṃ bandhaiyya yāvadaiva udakassa anatik‍kamanāya; aivamaiva khau, ānanda, mayā paṭikac‍caiva bhikkhunīnaṃ aṭṭha garudhammā pañ‍ñattā yāvajīvaṃ anatik‍kamanīyā’’ti|

bhikkhunīnaṃ aṭṭha garudhammā niṭṭhitā|

bhikkhunīupasampadānujānanaṃ

404. atha khau mahāpajāpati gautamī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā  khau mahāpajāpatī gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathāhaṃ, bhantai, imāsu sākiyānīsu  paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti? atha khau bhagavā mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau mahāpajāpati gautamī bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhuniyau upasampādaitu’’nti|

atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ayyā anupasampan‍nā, mayañ‍camhā upasampan‍nā; aivañhi bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ bhikkhūhi bhikkhuniyau upasampādaitabbā’’ti| atha khau mahāpajāpati gautamī  yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā  āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau mahāpajāpati gautamī āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imā maṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhikkhuniyau aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ayyā anupasampan‍nā, mayañ‍camhā upasampan‍nā; aivañhi bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ bhikkhūhi bhikkhuniyau upasampādaitabbā’’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘mahāpajāpati, bhantai, gautamī aivamāha – ‘imā maṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhikkhuniyau aivamāhaṃsu – ayyā anupasampan‍nā, mayañ‍camhā upasampan‍nā; aivañhi bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ bhikkhūhi bhikkhuniyau upasampādaitabbā’’’ti|

‘‘yadaggaina, ānanda, mahāpajāpatiyā gautamiyā aṭṭha garudhammā paṭiggahitā, tadaiva sā tadaivassā (ka॰) upasampan‍nā’’ti|

405. atha khau mahāpajāpati gautamī yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau mahāpajāpati gautamī āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aikāhaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhagavantaṃ varaṃ yācāmi| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā anujānaiyya bhikkhūnañ‍ca bhikkhunīnañ‍ca yathāvuḍḍhaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikamma’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mahāpajāpati, bhantai, gautamī aivamāha – ‘aikāhaṃ, bhantai ānanda, bhagavantaṃ varaṃ yācāmi| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā anujānaiyya bhikkhūnañ‍ca bhikkhunīnañ‍ca yathāvuḍḍhaṃ abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikamma’’’nti|

‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, ānanda, anavakāsau, yaṃ tathāgatau anujānaiyya mātugāmassa abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ| imaihi nāma, ānanda, añ‍ñatitthiyā durakkhātadhammā mātugāmassa abhivādanaṃ  pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ  sāmīcikammaṃ na karissanti; kimaṅgaṃ pana tathāgatau anujānissati mātugāmassa abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikamma’’nti?

atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, mātugāmassa abhivādanaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ sāmīcikammaṃ kātabbaṃ| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

atha khau mahāpajāpati gautamī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau mahāpajāpati gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yāni tāni, bhantai, bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadāni bhikkhūhi sādhāraṇāni, kathaṃ mayaṃ, bhantai, taisu sikkhāpadaisu paṭipaj‍jāmā’’ti? ‘‘yāni tāni, gautami, bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadāni bhikkhūhi sādhāraṇāni, yathā bhikkhū sikkhanti tathā taisu sikkhāpadaisu sikkhathā’’ti| ‘‘yāni pana tāni, bhantai, bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadāni bhikkhūhi asādhāraṇāni, kathaṃ mayaṃ, bhantai, taisu sikkhāpadaisu paṭipaj‍jāmā’’ti? ‘‘yāni tāni, gautami, bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadāni bhikkhūhi asādhāraṇāni, yathāpañ‍ñattaisu sikkhāpadaisu sikkhathā’’ti|

406. a॰ ni॰ 8.53 atha khau mahāpajāpati gautamī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau mahāpajāpati gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ  sutvā aikā vūpakaṭṭhā appamattā ātāpinī pahitattā viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yai khau tvaṃ, gautami, dhammai jānaiyyāsi – imai dhammā sarāgāya saṃvattanti nau virāgāya, sañ‍ñaugāya saṃvattanti nau visañ‍ñaugāya, ācayāya saṃvattanti nau apacayāya, mahicchatāya saṃvattanti nau appicchatāya, asantuṭṭhiyā saṃvattanti nau santuṭṭhiyā, saṅgaṇikāya saṃvattanti nau pavivaikāya, kausaj‍jāya saṃvattanti nau vīriyārambhāya, dubbharatāya  saṃvattanti nau subharatāya; aikaṃsaina, gautami, dhāraiyyāsi – naisau dhammau, naisau vinayau, naitaṃ satthusāsananti| yai ca khau tvaṃ, gautami, dhammai jānaiyyāsi – imai dhammā virāgāya saṃvattanti nau sarāgāya, visañ‍ñaugāya saṃvattanti nau sañ‍ñaugāya, apacayāya saṃvattanti nau ācayāya, appicchatāya saṃvattanti nau  mahicchatāya, santuṭṭhiyā saṃvattanti nau asantuṭṭhiyā, pavivaikāya saṃvattanti nau saṅgaṇikāya, vīriyārambhāya  saṃvattanti nau kausaj‍jāya, subharatāya saṃvattanti nau dubbharatāya; aikaṃsaina, gautami, dhāraiyyāsi – aisau dhammau, aisau vinayau, aitaṃ satthusāsana’’nti|

407. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhunīnaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ na uddisīyati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ…pai॰…  ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīnaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitu’’nti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau bhikkhunīnaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhunīnaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘jāyāyau imā imaisaṃ, jāriyau imā imaisaṃ, idāni imai imāhi saddhiṃ abhiramissantī’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabbaṃ| yau uddisaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīhi bhikkhunīnaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitu’’nti| bhikkhuniyau na jānanti – ‘‘aivaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisitabba’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ ācikkhituṃ – ‘aivaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisaiyyāthā’’’ti|

408. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau āpattiṃ na paṭikaraunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā āpatti na paṭikātabbā| yā na paṭikaraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti| bhikkhuniyau na jānanti – ‘‘aivaṃ āpatti paṭikātabbā’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ ācikkhituṃ – ‘aivaṃ āpattiṃ paṭikaraiyyāthā’’’ti| atha  khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau bhikkhunīnaṃ āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ āpattiṃ paṭiggahaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau rathikāyapi byūhaipi siṅghāṭakaipi bhikkhuṃ passitvā pattaṃ bhūmiyaṃ nikkhipitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ  nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā āpattiṃ paṭikaraunti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘jāyāyau imā imaisaṃ, jāriyau imā imaisaṃ, rattiṃ vimānaitvā idāni khamāpaintī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā| yau paṭiggaṇhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīhi bhikkhunīnaṃ āpattiṃ paṭiggahaitu’’nti| bhikkhuniyau na jānanti – ‘‘aivaṃ āpatti paṭiggahaitabbā’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ ācikkhituṃ – ‘aivaṃ āpattiṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyyāthā’’’ti|

409. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhunīnaṃ kammaṃ na kariyati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīnaṃ kammaṃ kātu’’nti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kaina nu khau bhikkhunīnaṃ kammaṃ kātabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ kammaṃ kātu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina katakammā bhikkhuniyau rathikāyapi byūhaipi siṅghāṭakaipi bhikkhuṃ passitvā pattaṃ bhūmiyaṃ nikkhipitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā khamāpainti ‘aivaṃ nūna kātabba’nti mañ‍ñamānā| manussā tathaiva ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘jāyāyau imā imaisaṃ, jāriyau imā imaisaṃ, rattiṃ vimānaitvā idāni khamāpaintī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| yau karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīhi bhikkhunīnaṃ kammaṃ kātu’’nti| bhikkhuniyau na jānanti – ‘‘aivaṃ kammaṃ kātabba’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ ācikkhituṃ – ‘aivaṃ kammaṃ karaiyyāthā’’’ti|

410. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau saṅghamajjhai bhaṇḍanajātā  kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti| na sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitu’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhū bhikkhunīnaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamainti| tasmiṃ khau pana adhikaraṇai vinicchiyamānai dissanti bhikkhuniyau kammappattāyaupi āpattigāminiyaupi| bhikkhuniyau aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai, ayyāva bhikkhunīnaṃ kammaṃ karauntu, ayyāva bhikkhunīnaṃ āpattiṃ paṭiggaṇhantu; aivañhi bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ  vūpasamaitabba’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ kammaṃ āraupaitvā bhikkhunīnaṃ niyyādaituṃ – bhikkhunīhi bhikkhunīnaṃ kammaṃ kātuṃ, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ āpattiṃ āraupaitvā bhikkhunīnaṃ niyyādaituṃ, bhikkhunīhi bhikkhunīnaṃ āpattiṃ paṭiggahaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina uppalavaṇṇāya bhikkhuniyā antaivāsinī bhikkhunī satta vassāni bhagavantaṃ anubandhā hauti vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇantī| tassā muṭṭhassatiniyā gahitau gahitau mussati| assausi khau sā bhikkhunī – ‘‘bhagavā kira sāvatthiṃ gantukāmau’’ti| atha khau tassā bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘ahaṃ khau satta vassāni bhagavantaṃ anubandhiṃ vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇantī| tassā mai muṭṭhassatiniyā gahitau gahitau mussati| duk‍karaṃ khau pana mātugāmaina yāvajīvaṃ satthāraṃ  anubandhituṃ| kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? atha khau sā bhikkhunī bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ vinayaṃ vācaitu’’nti|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

2. dutiyabhāṇavārau

411. atha khau bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina sāvatthi taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sāvatthi tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau kaddamaudakaina ausiñ‍canti – appaiva nāma amhaisu sāraj‍jaiyyunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā  bhikkhuniyau kaddamaudakaina ausiñ‍citabbā| yau ausiñ‍caiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tassa bhikkhunau daṇḍakammaṃ kātu’’nti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau daṇḍakammaṃ kātabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘avandiyau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhunisaṅghaina kātabbau’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kāyaṃ vivaritvā bhikkhunīnaṃ dassainti  …pai॰… ūruṃ vivaritvā bhikkhunīnaṃ dassainti, aṅgajātaṃ vivaritvā bhikkhunīnaṃ dassainti, bhikkhuniyau aubhāsainti, bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ sampayaujainti – appaiva nāma amhaisu sāraj‍jaiyyunti| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā kāyau vivaritvā bhikkhunīnaṃ dassaitabbau, na ūru vivaritvā bhikkhunīnaṃ dassaitabbau, na aṅgajātaṃ vivaritvā bhikkhunīnaṃ dassaitabbaṃ, na bhikkhuniyau aubhāsitabbā, na bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ sampayaujaitabbaṃ| yau sampayaujaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tassa bhikkhunau daṇḍakammaṃ kātu’’nti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau daṇḍakammaṃ kātabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘avandiyau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhikkhunisaṅghaina kātabbau’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhuṃ kaddamaudakaina ausiñ‍canti – appaiva nāma amhaisu sāraj‍jaiyyunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā bhikkhu kaddamaudakaina ausiñ‍citabbau| yā ausiñ‍caiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tassā bhikkhuniyā daṇḍakammaṃ kātu’’nti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau daṇḍakammaṃ kātabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi bhikkhavai, āvaraṇaṃ kātu’’nti| āvaraṇai katai na ādiyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, auvādaṃ ṭhapaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau kāyaṃ vivaritvā bhikkhūnaṃ dassainti, thanaṃ vivaritvā bhikkhūnaṃ dassainti, ūruṃ vivaritvā  bhikkhūnaṃ dassainti, aṅgajātaṃ vivaritvā bhikkhūnaṃ dassainti, bhikkhū aubhāsainti, bhikkhūhi  saddhiṃ sampayaujainti – appaiva nāma amhaisu sāraj‍jaiyyunti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā kāyau vivaritvā bhikkhūnaṃ dassaitabbau…pai॰… na thanau vivaritvā  bhikkhūnaṃ dassaitabbau, na ūru vivaritvā bhikkhūnaṃ dassaitabbau, na aṅgajātaṃ vivaritvā bhikkhūnaṃ dassaitabbaṃ, na bhikkhū aubhāsitabbā, na bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ sampayaujaitabbaṃ| yā sampayaujaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, tassā bhikkhuniyā daṇḍakammaṃ kātu’’nti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau daṇḍakammaṃ kātabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āvaraṇaṃ kātu’’nti| āvaraṇai katai na ādiyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, auvādaṃ ṭhapaitu’’nti| atha khau bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kappati nu khau auvādaṭṭhapitāya auvādaṇṭhapitāya (syā॰), auvādaṃṭhapitāya (ka॰) bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ upausathaṃ kātuṃ, na nu khau  kappatī’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, auvādaṭṭhapitāya bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ upausathau kātabbau, yāva na taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasantaṃ hautī’’ti|

412. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā udāyī auvādaṃ ṭhapaitvā cārikaṃ  pak‍kāmi| bhikkhuniyau ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma ayyau udāyī auvādaṃ ṭhapaitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, auvādaṃ ṭhapaitvā cārikā pak‍kamitabbā| yau pak‍kamaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū (syā॰ kaṃ॰) bālā abyattā auvādaṃ ṭhapainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bālaina abyattaina auvādau ṭhapaitabbau| yau ṭhapaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai auvādaṃ ṭhapainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai auvādau ṭhapaitabbau| yau ṭhapaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū auvādaṃ ṭhapaitvā vinicchayaṃ na dainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, auvādaṃ ṭhapaitvā vinicchayau na dātabbau| yau na dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

413. pāci॰ 1054 taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau auvādaṃ na gacchanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā auvādau na  gantabbau| yā na gacchaiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina sabbau bhikkhunisaṅghau auvādaṃ gacchati| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti  vipācainti – ‘‘jāyāyau imā imaisaṃ, jāriyau imā imaisaṃ, idāni imai imāhi saddhiṃ abhiramissantī’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, sabbaina bhikkhunisaṅghaina auvādau gantabbau| gacchaiyya cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, catūhi pañ‍cahi bhikkhunīhi auvādaṃ gantu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina catassau pañ‍ca bhikkhuniyau auvādaṃ gacchanti| manussā tathaiva ujjhāyanti  khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘jāyāyau imā imaisaṃ, jāriyau imā imaisaṃ, idāni imai imāhi saddhiṃ abhiramissantī’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, catūhi pañ‍cahi bhikkhunīhi auvādau gantabbau| gacchaiyyuṃ cai, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dvai tissau bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīhi (ka॰) auvādaṃ gantuṃ| aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘bhikkhunisaṅghau, ayya, bhikkhusaṅghassa pādai vandati, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca yācati; labhatu kira, ayya, bhikkhunisaṅghau auvādūpasaṅkamana’nti| taina bhikkhunā pātimaukkhuddaisakau upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘bhikkhunīsaṅghau, bhantai, bhikkhusaṅghassa pādai vandati, auvādūpasaṅkamanañ‍ca  yācati; labhatu kira, bhantai, bhikkhunisaṅghau auvādūpasaṅkamana’nti| pātimaukkhuddaisakaina vattabbau – ‘atthi kauci bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau’ti? sacai hauti kauci bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau, pātimaukkhuddaisakaina vattabbau – ‘itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau, taṃ bhikkhunisaṅghau upasaṅkamatū’ti| sacai na hauti kauci bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau, pātimaukkhuddaisakaina vattabbau – ‘kau āyasmā ussahati bhikkhuniyau auvaditu’nti? sacai kauci ussahati bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ, sau ca hauti aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau, samman‍nitvā vattabbau – ‘itthan‍nāmau bhikkhu  bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau, taṃ bhikkhunisaṅghau upasaṅkamatū’ti| sacai na kauci ussahati bhikkhuniyau auvadituṃ, pātimaukkhuddaisakaina vattabbau – ‘natthi kauci bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau sammatau, pāsādikaina bhikkhunisaṅghau sampādaitū’’ti|

414. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū auvādaṃ na gaṇhanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, auvādau na gahaitabbau| yau na gaṇhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bālau hauti| taṃ bhikkhuniyau upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘auvādaṃ, ayya, gaṇhāhī’’ti| ‘‘ahañhi, bhaginī, bālau; kathāhaṃ auvādaṃ gaṇhāmī’’ti? ‘‘gaṇhāhayya, auvādaṃ; aivañhi bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ – bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau gahaitabbau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ṭhapaitvā bālaṃ, avasaisaihi auvādaṃ gahaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gilānau hauti| taṃ bhikkhuniyau upasaṅkamitvā  aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘auvādaṃ, ayya gaṇhāhī’’ti| ‘‘ahañhi, bhaginī, gilānau; kathāhaṃ auvādaṃ gaṇhāmī’’ti? ‘‘gaṇhāhayya, auvādaṃ; aivañhi bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ – ṭhapaitvā bālaṃ, avasaisaihi auvādau gahaitabbau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ṭhapaitvā bālaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gilānaṃ, avasaisaihi auvādaṃ gahaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu gamikau hauti| taṃ bhikkhuniyau upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘auvādaṃ, ayya, gaṇhāhī’’ti| ‘‘ahañhi, bhaginī, gamikau; kathāhaṃ auvādaṃ gaṇhāmī’’ti? ‘‘gaṇhāhayya, auvādaṃ; aivañhi bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ – ṭhapaitvā bālaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gilānaṃ, avasaisaihi auvādau gahaitabbau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ṭhapaitvā bālaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gilānaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gamikaṃ, avasaisaihi auvādaṃ gahaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu arañ‍ñai viharati| taṃ bhikkhuniyau upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘auvādaṃ, ayya, gaṇhāhī’’ti| ‘‘ahañhi, bhaginī, arañ‍ñai viharāmi; kathāhaṃ auvādaṃ  gaṇhāmī’’ti? ‘‘gaṇhāhayya, auvādaṃ; aivañhi bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ – ṭhapaitvā bālaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gilānaṃ, ṭhapaitvā gamikaṃ, avasaisaihi auvādau gahaitabbau’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikaina bhikkhunā auvādaṃ gahaituṃ, saṅkaitañ‍ca kātuṃ – atra patiharissāmī’’ti|

415. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū auvādaṃ gahaitvā na āraucainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, auvādau na āraucaitabbau| yau na āraucaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū auvādaṃ gahaitvā na pac‍cāharanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, auvādau na pac‍cāharitabbau| yau na pac‍cāharaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau saṅkaitaṃ na gacchanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā saṅkaitaṃ na gantabbaṃ| yā na gacchaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

416. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau dīghāni kāyabandhanāni dhārainti, taihaiva phāsukā nāmainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihinī kāmabhauginiyauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā dīghaṃ kāyabandhanaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yā dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā aikapariyākataṃ aikapariyāyakataṃ (syā॰) kāyabandhanaṃ, na ca taina phāsukā nāmaitabbā| yā nāmaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau vilīvaina vilivaina (ka॰) paṭṭaina phāsukā nāmainti…pai॰… cammapaṭṭaina phāsukā nāmainti| dussapaṭṭaina phāsukā nāmainti| dussavaiṇiyā phāsukā nāmainti| dussavaṭṭiyā phāsukā nāmainti| cauḷapaṭṭaina phāsukā nāmainti| cauḷavaiṇiyā phāsukā nāmainti| cauḷavaṭṭiyā phāsukā nāmainti| suttavaiṇiyā phāsukā nāmainti| suttavaṭṭiyā phāsukā nāmainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihinī kāmabhauginiyauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā vilīvaina paṭṭaina phāsukā nāmaitabbā…pai॰… na suttavaṭṭiyā phāsukā nāmaitabbā| yā nāmaiyya āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau aṭṭhil‍laina jaghanaṃ ghaṃsāpainti…pai॰…  gauhanukaina jaghanaṃ kauṭṭāpainti, hatthaṃ kauṭṭāpainti, hatthakaucchaṃ kauṭṭāpainti, pādaṃ kauṭṭāpainti, pādakaucchaṃ kauṭṭāpainti, ūruṃ kauṭṭāpainti, mukhaṃ kauṭṭāpainti, dantamaṃsaṃ kauṭṭāpainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihinī kāmabhauginiyauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā aṭṭhil‍laina jaghanaṃ ghaṃsāpaitabbaṃ…pai॰… na dantamaṃsaṃ kauṭṭāpaitabbaṃ| yā kauṭṭāpaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

417. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau mukhaṃ ālimpanti…pai॰… mukhaṃ ummaddainti, mukhaṃ cuṇṇainti, manausilikāya mukhaṃ lañchainti, aṅgarāgaṃ karaunti, mukharāgaṃ karaunti, aṅgarāgamukharāgaṃ karaunti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihinī kāmabhauginiyauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā mukhaṃ ālimpitabbaṃ…pai॰… na mukhaṃ ummadditabbaṃ, na mukhaṃ cuṇṇaitabbaṃ, na manausilikāya mukhaṃ lañchitabbaṃ, na aṅgarāgau kātabbau, na mukharāgau kātabbau, na aṅgarāgamukharāgau kātabbau| yā karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau avaṅgaṃ apāṅgaṃ (?) karaunti…pai॰… visaisakaṃ karaunti, aulaukanakaina aulaukainti, sālaukai tiṭṭhanti; nac‍caṃ sanac‍caṃ (sī॰ syā॰), samaj‍jaṃ (ka॰) kārāpainti, vaisiṃ vuṭṭhāpainti, pānāgāraṃ ṭhapainti, sūnaṃ ṭhapainti, āpaṇaṃ pasārainti, vaḍḍhiṃ payaujainti, vaṇij‍jaṃ payaujainti, dāsaṃ upaṭṭhāpainti, dāsiṃ upaṭṭhāpainti, kammakāraṃ upaṭṭhāpainti, kammakāriṃ upaṭṭhāpainti, tiracchānagataṃ upaṭṭhāpainti, harītakapak‍kikaṃ harītakapaṇṇikaṃ (ka॰) pakiṇanti, namatakaṃ dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihinī kāmabhauginiyauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā avaṅgaṃ kātabbaṃ…pai॰… na visaisakaṃ kātabbaṃ, na aulaukanakaina  aulaukaitabbaṃ, na sālaukai ṭhātabbaṃ, na nac‍caṃ kārāpaitabbaṃ, na vaisī vuṭṭhāpaitabbā, na pānāgāraṃ ṭhapaitabbaṃ, na sūnā ṭhapaitabbā, na āpaṇau pasāraitabbau, na vaḍḍhi payaujaitabbā, na vaṇij‍jā payaujaitabbā, na dāsau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na dāsī upaṭṭhāpaitabbā, na kammakārau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na kammakārī upaṭṭhāpaitabbā, na tiracchānagatau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na harītakapak‍kikaṃ pakiṇitabbaṃ, na namatakaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yā dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

418. taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau sabbanīlakāni cīvarāni  dhārainti…pai॰… sabbapītakāni cīvarāni dhārainti, sabbalauhitakāni cīvarāni dhārainti, sabbamañ‍jiṭṭhikāni cīvarāni dhārainti, sabbakaṇhāni cīvarāni dhārainti, sabbamahāraṅgarattāni cīvarāni dhārainti, sabbamahānāmarattāni cīvarāni dhārainti, acchin‍nadasāni cīvarāni dhārainti, dīghadasāni cīvarāni dhārainti, pupphadasāni cīvarāni dhārainti, phaladasāni cīvarāni dhārainti, kañ‍cukaṃ dhārainti, tirīṭakaṃ dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihinī kāmabhauginiyauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā sabbanīlakāni cīvarāni dhāraitabbāni…pai॰… na tirīṭakaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yā dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|
