419. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī kālaṃ karauntī aivamāha – ‘‘mamac‍cayaina mayhaṃ parikkhārau saṅghassa hautū’’ti| tattha bhikkhū ca bhikkhuniyau ca vivadanti  – ‘‘amhākaṃ hauti, amhākaṃ hautī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘bhikkhunī cai, bhikkhavai, kālaṃ karauntī aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘mamac‍cayaina mayhaṃ parikkhārau saṅghassa hautū’ti, anissarau tattha bhikkhusaṅghau, bhikkhunisaṅghassaivaitaṃ| sikkhamānā cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sāmaṇairī cai, bhikkhavai, kālaṃ karauntī aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘mamac‍cayaina mayhaṃ parikkhārau  saṅghassa hautū’ti, anissarau tattha bhikkhusaṅghau, bhikkhunisaṅghassaivaitaṃ| bhikkhu cai, bhikkhavai, kālaṃ karauntau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘mamac‍cayaina mayhaṃ parikkhārau saṅghassa hautū’ti, anissarau tattha bhikkhunisaṅghau, bhikkhusaṅghassaivaitaṃ| sāmaṇairau cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… upāsakau cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… upāsikā cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… añ‍ñau cai, bhikkhavai, kauci kālaṃ karauntau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘mamac‍cayaina mayhaṃ parikkhārau  saṅghassa hautū’ti, anissarau tattha bhikkhunisaṅghau, bhikkhusaṅghassaivaita’’nti|

420. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī purāṇamal‍lī bhikkhunīsu pabbajitā hauti| sā rathikāya dubbalakaṃ bhikkhuṃ passitvā aṃsakūṭaina pahāraṃ datvā pātaisi pavaṭṭaisi (sī॰)| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma, bhikkhunī, bhikkhussa pahāraṃ dassatī’’ti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā bhikkhussa pahārau dātabbau| yā dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā  bhikkhuṃ passitvā dūratauva auk‍kamitvā maggaṃ dātu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī pavutthapatikā jāraina gabbhinī hauti| sā gabbhaṃ pātaitvā kulūpikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handayyai, imaṃ gabbhaṃ pattaina nīharā’’ti| atha khau sā bhikkhunī taṃ gabbhaṃ pattai pakkhipitvā saṅghāṭiyā paṭicchādaitvā agamāsi| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraina piṇḍacārikaina bhikkhunā samādānaṃ kataṃ hauti – ‘yāhaṃ paṭhamaṃ bhikkhaṃ labhissāmi, na taṃ adatvā bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā paribhuñ‍jissāmī’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuniṃ passitvā aitadavauca – ‘‘handa, bhagini, bhikkhaṃ paṭiggaṇhā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ ayyā’’ti| dutiyampi khau…pai॰… tatiyampi khau sau bhikkhu taṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘handa, bhagini, bhikkhaṃ paṭiggaṇhā’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ ayyā’’ti| ‘‘mayā khau, bhagini, samādānaṃ kataṃ – ‘yāhaṃ paṭhamaṃ bhikkhaṃ labhissāmi, na taṃ adatvā bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā paribhuñ‍jissāmī’ti| handa, bhagini, bhikkhaṃ paṭiggaṇhā’’ti| atha khau sā bhikkhunī taina bhikkhunā nippīḷiyamānā nīharitvā pattaṃ dassaisi – ‘‘passa, ayya, pattai gabbhaṃ; mā ca kassaci āraucaisī’’ti| atha khau sau bhikkhu ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi  nāma bhikkhunī pattaina gabbhaṃ nīharissatī’’ti! atha khau sau bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi| yai tai bhikkhū appicchā…pai॰… tai ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhunī pattaina gabbhaṃ nīharissatī’’ti! bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā pattaina gabbhau  nīharitabbau| yā  nīharaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā bhikkhuṃ passitvā nīharitvā pattaṃ dassaitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhuṃ passitvā parivattaitvā pattamūlaṃ dassainti| bhikkhū ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhuṃ passitvā parivattaitvā pattamūlaṃ dassaissantī’’ti! atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā bhikkhuṃ passitvā parivattaitvā pattamūlaṃ dassaitabbaṃ| yā dassaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā bhikkhuṃ passitvā uk‍kuj‍jitvā pattaṃ dassaituṃ| yañ‍ca pattai āmisaṃ hauti, taina ca bhikkhu nimantaitabbau’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ rathikāya purisabyañ‍janaṃ chaḍḍitaṃ hauti| taṃ bhikkhuniyau sak‍kac‍caṃ upanijjhāyiṃsu| manussā uk‍kuṭṭhiṃ akaṃsu| tā bhikkhuniyau maṅkū ahaisuṃ| atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau upassayaṃ gantvā bhikkhunīnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| yā tā bhikkhuniyau appicchā…pai॰…  tā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau purisabyañ‍janaṃ upanijjhāyissantī’’ti! atha khau tā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| bhikkhū bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā purisabyañ‍janaṃ upanijjhāyitabbaṃ| yā upanijjhāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

421. taina khau pana samayaina manussā bhikkhūnaṃ āmisaṃ dainti| bhikkhū bhikkhunīnaṃ dainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhadantā attanau paribhaugatthāya  din‍naṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ dassanti! mayampi na jānāma dānaṃ dātu’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, attanau paribhaugatthāya din‍naṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ dātabbaṃ| yau dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ āmisaṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṅghassa dātu’’nti| bāḷhataraṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, puggalikampi dātu’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ san‍nidhikataṃ āmisaṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ san‍nidhiṃ bhikkhunīhi bhikkhunīhi bhikkhūhi (sī॰) paṭiggāhāpaitvā paṭiggahāpaitvā (ka॰) paribhuñ‍jitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina manussā bhikkhunīnaṃ āmisaṃ dainti| bhikkhuniyau  bhikkhūnaṃ dainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma bhikkhuniyau attanau paribhaugatthāya din‍naṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ dassanti! mayampi na jānāma dānaṃ dātu’’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā attanau paribhaugatthāya din‍naṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ dātabbaṃ| yā dadaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhunīnaṃ āmisaṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṅghassa dātu’’nti| bāḷhataraṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, puggalikampi dātu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhunīnaṃ san‍nidhikataṃ āmisaṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīnaṃ san‍nidhiṃ bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi (sī॰) paṭiggāhāpaitvā paribhuñ‍jitu’’nti|

422. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhūnaṃ saināsanaṃ ussan‍naṃ hauti, bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ saināsanaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) na hauti| bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisuṃ – ‘sādhu, bhantai, ayyā amhākaṃ saināsanaṃ daintu tāvakālika’nti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunīnaṃ saināsanaṃ dātuṃ tāvakālika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina utuniyau bhikkhuniyau aunaddhamañ‍caṃ aunaddhapīṭhaṃ abhinisīdantipi abhinipaj‍jantipi| saināsanaṃ lauhitaina makkhiyyati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā  aunaddhamañ‍caṃ aunaddhapīṭhaṃ abhinisīditabbaṃ abhinipaj‍jitabbaṃ| yā abhinisīdaiyya vā abhinipaj‍jaiyya vā āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āvasathacīvara’’nti| āvasathacīvaraṃ lauhitaina makkhiyyati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, āṇicauḷaka’’nti| cauḷakaṃ nipatati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, suttakaina bandhitvā ūruyā bandhitu’’nti| suttaṃ chij‍jati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, saṃvail‍liyaṃ, kaṭisuttaka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau sabbakālaṃ kaṭisuttakaṃ dhārainti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – saiyyathāpi gihinī kāmabhauginiyauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā sabbakālaṃ kaṭisuttakaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yā dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, utuniyā kaṭisuttaka’’nti|

dutiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

3. tatiyabhāṇavārau

423. taina khau pana samayaina upasampan‍nāyau dissanti – animittāpi, nimittamattāpi, alauhitāpi, dhuvalauhitāpi, dhuvacauḷāpi, paggharantīpi, sikhariṇīpi, itthipaṇḍakāpi, vaipurisikāpi, sambhin‍nāpi, ubhataubyañ‍janāpi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upasampādaintiyā catuvīsati antarāyikai dhammai pucchituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, pucchitabbā – ‘nasi animittā, nasi nimittamattā, nasi alauhitā, nasi dhuvalauhitā, nasi dhuvacauḷā, nasi paggharantī, nasi sikharaṇī sikhariṇī (sī॰ syā॰), nasi itthipaṇḍakā, nasi vaipurisikā, nasi sambhin‍nā, nasi ubhataubyañ‍janā? santi tai aivarūpā ābādhā – kuṭṭhaṃ, gaṇḍau, kilāsau, sausau, apamārau? manussāsi, itthīsi, bhujissāsi, aṇaṇāsi, nasi rājabhaṭī? anuñ‍ñātāsi mātāpitūhi, sāmikaina? paripuṇṇavīsativassāsi, paripuṇṇaṃ tai pattacīvaraṃ, kin‍nāmāsi, kānāmā tai pavattinī’’ti?

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū bhikkhunīnaṃ antarāyikai dhammai pucchanti| upasampadāpaikkhāyau vitthāyanti, maṅkū haunti, na sak‍kaunti vissaj‍jaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikatauupasampan‍nāya bhikkhunisaṅghai visuddhāya bhikkhusaṅghai upasampādaitu’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau ananusiṭṭhā upasampadāpaikkhāyau antarāyikai dhammai pucchanti| upasampadāpaikkhāyau  vitthāyanti, maṅkū haunti, na sak‍kaunti vissaj‍jaituṃ| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, paṭhamaṃ anusāsitvā pacchā antarāyikai dhammai pucchitu’’nti|

tatthaiva saṅghamajjhai anusāsanti| upasampadāpaikkhāyau tathaiva vitthāyanti, maṅkū haunti, na sak‍kaunti vissaj‍jaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikamantaṃ anusāsitvā saṅghamajjhai antarāyikai dhammai pucchituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, anusāsitabbā|

424. ‘‘paṭhamaṃ upajjhaṃ gāhāpaitabbā| upajjhaṃ gāhāpaitvā pattacīvaraṃ ācikkhitabbaṃ – ‘ayaṃ tai pattau, ayaṃ saṅghāṭi, ayaṃ uttarāsaṅgau, ayaṃ antaravāsakau, idaṃ saṃkac‍cikaṃ saṅkacchikaṃ (syā॰), ayaṃ udakasāṭikā; gaccha amumhi aukāsai tiṭṭhāhī’’ti|

bālā abyattā anusāsanti| duranusiṭṭhā upasampadāpaikkhāyau vitthāyanti, maṅkū haunti, na sak‍kaunti vissaj‍jaituṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bālāya abyattāya anusāsitabbā| yā anusāsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya anusāsitu’’nti|

asammatā anusāsanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, asammatāya anusāsitabbā| yā anusāsaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sammatāya anusāsituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbā – attanā vā attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ, parāya vā parā samman‍nitabbā|

‘‘kathañ‍ca attanāva attanā vā (ka॰) attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ? byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau – ‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmaṃ anusāsaiyya’nti| aivaṃ attanāva  attānaṃ samman‍nitabbaṃ|

‘‘kathañ‍ca parāya parāya vā (ka॰) parā samman‍nitabbā? byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau – ‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya  ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmaṃ anusāsaiyyā’ti| aivaṃ parāya parā samman‍nitabbā|

‘‘tāya  sammatāya bhikkhuniyā upasampadāpaikkhaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamassa vacanīyā – ‘suṇasi itthan‍nāmai| ayaṃ tai sac‍cakālau, bhūtakālau| yaṃ jātaṃ taṃ saṅghamajjhai pucchantai santaṃ atthīti vattabbaṃ, asantaṃ natthīti vattabbaṃ| mā khau vitthāyi vitthāsi (ka॰), mā khau maṅku ahausi| aivaṃ taṃ pucchissanti – nasi animittā, nasi nimittamattā, nasi alauhitā, nasi dhuvalauhitā, nasi dhuvacauḷā, nasi paggharantī, nasi sikhariṇī, nasi itthipaṇḍakā, nasi vaipurisikā, nasi sambhin‍nā, nasi ubhataubyañ‍janā? santi tai aivarūpā ābādhā – kuṭṭhaṃ, gaṇḍau, kilāsau, sausau, apamārau? manussāsi, itthīsi, bhujissāsi, aṇaṇāsi, nasi rājabhaṭī? anuñ‍ñātāsi mātāpitūhi, sāmikaina? paripuṇṇavīsativassāsi, paripuṇṇaṃ tai pattacīvaraṃ, kin‍nāmāsi, kānāmā tai pavattinī’ti?

‘‘aikatau āgacchanti| na aikatau āgantabbaṃ| anusāsikāya paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā saṅghau ñāpaitabbau – ‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya  ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| anusiṭṭhā sā mayā| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, itthan‍nāmā āgacchaiyyā’ti| ‘āgacchāhī’ti vattabbā|

‘‘aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ kārāpaitvā bhikkhunīnaṃ pādai vandāpaitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīdāpaitvā añ‍jaliṃ paggaṇhāpaitvā upasampadaṃ yācāpaitabbā – ‘saṅghaṃ, ayyai, upasampadaṃ yācāmi| ul‍lumpatu maṃ, ayyai, saṅghau anukampaṃ upādāya| dutiyampi, ayyai, saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācāmi| ul‍lumpatu maṃ, ayyai, saṅghau anukampaṃ upādāya| tatiyampi, ayyai, saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācāmi| ul‍lumpatu maṃ, ayyai, saṅghau anukampaṃ upādāyā’ti| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ itthan‍nāmaṃ antarāyikai dhammai pucchaiyyanti|

‘‘suṇasi  itthan‍nāmai| ayaṃ tai sac‍cakālau, bhūtakālau| yaṃ jātaṃ taṃ pucchāmi santaṃ atthīti vattabbaṃ, asantaṃ natthīti vattabbaṃ| nasi animittā…pai॰… kin‍nāmāsi, kānāmā tai pavattinīti| byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

425. ‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| parisuddhā antarāyikaihi dhammaihi, paripuṇṇassā pattacīvaraṃ| itthan‍nāmā saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya pavattiniyā| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiyya  itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya pavattiniyā| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| parisuddhā antarāyikaihi dhammaihi, paripuṇṇassā pattacīvaraṃ| itthan‍nāmā saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya pavattiniyā| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiti itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya pavattiniyā| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya upasampadā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya pavattiniyā, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| parisuddhā antarāyikaihi dhammaihi, paripuṇṇassā pattacīvaraṃ| itthan‍nāmā saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya pavattiniyā| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiti itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya pavattiniyā| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya upasampadā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya pavattiniyā, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘upasampan‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya pavattiniyā| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

‘‘tāvadaiva taṃ ādāya bhikkhusaṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ kārāpaitvā bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vandāpaitvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīdāpaitvā  añ‍jaliṃ paggaṇhāpaitvā upasampadaṃ yācāpaitabbā – ‘ahaṃ, ayyā, itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā; saṅghaṃ, ayyā, upasampadaṃ yācāmi| ul‍lumpatu maṃ, ayyā, saṅghau anukampaṃ upādāya| ahaṃ, ayyā, itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya  upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā| dutiyampi, ayyā, saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācāmi| ul‍lumpatu maṃ, ayyā, saṅghau anukampaṃ upādāya| ahaṃ, ayyā, itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā| tatiyampi, ayyā, saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācāmi| ul‍lumpatu maṃ, ayyā, saṅghau anukampaṃ upādāyā’ti| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā| itthan‍nāmā saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiyya, itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā| itthan‍nāmā saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiti itthan‍nāmāya  pavattiniyā| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmāya upasampadā itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi – suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| ayaṃ itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā| itthan‍nāmā saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiti itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmāya upasampadā itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘upasampan‍nā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

‘‘tāvadaiva chāyā maitabbā, utuppamāṇaṃ ācikkhitabbaṃ, divasabhāgau ācikkhitabbau, saṅgīti ācikkhitabbā, bhikkhuniyau vattabbā – imissā tayau ca nissayai, aṭṭha ca akaraṇīyāni ācikkhaiyyāthā’’ti|

426. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau bhattaggai āsanaṃ saṃkasāyantiyau kālaṃ vītināmaisuṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhan‍naṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ yathāvuḍḍhaṃ, avasaisānaṃ yathāgatika’’nti|

taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau – bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ yathāvuḍḍhaṃ, avasaisānaṃ yathāgatikanti – sabbattha aṭṭhaiva bhikkhuniyau yathāvuḍḍhaṃ paṭibāhanti, avasaisāyau  yathāgatikaṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhattaggai aṭṭhan‍naṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ yathāvuḍḍhaṃ, avasaisānaṃ yathāgatikaṃ; añ‍ñattha sabbattha yathāvuḍḍhaṃ añ‍ñattha yathāvuḍḍhaṃ (syā॰) na paṭibāhitabbaṃ| yā paṭibāhaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

427. pāci॰ 1050 ādayau taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau na pavārainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā na pavāraitabbaṃ| yā na pavāraiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau attanā pavāraitvā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ na pavārainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā attanā pavāraitvā bhikkhusaṅghau na pavāraitabbau| yā na pavāraiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ aikatau pavāraintiyau kaulāhalaṃ akaṃsu| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ aikatau pavāraitabbaṃ| yā pavāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau puraibhattaṃ pavāraintiyau kālaṃ  vītināmaisuṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pacchābhattaṃ bhikkhavai bhikkhuniyā pacchābhattaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) pavāraitu’’nti| pacchābhattaṃ pavāraintiyau vikālai vikālaṃ (ka॰) ahaisuṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aj‍jatanā bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ aj‍jatanā (sī॰), aj‍jatanāya (ka॰) pavāraitvā aparaj‍ju bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pavāraitu’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina sabbau bhikkhunisaṅghau pavāraintau kaulāhalaṃ akāsi| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ byattaṃ paṭibalaṃ samman‍nituṃ – bhikkhunisaṅghassa atthāya bhikkhusaṅghaṃ  pavāraituṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbā| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhunī yācitabbā, yācitvā byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samman‍naiyya bhikkhunisaṅghassa atthāya bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pavāraituṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samman‍nati bhikkhunisaṅghassa atthāya bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pavāraituṃ| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā sammuti bhikkhunisaṅghassa atthāya  bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pavāraituṃ, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī bhikkhunisaṅghassa atthāya bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pavāraituṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

‘‘tāya  sammatāya bhikkhuniyā bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ ādāya bhikkhusaṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘bhikkhunīsaṅghau, ayyā, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pavāraiti – diṭṭhaina vā, sutaina vā parisaṅkāya vā| vadatu vadatu maṃ (ka॰), ayyā, bhikkhusaṅghau bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ anukampaṃ upādāya, passantau paṭikarissati| dutiyampi, ayyā…pai॰… tatiyampi, ayyā, bhikkhunisaṅghau bhikkhusaṅghaṃ pavāraiti – diṭṭhaina vā, sutaina vā, parisaṅkāya vā| vadatu, ayyā, bhikkhusaṅghau bhikkhunisaṅghaṃ anukampaṃ upādāya, passantau paṭikarissatī’’ti|

428. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūnaṃ upausathaṃ ṭhapainti, pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapainti, savacanīyaṃ karaunti, anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapainti, aukāsaṃ kārainti, caudainti, sārainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā bhikkhussa upausathau ṭhapaitabbau; ṭhapitaupi aṭṭhapitau; ṭhapaintiyā āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na pavāraṇā ṭhapaitabbā; ṭhapitāpi aṭṭhapitā; ṭhapaintiyā āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na savacanīyaṃ kātabbaṃ; katampi akataṃ; karauntiyā āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na anuvādau paṭṭhapaitabbau; paṭṭhapitaupi appaṭṭhapitau; paṭṭhapaintiyā āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na aukāsau kāraitabbau; kāritaupi akāritau; kāraintiyā āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na caudaitabbau; cauditaupi acauditau; caudaintiyā āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| na sāraitabbau; sāritaupi asāritau; sāraintiyā āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhū bhikkhunīnaṃ upausathaṃ ṭhapainti, pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapainti, savacanīyaṃ karaunti, anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapainti, aukāsaṃ kārainti, caudainti, sārainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai bhikkhunā bhikkhuniyā upausathaṃ ṭhapaituṃ; ṭhapitaupi suṭṭhapitau; ṭhapaintassa anāpatti| pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaituṃ; ṭhapitāpi suṭṭhapitā; ṭhapaintassa anāpatti| savacanīyaṃ kātuṃ; katampi sukataṃ; karauntassa anāpatti| anuvādaṃ paṭṭhapaituṃ; paṭṭhapitaupi suppaṭṭhapitau; paṭṭhapaintassa anāpatti| aukāsaṃ kāraituṃ; kāritaupi sukāritau; kāraintassa  anāpatti| caudaituṃ; cauditāpi sucauditā; caudaintassa anāpatti| sāraituṃ; sāritāpi susāritā; sāraintassa anāpattī’’ti|

429. pāci॰ 1184 taina khau pana samayaina chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau yānaina yāyanti – itthiyuttainapi purisantaraina, purisayuttainapi itthantaraina| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… ‘‘saiyyathāpi gaṅgāmahiyāyā’’ti gaṅgāmahiyāti (sī॰)| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā yānaina yāyitabbaṃ| yā yāyaiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī gilānā hauti, na sak‍kauti padasā gantuṃ| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, gilānāya yāna’’nti| atha khau bhikkhunīnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘itthiyuttaṃ nu khau, purisayuttaṃ nu khau’’ti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, itthiyuttaṃ purisayuttaṃ hatthavaṭṭaka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarissā bhikkhuniyā yānugghātaina bāḷhataraṃ aphāsu ahausi| bhagavatau  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sivikaṃ pāṭaṅki’’nti|

430. taina  khau pana samayaina aḍḍhakāsī gaṇikā bhikkhunīsu pabbajitā hauti| sā ca sāvatthiṃ gantukāmā hauti – ‘bhagavatau santikai upasampaj‍jissāmī’ti| assausuṃ khau dhuttā – ‘aḍḍhakāsī kira gaṇikā sāvatthiṃ gantukāmā’ti| tai maggai pariyuṭṭhiṃsu| assausi khau aḍḍhakāsī gaṇikā – ‘dhuttā kira maggai pariyuṭṭhitā’ti| bhagavatau santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘ahañhi upasampaj‍jitukāmā; kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? atha khau bhagavā aitasmiṃ nidānai aitasmiṃ pakaraṇai dhammiṃ kathaṃ katvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, dūtainapi upasampādaitu’’nti|

bhikkhudūtaina upasampādainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhudūtaina upasampādaitabbā| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti| sikkhamānadūtaina  upasampādainti…pai॰… sāmaṇairadūtaina upasampādainti…pai॰… sāmaṇairidūtaina upasampādainti…pai॰… bālāya abyattāya dūtaina upasampādainti| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bālāya abyattāya dūtaina upasampādaitabbā| yau upasampādaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya dūtaina upasampādaitunti|

‘‘tāya  dūtāya bhikkhuniyā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhikkhūnaṃ pādai vanditvā uk‍kuṭikaṃ nisīditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘itthan‍nāmā, ayyā, itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā| sā kainacidaiva antarāyaina na āgacchati| itthan‍nāmā, ayyā, saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati| ul‍lumpatu taṃ, ayyā, saṅghau anukampaṃ upādāya| itthan‍nāmā, ayyā, itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā| sā kainacidaiva antarāyaina na āgacchati| dutiyampi, ayyā, itthan‍nāmā saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati| ul‍lumpatu taṃ, ayyā, saṅghau anukampaṃ upādāya| itthan‍nāmā, ayyā, itthan‍nāmāya ayyāya upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā| sā kainacidaiva antarāyaina na āgacchati| tatiyampi, ayyā, itthan‍nāmā saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati| ul‍lumpatu taṃ, ayyā, saṅghau anukampaṃ upādāyā’ti| byattaina bhikkhunā paṭibalaina saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā| sā kainacidaiva antarāyaina na āgacchati| itthan‍nāmā saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiyya itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā| sā kainacidaiva antarāyaina na āgacchati| itthan‍nāmā saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiti itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmāya upasampadā itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘dutiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi…pai॰… tatiyampi aitamatthaṃ vadāmi| suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya upasampadāpaikkhā| aikatauupasampan‍nā bhikkhunisaṅghai, visuddhā| sā kainacidaiva antarāyaina na āgacchati| itthan‍nāmā saṅghaṃ upasampadaṃ yācati itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ upasampādaiti itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| yassāyasmatau khamati itthan‍nāmāya upasampadā itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa  nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘upasampan‍nā  saṅghaina itthan‍nāmā itthan‍nāmāya pavattiniyā| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmīti|

‘‘tāvadaiva chāyā maitabbā, utuppamāṇaṃ ācikkhitabbaṃ, divasabhāgau ācikkhitabbau, saṅgīti ācikkhitabbā, bhikkhuniyau vattabbā – tassā tayau ca nissayai, aṭṭha ca akaraṇīyāni ācikkhaiyyāthā’’ti|

431. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau arañ‍ñai viharanti| dhuttā dūsainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ, āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā arañ‍ñai vatthabbaṃ| yā vasaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraina upāsakaina bhikkhunisaṅghassa uddausitau din‍nau hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, uddausita’’nti| uddausitau na sammati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, upassaya’’nti| upassayau na sammati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, navakamma’’nti| navakammaṃ na sammati| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, puggalikampi kātu’’nti|

432. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā itthī san‍nisin‍nagabbhā bhikkhunīsu pabbajitā hauti| tassā pabbajitāya gabbhau vuṭṭhāti| atha khau tassā bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau mayā imasmiṃ dārakai paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pausaituṃ, yāva sau dārakau viñ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇātī’’ti|

atha khau tassā bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘mayā ca na labbhā aikikāya vatthuṃ, añ‍ñāya ca bhikkhuniyā na labbhā dārakaina saha vatthuṃ, kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samman‍nitvā tassā  bhikkhuniyā dutiyaṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbā| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhunī yācitabbā, yācitvā byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, ayyai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samman‍naiyya itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā dutiyaṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samman‍nati itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā dutiyaṃ| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā sammuti itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā dutiyāya, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatā saṅghaina itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā dutiyā| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha khau tassā dutiyikāya bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau mayā imasmiṃ dārakai paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, ṭhapaitvā sāgāraṃ yathā añ‍ñasmiṃ purisai paṭipaj‍janti paṭipaj‍jati (syā॰) aivaṃ tasmiṃ  dārakai paṭipaj‍jitu’’nti|

433. taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī garudhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍nā hauti mānattacārinī| atha khau tassā bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘mayā ca na labbhā aikikāya vatthuṃ, añ‍ñāya ca bhikkhuniyā na labbhā saha mayā vatthuṃ, kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti? bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, aikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samman‍nitvā tassā bhikkhuniyā dutiyaṃ dātuṃ| aivañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, samman‍nitabbā| paṭhamaṃ bhikkhunī yācitabbā, yācitvā byattāya bhikkhuniyā paṭibalāya saṅghau ñāpaitabbau –

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samman‍naiyya itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā dutiyaṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, ayyai, saṅghau| saṅghau itthan‍nāmaṃ bhikkhuniṃ samman‍nati itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā dutiyaṃ| yassā ayyāya khamati itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā sammuti itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā dutiyāya, sā tuṇhassa; yassā nakkhamati, sā bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatā  saṅghaina itthan‍nāmā bhikkhunī itthan‍nāmāya bhikkhuniyā dutiyā| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

434. taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya vibbhami| sā puna pac‍cāgantvā bhikkhuniyau upasampadaṃ yāci| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā sikkhāpac‍cakkhānaṃ; yadaiva sā vibbhantā tadaiva sā abhikkhunī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī sakāvāsā titthāyatanaṃ saṅkami| sā puna pac‍cāgantvā bhikkhuniyau upasampadaṃ yāci| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘yā sā, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunī sakāvāsā titthāyatanaṃ saṅkantā, sā āgatā na upasampādaitabbā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau purisaihi abhivādanaṃ, kaisacchaidanaṃ, nakhacchaidanaṃ, vaṇappaṭikammaṃ, kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā na sādiyanti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, sāditu’’nti sādiyituṃ (ka॰)|

435. taina  khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau pal‍laṅkaina nisīdanti paṇhīsamphassaṃ sādiyantī sādiyantā (ka॰)| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā pal‍laṅkaina nisīditabbaṃ| yā nisīdaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī gilānā hauti| tassā vinā pal‍laṅkaina na phāsu hauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā aḍḍhapal‍laṅka’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau vac‍cakuṭiyā vac‍caṃ karaunti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau tatthaiva gabbhaṃ pātainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā vac‍cakuṭiyā vac‍cau kātabbau| yā karaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, haiṭṭhā vivaṭai uparipaṭicchan‍nai vac‍caṃ kātu’’nti|

436. taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau cuṇṇaina nahāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihinī kāmabhauginiyauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā cuṇṇaina nahāyitabbaṃ| yā nahāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kusaṃ mattika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau vāsitakāya mattikāya nahāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihinī kāmabhauginiyauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā vāsitakāya mattikāya nahāyitabbaṃ| yā nahāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, pakatimattika’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau jantāgharai nahāyantiyau kaulāhalaṃ akaṃsu| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā jantāgharai nahāyitabbaṃ| yā nahāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau paṭisautai nahāyanti dhārāsamphassaṃ sādiyantī| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā  paṭisautai nahāyitabbaṃ| yā nahāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau atitthai nahāyanti| dhuttā dūsainti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā atitthai nahāyitabbaṃ| yā nahāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina bhikkhuniyau purisatitthai nahāyanti| manussā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti…pai॰… saiyyathāpi gihinī kāmabhauginiyauti| bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ  āraucaisuṃ| ‘‘na, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuniyā purisatitthai nahāyitabbaṃ| yā nahāyaiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi, bhikkhavai, mahilātitthai nahāyitu’’nti|

tatiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

bhikkhunikkhandhakau dasamau|

imasmiṃ khandhakai vatthū aikasataṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

pabbaj‍jaṃ  gautamī yāci, nānuñ‍ñāsi tathāgatau|

kapilavatthu vaisāliṃ, agamāsi vināyakau||

rajaukiṇṇaina kauṭṭhakai, ānandassa pavaidayi|

bhabbauti  nayatau yāci, mātāti pausikāti ca||

vassasataṃ tadahu ca, abhikkhupac‍cāsīsanā|

pavāraṇā garudhammā, dvai vassā anak‍kausanā||

auvaṭau ca aṭṭha dhammā, yāvajīvānuvattanā|

garudhammapaṭiggāhau sāvassā upasampadā||

vassasahassaṃ pañ‍caiva, kumbhathainakasaitaṭṭi|

mañ‍jiṭṭhikaupamāhi, aivaṃ saddhammahiṃsanā||

āḷiṃ bandhaiyya pāaiva, puna saddhammasaṇṭhiti|

upasampādaituṃ ayyā, yathāvuḍḍhābhivādanā||

na  karissanti kimaiva, sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇaṃ|

auvādaṃ pātimaukkhañ‍ca, kaina nu khau upassayaṃ||

na jānanti ca ācikkhi, na karaunti ca bhikkhuhi|

paṭiggahaituṃ bhikkhūhi, bhikkhunīhi paṭiggahau||

ācikkhi kammaṃ bhikkhūhi, ujjhāyanti bhikkhunīhi vā|

ācikkhituṃ bhaṇḍanañ‍ca, raupaitvā uppalāya ca||

sāvatthiyā kaddamauda, avandi kāya ūru ca|

aṅgajātañ‍ca aubhāsaṃ, sampayaujainti vaggikā||

avandiyau  daṇḍakammaṃ, bhikkhuniyau tathā puna|

āvaraṇañ‍ca  auvādaṃ, kappati nu khau pak‍kami||

bālā vatthuvinicchayā, auvādaṃ saṅghau pañ‍cahi|

duvai tissau na gaṇhanti, bālā gilānagamikaṃ||

ārañ‍ñikau nāraucainti, na pac‍cāgacchanti ca|

dīghaṃ vilīvacammañ‍ca, dussā ca vaiṇivaṭṭi ca|

cauḷavaiṇi ca vaṭṭi ca, suttavaiṇi ca vaṭṭikā||

aṭṭhil‍laṃ gauhanukaina, hatthakaucchaṃ pādaṃ tathā|

ūruṃ mukhaṃ dantamaṃsaṃ, ālimpaumaddacuṇṇanā||

lañchainti aṅgarāgañ‍ca, mukharāgaṃ tathā duvai|

avaṅgaṃ visaisaulaukau, sālaukaina nac‍caina ca sālaukai sanac‍caina ca (sī॰), sālaukaina sanac‍canaṃ (syā॰)||

vaisī  pānāgāraṃ sūnaṃ, āpaṇaṃ vaḍḍhi vaṇij‍jā|

dāsaṃ dāsiṃ kammakaraṃ, kammakāriṃ upaṭṭhayyuṃ||

tiracchānaharītaki, sandhārayanti namatakaṃ|

nīlaṃ pītaṃ lauhitakaṃ, mañ‍jiṭṭhakaṇhacīvarā||

mahāraṅgamahānāmaacchin‍nā dīghamaiva ca|

pupphaphalakañ‍cukañ‍ca, tirīṭakañ‍ca dhārayuṃ||

bhikkhunī  sikkhamānāya, sāmaṇairāya ac‍cayai|

niyyāditai  parikkhārai, bhikkhuniyauva issarā||

bhikkhussa sāmaṇairassa, upāsakassupāsikā|

añ‍ñaisañ‍ca parikkhārai, niyyātai bhikkhuissarā||

mal‍lī  gabbhaṃ pattamūlaṃ, byañ‍janaṃ āmisaina ca|

ussan‍nañ‍ca bāḷhataraṃ, san‍nidhikatamāmisaṃ||

bhikkhūnaṃ yādisaṃ bhauṭṭhaṃ haiṭṭhā (sī॰), haiṭṭhaṃ (syā॰), bhutti (ka॰), bhikkhunīnaṃ tathā karai|

saināsanaṃ utuniyau, makkhīyati paṭāṇi ca||

chij‍janti sabbakālañ‍ca, animittāpi dissarai|

nimittā lauhitā caiva, tathaiva dhuvalauhitā||

dhuvacauḷapaggharantī, sikharaṇitthipaṇḍakā|

vaipurisī ca sambhin‍nā, ubhataubyañ‍janāpi ca||

animittāditau katvā, yāva ubhataubyañ‍janā|

aitaṃ paiyyālatau haiṭṭhā, kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍau kilāsau ca||

sausāpamārau mānusī, itthīsi bhujissāsi ca|

aṇaṇā na rājabhaṭī, anuñ‍ñātā ca vīsati||

paripuṇṇā ca kin‍nāmā, kānāmā tai pavattinī|

catuvīsantarāyānaṃ, pucchitvā upasampadā||

vitthāyanti ananusiṭṭhā, saṅghamajjhai tathaiva ca|

upajjhāgāha saṅghāṭi, uttarantaravāsakau||

saṅkac‍cudakasāṭi ca, ācikkhitvāna paisayai|

bālā  asammataikatau, yācai pucchantarāyikā||

aikatauupasampan‍nā, bhikkhusaṅghai tathā puna|

chāyā utu divasā ca, saṅgīti tayau nissayai||

aṭṭha akaraṇīyāni, kālaṃ sabbattha aṭṭhaiva|

na pavārainti bhikkhunī, bhikkhusaṅghaṃ tathaiva ca||

kaulāhalaṃ  puraibhattaṃ, vikālai ca kaulāhalaṃ|

upausathaṃ pavāraṇaṃ, savacanīyānuvādanaṃ||

aukāsaṃ caudai sārainti, paṭikkhittaṃ mahaisinā|

tathaiva bhikkhu bhikkhunī, anuñ‍ñātaṃ mahaisinā||

yānaṃ gilānayuttañ‍ca, yānugghātaḍḍhakāsikā|

bhikkhu sikkhā sāmaṇaira, sāmaṇairī ca bālāya||

arañ‍ñai  upāsakaina, uddausitau upassayaṃ|

na sammati navakammaṃ, nisin‍nagabbhaaikikā||

sāgārañ‍ca garudhammaṃ, pac‍cakkhāya ca saṅkami|

abhivādanakaisā ca, nakhā ca vaṇakammanā||

pal‍laṅkaina gilānā ca, vac‍caṃ cuṇṇaina vāsitaṃ|

jantāgharai paṭisautai, atitthai purisaina ca||

mahāgautamī āyāci, ānandau cāpi yaunisau|

parisā catassau haunti, pabbaj‍jā jinasāsanai||

saṃvaigajananatthāya, saddhammassa ca vuddhiyā|

āturassāva bhaisaj‍jaṃ, aivaṃ buddhaina daisitaṃ||

aivaṃ vinītā saddhammai, mātugāmāpi itarā|

yāyanti tāyanti (sī॰ syā॰) ac‍cutaṃ ṭhānaṃ, yattha gantvā na saucaraiti||

bhikkhunikkhandhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



11. pañ‍casatikakkhandhakaṃ

1. saṅgītinidānaṃ

437. atha  khau āyasmā mahākassapau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, āvusau, samayaṃ pāvāya kusināraṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi| atha khvāhaṃ, āvusau, maggā auk‍kamma añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdiṃ|

dī॰ ni॰ 2.231 ‘‘taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau ājīvakau kusinārāya mandāravapupphaṃ gahaitvā pāvaṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau hauti| addasaṃ khau ahaṃ, āvusau, taṃ ājīvakaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna taṃ ājīvakaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘apāvusau, amhākaṃ satthāraṃ jānāsī’ti? ‘āmāvusau, jānāmi| aj‍ja sattāhaparinibbutau samaṇau gautamau| tatau mai idaṃ mandāravapupphaṃ gahita’nti| tatrāvusau, yai tai bhikkhū avītarāgā appaikac‍cai bāhā paggayha kandanti, chin‍napātaṃ papatanti, āvaṭṭanti, vivaṭṭanti – atikhippaṃ bhagavā parinibbutau, atikhippaṃ sugatau parinibbutau, atikhippaṃ cakkhuṃ laukai antarahitanti| yai pana tai bhikkhū vītarāgā tai satā sampajānā adhivāsainti – anic‍cā saṅkhārā, taṃ kutaittha labbhāti|

‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, āvusau, tai bhikkhū aitadavaucaṃ – ‘alaṃ, āvusau, mā saucittha; mā paridaivittha| nanvaitaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā paṭikac‍caiva akkhātaṃ – sabbaihaiva piyaihi manāpaihi  nānābhāvau vinābhāvau añ‍ñathābhāvau| taṃ kutaittha āvusau labbhā, yaṃ taṃ jātaṃ bhūtaṃ saṅkhataṃ palaukadhammaṃ, taṃ vata mā paluj‍jīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatī’ti|

‘‘taina khau panāvusau, samayaina subhaddau nāma vuḍḍhapabbajitau tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau, āvusau, subhaddau vuḍḍhapabbajitau tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘alaṃ, āvusau, mā saucittha; mā paridaivittha| sumuttā mayaṃ taina mahāsamaṇaina; upaddutā ca mayaṃ hauma – idaṃ vau kappati, idaṃ vau na kappatīti| idāni pana mayaṃ yaṃ icchissāma taṃ karissāma, yaṃ na icchissāma na taṃ karissāmā’ti| handa  mayaṃ, āvusau, dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyāma| purai adhammau dippati dibbati (ka॰), dhammau paṭibāhiyyati; purai avinayau dippati vinayau paṭibāhiyyati; purai adhammavādinau balavantau haunti, dhammavādinau dubbalā haunti; purai avinayavādinau balavantau haunti, vinayavādinau dubbalā hauntī’’ti|

‘‘taina hi, bhantai, thairau bhikkhū uc‍cinatū’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau aikainūnapañ‍caarahantasatāni uc‍cini| bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau kiñ‍cāpi saikkhau, abhabbau chandā dausā mauhā bhayā agatiṃ gantuṃ| bahu ca anaina bhagavatau santikai dhammau ca vinayau ca pariyattau| taina hi, bhantai, thairau āyasmantampi ānandaṃ uc‍cinatū’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantampi ānandaṃ uc‍cini|

atha khau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kattha nu khau mayaṃ dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyaiyyāmā’’ti? atha khau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘rājagahaṃ khau mahāgaucaraṃ pahūtasaināsanaṃ, yaṃnūna mayaṃ rājagahai vassaṃ vasantā dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyaiyyāma| na añ‍ñai bhikkhū rājagahai vassaṃ upagacchaiyyu’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

438. ‘‘suṇātu mai, āvusau, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau imāni pañ‍ca bhikkhusatāni samman‍naiyya – rājagahai vassaṃ vasantāni dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyituṃ, na añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi rājagahai vassaṃ vasitabbanti| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, āvusau, saṅghau| imāni pañ‍ca bhikkhusatāni samman‍nati – rājagahai vassaṃ vasantāni dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyituṃ, na añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi rājagahai vassaṃ vasitabbanti| yassāyasmatau khamati imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ sammuti – rājagahai vassaṃ vasantānaṃ  dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyituṃ, na añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi rājagahai vassaṃ vasitabbanti – sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatāni  saṅghaina imāni pañ‍ca bhikkhusatāni rājagahai vassaṃ vasantāni dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyituṃ, na añ‍ñaihi bhikkhūhi rājagahai vassaṃ vasitabbanti| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ  dhārayāmī’’ti|

atha  khau thairā bhikkhū rājagahaṃ agamaṃsu dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyituṃ| atha khau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘bhagavatā khau, āvusau, khaṇḍaphul‍lappaṭisaṅkharaṇaṃ vaṇṇitaṃ| handa mayaṃ, āvusau, paṭhamaṃ māsaṃ khaṇḍaphul‍laṃ paṭisaṅkharauma; majjhimaṃ māsaṃ san‍nipatitvā dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyissāmā’’ti|

atha khau thairā bhikkhū paṭhamaṃ māsaṃ khaṇḍaphul‍laṃ paṭisaṅkhariṃsu| atha khau āyasmā ānandau – svai san‍nipātau san‍nipātauti (ka॰) na khau maitaṃ patirūpaṃ, yauhaṃ saikkhau samānau san‍nipātaṃ gacchaiyyanti – bahudaiva rattiṃ kāyagatāya satiyā vītināmaitvā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ ‘nipaj‍jissāmī’ti kāyaṃ āvaj‍jaisi| appattañ‍ca sīsaṃ bibbauhanaṃ, bhūmitau ca pādā muttā| aitasmiṃ antarai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍ci|

439. atha khau āyasmā ānandau arahā samānau san‍nipātaṃ agamāsi| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu mai, āvusau, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ upāliṃ vinayaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti|

āyasmā upāli saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ āyasmatā mahākassapaina vinayaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiyya’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭhamaṃ, āvusau upāli, pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñatta’’nti? ‘‘vaisāliyaṃ bhantai’’ti| ‘‘kaṃ ārabbhā’’ti? ‘‘sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ ārabbhā’’ti| ‘‘kismiṃ vatthusmi’’nti? ‘‘maithunadhammai’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ paṭhamassa pārājikassa vatthumpi pucchi, nidānampi pucchi, puggalampi pucchi, pañ‍ñattimpi pucchi, anupañ‍ñattimpi pucchi, āpattimpi pucchi, anāpattimpi  pucchi| ‘‘dutiyaṃ panāvusau upāli, pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñatta’’nti? ‘‘rājagahai bhantai’’ti| ‘‘kaṃ ārabbhā’’ti? ‘‘dhaniyaṃ kumbhakāraputtaṃ ārabbhā’’ti| ‘‘kismiṃ vatthusmi’’nti? ‘‘adin‍nādānai’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ dutiyassa pārājikassa vatthumpi pucchi, nidānampi pucchi, puggalampi pucchi, pañ‍ñattimpi pucchi, anupañ‍ñattimpi pucchi, āpattimpi pucchi, anāpattimpi pucchi| ‘‘tatiyaṃ panāvusau upāli, pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñatta’’nti? ‘‘vaisāliyaṃ bhantai’’ti| ‘‘kaṃ ārabbhā’’ti? ‘‘sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbhā’’ti| ‘‘kismiṃ vatthusmi’’nti? ‘‘manussaviggahai’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ  tatiyassa pārājikassa vatthumpi pucchi, nidānampi pucchi, puggalampi pucchi, pañ‍ñattimpi pucchi, anupañ‍ñattimpi pucchi, āpattimpi pucchi, anāpattimpi pucchi| ‘‘catutthaṃ panāvusau upāli, pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñatta’’nti? ‘‘vaisāliyaṃ bhantai’’ti| ‘‘kaṃ ārabbhā’’ti? ‘‘vaggumudātīriyai bhikkhū ārabbhā’’ti| ‘‘kismiṃ vatthusmi’’nti? ‘‘uttarimanussadhammai’’ti| atha khau āyasmā  mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ catutthassa pārājikassa vatthumpi pucchi, nidānampi pucchi, puggalampi pucchi, pañ‍ñattimpi pucchi, anupañ‍ñattimpi pucchi, āpattimpi pucchi, anāpattimpi pucchi| aitainaiva upāyaina ubhatauvibhaṅgai pucchi| puṭṭhau puṭṭhau āyasmā upāli vissaj‍jaisi|

440. atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu mai, āvusau, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ ānandaṃ dhammaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti|

āyasmā ānandau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ āyasmatā mahākassapaina dhammaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiyya’’nti|

atha  khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘brahmajālaṃ, āvusau ānanda, kattha bhāsita’’nti? ‘‘antarā ca, bhantai, rājagahaṃ antarā ca nāḷandaṃ rājāgārakai ambalaṭṭhikāyā’’ti| ‘‘kaṃ ārabbhā’’ti? ‘‘suppiyañ‍ca paribbājakaṃ brahmadattañ‍ca māṇava’’nti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ brahmajālassa nidānampi pucchi, puggalampi pucchi| ‘‘sāmañ‍ñaphalaṃ panāvusau ānanda, kattha bhāsita’’nti? ‘‘rājagahai, bhantai, jīvakambavanai’’ti| ‘‘kaina saddhi’’nti? ‘‘ajātasattunā vaidaihiputtaina  saddhi’’nti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ sāmañ‍ñaphalassa nidānampi pucchi, puggalampi  pucchi| aitainaiva upāyaina pañ‍capi nikāyai pucchi| puṭṭhau puṭṭhau āyasmā ānandau vissaj‍jaisi|

2. khuddānukhuddakasikkhāpadakathā

441. atha khau āyasmā ānandau thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavā maṃ, bhantai, parinibbānakālai aivamāha – ‘ākaṅkhamānau, ānanda, saṅghau mamac‍cayaina khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadāni samūhanaiyyā’’’ti| ‘‘pucchi pana tvaṃ, āvusau ānanda, bhagavantaṃ – ‘katamāni pana, bhantai, khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadānī’’’ti? ‘‘na khauhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ pucchiṃ – ‘katamāni pana, bhantai, khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadānī’’ti| aikac‍cai thairā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘cattāri pārājikāni ṭhapaitvā, avasaisāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadānī’’ti| aikac‍cai thairā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘cattāri pārājikāni ṭhapaitvā, tairasa saṅghādisaisai ṭhapaitvā, avasaisāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadānī’’ti| aikac‍cai thairā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘cattāri pārājikāni ṭhapaitvā, tairasa saṅghādisaisai ṭhapaitvā, dvai aniyatai ṭhapaitvā, avasaisāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadānī’’ti| aikac‍cai thairā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘cattāri pārājikāni ṭhapaitvā, tairasa saṅghādisaisai ṭhapaitvā, dvai aniyatai ṭhapaitvā, tiṃsa nissaggiyai pācittiyai ṭhapaitvā, avasaisāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadānī’’ti| aikac‍cai thairā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘cattāri pārājikāni ṭhapaitvā, tairasa saṅghādisaisai ṭhapaitvā, dvai aniyatai ṭhapaitvā, tiṃsa nissaggiyai pācittiyai ṭhapaitvā, dvainavuti pācittiyai ṭhapaitvā, avasaisāni khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadānī’’ti| aikac‍cai thairā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘cattāri pārājikāni ṭhapaitvā, tairasa saṅghādisaisai ṭhapaitvā, dvai aniyatai ṭhapaitvā, tiṃsa nissaggiyai pācittiyai ṭhapaitvā, dvainavuti pācittiyai ṭhapaitvā, cattārau pāṭidaisanīyai ṭhapaitvā, avasaisāni khuddānukhuddakāni  sikkhāpadānī’’ti|

442. atha  khau āyasmā mahākassapau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu mai, āvusau, saṅghau| santamhākaṃ sikkhāpadāni gihigatāni| gihinaupi jānanti – ‘idaṃ vau samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ kappati, idaṃ vau na kappatī’ti| sacai mayaṃ khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadāni samūhanissāma, bhavissanti  vattārau – ‘dhūmakālikaṃ samaṇaina gautamaina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| yāvimaisaṃ satthā aṭṭhāsi tāvimai sikkhāpadaisu sikkhiṃsu| yatau imaisaṃ satthā parinibbutau, na dānimai sikkhāpadaisu sikkhantī’ti| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau appañ‍ñattaṃ nappañ‍ñapaiyya, pañ‍ñattaṃ na samucchindaiyya, yathāpañ‍ñattaisu sikkhāpadaisu samādāya vattaiyya| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, āvusau, saṅghau| santamhākaṃ sikkhāpadāni gihigatāni| gihinaupi jānanti – ‘idaṃ vau samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ kappati, idaṃ vau na kappatī’ti| sacai mayaṃ khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadāni samūhanissāma, bhavissanti vattārau – ‘dhūmakālikaṃ samaṇaina gautamaina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| yāvimaisaṃ satthā aṭṭhāsi tāvimai sikkhāpadaisu sikkhiṃsu| yatau imaisaṃ satthā parinibbutau, na dānimai sikkhāpadaisu sikkhantī’ti| saṅghau appañ‍ñattaṃ nappañ‍ñapaiti, pañ‍ñattaṃ na samucchindati, yathāpañ‍ñattaisu sikkhāpadaisu samādāya vattati| yassāyasmatau khamati appañ‍ñattassa appañ‍ñāpanā, pañ‍ñattassa asamucchaidau, yathāpañ‍ñattaisu sikkhāpadaisu samādāya vattanā, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘saṅghau appañ‍ñattaṃ nappañ‍ñapaiti, pañ‍ñattaṃ na samucchindati, yathāpañ‍ñattaisu sikkhāpadaisu samādāya vattati| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

443. atha khau thairā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idaṃ tai, āvusau ānanda, duk‍kaṭaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ bhagavantaṃ na pucchi – ‘katamāni pana, bhantai, khuddānukhuddakāni  sikkhāpadānī’ti| daisaihi taṃ duk‍kaṭa’’nti| ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, assatiyā bhagavantaṃ na pucchiṃ – ‘katamāni pana, bhantai, khuddānukhuddakāni sikkhāpadānī’ti| nāhaṃ taṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ passāmi, api cāyasmantānaṃ saddhāya daisaimi taṃ duk‍kaṭa’’nti| ‘‘idampi tai, āvusau ānanda, duk‍kaṭaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ bhagavatau vassikasāṭikaṃ ak‍kamitvā sibbaisi| daisaihi taṃ duk‍kaṭa’’nti| ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, na agāravaina bhagavatau vassikasāṭikaṃ ak‍kamitvā sibbaisiṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ passāmi, api cāyasmantānaṃ saddhāya daisaimi taṃ duk‍kaṭa’’nti| ‘‘idampi  tai, āvusau ānanda, duk‍kaṭaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ mātugāmaihi bhagavatau  sarīraṃ paṭhamaṃ vandāpaisi, tāsaṃ raudantīnaṃ bhagavatau sarīraṃ assukaina makkhitaṃ| daisaihi taṃ duk‍kaṭa’’nti| ahaṃ khau, bhantai – māyimāsaṃ māyimā (sī॰ syā॰) vikālai ahaisunti – mātugāmaihi bhagavatau sarīraṃ paṭhamaṃ vandāpaisiṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ passāmi, api cāyasmantānaṃ saddhāya daisaimi taṃ duk‍kaṭa’’nti| ‘‘idampi tai, āvusau ānanda, duk‍kaṭaṃ, yaṃ tvaṃ bhagavatā auḷārikai nimittai kayiramānai, auḷārikai aubhāsai kayiramānai, na bhagavantaṃ yāci – ‘tiṭṭhatu bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ, bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’nti| daisaihi taṃ duk‍kaṭa’’nti| ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, māraina pariyuṭṭhitacittau na bhagavantaṃ yāciṃ – ‘tiṭṭhatu bhagavā kappaṃ, tiṭṭhatu sugatau kappaṃ, bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’nti| nāhaṃ taṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ passāmi, api cāyasmantānaṃ saddhāya daisaimi taṃ duk‍kaṭa’’nti| ‘‘idampi tai, āvusau ānanda, duk‍kaṭaṃ yaṃ tvaṃ mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai pabbaj‍jaṃ ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi| daisaihi taṃ duk‍kaṭa’’nti| ‘‘ahaṃ khau, bhantai, ayaṃ mahāpajāpati gautamī bhagavatau mātucchā āpādikā pausikā khīrassa dāyikā bhagavantaṃ janaittiyā kālaṅkatāya thañ‍ñaṃ pāyaisīti mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai pabbaj‍jaṃ ussuk‍kaṃ akāsiṃ| nāhaṃ taṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ passāmi, api  cāyasmantānaṃ saddhāya daisaimi taṃ duk‍kaṭa’’nti|

444. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā purāṇau dakkhiṇāgirismiṃ cārikaṃ carati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi| atha khau āyasmā purāṇau thairaihi bhikkhūhi dhammai ca vinayai ca saṅgītai dakkhiṇāgirismiṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina rājagahaṃ yaina vaiḷuvanaṃ  kalandakanivāpau yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā thairaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammauditvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ purāṇaṃ thairā bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘thairaihi, āvusau purāṇa, dhammau ca vinayau ca saṅgītau| upaihi taṃ saṅgīti’’nti| ‘‘susaṅgītāvusau, thairaihi dhammau ca vinayau ca| apica yathaiva mayā bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ, sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ, tathaivāhaṃ dhāraissāmī’’ti|

3. brahmadaṇḍakathā

445. atha  khau āyasmā ānandau thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavā maṃ, bhantai, parinibbānakālai aivamāha – ‘taina hānanda, saṅghau mamac‍cayaina chan‍nassa bhikkhunau brahmadaṇḍaṃ āṇāpaitū’’’ti| ‘‘pucchi pana tvaṃ, āvusau ānanda, bhagavantaṃ – ‘katamau pana, bhantai, brahmadaṇḍau’’’ti? ‘‘pucchiṃ khauhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ – ‘katamau pana, bhantai, brahmadaṇḍau’’’ti? ‘‘chan‍nau, ānanda, bhikkhu yaṃ icchaiyya taṃ vadaiyya| bhikkhūhi chan‍nau bhikkhu naiva vattabbau, na auvaditabbau, nānusāsitabbau’’ti| ‘‘taina hāvusau ānanda, tvaṃyaiva chan‍nassa bhikkhunau  brahmadaṇḍaṃ āṇāpaihī’’ti| ‘‘kathāhaṃ, bhantai, chan‍nassa bhikkhunau brahmadaṇḍaṃ āṇāpaimi, caṇḍau sau bhikkhu pharusau’’ti? ‘taina hāvusau ānanda, bahukaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ gacchāhī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭissutvā mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi nāvāya uj‍javanikāya kausambiṃ uj‍javi, nāvāya pac‍caurauhitvā rañ‍ñau udainassa utainassa (ka॰) uyyānassa avidūrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi| taina khau pana samayaina rājā udainau uyyānai paricāraisi saddhiṃ auraudhaina| assausi khau rañ‍ñau udainassa auraudhau – ‘‘amhākaṃ kira ācariyau ayyau ānandau uyyānassa avidūrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍nau’’ti| atha khau rañ‍ñau udainassa auraudhau rājānaṃ udainaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘amhākaṃ kira, daiva, ācariyau ayyau ānandau uyyānassa avidūrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍nau| icchāma mayaṃ, daiva, ayyaṃ ānandaṃ passitu’’nti| ‘‘taina hi tumhai samaṇaṃ ānandaṃ passathā’’ti|

atha khau rañ‍ñau udainassa auraudhau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau rañ‍ñau udainassa auraudhaṃ āyasmā ānandau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi  samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau rañ‍ñau udainassa auraudhau āyasmatā ānandaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau āyasmatau ānandassa pañ‍ca uttarāsaṅgasatāni pādāsi| atha  khau rañ‍ñau udainassa auraudhau āyasmatau ānandassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina rājā udainau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau rājā udainau auraudhaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna auraudhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘api nu khau tumhai samaṇaṃ ānandaṃ passitthā’’ti? ‘‘apassimhā khau mayaṃ, daiva, ayyaṃ ānanda’’nti| ‘‘api nu tumhai samaṇassa ānandassa kiñ‍ci adatthā’’ti? ‘‘adamhā khau mayaṃ, daiva, ayyassa ānandassa pañ‍ca uttarāsaṅgasatānī’’ti| rājā udainau ujjhāyati khiyyati vipācaiti – ‘‘kathañhi nāma samaṇau ānandau tāva bahuṃ cīvaraṃ paṭiggahaissati! dussavāṇij‍jaṃ vā samaṇau ānandau karissati, paggāhikasālaṃ vā pasāraissatī’’ti!

atha  khau rājā udainau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau rājā udainau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamā nu  khvidha, bhau ānanda, amhākaṃ auraudhau’’ti? ‘‘āgamāsi khau tai idha, mahārāja, auraudhau’’ti| ‘‘api pana bhautau ānandassa kiñ‍ci adāsī’’ti? ‘‘adāsi khau mai, mahārāja, pañ‍ca uttarāsaṅgasatānī’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana bhavaṃ ānandau tāva bahuṃ cīvaraṃ karissatī’’ti? ‘‘yai yai pana (ka॰) tai, mahārāja, bhikkhū dubbalacīvarā taihi saddhiṃ saṃvibhajissāmī’’ti| ‘‘yāni khau pana, bhau ānanda, paurāṇakāni dubbalacīvarāni tāni kathaṃ karissathā’’ti? ‘‘tāni, mahārāja, uttarattharaṇaṃ karissāmā’’ti| ‘‘yāni pana, bhau ānanda, paurāṇakāni uttarattharaṇāni tāni kathaṃ karissathā’’ti? ‘‘tāni, mahārāja, bhisicchaviyau karissāmā’’ti| ‘‘yā pana, bhau ānanda, paurāṇakā bhisicchaviyau tā kathaṃ karissathā’’ti? ‘‘tā, mahārāja, bhūmattharaṇaṃ karissāmā’’ti| ‘‘yāni pana, bhau ānanda, paurāṇakāni bhūmattharaṇāni tāni kathaṃ karissathā’’ti? ‘‘tāni, mahārāja, pādapuñchaniyau karissāmā’’ti| ‘‘yā pana, bhau ānanda, paurāṇakā pādapuñchaniyau tā kathaṃ karissathā’’ti?| ‘‘tā, mahārāja, rajauharaṇaṃ karissāmā’’ti| ‘‘yāni pana, bhau  ānanda, paurāṇakāni rajauharaṇāni tāni kathaṃ karissathā’’ti? ‘‘tāni, mahārāja, kauṭṭaitvā cikkhal‍laina madditvā paribhaṇḍaṃ limpissāmā’’ti|

atha khau rājā udainau – sabbaivimai  samaṇā sakyaputtiyā yaunisau upanainti, na kulavaṃ gamaintīti  – āyasmatau ānandassa añ‍ñānipi pañ‍ca dussasatāni pādāsi| ayañ‍carahi āyasmatau ānandassa paṭhamaṃ cīvarabhikkhā uppaj‍ji cīvarasahassaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina ghausitārāmau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau āyasmā chan‍nau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca – ‘‘saṅghaina tai, āvusau chan‍na, brahmadaṇḍau āṇāpitau’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai ānanda, brahmadaṇḍau āṇāpitau’’ti? ‘‘tvaṃ, āvusau chan‍na, bhikkhū yaṃ icchaiyyāsi taṃ vadaiyyāsi| bhikkhūhi tvaṃ naiva vattabbau, na auvaditabbau, nānusāsitabbau’’ti| ‘‘nanvāhaṃ, bhantai ānanda, hatau aittāvatā, yatauhaṃ bhikkhūhi naiva vattabbau, na auvaditabbau, nānusāsitabbau’’ti tatthaiva mucchitau papatau| atha khau āyasmā chan‍nau  brahmadaṇḍaina aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā chan‍nau arahataṃ ahausi| atha khau āyasmā chan‍nau arahattaṃ pattau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭippassambhaihi dāni mai, bhantai ānanda, brahmadaṇḍa’’nti| ‘‘yadaggaina tayā, āvusau chan‍na, arahattaṃ sacchikataṃ tadaggaina tai brahmadaṇḍau paṭippassaddhau’’ti| imāya khau pana vinayasaṅgītiyā pañ‍ca bhikkhusatāni anūnāni anadhikāni ahaisuṃ| tasmā ayaṃ vinayasaṅgīti ‘‘pañ‍casatikā’’ti vuc‍catīti|

pañ‍casatikakkhandhakau aikādasamau|

imamhi khandhakai vatthū taivīsati|

tassuddānaṃ –

parinibbutai  sambuddhai, thairau kassapasavhayau|

āmantayi bhikkhugaṇaṃ, saddhammamanupālakau|

pāvāyaddhānamaggamhi, subhaddaina pavaiditaṃ|

saṅgāyissāma  saddhammaṃ, adhammau purai dippati||

aikainūna pañ‍casataṃ, ānandampi ca uc‍cini|

dhammavinayasaṅgītiṃ, vasantau guhamuttamai||

upāliṃ  vinayaṃ pucchi, suttantānandapaṇḍitaṃ|

piṭakaṃ tīṇi saṅgītiṃ, akaṃsu jinasāvakā||

na pucchi ak‍kamitvāna, vandāpaisi na yāci ca||

pabbaj‍jaṃ  mātugāmassa, saddhāya duk‍kaṭāni mai|

purāṇau brahmadaṇḍañ‍ca, auraudhau udainaina saha||

tāva bahu dubbalañ‍ca, uttarattharaṇā bhisi|

bhūmattharaṇā puñchaniyau, rajau cikkhal‍lamaddanā||

sahassacīvaraṃ uppaj‍ji, paṭhamānandasavhayau|

taj‍jitau brahmadaṇḍaina, catussac‍caṃ apāpuṇi|

vasībhūtā pañ‍casatā, tasmā pañ‍casatī iti||

pañ‍casatikakkhandhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



12. sattasatikakkhandhakaṃ

1. paṭhamabhāṇavārau

446. taina  khau pana samayaina vassasataparinibbutai bhagavati vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū vaisāliyaṃ dasa vatthūni dīpainti – kappati siṅgilauṇakappau, kappati dvaṅgulakappau, kappati gāmantarakappau, kappati āvāsakappau, kappati anumatikappau, kappati āciṇṇakappau, kappati amathitakappau, kappati jaḷaugiṃ pātuṃ, kappati adasakaṃ nisīdanaṃ, kappati jātarūparajatanti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau vaj‍jīsu cārikaṃ caramānau yaina vaisālī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū tadahupausathai kaṃsapātiṃ kaṃsacāṭiṃ (syā॰) udakaina pūraitvā majjhai bhikkhusaṅghassa ṭhapaitvā āgatāgatai vaisālikai upāsakai aivaṃ vadanti – ‘‘daithāvusau, saṅghassa kahāpaṇampi aḍḍhampi pādampi māsakarūpampi| bhavissati saṅghassa parikkhāraina karaṇīya’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau vaisālikai upāsakai aitadavauca – ‘‘māvusau, adattha saṅghassa kahāpaṇampi aḍḍhampi pādampi māsakarūpampi| na  kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ jātarūparajataṃ; na sādiyanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ; na paṭiggaṇhanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ; nikkhittamaṇisuvaṇṇā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā apaitajātarūparajatā’’ti| aivampi khau vaisālikā upāsakā āyasmatā yasaina kākaṇḍakaputtaina vuc‍camānā adaṃsuyaiva saṅghassa kahāpaṇampi aḍḍhampi pādampi māsakarūpampi|

atha khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina taṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ bhikkhaggaina bhikkhuggaina (syā॰)  paṭivīsaṃ paṭiviṃsaṃ (sī॰), paṭivisaṃ (syā॰) ṭhapaitvā bhājaisuṃ| atha khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ yasaṃ kākaṇḍakaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aisau  tai, āvusau yasa, hirañ‍ñassa paṭivīsau’’ti| ‘‘natthi, mai āvusau, hirañ‍ñassa paṭivīsau, nāhaṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ sādiyāmī’’ti| atha  khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū – ‘‘ayaṃ āvusau yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau upāsakai saddhai pasan‍nai ak‍kausati, paribhāsati, appasādaṃ karauti; handassa mayaṃ paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaumā’’ti tai| tassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ akaṃsu| atha khau āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau vaisālikai vaj‍jiputtakai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavatā, āvusau, pañ‍ñattaṃ – ‘paṭisāraṇīyakammakatassa bhikkhunau anudūtau dātabbau’ti| daitha mai, āvusau, anudūtaṃ bhikkhu’’nti|

atha khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ samman‍nitvā āyasmatau yasassa kākaṇḍakaputtassa anudūtaṃ adaṃsu| atha khau āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau  anudūtaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ vaisāliṃ pavisitvā vaisālikai upāsakai aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ kirāyasmantai upāsakai saddhai pasan‍nai ak‍kausāmi, paribhāsāmi, appasādaṃ karaumi; yauhaṃ adhammaṃ adhammauti vadāmi, dhammaṃ dhammauti vadāmi, avinayaṃ avinayauti vadāmi, vinayaṃ vinayauti vadāmi|

447. ‘‘aikamidaṃ, āvusau, samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau, āvusau, bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – a॰ ni॰ 4.50 ‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti, na bhāsanti, na viraucanti| katamai cattārau? abbhaṃ, bhikkhavai, candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti, na bhāsanti na viraucanti| mahikā, bhikkhavai, candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti, na bhāsanti, na viraucanti, dhūmarajau, bhikkhavai, candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti, na bhāsanti, na viraucanti| rāhu, bhikkhavai, asurindau candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti, na bhāsanti, na viraucanti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau candimasūriyānaṃ upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhā candimasūriyā na tapanti, na bhāsanti, na viraucanti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cattāraumai samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ  upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapanti, na bhāsanti, na viraucanti| katamai cattārau? santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā  suraṃ pivanti, mairayaṃ pivanti, surāmairayapānā appaṭiviratā – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṭhamau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na  tapanti, na bhāsanti, na viraucanti| puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivanti, maithunadhammā appaṭiviratā – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dutiyau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapanti, na bhāsanti, na viraucanti| puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā jātarūparajataṃ sādiyanti, jātarūparajatappaṭiggahaṇā appaṭiviratā – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatiyau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapanti, na bhāsanti, na viraucanti| puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā micchājīvaina jīvitaṃ kappainti; micchājīvā appaṭiviratā – ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, catutthau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upak‍kilaisau, yaina upak‍kilaisaina upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapanti, na bhāsanti, na viraucanti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ upak‍kilaisā, yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhā aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā na tapanti, na bhāsanti, na viraucanti’| ‘‘idamavaucāvusau, bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘rāgadausaparik‍liṭṭhā, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā|

avij‍jānivuṭā avij‍jānīvutā (syā॰) pausā, piyarūpābhinandinau||

‘‘suraṃ pivanti mairayaṃ, paṭisaivanti maithunaṃ|

rajataṃ jātarūpañ‍ca, sādiyanti aviddasū||

‘‘micchājīvaina jīvanti, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā|

aitai upak‍kilaisā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā||

‘‘yaihi upak‍kilaisaihi upak‍kiliṭṭhā, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā|

na tapanti na bhāsanti, asuddhā sarajā magā||

‘‘andhakāraina aunaddhā, taṇhādāsā sanaittikā|

vaḍḍhainti kaṭasiṃ ghauraṃ, ādiyanti punabbhavanti||

‘‘aivaṃvādī  kirāhaṃ āyasmantai upāsakai saddhai pasan‍nai ak‍kausāmi, paribhāsāmi, appasādaṃ karaumi; yauhaṃ adhammaṃ adhammauti vadāmi, dhammaṃ dhammauti vadāmi, avinayaṃ avinayauti vadāmi, vinayaṃ vinayauti vadāmi|

448. saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.362 idaṃ vatthu āgataṃ ‘‘aikamidaṃ, āvusau, samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina rājantaipurai rājaparisāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ  san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarakathā udapādi – ‘kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ jātarūparajataṃ; sādiyanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ; paṭiggaṇhanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajata’nti| taina khau panāvusau samayaina, maṇicūḷakau gāmaṇī tassaṃ parisāyaṃ nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau, āvusau, maṇicūḷakau gāmaṇī taṃ parisaṃ aitadavauca – ‘mā ayyā aivaṃ avacuttha| na kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ jātarūparajataṃ; na sādiyanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ; na paṭiggaṇhanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ; nikkhittamaṇisuvaṇṇā  samaṇā sakyaputtiyā apaitajātarūparajatā’ti| asakkhi khau, āvusau, maṇicūḷakau gāmaṇī taṃ parisaṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ|

‘‘atha khau, āvusau, maṇicūḷakau gāmaṇī taṃ parisaṃ sañ‍ñāpaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau, āvusau, maṇicūḷakau gāmaṇī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘idha, bhantai, rājantaipurai rājaparisāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarakathā udapādi – kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ jātarūparajataṃ; sādiyanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ; paṭiggaṇhanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajatanti| aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ bhantai, taṃ parisaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – mā ayyā aivaṃ avacuttha| na kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ jātarūparajataṃ; na sādiyanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ; na paṭiggaṇhanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ; nikkhittamaṇisuvaṇṇā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā apaitajātarūparajatāti| asakkhiṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, taṃ parisaṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ| kac‍cāhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaramānau vuttavādī caiva bhagavatau haumi, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhāmi, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraumi, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchatī’ti? ‘taggha tvaṃ, gāmaṇi, aivaṃ byākaramānau vuttavādī caiva mai hausi; na ca maṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhasi  pārā॰ 582, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākarausi, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchati| na hi, gāmaṇi, kappati samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ jātarūparajataṃ; na sādiyanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā  jātarūparajataṃ; na paṭiggaṇhanti samaṇā sakyaputtiyā jātarūparajataṃ; nikkhittamaṇisuvaṇṇā samaṇā sakyaputtiyā apaitajātarūparajatā| yassa khau, gāmaṇi, jātarūparajataṃ kappati, pañ‍capi tassa kāmaguṇā kappanti| yassa pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā kappanti aikaṃsainaitaṃ, gāmaṇi, dhāraiyyāsi – assamaṇadhammau asakyaputtiyadhammauti| api cāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, aivaṃ vadāmi tiṇaṃ tiṇatthikaina pariyaisitabbaṃ; dāru dārutthikaina pariyaisitabbaṃ; sakaṭaṃ sakaṭatthikaina pariyaisitabbaṃ; purisau purisatthikaina pariyaisitabbau| na tvaivāhaṃ, gāmaṇi, kainaci pariyāyaina jātarūparajataṃ sāditabbaṃ pariyaisitabbanti  vadāmī’ti|

‘‘aivaṃvādī kirāhaṃ āyasmantai upāsakai saddhai pasan‍nai ak‍kausāmi, paribhāsāmi, appasādaṃ karaumi; yauhaṃ adhammaṃ adhammauti vadāmi, dhammaṃ dhammauti vadāmi, avinayaṃ avinayauti vadāmi, vinayaṃ vinayauti vadāmi|

449. ‘‘aikamidaṃ, āvusau, samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha jātarūparajataṃ paṭikkhipi, sikkhāpadañ‍ca pañ‍ñapaisi| aivaṃvādī kirāhaṃ āyasmantai upāsakai saddhai pasan‍nai ak‍kausāmi, paribhāsāmi, appasādaṃ karaumi; yauhaṃ adhammaṃ  adhammauti vadāmi, dhammaṃ dhammauti vadāmi, avinayaṃ avinayauti vadāmi, vinayaṃ vinayauti vadāmī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai vaisālikā upāsakā āyasmantaṃ yasaṃ kākaṇḍakaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aikauva bhantai, ayyau yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau samaṇau sakyaputtiyau| sabbaivimai assamaṇā asakyaputtiyā| vasatu, bhantai, ayyau yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau vaisāliyaṃ| mayaṃ ayyassa yasassa kākaṇḍakaputtassa ussuk‍kaṃ karissāma cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’’nti| atha khau āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau vaisālikai upāsakai sañ‍ñāpaitvā anudūtaina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ ārāmaṃ agamāsi|

atha khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū anudūtaṃ bhikkhuṃ pucchiṃsu – ‘‘khamāpitāvusau, yasaina kākaṇḍakaputtaina vaisālikā upāsakā’’ti? ‘‘upāsakaihi pāpikaṃ nau, āvusau, kataṃ| aikauva yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau samaṇau sakyaputtiyau katau| sabbaiva mayaṃ assamaṇā asakyaputtiyā katā’’ti| atha khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū – ‘‘ayaṃ, āvusau, yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau amhaihi asammatau gihīnaṃ pakāsaisi; handassa mayaṃ ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ  karaumā’’ti| tai tassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kattukāmā san‍nipatiṃsu| atha khau āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau vaihāsaṃ abbhuggantvā kausambiyaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāsi|

450. atha khau āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau pāvaiyyakānañ‍ca pāṭhaiyyakānañ‍ca (syā॰) avantidakkhiṇāpathakānañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dūtaṃ pāhaisi – ‘‘āgacchantu āyasmantā; imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ ādiyissāma| purai adhammau dippati, dhammau paṭibāhiyyati; avinayau dippati, vinayau paṭibāhiyyati; purai adhammavādinau balavantau haunti, dhammavādinau dubbalā haunti; avinayavādinau balavantau haunti, vinayavādinau dubbalā hauntī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sambhūtau sāṇavāsī ahaugaṅgai pabbatai paṭivasati| atha khau āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau yaina ahaugaṅgau pabbatau, yaināyasmā sambhūtau sāṇavāsī tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sambhūtaṃ sāṇavāsiṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau āyasmantaṃ sambhūtaṃ sāṇavāsiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imai, bhantai, vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū vaisāliyaṃ dasa vatthūni dīpainti – kappati siṅgilauṇakappau, kappati dvaṅgulakappau, kappati gāmantarakappau, kappati āvāsakappau, kappati anumatikappau, kappati āciṇṇakappau, kappati amathitakappau, kappati jaḷaugiṃ pātuṃ, kappati adasakaṃ nisīdanaṃ, kappati jātarūparajatanti| handa  mayaṃ, bhantai, imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ ādiyissāma| purai adhammau dippati, dhammau paṭibāhiyyati; avinayau dippati, vinayau paṭibāhiyyati; purai adhammavādinau balavantau haunti, dhammavādinau dubbalā haunti; avinayavādinau balavantau haunti, vinayavādinau dubbalā hauntī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā sambhūtau sāṇavāsī āyasmatau yasassa kākaṇḍakaputtassa pac‍cassausi| atha  khau saṭṭhimattā pāvaiyyakā bhikkhū – sabbai ārañ‍ñikā, sabbai piṇḍapātikā, sabbai paṃsukūlikā, sabbai taicīvarikā, sabbaiva arahantau – ahaugaṅgai pabbatai san‍nipatiṃsu| aṭṭhāsītimattā avantidakkhiṇāpathakā bhikkhū – appaikac‍cai ārañ‍ñikā, appaikac‍cai piṇḍapātikā, appaikac‍cai paṃsukūlikā, appaikac‍cai taicīvarikā, sabbaiva arahantau – ahaugaṅgai pabbatai san‍nipatiṃsu| atha  khau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mantayamānānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷañ‍ca, vāḷañ‍ca; kaṃ nu khau mayaṃ pakkhaṃ labhaiyyāma, yaina mayaṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai balavantatarā assāmā’’ti|

451. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā raivatau sauraiyyai paṭivasati – bahussutau āgatāgamau  dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| atha khau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā raivatau sauraiyyai paṭivasati – bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| sacai mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ pakkhaṃ labhissāma, aivaṃ mayaṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai balavantatarā assāmā’’ti| assausi khau āyasmā raivatau – dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya – thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mantayamānānaṃ| sutvānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷañ‍ca vāḷañ‍ca| na khau maitaṃ patirūpaṃ yauhaṃ aivarūpai adhikaraṇai ausak‍kaiyyaṃ| idāni ca pana tai bhikkhū āgacchissanti| sauhaṃ taihi ākiṇṇau na phāsu gamissāmi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ paṭikac‍caiva gacchaiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā raivatau sauraiyyā saṅkassaṃ agamāsi|

atha khau thairā bhikkhū sauraiyyaṃ gantvā pucchiṃsu – ‘‘kahaṃ āyasmā raivatau’’ti? tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘aisāyasmā raivatau saṅkassaṃ  gatau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā raivatau saṅkassā kaṇṇakuj‍jaṃ kan‍nakuj‍jaṃ (sī॰) agamāsi| atha khau thairā bhikkhū saṅkassaṃ gantvā pucchiṃsu – ‘‘kahaṃ āyasmā raivatau’’ti? tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘aisāyasmā raivatau kaṇṇakuj‍jaṃ gatau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā raivatau kaṇṇakuj‍jā udumbaraṃ  agamāsi| atha khau thairā bhikkhū kaṇṇakuj‍jaṃ gantvā pucchiṃsu – ‘‘kahaṃ āyasmā raivatau’’ti? tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘aisāyasmā raivatau udumbaraṃ gatau’’ti| atha  khau āyasmā raivatau udumbarā aggaḷapuraṃ agamāsi| atha khau thairā bhikkhū udumbaraṃ gantvā pucchiṃsu – ‘‘kahaṃ āyasmā raivatau’’ti? tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘aisāyasmā raivatau aggaḷapuraṃ gatau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā raivatau aggaḷapurā sahajātiṃ sahaṃjātiṃ (ka॰) agamāsi| atha khau thairā bhikkhū aggaḷapuraṃ gantvā pucchiṃsu – ‘‘kahaṃ āyasmā raivatau’’ti? tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘aisāyasmā raivatau sahajātiṃ gatau’’ti| atha khau thairā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ sahajātiyaṃ sambhāvaisuṃ|

452. atha khau āyasmā sambhūtau sāṇavāsī āyasmantaṃ yasaṃ kākaṇḍakaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, āvusau, āyasmā raivatau bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| sacai mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ pañhaṃ pucchissāma, paṭibalau āyasmā raivatau aikainaiva pañhaina sakalampi rattiṃ vītināmaituṃ| idāni ca panāyasmā raivatau  antaivāsikaṃ antaivāsiṃ (syā॰) sarabhāṇakaṃ bhikkhuṃ ajjhaisissati| sau tvaṃ tassa bhikkhunau sarabhañ‍ñapariyausānai āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ upasaṅkamitvā imāni  dasa vatthūni pucchaiyyāsī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau āyasmatau sambhūtassa sāṇavāsissa pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmā raivatau antaivāsikaṃ sarabhāṇakaṃ bhikkhuṃ ajjhaisi| atha khau āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau tassa bhikkhunau sarabhañ‍ñapariyausānai yaināyasmā raivatau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā yasau kākaṇḍakaputtau āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kappati, bhantai, siṅgilauṇakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, siṅgilauṇakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, siṅginā lauṇaṃ pariharituṃ – yattha alauṇakaṃ bhavissati tattha paribhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti| ‘‘kappati, bhantai, dvaṅgulakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, dvaṅgulakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, dvaṅgulāya chāyāya vītivattāya, vikālai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jitu’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti| ‘‘kappati, bhantai, gāmantarakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, gāmantarakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai – idāni gāmantaraṃ gamissāmīti – bhuttāvinā pavāritaina anatirittaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jitu’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti| ‘‘kappati, bhantai, āvāsakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, āvāsakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, sambahulā āvāsā samānasīmā nānupausathaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti| ‘‘kappati, bhantai, anumatikappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, anumatikappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, vaggaina saṅghaina kammaṃ kātuṃ – āgatai bhikkhū anumānaissāmā’’ti anujānissāmāti, anujānaissāmāti, anumatiṃ ānaissāmāti (ka॰)? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti| ‘‘kappati, bhantai, āciṇṇakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, āciṇṇakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, idaṃ mai upajjhāyaina ajjhāciṇṇaṃ, idaṃ mai ācariyaina ajjhāciṇṇaṃ, taṃ ajjhācaritu’’nti? ‘‘āciṇṇakappau khau, āvusau, aikac‍cau kappati, aikac‍cau na kappatī’’ti| ‘‘kappati, bhantai, amathitakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, amathitakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, yaṃ taṃ khīraṃ khīrabhāvaṃ vijahitaṃ, asampattaṃ dadhibhāvaṃ, taṃ bhuttāvinā pavāritaina anatirittaṃ pātu’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, jaḷaugiṃ pātu’’nti? ‘‘kā sā, āvusau, jaḷaugī’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, yā sā surā āsutā, asampattā maj‍jabhāvaṃ, sā pātu’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti| ‘‘kappati, bhantai, adasakaṃ nisīdana’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti| ‘‘kappati, bhantai, jātarūparajata’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti| ‘‘imai, bhantai, vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū vaisāliyaṃ imāni dasa vatthūni dīpainti| handa mayaṃ, bhantai, imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ ādiyissāma| purai adhammau dippati, dhammau paṭibāhiyyati; avinayau  dippati, vinayau paṭibāhiyyati; purai adhammavādinau balavantau haunti, dhammavādinau dubbalā  haunti; avinayavādinau balavantau haunti, vinayavādinau dubbalā hauntī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā raivatau āyasmatau yasassa kākaṇḍakaputtassa pac‍cassausi|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

2. dutiyabhāṇavārau

453. assausuṃ  khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū – ‘‘yasau kira kākaṇḍakaputtau idaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ ādiyitukāmau pakkhaṃ pariyaisati, labhati ca kira pakkha’’nti| atha khau vaisālikānaṃ vaj‍jiputtakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau adhikaraṇaṃ kakkhaḷañ‍ca vāḷañ‍ca| kaṃ nu khau mayaṃ pakkhaṃ labhaiyyāma, yaina mayaṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai balavantatarā assāmā’’ti|

atha khau vaisālikānaṃ vaj‍jiputtakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā raivatau bahussutau āgatāgamau dhammadharau vinayadharau mātikādharau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī laj‍jī kuk‍kuc‍cakau sikkhākāmau| sacai mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ pakkhaṃ labhaiyyāma, aivaṃ mayaṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai balavantatarā assāmā’’ti|

atha khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū pahūtaṃ sāmaṇakaṃ parikkhāraṃ paṭiyādaisuṃ – pattampi, cīvarampi, nisīdanampi, sūcigharampi, kāyabandhanampi, parissāvanampi, dhammakaraṇampi| atha khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū taṃ sāmaṇakaṃ parikkhāraṃ ādāya nāvāya sahajātiṃ uj‍javiṃsu; nāvāya pac‍caurauhitvā  añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai  bhattavissaggaṃ karaunti| atha khau āyasmatau sāḷhassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘kai nu khau dhammavādinau – pācīnakā vā bhikkhū, pāvaiyyakā vā’’ti? atha khau āyasmatau sāḷhassa, dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca caitasā pac‍cavaikkhantassa, aitadahausi – ‘‘adhammavādinau pācīnakā bhikkhū, dhammavādinau pāvaiyyakā pāṭhaiyyakā (syā॰) bhikkhū’’ti|

atha khau añ‍ñatarā suddhāvāsakāyikā daivatā āyasmatau sāḷhassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ sammiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva suddhāvāsaisu daivaisu antarahitā – āyasmatau sāḷhassa  sammukhai pāturahausi| atha khau sā daivatā āyasmantaṃ sāḷhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, bhantai sāḷha, adhammavādī pācīnakā bhikkhū, dhammavādī pāvaiyyakā bhikkhū| taina hi, bhantai sāḷha, yathādhammau tathā tiṭṭhāhī’’ti| ‘‘pubbaipi cāhaṃ, daivatai, aitarahi ca yathādhammau tathā ṭhitau; api cāhaṃ na tāva diṭṭhiṃ āvi karaumi, appaiva nāma maṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai samman‍naiyyā’’ti|

454. atha khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū taṃ sāmaṇakaṃ parikkhāraṃ ādāya yaināyasmā raivatau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu, bhantai, thairau sāmaṇakaṃ  parikkhāraṃ – pattampi, cīvarampi, nisīdanampi, sūcigharampi, kāyabandhanampi, parissāvanampi, dhammakaraṇampī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau, paripuṇṇaṃ mai pattacīvara’’nti na icchi paṭiggahaituṃ|

taina khau pana samayaina uttarau nāma bhikkhu vīsativassau āyasmatau raivatassa upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū yaināyasmā uttarau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ uttaraṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu āyasmā uttarau sāmaṇakaṃ parikkhāraṃ – pattampi, cīvarampi, nisīdanampi, sūcigharampi, kāyabandhanampi, parissāvanampi, dhammakaraṇampī’’ti| ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau, paripuṇṇaṃ mai pattacīvara’’nti na icchi paṭiggahaituṃ| ‘‘manussā khau, āvusau uttara, bhagavatau sāmaṇakaṃ parikkhāraṃ upanāmainti| sacai bhagavā paṭiggaṇhāti, tainaiva tai attamanā haunti| nau cai bhagavā paṭiggaṇhāti, āyasmatau āyasmatau ca (syā॰) ānandassa upanāmainti – paṭiggaṇhātu, bhantai, thairau sāmaṇakaṃ parikkhāraṃ| yathā bhagavatā paṭiggahitau, aivamaiva sau bhavissatīti| paṭiggaṇhātu āyasmā uttarau sāmaṇakaṃ  parikkhāraṃ| yathā thairaina paṭiggahitau, aivamaiva sau bhavissatī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā uttarau vaisālikaihi vaj‍jiputtaihi bhikkhūhi nippīḷiyamānau aikaṃ cīvaraṃ aggahaisi| ‘‘vadaiyyātha, āvusau, yaina atthau’’ti| ‘‘aittakaṃ āyasmā uttarau thairaṃ  vadaitu; aittakañ‍ca, bhantai, thairau saṅghamajjhai vadaitu – ‘puratthimaisu janapadaisu buddhā bhagavantau uppaj‍janti| dhammavādī pācīnakā bhikkhū, adhammavādī pāvaiyyakā bhikkhū’’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā uttarau vaisālikānaṃ vaj‍jiputtakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭissutvā yaināyasmā raivatau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aittakaṃ, bhantai, thairau saṅghamajjhai vadaitu – ‘puratthimaisu janapadaisu buddhā bhagavantau uppaj‍janti| dhammavādī pācīnakā bhikkhū, adhammavādī  pāvaiyyakā bhikkhū’’’ti| ‘‘adhammai maṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, niyaujaisī’’ti thairau āyasmantaṃ uttaraṃ paṇāmaisi|
