atha khau vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ uttaraṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kiṃ, āvusau uttara, thairau āhā’’ti? ‘‘pāpikaṃ nau, āvusau, kataṃ| ‘adhammai maṃ tvaṃ, bhikkhu, niyaujaisī’’’ti thairau maṃ paṇāmaisīti| ‘‘nanu tvaṃ, āvusau āvusau uttara (syā॰ kaṃ॰), vuḍḍhau vīsativassausī’’ti? ‘‘āmāvusau, api ca mayaṃ garunissayaṃ gaṇhāmā’’ti|

455. atha khau saṅghau taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vinicchinitukāmau san‍nipati| atha khau āyasmā raivatau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu mai, āvusau, saṅghau| sacai mayaṃ imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ idha vūpasamaissāma, siyāpi mūlādāyakā mūladāyakā (sī॰) bhikkhū punakammāya uk‍kauṭaiyyuṃ| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, yatthaivimaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ samuppan‍naṃ, saṅghau tatthaivimaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ  vūpasamaiyyā’’ti|

atha khau thairā bhikkhū vaisāliṃ agamaṃsu – taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vinicchinitukāmā|

taina khau pana samayaina sabbakāmī nāma pathabyā saṅghatthairau vīsavassasatikau upasampadāya, āyasmatau ānandassa saddhivihārikau, vaisāliyaṃ paṭivasati| atha khau āyasmā raivatau āyasmantaṃ sambhūtaṃ sāṇavāsiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ, āvusau, yasmiṃ vihārai sabbakāmī thairau viharati, taṃ vihāraṃ upagacchāmi| sau tvaṃ kālassaiva āyasmantaṃ sabbakāmiṃ upasaṅkamitvā imāni dasa vatthūni pucchaiyyāsī’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā sambhūtau sāṇavāsī āyasmatau raivatassa pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmā raivatau, yasmiṃ vihārai sabbakāmī thairau viharati, taṃ vihāraṃ upagacchi| gabbhai āyasmatau sabbakāmissa saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ hauti, gabbhappamukhai āyasmatau raivatassa| atha khau āyasmā raivatau – ayaṃ thairau  mahal‍lakau na nipaj‍jatīti – na saiyyaṃ kappaisi| āyasmā sabbakāmī – ayaṃ bhikkhu āgantukau kilantau na nipaj‍jatīti – na saiyyaṃ kappaisi| atha khau āyasmā  sabbakāmī rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katamaina tvaṃ bhūmi vihāraina aitarahi bahulaṃ viharasī’’ti? ‘‘maittāvihāraina khau ahaṃ, bhantai, aitarahi bahulaṃ viharāmī’’ti| ‘‘kul‍lakavihāraina kira tvaṃ bhūmi aitarahi bahulaṃ viharasi| kul‍lakavihārau aisau haisau (syā॰) bhūmi yadidaṃ maittā’’ti| ‘‘pubbaipi mai, bhantai, gihibhūtassa āciṇṇā maittā| taināhaṃ aitarahipi maittāvihāraina bahulaṃ viharāmi, api ca khau mayā cirappattaṃ arahatta’’nti| ‘‘thairau pana, bhantai, katamaina vihāraina aitarahi bahulaṃ viharatī’’ti? ‘‘suñ‍ñatāvihāraina khau ahaṃ bhūmi aitarahi bahulaṃ viharāmī’’ti| ‘‘mahāpurisavihāraina kira, bhantai, thairau aitarahi bahulaṃ viharati| mahāpurisavihārau aisau, bhantai, yadidaṃ suñ‍ñatā’’ti| ‘‘pubbaipi mai bhūmi gihibhūtassa āciṇṇā suñ‍ñatā| taināhaṃ aitarahipi suñ‍ñatāvihāraina bahulaṃ viharāmi, api ca mayā cirappattaṃ arahatta’’nti| ayañ‍carahi thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ antarākathā vippakatā, athāyasmā sambhūtau sāṇavāsī tasmiṃ anuppattau hauti| atha khau āyasmā sambhūtau sāṇavāsī yaināyasmā sabbakāmī tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sabbakāmiṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sambhūtau sāṇavāsī āyasmantaṃ sabbakāmiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imai, bhantai, vaisālikā vaj‍jiputtakā bhikkhū vaisāliyaṃ dasa vatthūni dīpainti – kappati siṅgilauṇakappau, kappati dvaṅgulakappau, kappati gāmantarakappau, kappati āvāsakappau, kappati anumatikappau, kappati āciṇṇakappau, kappati amathitakappau, kappati jaḷaugiṃ, pātuṃ kappati adasakaṃ nisīdanaṃ, kappati jātarūparajatanti| thairaina, bhantai, upajjhāyassa mūlai bahudhammau ca vinayau ca pariyattau| thairassa bhantai, dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca pac‍cavaikkhantassa kathaṃ hauti? kai nu khau dhammavādinau – pācīnakā vā bhikkhū, pāvaiyyakā vā’’ti? ‘‘tayāpi khau, āvusau, upajjhāyassa mūlai bahu dhammau ca vinayau ca pariyattau| tuyhaṃ pana, āvusau, dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca pac‍cavaikkhantassa kathaṃ hauti? kai nu khau dhammavādinau – pācīnakā vā bhikkhū, pāvaiyyakā vā’’ti? ‘‘mayhaṃ khau, bhantai, dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca pac‍cavaikkhantassa aivaṃ hauti – adhammavādī pācīnakā bhikkhū, dhammavādī pāvaiyyakā bhikkhūti; api cāhaṃ na  tāva diṭṭhiṃ āvi karaumi, appaiva nāma maṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai samman‍naiyyā’’ti| ‘‘mayhampi khau, āvusau, dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca pac‍cavaikkhantassa aivaṃ hauti  – adhammavādī pācīnakā bhikkhū, dhammavādī pāvaiyyakā bhikkhūti; api cāhaṃ na tāva diṭṭhiṃ āvi karaumi, appaiva nāma maṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai samman‍naiyyā’’ti|

456. atha khau saṅghau taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vinicchinitukāmau san‍nipati| tasmiṃ khau pana adhikaraṇai vinicchiyamānai anaggāni caiva bhassāni jāyanti, na caikassa bhāsitassa atthau viñ‍ñāyati| atha khau āyasmā raivatau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ  imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai vinicchiyamānai anaggāni caiva bhassāni jāyanti, na caikassa bhāsitassa atthau viñ‍ñāyati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ ubbāhikāya vūpasamaiyyā’’ti| saṅghau cattārau pācīnakai bhikkhū, cattārau pāvaiyyakai bhikkhū uc‍cini| pācīnakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ – āyasmantañ‍ca sabbakāmiṃ, āyasmantañ‍ca sāḷhaṃ, āyasmantañ‍ca khuj‍jasaubhitaṃ, āyasmantañ‍ca vāsabhagāmikaṃ; pāvaiyyakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ – āyasmantañ‍ca raivataṃ, āyasmantañ‍ca sambhūtaṃ sāṇavāsiṃ, āyasmantañ‍ca yasaṃ kākaṇḍakaputtaṃ, āyasmantañ‍ca sumananti| atha khau āyasmā raivatau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai vinicchiyamānai anaggāni caiva bhassāni jāyanti, na caikassa bhāsitassa atthau viñ‍ñāyati| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, saṅghau cattārau pācīnakai bhikkhū, cattārau pāvaiyyakai bhikkhū samman‍naiyya ubbāhikāya imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| aisā ñatti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| amhākaṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai vinicchiyamānai anaggāni caiva bhassāni jāyanti, na caikassa bhāsitassa atthau viñ‍ñāyati| saṅghau cattārau pācīnakai bhikkhū, cattārau pāvaiyyakai bhikkhū samman‍nati ubbāhikāya imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| yassāyasmatau khamati catun‍naṃ pācīnakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, catun‍naṃ pāvaiyyakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sammuti, ubbāhikāya imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ, sau tuṇhassa; yassa nakkhamati, sau bhāsaiyya|

‘‘sammatā  saṅghaina cattārau pācīnakā bhikkhū, cattārau pāvaiyyakā bhikkhū, ubbāhikāya imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| khamati saṅghassa, tasmā tuṇhī, aivamaitaṃ dhārayāmī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina ajitau nāma bhikkhu dasavassau saṅghassa pātimaukkhuddaisakau hauti| atha khau saṅghau āyasmantampi ajitaṃ samman‍nati – thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ āsanapañ‍ñāpakaṃ| atha khau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘kattha nu khau mayaṃ imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaiyyāmā’’ti? atha khau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau vālikārāmau ramaṇīyau appasaddau appanigghausau| yaṃnūna mayaṃ vālikārāmai imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaiyyāmā’’ti|

457. atha  khau thairā bhikkhū vālikārāmaṃ agamaṃsu – taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vinicchinitukāmā| atha khau āyasmā raivatau saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ āyasmantaṃ sabbakāmiṃ vinayaṃ pucchaiyya’’nti|

āyasmā sabbakāmī saṅghaṃ ñāpaisi –

‘‘suṇātu mai, āvusau, saṅghau| yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ raivataina vinayaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiyya’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā raivatau āyasmantaṃ sabbakāmiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kappati, bhantai, siṅgilauṇakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, siṅgilauṇakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, siṅginā lauṇaṃ pariharituṃ – yattha alauṇakaṃ bhavissati  tattha paribhuñ‍jissāmā’’ti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti| pāci॰ 254 ādayau ‘‘kattha paṭikkhitta’’nti? ‘‘sāvatthiyaṃ, suttavibhaṅgai’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘san‍nidhikārakabhaujanai pācittiya’’nti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ vatthu saṅghaina vinicchitaṃ| itipidaṃ vatthu uddhammaṃ, ubbinayaṃ, apagatasatthusāsanaṃ| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ salākaṃ nikkhipāmi’’|

‘‘kappati, bhantai, dvaṅgulakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, dvaṅgulakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, dvaṅgulāya chāyāya vītivattāya vikālai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jitu’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti pāci॰ 245| ‘‘kattha paṭikkhitta’’nti? ‘‘rājagahai, suttavibhaṅgai’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti? vikālabhaujanai pācittiya’’nti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ dutiyaṃ vatthu saṅghaina vinicchitaṃ| itipidaṃ vatthu uddhammaṃ, ubbinayaṃ, apagatasatthusāsanaṃ| idaṃ dutiyaṃ salākaṃ nikkhipāmi’’|

‘‘kappati, bhantai, gāmantarakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, gāmantarakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai – idāni gāmantaraṃ gamissāmīti – bhuttāvinā pavāritaina anatirittaṃ bhaujanaṃ  bhuñ‍jitu’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti pāci॰ 234| ‘‘kattha paṭikkhitta’’nti? ‘‘sāvatthiyaṃ, suttavibhaṅgai’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘anatirittabhaujanai pācittiya’’nti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ tatiyaṃ vatthu saṅghaina vinicchitaṃ| itipidaṃ vatthu uddhammaṃ, ubbinayaṃ, apagatasatthusāsanaṃ| idaṃ tatiyaṃ salākaṃ nikkhipāmi’’|

‘‘kappati, bhantai, āvāsakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, āvāsakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati bhantai, sambahulā āvāsā samānasīmā nānupausathaṃ kātu’’nti| ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti mahāva॰ 141 ādayau| ‘‘kattha paṭikkhitta’’nti? ‘‘rājagahai, upausathasaṃyuttai’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘vinayātisārai duk‍kaṭa’’nti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ catutthaṃ vatthu saṅghaina vinicchitaṃ| itipidaṃ vatthu uddhammaṃ, ubbinayaṃ, apagatasatthusāsanaṃ| idaṃ catutthaṃ salākaṃ nikkhipāmi’’|

‘‘kappati, bhantai, anumatikappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, anumatikappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, vaggaina saṅghaina kammaṃ kātuṃ – āgatai bhikkhū anumānaissāmā’’ti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti  mahāva॰ 383 ādayau| ‘‘kattha paṭikkhitta’’nti? ‘‘campaiyyakai, vinayavatthusmi’’nti| ‘‘kiṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘vinayātisārai duk‍kaṭa’’nti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ vatthu saṅghaina vinicchitaṃ| itipidaṃ vatthu uddhammaṃ, ubbinayaṃ, apagatasatthusāsanaṃ| idaṃ pañ‍camaṃ salākaṃ nikkhipāmi’’|

‘‘kappati, bhantai, āciṇṇakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, āciṇṇakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai – idaṃ mai upajjhāyaina ajjhāciṇṇaṃ, idaṃ mai ācariyaina  ajjhāciṇṇaṃ – taṃ ajjhācaritu’’nti? ‘‘āciṇṇakappau khau, āvusau, aikac‍cau kappati, aikac‍cau na kappatī’’ti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ vatthu saṅghaina vinicchitaṃ| itipidaṃ vatthu uddhammaṃ, ubbinayaṃ, apagatasatthusāsanaṃ| idaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ salākaṃ nikkhipāmi’’|

‘‘kappati, bhantai, amathitakappau’’ti? ‘‘kau sau, āvusau, amathitakappau’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, yaṃ taṃ khīraṃ khīrabhāvaṃ vijahitaṃ, asampattaṃ dadhibhāvaṃ, taṃ bhuttāvinā pavāritaina anatirittaṃ pātu’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti pāci॰ 234| ‘‘kattha paṭikkhitta’’nti? ‘‘sāvatthiyaṃ, suttavibhaṅgai’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘anatirittabhaujanai pācittiya’’nti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ sattamaṃ vatthu saṅghaina vinicchitaṃ| itipidaṃ vatthu uddhammaṃ, ubbinayaṃ, apagatasatthusāsanaṃ| idaṃ sattamaṃ salākaṃ nikkhipāmi’’|

‘‘kappati, bhantai, jaḷaugiṃ pātu’’nti? ‘‘kā sā, āvusau, jaḷaugī’’ti? ‘‘kappati, bhantai, yā sā surā āsutā asampattā maj‍jabhāvaṃ, sā pātu’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti pāci॰ 326| ‘‘kattha paṭikkhitta’’nti? ‘‘kausambiyaṃ, suttavibhaṅgai’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti| ‘‘surāmairayapānai pācittiya’’nti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ vatthu saṅghaina vinicchitaṃ| itipidaṃ vatthu uddhammaṃ, ubbinayaṃ, apagatasatthusāsanaṃ| idaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ salākaṃ nikkhipāmi’’|

‘‘kappati, bhantai, adasakaṃ nisīdana’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti pāci॰ 531 ādayau| ‘‘kattha paṭikkhitta’’nti? ‘‘sāvatthiyaṃ, suttavibhaṅgai’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘chaidanakai pācittiya’’nti|

‘‘suṇātu mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ navamaṃ vatthu  saṅghaina vinicchitaṃ| itipidaṃ vatthu uddhammaṃ, ubbinayaṃ, apagatasatthusāsanaṃ| idaṃ navamaṃ salākaṃ nikkhipāmi’’|

‘‘kappati, bhantai, jātarūparajata’’nti? ‘‘nāvusau, kappatī’’ti pārā॰ 580 ādayau| ‘‘kattha paṭikkhitta’’nti| ‘‘rājagahai, suttavibhaṅgai’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇai pācittiya’’nti|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| idaṃ dasamaṃ vatthu saṅghaina vinicchitaṃ| itipidaṃ vatthu uddhammaṃ, ubbinayaṃ, apagatasatthusāsanaṃ| idaṃ dasamaṃ salākaṃ nikkhipāmi|

‘‘suṇātu  mai, bhantai, saṅghau| imāni dasa vatthūni saṅghaina vinicchitāni| itipimāni dasavatthūni uddhammāni, ubbinayāni, apagatasatthusāsanānī’’ti|

458. ‘‘nihatamaitaṃ, āvusau, adhikaraṇaṃ, santaṃ vūpasantaṃ suvūpasantaṃ| api ca maṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, saṅghamajjhaipi imāni dasa vatthūni pucchaiyyāsi – taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sañ‍ñattiyā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā raivatau āyasmantaṃ sabbakāmiṃ saṅghamajjhaipi imāni dasa vatthūni pucchi| puṭṭhau puṭṭhau āyasmā sabbakāmī vissaj‍jaisi| imāya khau pana vinayasaṅgītiyā satta bhikkhusatāni anūnāni anadhikāni ahaisuṃ, tasmāyaṃ vinayasaṅgīti ‘‘sattasatikā’’ti vuc‍catīti|

dutiyabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

sattasatikakkhandhakau dvādasamau|

imamhi  khandhakai vatthū pañ‍cavīsati|

tassuddānaṃ –

dasa  vatthūni pūraitvā, kammaṃ dūtaina pāvisi|

cattārau puna rūpañ‍ca, kausambi ca pāvaiyyakau||

maggau sauraiyyaṃ saṅkassaṃ, kaṇṇakuj‍jaṃ udumbaraṃ|

sahajāti ca majjhaisi, assausi kaṃ nu khau mayaṃ||

pattanāvāya uj‍javi, rahausi upanāmayaṃ dūrataupi udapādi (ka॰)|

garu dāruṇaṃ (syā॰) saṅghau ca vaisāliṃ, maittā saṅghau ubbāhikāti||

sattasatikakkhandhakau niṭṭhitau|


cūḷavaggau cul‍lavaggau (sī॰) niṭṭhitau|

cūḷavaggapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
